# Siva Purana. Chapter 3

## Section 7 - Śatarudra-saṃhitā

## Chapter 1 - The five incarnations of the supreme Brahman

## \|\| 7.1.1 \|\|

*vande mahānandamanantalīlammaheśvaraṃ sarvvavibhummahāntam \|*  
*gaurīpriyaṃ kārtikavighnarājasamudbhavaṃ śaṃkaramādidevam \|*

*śaunaka uvāca \|*  
*vyāsaśiṣyamahābhāga sūta jñānadayānidhe \|*  
*vada śaṃbhvavatārāṃśca yairakārṣītsatāṃ śivam \|\| 1 \|\|*

I bow to lord Śiva of great bliss and of endles divine sports, who is great and omnipresent, who is the beloved consort of Pārvatī, who is the progenitor of Kārttikeya and Gaṇeśa, and who is the primordial lord of the universe.

Śaunaka said:—

1\. O Sūta, O great fortunate disciple of Vyāsa, O storehouse of wisdom and mercy; please narrate the incarnations of lord Śiva by means of which the lord enhanced the welfare of the good.

## \|\| 7.1.2 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*mune śaunaka sadbhaktyā dattacitto jitendriyaḥ \|*  
*avatārāñchivasyāhaṃ vacmi te munaye śṛṇu \|\| 2 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

2\. O sage Śaunaka, with great devotion I shall narrate the incarnations of Śiva to you a sage. Listen to it with attention and control over the senses.

## \|\| 7.1.3 \|\|

*etatpṛṣṭaḥ purā nandī śivamūrtissatāṃ gatiḥ \|*  
*sanatkumāreṇa mune tamuvāca śivaṃ smaran \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. O sage, formerly, Nandin, a form of Śiva and of the good, when asked by Sanatkumāra, told him the narrative with devotion to Siva.

## \|\| 7.1.4 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*asaṃkhyātā hi kalpeṣu vibhoḥ sarvveśvarasya vai \|*  
*avatārāstathāpīha vacmyahaṃ tānyathāmati \|\| 4 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

4\. The incarnations of the omnipresent lord Śiva are countless in the different aeons. Yet I shall narrate them as far as I know.

## \|\| 7.1.5 \|\|

*ekonaviṃśakaḥ kalpo vijñeyaḥ śveta lohitaḥ \|*  
*sadyojātāvatārastu prathamaḥ parikīrtitaḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. The nineteenth Kalpa is known as Śvetalohita. The first incarnation is glorified as the incarnation of Sadyojāta.

## \|\| 7.1.6 \|\|

*tasmiṃstatparamaṃ brahma dhyāyato brahmaṇastathā \|*  
*utpannastu śikhāyuktaḥ kumāraḥ śvetalohitaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. In that Kalpa, Śvetalohita, endowed with a tuft, was born as the son of Brahmā as he meditated upon the supreme Brahman.

## \|\| 7.1.7 \|\|

*taṃ dṛṣṭvā puruṣaṃ brahmā brahmarūpiṇamīśvaram \|*  
*jñātvā dhyātvā sa hṛdaye vavande prayatāñjaliḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. On seeing that Being in the form of Brahman and realising that to be Īśvara, Brahmā meditated on in his heart and saluted him with palms joined in reverence.

## \|\| 7.1.8 \|\|

*sadyojātaṃ śivaṃ buddhvā jaharṣa bhuvaneśvaraḥ \|*  
*muhurmuhuśca sadbuddhyā paraṃ taṃ samacintayat \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Knowing that Sadyojāta to be Śiva, Brahmā, the lord of the worlds became delighted. With pure intelligence he pondered over that great Being again and again.

## \|\| 7.1.9 \|\|

*tato'sya dhyāyataḥ śvetāḥ prādurbhūtā yaśasvinaḥ \|*  
*kumārāḥ paravijñānaparabrahmasvarūpiṇaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. As he thus meditated, sons white in colour and famous for valour, were born to him. They were of great wisdom and had the form of the Supreme Brahman.

## \|\| 7.1.10 \|\|

*sunando nandanaścaiva viśvanandopanandanau \|*  
*śiṣyāstasya mahātmāno yaistadbrahma samāvṛtam \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. They were Sunanda, Nandana, Viśvananda and Upanandana. They were the disciples of that noble soul. By them the Brahman was encircled.

## \|\| 7.1.11 \|\|

*sadyojātaśca vai śambhurdadau jñānaṃ ca vedhase \|*  
*sargaśaktimapi prītyā prasannaḥ parameśvaraḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. The delighted great lord, the Sadyojāta incarnation of Śiva, lovingly bestowed on Brahmā the perfect wisdom and the power of creation.

## \|\| 7.1.12 \|\|

*tato viṃśatimaḥ kalpo rakto nāma prakīrtitaḥ \|*  
*brahmā yatra mahātejā raktavarṇamadhārayat \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. The next Kalpa, the twentieth one is named and glorified as Rakta (red) wherein Brahmā assumed a great splendour red in hue.

## \|\| 7.1.13 \|\|

*dhyāyataḥ putrakāmasya prādurbhū to vidhessutaḥ \|*  
*raktamālyāmbaradharo raktākṣo raktabhūṣaṇaḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. As Brahmā desirous of sons meditated, a son appeared in front of him wearing red garments and garlands. His eyes were red and his ornaments too were red.

## \|\| 7.1.14 \|\|

*sa taṃ dṛṣṭvā mahātmānaṃ kumāraṃ dhyānamāśritaḥ \|*  
*vāmadevaṃ śivaṃ jñātvā praṇanāma kṛtāṃjaliḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. On seeing that noble-souled son, he resorting to meditation knew him as the Vāmadeva incarnation of Śiva and so bowed to him with palms joined in reverence.

## \|\| 7.1.15 \|\|

*tatastasya sutā hyāsaṃścatvāro raktavāsasaḥ \|*  
*virajāśca vivāhaśca viśoko viśvabhāvanaḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. Your sons wearing red garments were born of him Viz. Virajas, Vivāha, Viśoka and Viśvabhāvana.

## \|\| 7.1.16 \|\|

*vāmadevaḥ sa vai śambhurdadau jñānaṃ ca vedhase \|*  
*sargaśaktimapi prītyā prasannaḥ parameśvaraḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. The delighted Parameśvara, the Vāmadeva incarnation of Śiva bestowed on Brahmā lovingly, the power of cation and perfect wisdom.

## \|\| 7.1.17 \|\|

*ekaviṃśatimaḥ kalpaḥ pītavāsā iti smṛtaḥ \|*  
*brahmā yatra mahābhāgaḥ pītavāsā babhūva ha \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. The twenty-first Kalpa is said to be Pītavāsas wherein Brahmā of great achievement became yellow-robed.

## \|\| 7.1.18 \|\|

*dhyāyataḥ putrakāmasya vidherjātaḥ kumārakaḥ \|*  
*pītavastrādika prauḍho mahātejā mahābhujaḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. As Brahmā desirous of sons meditated, a son born unto him mature, of great arms and great splendour and wearing yellow garments.

## \|\| 7.1.19-20 \|\|

*taṃ dṛṣṭvā dhyānasaṃyuktaṃ jñātvā tatpuruṣaṃ śivam \|*  
*praṇanāma tato buddhyā gāyatrīṃ śāṃkarīṃ vidhiḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

*japitvā tu mahādevīṃ sarvalokanamaskṛtām \|*  
*prasannastu mahādevo dhyānayuktena cetasā \|\| 20 \|\|*

19-20. On seeing him engrossed in meditation and realising that Being as Śiva, the Creator bowed to him after performing the Japa of Śiva-Gāyatrī, who is the great goddess, bowed to by all the worlds. The great god was delighted with the mind in contemplation.

## \|\| 7.1.21 \|\|

*tato'sya pārśvato divyāḥ prādurbhūtāḥ kumārakāḥ \|*  
*pītavastrā hi sakalā yogamārgapravartakāḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. Then from his sides sons of divine features issued forth. All of them had yellow garments and they were the instigators of the Yogic path.

## \|\| 7.1.22 \|\|

*tatastasmingate kalpe pītavarṇe svayaṃbhuvaḥ \|*  
*punaranyaḥ pravṛttastu kalpo nāmnā śivastu sa \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. When that Kalpa of yellow colour of the self-born lord elapsed, another Kalpa set in and it was named Śiva.

## \|\| 7.1.23 \|\|

*ekārṇave saṃvyatīte divyavarṣasahasrake \|*  
*sraṣṭukāmaḥ prajā brahmā cintayāmāsa duḥkhitaḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. After a thousand divine years had elapsed and the entire universe had become one vast ocean, Brahmā desirous of creating subjects thought in sorrow.

## \|\| 7.1.24 \|\|

*tato'paśyanmahātejā prādurbhūtaṃ kumārakam \|*  
*kṛṣṇavarṇaṃ mahāvīryaṃ dīpyamānaṃ svatejasā \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. That lord of great splendour saw a son coming into view. He was black in colour, possessed of great strength. He was shining in his own splendour.

## \|\| 7.1.25 \|\|

*dhṛtakṛṣṇāmbaroṣṇīṣaṃ kṛṣṇayajñopavītinam \|*  
*kṛṣṇena maulināyuktaṃ kṛṣṇasnānānulepanam \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. He was clad in black clothes and a black turban. He wore a black sacred thread. He had a black coronet, black toilet and unguent articles.

## \|\| 7.1.26 \|\|

*sa taṃ dṛṣṭvā mahātmānamaghoraṃ ghoravikramam \|*  
*vavande devadeveśamadbhutaṃ kṛṣṇapiṃgalam \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. On seeing that noble soul of terrible exploits, (named) Aghora (non-terrible), he worshipped him—the lord of god, of wonderful black and tawny colour.

## \|\| 7.1.27 \|\|

*aghoraṃ tu tato brahmā brahmarūpaṃ vyaciṃtayat \|*  
*tuṣṭāva vāgbhiriṣṭābhirbhaktavatsalamavyayam \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Brahmā then contemplated on Aghora in the form of Brahman. He eulogised that unperishing Being favourably disposed towards his devotees, by means of pleasing words.

## \|\| 7.1.28 \|\|

*athāsya pārśvataḥ kṛṣṇāḥ kṛṣṇasnānānulepanāḥ \|*  
*catvārastu mahātmānaḥ saṃbabhūvuḥ kumārakāḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. Then from his sides sprang up four noble-souled sons all of whom were black in colour and had black toilet and unguent articles.

## \|\| 7.1.29 \|\|

*kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇaśikhaścaiva kṛṣṇā syaḥ kṛṣṇakaṇṭhadhṛk \|*  
*iti te'vyaktanāmānaḥ śivarūpāḥ sutejasaḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. These brilliant beings in the form of Śiva had the clear names Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇaśikḥa, Kṛṣṇāsya and Kṛṣṇakaṇṭha-dhṛk.

## \|\| 7.1.30 \|\|

*evaṃbhūtā mahātmāno brahmaṇaḥ sṛṣṭihetave \|*  
*yogaṃ pravarttayā māsurghorākhyaṃ mahadadbhutam \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. The noble-souled beings of this nature initiated the great and wonderful Yoga called Ghora for the purpose of Brahmā’s creation.

## \|\| 7.1.31 \|\|

*athānyo brahmaṇaḥ kalpaḥ prāvarttata munīśvarāḥ \|*  
*viśvarūpa iti khyāto nāmataḥ paramādbhutaḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. O great sages, then another extremely wonderful Kalpa of Brahmā, named Viśvarūpa began to function.

## \|\| 7.1.32 \|\|

*brahmaṇaḥ putrakāmasya dhyāyato manasā śivam \|*  
*prādurbhūtā mahānādā viśvarūpā sarasvatī \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. As Brahmā, desirous of sons, meditated on Śiva mentally, Sarasvatī (Goddess of speech) of great sound manifested herself. She was Viśvarūpā (Universe-formed).

## \|\| 7.1.33 \|\|

*tathāvidhaḥ sa bhagavānīśānaḥ parameśvaraḥ \|*  
*śuddhasphaṭikasaṃkāśaḥ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitaḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. Īśāna of such renowned features, supreme lord of crystal-like pure brilliance and bedecked in all ornaments manifested himself.

## \|\| 7.1.34 \|\|

*taṃ dṛṣṭvā praṇanāmāsau brahmeśānamajaṃ vibhum \|*  
*sarvagaṃ sarvadaṃ sarvaṃ surūpaṃ rūpavarjitam \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. On seeing Īśāna the unborn lord, all pervading, all-bestowing, the all-in-all, of good forms as well as formless, Brahmā bowed to him.

## \|\| 7.1.35 \|\|

*īśāno'pi tathādiśya sanmārgaṃ brahmaṇe vibhuḥ \|*  
*saśaktiḥ kalpayāṃcakre sa bālāṃścaturaḥ śubhān \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. Lord Īśāna after instructing the path of the \[good\] to Brahmā, created four auspicious sons in collaboration with his Energy.

## \|\| 7.1.36 \|\|

*jaṭīmuṇḍī śikhaṇḍī ca arddhamuṇḍaśca jajñire \|*  
*yogenādiśya saddharmaṃ kṛtvā yogagatiṃ gatāḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. There were born Jaṭin, Muṇḍin, Sikhaṇḍin \[Śikhaṇḍin?\] and Ardhamuṇḍa. By means of Yoga they imparted good virtue and attained the goal of Yoga.

## \|\| 7.1.37 \|\|

*evaṃ saṃkṣepataḥ proktaḥ sadyādīnāṃ samudbhavaḥ \|*  
*sanatkumāra sarvajña lokānāṃ hitakāmyayā \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. Thus I have succinctly narrated the origin of Sadyojāta and others, O Sanatkumāra, O omniscient one, being desirous of the welfare of all the worlds.

## \|\| 7.1.38 \|\|

*atha teṣāṃ mahāprājña vyavahāraṃ yathāyatham \|*  
*trilokahitakāraṃ hi sarvaṃ brahmāṇḍasaṃsthitam \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Then, O intelligent one, their dealings are beneficial to the three worlds and everything that is existent in the universe.

## \|\| 7.1.39 \|\|

*īśānaḥ puruṣo ghoro vāmasaṃjñastathaiva ca \|*  
*brahmasaṃjño maheśasya mūrtayaḥ paṃca viśrutāḥ \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. The five famous forms of Maheśa are thus named Īśāna, Puruṣa, Aghora, Vāma and Brahman. (also see Appendix on the five faces of Śiva)

## \|\| 7.1.40 \|\|

*īśānaḥ śivarūpaśca garīyānprathamaḥ smṛtaḥ \|*  
*bhoktāraṃ prakṛteḥ sākṣātkṣetrajñamadhitiṣṭhati \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. Īśāna form of Śiva is the first and the principal one. It directly occupies and presides over the individual soul, the enjoyer of Prakṛti.

## \|\| 7.1.41 \|\|

*śaivastatpuruṣākhyaśca svarūpo hi dvitīyakaḥ \|*  
*guṇāśrayātmakaṃ bhogyaṃ sarvajñamadhitiṣṭhati \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. Tatpuruṣa form of Śiva is the second. It occupies and presides over every object of enjoyment, the support of the attributes.

## \|\| 7.1.42 \|\|

*dharmāya svāṃgasaṃyuktaṃ buddhitattvaṃ pinākinaḥ \|*  
*aghorākhyasvarūpo yastiṣṭhatyaṃtastṛtīyakaḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. The third form of Śiva is Aghora that stands within, occupies.and presides over the principle of intelligence with all its ramifications for the sake of Dharma.

## \|\| 7.1.43 \|\|

*vāmadevāhvayo rūpaścaturthaḥ śaṅkarasya hi \|*  
*ahaṃkṛteradhiṣṭhāno bahukāryakaraḥ sadā \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. The fourth form of Śiva is Vāmadeva who presides over the ego and is engaged in many incessant activities.

## \|\| 7.1.44 \|\|

*īśānāhvasvarūpo hi śaṃkarasyeśvaraḥ sadā \|*  
*śrotrasya vacasaścāpi vibhorvyomnastathaiva ca \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. The Īśāna form of Śiva is the permanent lord of the organ of hearing, speech and the all-pervading sky.

## \|\| 7.1.45 \|\|

*tvakpāṇisparśavāyūnāmīśvaraṃ rūpamaiśvaram \|*  
*puruṣākhyaṃ vicārajñā matimantaḥ pracakṣate \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. Intelligent and wise persons call Tatpuruṣa as the lord of Tvac (organ of touch perception), hand, sense of touch and the wind element.

## \|\| 7.1.46 \|\|

*vapuṣaśca rasasyāpi rūpasyāgnestathaiva ca \|*  
*aghorākhyamadhiṣṭhānaṃ rūpamāhurmanīṣiṇaḥ \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. Intelligent persons call the form Aghora, the support of the physical body, the sense of taste, of colour and of fire as well.

## \|\| 7.1.47 \|\|

*raśanāyāśca pāyośca rasasyāpāṃ tathaiva ca \|*  
*īśvaraṃ vāmadevākhyaṃ svarūpaṃ śāṃkaraṃ smṛtam \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. Vāmadeva form of Śiva is remembered as the lord of the organ of taste, of the organ of excretion, of taste itself and of waters as well.

## \|\| 7.1.48 \|\|

*prāṇasya caivopasthasya gaṃdhasya ca bhuvastathā \|*  
*sadyojātāhvayaṃ rūpamīśvaraṃ śāṃkaraṃ viduḥ \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. They know the form of Sadyojāta as the lord of the organ of smell, of smell itself, of the earth and of the generative organ.

## \|\| 7.1.49 \|\|

*ime svarūpāḥ śaṃbhorhi vandanīyāḥ prayatnataḥ \|*  
*śreyorthibhirnarairnityaṃ śreyasāmekahetavaḥ \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. These forms of Śiva, the source of all glory shall be honoured and bowed to strenuously by those who seek glory.

## \|\| 7.1.50 \|\|

*yaḥ paṭhecchṛṇuyādvāpi sadyādīnāṃ samudbhavam \|*  
*sa bhuktvā sakalānkāmānprayāti paramāṃ gatim \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. He who reads and listens to the origin of Sadyojāta and others, enjoys all desires and attains the ultimate goal.

## Chapter 2 - The eight forms of Śiva

## \|\| 7.2.1 \|\|

*\|\| nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*śṛṇu tāta maheśasyāvatārānparamānprabho \|*  
*sarvakāryakarāṃlloke sarvasya sukhadānmune \|\| 1 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

1\. O dear sage, listen to the great incarnations of lord Śiva that carry out all worldly activities and bestow happiness on all.

## \|\| 7.2.2 \|\|

*tasya śaṃbhoḥ pareśasya mūrtyaṣṭakamayaṃ jagat \|*  
*tasminvyāpya sthitaṃ viśvaṃ sūtre maṇigaṇā iva \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. The universe consists of the eight forms of lord Śiva. Pervading that the universe subsists like the pearls in the string.

## \|\| 7.2.3 \|\|

*śarvo bhavastathā rudra ugro bhīmaḥ paśo patiḥ \|*  
*īśānaśca mahādevo mūrtayaścāṣṭa viśrutāḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. The eight presiding deities are Śarva, Bhava, Rudra, Ugra, Bhīma, Paśupati, Īśāna and Mahādeva. They are well known.

## \|\| 7.2.4 \|\|

*bhūmyaṃbhognimarudvyomakṣetrajñārkaniśākarāḥ \|*  
*adhiṣṭhitāśca śarvādyairaṣṭarūpaiḥ śivasya hi \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. By these eight forms of Śiva, viz. Śarva etc, the earth, the waters, the fire, the wind, the sky, the individual soul, the sun and the moon are presided over.

## \|\| 7.2.5 \|\|

*dhatte carācaraṃ viśvaṃ rūpaṃ viśvaṃbharātmakam \|*  
*śaṃkarasya maheśasya śāstrasyaiveti niścayaḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. The conclusion of all sacred lore is that the form of lord Śiva consisting of the earth that holds the entire universe of the mobile and immobile beings is called Śarva.

## \|\| 7.2.6 \|\|

*saṃjīvanaṃ samastasya jagataḥ salilātmakam \|*  
*bhava ityucyate rūpaṃ bhavasya paramātmanaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. The form of the Supreme Soul, consisting of water that enlivens the entire world, is called Bhava.

## \|\| 7.2.7 \|\|

*bahiraṃtarjagadviśvaṃ bibharti spandatesya yam \|*  
*ugra ityucyate sadbhī rūpamugrasya satprabho \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. The form of the great lord Śiva that holds the outer and inner universe and throbs by itself is called Ugra by the noble.

## \|\| 7.2.8 \|\|

*sarvāvakāśadaṃ sarvavyāpakaṃ gaganātmakam \|*  
*rūpaṃ bhīmasya bhīmākhyaṃ bhūpavṛndasva bhedakam \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. That form of lord Śiva consisting of the firmament that pervades everything and affords space to all is scaled Bhīma and it splits and differentiates the group of elements.

## \|\| 7.2.9 \|\|

*sarvātmanāmadhiṣṭhānaṃ sarvakṣetranivāsakam \|*  
*rūpaṃ paśupaterjñeyaṃ paśupāśanikṛntanam \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. That form of Śiva shall be known as Paśupati which is the support of individual souls, and abiding therein splits the bondage that encircles them.

## \|\| 7.2.10 \|\|

*sandīpayañjagatsarvaṃ divākarasamāhvayam \|*  
*īśānākhyaṃ maheśasya rūpaṃ divi visarpati \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Brightening the entire universe in the form of the sun, the form of Śiva, Īśāna, moves about in the heaven.

## \|\| 7.2.11 \|\|

*āpyāyayati yo viśvamamṛtāṃśurniśākaraḥ \|*  
*mahādevasya tadrūpaṃ mahādevasya cāhvayam \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. The moon of nectarlike rays that strengthens and fattens the universe to satiety, is the form of lord Śiva called Mahādeva.

## \|\| 7.2.12 \|\|

*ātmā tasyāṣṭamaṃ rūpaṃ śivasya paramātmanaḥ \|*  
*vyāpiketaramūrtīnāṃ viśvaṃ tasmācchivātmakam \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. The eighth form of Śiva, the great soul, is the Ātman, more pervasive than the other forms. The universe is pervaded by that form.

## \|\| 7.2.13 \|\|

*śākhāḥ puṣyanti vṛkṣasya vṛkṣamūlasya secanāt \|*  
*tadvadasya vapurviśvaṃ puṣyate ca śivārcanāta \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. By watering the root of a tree the branches of the tree blossom. Similarly the body of the universe develops by means of worshipping Śiva.

## \|\| 7.2.14 \|\|

*yatheha putrapautrādeḥ prītyā prīto bhavetpitā \|*  
*tathā viśvasya samprītyā prīto bhavati śaṃkaraḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Just as a father is delighted at the pleasures of sons, grandsons and others, so also Śiva is delighted at the pleasures of the universe.

## \|\| 7.2.15 \|\|

*kriyate yasya kasyāpi dehino yadi nigrahaḥ \|*  
*aṣṭamūrtteraniṣṭaṃ tatkṛtameva na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. Should the slaughter or captivity of a person be carried out, undoubtedly it is an offence committed against the eight-formed lord Śiva.

## \|\| 7.2.16 \|\|

*aṣṭamūrtyātmanā viśvamadhiṣṭhāyāsthitaṃ śivam \|*  
*bhajasva sarvabhāvena rudraṃ paramakāraṇam \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Worship Śiva who presides over the universe in the eightfold forms with all devotion. Worship Rudra, the great cause.

## \|\| 7.2.17 \|\|

*iti proktāḥ svarūpāste vidhiputrāṣṭaviśrutāḥ \|*  
*sarvopakāraniratāḥ sevyāḥ śreyorthibhirnaraiḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. O son of Brahmā, thus the well known eight forms have been explained to you. These forms engaged in helping everyone shall be resorted to by those who seek glory.

Notes on the eight forms of lord Śiva:

(i) Śarva—earth (ii) Bhava—water (iii) Rudra—fire (iv) Ugra— wind (v) Bhīma—ether (vi) Paśupati—soul (vii) Īśāna—sun (viii) Mahādeva—moon.

## Chapter 3 - The half-female incarnation of Lord Śiva

## \|\| 7.3.1 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*śṛṇu tāta mahāprājña vidhikāmaprapūrakam \|*  
*arddhanārīnarākhyaṃ hi śivarūpamanuttamam \|\| 1 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

1\. O dear one of great intellect, please listen to the description of Śiva’s form half-male and half-female, than which there is nothing more excellent and which completes the work of the Creator.

## \|\| 7.3.2 \|\|

*yadā sṛṣṭāḥ prajā sarvāḥ na vyavarddhaṃta vedhasā \|*  
*tadā ciṃtākulo'bhūtsa tena duḥkhena dukhitaḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. When the subjects created by the Creator did not multiply, he was very much distressed and worried.

## \|\| 7.3.3 \|\|

*nabhovāṇī tadābhūdvai sṛṣṭiṃ mithunajāṃ kuru \|*  
*tacchrutvā maithunīṃ sṛṣṭiṃ brahmā kartumamanyata \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Then a celestial voice said—“Carry on the creation produced by couples.” On hearing it, Brahmā pondered over the creation produced by couples in order to carry it out.

## \|\| 7.3.4 \|\|

*nārīṇāṃ kulamīśānānnirgataṃ na purā yataḥ \|*  
*tato maithunajāṃ sṛṣṭiṃ kartuṃ śeke na padmabhūḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. Since no generation of women had formerly come out of Iśāna, the lotus-born deity (Brahmā) was unable to produce the creation by means of copulation.

## \|\| 7.3.5 \|\|

*prabhāveṇa vinā śaṃbhorna jāyerannimāḥ prajāḥ \|*  
*evaṃ saṃcintayanbrahmā tapaḥ karttuṃ pracakrame \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Brahmā then began to perform penance thinking “Without the power of Śiva, the subjects will not be born.”

## \|\| 7.3.6 \|\|

*śivayā parayā śaktyā saṃyuktaṃ parameśvaram \|*  
*saṃciṃtya hṛdaye prītyā tepe sa paramaṃ tapaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Within his heart he pondered lovingly over lord Śiva united with the great Śakti called Śivā. He then performed great penance.

## \|\| 7.3.7 \|\|

*tīvreṇa tapasā tasya saṃyuktasya svayaṃbhuvaḥ \|*  
*acireṇaiva kālena tutoṣa sa śivo drutam \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Ere long Śiva became satisfied with the severe penance of the self-born lord Brahmā.

## \|\| 7.3.8 \|\|

*tataḥ pūrṇacidīśasya mūrtimāviśya kāmadām \|*  
*arddhanārīnaro bhūtvā tato brahmāntikaṃ haraḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Then assuming the wish-yielding form of ISāna—the perfect consciousness, and in the guise of half-male and halffemale form Śiva approached Brahmā.

## \|\| 7.3.9 \|\|

*taṃ dṛṣṭvā śaṃkaraṃ devaṃ śaktyā praramayānvitam \|*  
*praṇamya daṇḍavadbrahmā sa tuṣṭāva kṛtāñjaliḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. On seeing lord Śiva united with greatŚakti and bowing to him prostrate, Brahmā eulogised him with palms joined in revt:!"ence.

## \|\| 7.3.10 \|\|

*atha devo mahādevo vācā meghagabhīrayā \|*  
*saṃbhavāya susaṃprīto viśvakarttā maheśvaraḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Then the delighted lord Śiva, the creator of the universe, spoke in words majestic in sound like that of rumbling cloud.

## \|\| 7.3.11 \|\|

*īśvara uvāca \|*  
*vatsavatsa mahābhāga mama putra pitāmaha \|*  
*jñātavānasmi sarva tattattvataste manoratha \|\| 11 \|\|*

Lord Śiva said:—

11\. “O dear son of great fortune, O grandfather, I have understood correctly all that you have cherished in your mind.

## \|\| 7.3.12 \|\|

*prajānāmeva vṛddhyarthaṃ tapastaptaṃ tvayādhunā \|*  
*tapasā tena tuṣṭo'smi dadāmi ca tavepsitam \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. It is for the increase of the subjects that this penance has been performed by you now. I am delighted by that penance. I shall confer on you what you desire.”

## \|\| 7.3.13 \|\|

*ityuktvā paramodāraṃ svabhāvamadhuraṃ vacaḥ \|*  
*pṛthakcakāra vapuṣo bhāgāddevīṃ śivāṃ śivaḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. After saying these naturally sweet and magnanimous words, lordŚiva detached Śivā from his body.

## \|\| 7.3.14 \|\|

*tāṃ dṛṣṭvā paramāṃ śaktiṃ pṛthagbhūtāṃ śivāgatām \|*  
*praṇipatya vinītātmā prārthayāmāsa tāṃ vidhiḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. On seeing that great Śakti, detached separately from Śiva, Brahmā bowed to her politely and requested her.

## \|\| 7.3.15 \|\|

*brahmovāca \|*  
*devadevena sṛṣṭohamādau tvatpatinā śive \|*  
*prajāḥ sarvā niyuktāśca śaṃbhunā paramātmanā \|\| 15 \|\|*

Brahmā said:—

15\. “O Śivā, I was created at first by your husband the lord of gods. I was ordered by Śiva the great soul to create the- subjects.

## \|\| 7.3.16 \|\|

*manasā nirmitāḥ sarve śive devādayo mayā \|*  
*na vṛddhimupagacchaṃti sṛjyamānāḥ punaḥpunaḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. O Śivā, the gods and others were mentally created by me. But they do not multiply themselves. Hence they are being created again and again.

## \|\| 7.3.17 \|\|

*mithunaprabhavāmeva kṛtvā sṛṣṭimataḥ param \|*  
*saṃvarddhayitumicchāmi sarvā eva mama prajāḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Hereafter I wish to make all my subjects flourish by making the creation originate from pairs.

## \|\| 7.3.18 \|\|

*na nirgataṃ purā tvatto nārīṇāṃ kulamavyayam \|*  
*tena nārīkulaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ mama śaktirna vidyate \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. The unchanging generation of women has not come out of you before. Hence I do not have the power to create women.

## \|\| 7.3.19 \|\|

*sarvāsāmeva śaktīnāṃ tvattaḥ khalu samudbhavaḥ \|*  
*tasmāttvaṃ paramāṃ śaktiṃ prārthayāmyakhileśvarīm \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. All Śaktis spring up from you. Hence I request you the great Śakti, the goddess of all.

## \|\| 7.3.20 \|\|

*śive nārīkulaṃ sraṣṭuṃ śaktiṃ dehi namo'stu te \|*  
*carācaraṃ jagadviddhi hetormātaḥ śivaṃ priye \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. O Śivā, O mother, O beloved of Śiva, for the increase of the mobile and immobile beings of the universe, give me the power to create women. Obeisance be to you.

## \|\| 7.3.21 \|\|

*anyaṃ tvattaḥ prārthayāmi varaṃ ca varadeśvari \|*  
*dehi me taṃ kṛpāṃ kṛtvā jaganmātarnamo'stu te \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. O goddess bestowing boons, I request for another boon from you. O mother of the universe, take pity on me and grant that too unto me. Obeisance be to you.

## \|\| 7.3.22 \|\|

*carācaravivṛddhyarthamīśenaikena sarvage \|*  
*dakṣasya mama putrasya putrī bhava bhavāmbike \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. O omnipresent mother, be born as the daughter of Dakṣa, my son, for the increase of the mobile and immobile beings through only Īśa.”

## \|\| 7.3.23 \|\|

*evaṃ saṃyācitā devī brahmaṇā parameśvarī \|*  
*tathāstviti vacaḥ procya tacchaktiṃ vidhaye dadau \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Thus implored by the Creator, the Supreme goddess consented and bestowed that power upon the Creator.

## \|\| 7.3.24 \|\|

*tasmāddhi sā śivā devī śivaśaktirjaganmayī \|*  
*śaktimekāṃ bhruvormadhyātsasarjātmasamaprabhām \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. Hence that goddess Śivā, the Śakti of Śiva constituting the universe, created a Śakti equal in lustre unto her self, from the middle of her eyebrows.

## \|\| 7.3.25 \|\|

*tāmāha prahasanprekṣya śaktiṃ devavaro haraḥ \|*  
*kṛpāsindhurmaheśāno līlākārī bhavāmbikām \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Śiva, the foremost among gods, the ocean of mercy, the great Īśāna indulging in divine sports saw that Śakti and laughingly spoke to her.

## \|\| 7.3.26 \|\|

*śiva uvāca \|*  
*tapasārādhitā devi brahmaṇā parameṣṭhinā \|*  
*prasannā bhava suprītyā kuru tasyākhilepsitam \|\| 26 \|\|*

Lord Śiva said:—

26\. “O goddess, yon have been propitiated by Brahmā, through his penance. Lovingly become pleased with him and fulfil his desires.”

## \|\| 7.3.27 \|\|

*tāmājñāṃ parameśasya śirasā pratigṛhya sā \|*  
*brahmaṇo vacanāddevī dakṣasya duhitābhavat \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Receiving that behest of lord Śiva with her bent head, the goddess became the daughter of Dakṣa on the importunity of Brahmā.

## \|\| 7.3.28 \|\|

*dattvaivamatulāṃ śaktiṃ brahmaṇo sā śivā mune \|*  
*viveśa dehaṃ śaṃbhorhi śaṃbhuścāntardadhe prabhuḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. O sage, after giving the unequalled power to Brahmā, the goddess śivā entered the body of Śiva. And lord Śiva vanished from the scene.

## \|\| 7.3.29 \|\|

*tadāprabhṛti loke'sminstriyā bhāgaḥ prakalpitaḥ \|*  
*ānandaṃ prāpa sa vidhiḥ sṛṣṭirjātā ca maithunī \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. Ever since then, the section of women created in the world, Brahmā attained bliss. Creation became copulatory.

## \|\| 7.3.30 \|\|

*etatte kathitaṃ tāta śivarūpaṃ mahottamam \|*  
*arddhanārīnarārddhaṃ hi mahāmaṃgaladaṃ satām \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. Thus O dear, the excellent form of Śiva, half-male and half-female that bestows auspiciousness upon the has been told.

## \|\| 7.3.31 \|\|

*etadākhyānamanaghaṃ yaḥ paṭhcchṛṇuyādapi \|*  
*sa bhuktvā sakalānbhogānprayāti paramāṃ gatim \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. He who reads or listens to this pious narrative enjoys all pleasures and attains to the greatest goal.

## Chapter 4 - The story of Ṛṣabha

## \|\| 7.4.1 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*sanatkumāra sarvajña caritaṃ śāṃkaraṃ mudā \|*  
*rudreṇa kathitaṃ prītyā brahmaṇe sukhadaṃ sadā \|\| 1 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

1\. O omniscient Sanatkumāra, the story of Śiva that ever confers happiness, was gladly and lovingly narrated by Śiva to Brahmā.

## \|\| 7.4.2-3 \|\|

*śiva uvāca \|*  
*saptame caiva vārāhe kalpe manvantarābhidhe \|*  
*kalpeśvaro'tha bhagavānsarvaṃ lokaprakāśanaḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

*marnorvaivasvatasyaiva te praputro bhaviṣyati \|*  
*tadā caturyugāścaiva tasminmanvantare vidhe \|\| 3 \|\|*

Lord Śiva said:—

2-3. In the seventh Vārāha Kalpa, in the Vaivasvata Manvantara, the lord Kalpeśvara who illuminates all the worlds will be born as your great grandson and son of Vaivasvata Manu. O Creator, in that Manvantara all the four Yugas take place.

## \|\| 7.4.4 \|\|

*anugrahārthaṃ lokānāṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ hitāya ca \|*  
*utpaśyāmi vidhe brahmandvāparākhyayugāntike \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. For the welfare of the worlds and for the benefit of the brahmins O Creator, whatever take place in the Yuga called Dvāpara, I foresee now.

## \|\| 7.4.5-6 \|\|

*yugapravṛttyā ca tadā tasmiṃśca prathame yuge \|*  
*dvāpare prathame brahmanyadā vyāsaḥ svayaṃprabhuḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

*tadāhaṃ brāhmaṇārthāya kalau tasminyugāntike \|*  
*bhaviṣyāmi śivāyuktaḥ śveto nāma mahāmuniḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

5-6. In that first Yuga, towards the close of Dvāpara and the beginning of Kali when Vyāsa is Svayamprabhu I will be born as the great sage named Śveta, with a tuft, for the welfare of Brahmins.

## \|\| 7.4.7 \|\|

*himavacchikhare ramye chāgale parvatottame \|*  
*tadā śiṣyāḥ śikhāyuktā bhaviṣyanti vidhe mama \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Then O Brahmā, on the mountain Chāgala—the beautiful summit of Himavat, will be born my disciples endowed with tuft.

## \|\| 7.4.8 \|\|

*śvetaḥ śvetaśikhaścaiva śvetāśvaḥ śvetalohitaḥ \|*  
*catvāro dhyānayogātte gamiṣyanti puraṃ mama \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Those four disciples Śveta, Śvetaśikha, Śvetāśva, and Śvetalohita will come to my city by the Yoga of meditation.

## \|\| 7.4.9 \|\|

*tato bhaktā bhaviṣyanti jñātvā māṃ tattvato'vyayam \|*  
*janmamṛtyujarāhīnāḥ parabrahmasamādhayaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. Realising my unchanging real form, the devotees will become devoid of birth, death and old age and will meditate on the Supreme Brahman.

## \|\| 7.4.10 \|\|

*draṣṭuṃ śakyo narairnāhamṛte dhyānātpitāmaha \|*  
*dānadharmādibhirvatsa sādhanaiḥ karmahetubhiḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. O dear Pitāmaha, except through meditation I can not be seen by men by resorting to charitable gifts and sacred rites that bring about the bondage of Karman.

## \|\| 7.4.11 \|\|

*dvitīye dvāpare vyāsaḥ satyo nāma prajāpatiḥ \|*  
*yadā tadā bhaviṣyāmi sutāro nāmataḥ kalau \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. In the second aeon of Dvāpara when the patriarch Satya becomes Vyāsa I will be born as Sutāra and continue to live in Kali age.

## \|\| 7.4.12 \|\|

*tatrāpi me bhaviṣyanti śiṣyā vedavido dvijāḥ \|*  
*dundubhiḥ śatarūpaśca hṛṣīkaḥ ketumāṃstathā \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. There too my disciples will be the four Brahmins well-versed in the Vedic lore viz. Dundubhi, Śatarūpa, Hṛṣīka and Ketumān.

## \|\| 7.4.13 \|\|

*catvāro dhyānayogātte gamiṣyanti puraṃ mama \|*  
*tato muktā bhaviṣyanti jñātvā māṃ tattvato'vyayam \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. By resorting to the Yoga of meditation all the four go to my city and then realising my unchanging real form they will become liberated.

## \|\| 7.4.14 \|\|

*tṛtīye dvāpare caiva yadā vyāsastu bhārgavaḥ \|*  
*tadāpyahaṃ bhaviṣyāmi damanastu purāntike \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. In the third aeon of Dvāpara in the age of Bhārgava Vyāsa I will be born as Damana at the outskirts of the city.

## \|\| 7.4.15 \|\|

*tatrāpi ca bhaviṣyanti catvāro mama putrakāḥ \|*  
*viśokaśca viśeṣaśca vipāpaḥ pāpanāśanaḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. Four sons be born of me there viz—Viśoka, Viśesa, Vipāpa and Pāpanāśana.

## \|\| 7.4.16 \|\|

*śiṣyaiḥ sāhāyyaṃ vyāsasya kariṣye caturānana \|*  
*nivṛttimārgaṃ sudṛḍhaṃ varttayiṣye kalāviha \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. O four-faced deity, I will help Vyāsa and will propagate firmly the path of renunciation in the Kali age.

## \|\| 7.4.17 \|\|

*caturthe dvāpare caiva yadā vyāsoṃ'girāḥ smṛtaḥ \|*  
*tadāpyahaṃ bhaviṣyāmi suhotro nāma nāmataḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. In the fourth aeon of Dvāpara in the age of Aṅgiras Vyāsa, I shall be born as Suhotra.

## \|\| 7.4.18 \|\|

*tatrāpi mama te putrāścatvāro yogasādhakāḥ \|*  
*bhaviṣyaṃti mahātmānastannāmāni bruve vidhe \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. There also four sons will be born of me all aspiring after the Yogic path and noble-souled. O Brahmā, I shall mention their names.

## \|\| 7.4.19 \|\|

*sumukho durmukhaścaiva dudarbho duratikramaḥ \|*  
*śiṣyaiḥ sāhāyyaṃ vyāsasya kariṣye'haṃ tadā vidhe \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. They are Sumukha, Durmukha, Durdarbha and Duratikrama. O Brahmā, I shall then render help to Vyāsa through the disciples.

## \|\| 7.4.20 \|\|

*pañcame dvāpare caiva vyāsastu savitā smṛtaḥ \|*  
*tadā yogī bhaviṣyāmi kaṃko nāma mahātapāḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. In the fifth aeon of Dvāpara there will be Savitṛ Vyāsa. I shall be born as a Yogin of great penance and called Kaṅka.

## \|\| 7.4.21 \|\|

*tatrāpi mama te putrāścatvāro yogasādhakāḥ \|*  
*bhaviṣyanti mahātmānastannāmāni śṛṇuṣva me \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. There also four sons will be born of me, all aspiring after the Yogic path and noble-souled. Please hear their names from me.

## \|\| 7.4.22 \|\|

*sanakaḥ sanātanaścaiva prabhuryaśca sanandanaḥ \|*  
*vibhuḥ sanatkumāraśca nirmalo nirahaṃkṛtiḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. They are Sanaka, Sanātana, Sanandana and Sanatkumāra freed from sin and egotism.

## \|\| 7.4.23 \|\|

*tatrāpi kaṃkanāmāhaṃ sāhāyyaṃ saviturvidhe \|*  
*vyāsasya hi kariṣyāmi nivṛttipathavarddhakaḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. There also O Brahmā, I, named Kaṅka, enhancing the path of renunciation shall render help to Vyāsa in the form of Savitṛ.

## \|\| 7.4.24 \|\|

*parivarte punaḥ ṣaṣṭhe dvāpare lokakārakaḥ \|*  
*kartā vedavibhāgasya mṛtyurvyāso bhaviṣyati \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. When the sixth aeon of Dvāpara takes place there will be Mṛtyu Vyāsa who will classify the Vedas.

## \|\| 7.4.25 \|\|

*tadā'pyahaṃ bhaviṣyāmi lokākṣirnāma nāmataḥ \|*  
*vyāsasya susāhā yyārthaṃ nivṛttipathavarddhanaḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Then I will be born as Lokākṣi, making the path of renunciation flourish and helping Vyāsa.

## \|\| 7.4.26 \|\|

*tatrāpi śiṣyāścatvāro bhaviṣyanti dṛḍhavratāḥ \|*  
*sudhāmā virajāścaiva saṃjayo vijayastathā \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Four disciples of steady rites viz. Sudhāmā, Virajas, Sañjaya and Vijaya will be born then.

## \|\| 7.4.27 \|\|

*saptame parivarte tu yadā vyāsaḥ śatakratuḥ \|*  
*tadāpyahaṃ bhaviṣyāmi jaigīṣavyo vibhurvidhe \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. O Brahmā, in the seventh aeon of Śatakratu Vyāsa I shall be born as the lord Jaigīṣavya.

## \|\| 7.4.28 \|\|

*yogaṃ saṃdraḍhayiṣyāmi mahāyogavicakṣaṇaḥ \|*  
*kāśyāṃ guhāntare saṃstho divyadeśe kuśāstariḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. Seated on a Kuśa mat in a divine spot in the middle of a mountain-cavern in Kāśī I, an expert in the great Yoga, will stabilise the path of Yoga.

## \|\| 7.4.29 \|\|

*sāhāyyaṃ ca kariṣyāmi vyāsasya hi śatakratoḥ \|*  
*uddhariṣyāmi bhaktāṃśca saṃsārabhayato vidhe \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. O Brahmā, I shall render help to Śatakratu Vyāsa I shall lift up the devotees from the fear of worldly existence.

## \|\| 7.4.30 \|\|

*tatrāpi mama catvāro bhaviṣyanti sutā yuge \|*  
*sārasvataśca yogīśo meghavāhaḥ suvāhanaḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. There also in that aeon, four sons will be born of me viz. Sārasvata, Yogīśa, Meghavāha and Suvāhana.

## \|\| 7.4.31 \|\|

*aṣṭame parivarte hi vasiṣṭho munisattamaḥ \|*  
*karttā vedavibhāgasya vedavyāso bhaviṣyati \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. In the eighth aeon there will be Vasiṣṭha Vedavyāsa—the excellent sage who will classify the Vedas.

## \|\| 7.4.32 \|\|

*tatrāpyahaṃ bhaviṣyāmi nāmato dadhivāhanaḥ \|*  
*vyāsasya hi kariṣyāmi sāhāyyaṃ yogavittama \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. There I will be born as Dadhivāhana by name. As the foremost among those who know Yoga I will render help to Vyāsa.

## \|\| 7.4.33 \|\|

*kapilaścāsuriḥ pañcaśikhaḥ śālvalapūrvakaḥ \|*  
*catvāro yoginaḥ putrā bhaviṣyanti samā mama \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. Four sons, all Yogins on a par with me, viz—Kapila, Āsuri, Śālvala and Pañcaśikha will be born of me.

## \|\| 7.4.34 \|\|

*navame parivarte tu tasminneva yuge vidhe \|*  
*bhaviṣyati muniśreṣṭho vyāsaḥ sārasvatāhvayaḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. O Brahmā, in the ninth aeon of the same Yuga, there will be Sārasvata Vyāsa, the excellent sage.

## \|\| 7.4.35 \|\|

*vyāsasya dhyāyatastasya nivṛttipathavṛddhaye \|*  
*tadāpyahaṃ bhaviṣyāmi ṛṣabho nāmataḥ smṛtaḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. As Vyāsa sits in meditation for the flourish of the path of renunciation, I will be born as Ṛṣabha.

## \|\| 7.4.36 \|\|

*parāśaraśca gargaśca bhārgavo giriśastathā \|*  
*catvārastatra śiṣyā me bhaviṣyanti suyoginaḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. There, Parāśara, Garga, Bhārgava and Giriśa, all good Yogins, will be my four disciples.

## \|\| 7.4.37 \|\|

*taiḥ sākaṃ draḍhayiṣyāmi yogamārgaṃ prajāpate \|*  
*kariṣyāmi sāhāyyaṃ vai vedavyāsasya sanmune \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. O Patriarch, with them I will stabilise the path of Yoga. O good sage, I will render help to Vedavyāsa.

## \|\| 7.4.38 \|\|

*tena rūpeṇa bhaktānāṃ bahūnāṃ duḥkhināṃ vidhe \|*  
*uddhāraṃ bhavato'haṃ vai kariṣyāmi dayākaraḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. O Brahmā, in that form, I, the merciful, will cause the uplift of many distressed devotees, from the worldly existence.

## \|\| 7.4.39 \|\|

*so'vatāro vidhe me hi ṛṣabhākhyassuyogakṛt \|*  
*sārasvatavyāsamanaḥ karttā nānotikārakaḥ \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. O Brahmā, such is my incarnation named Ṛṣabha. It causes Yogic practice; it fulfils the desire of Vyāsa in the guise of Sārasvata and it consists of many divine sports.

## \|\| 7.4.40 \|\|

*avatāreṇa me yena bhadrāyurnṛpabālakaḥ \|*  
*jīvito hi mṛtaḥ kṣveḍadoṣato janakojjhitaḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. Bhadrāyu a prince who had been abandoned by his father and who had died of poison infection, was resuscitated by me in that in carnation.

## \|\| 7.4.41 \|\|

*prāpte'tha ṣoḍaśe varṣe tasya rājaśiśoḥ punaḥ \|*  
*yayau tadveśma sahasā ṛṣabhaḥ sa madātmakaḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. When that prince reached his sixteenth year I as Ṛṣabba, went to his mansion suddenly.

## \|\| 7.4.42 \|\|

*pūjitastena sa muniḥ sadrūpaśca kṛpānidhiḥ \|*  
*upādideśa taddharmānrājyayogānprajāpate \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. O patriarch, being duly worshipped by him, that sage, of good form, the store-house of mercy instructed him in virtues in connection with the administration of his kingdom.

## \|\| 7.4.43 \|\|

*tataḥ sa kavacaṃ divyaṃ śaṃkhaṃ khaṅgaṃ ca bhāsvaram \|*  
*dadau tasmai prasannātmā sarvaśatruvināśanam \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. Becoming excessively delighted, he gave him a divine conch and a lustruous sword that could destroy his enemies.

## \|\| 7.4.44 \|\|

*tadaṅga bhasmanāmṛśya kṛpayā dīnavatsalaḥ \|*  
*sa dvādaśasahasrasya gajānāṃ ca balaṃ dadau \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. The lord, favourably disposed to the indigent, smeared his body with the ashes from his own body and gave him the strength of twelve thousand elephants.

## \|\| 7.4.45 \|\|

*iti bhadrāyuṣaṃ samyaganuśvāsya samātṛkam \|*  
*yayau svairagatastasyāṃ pūjitastvṛṣabhaḥ prabhuḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. After consoling Bhadrāyus and his mother very well, lord Ṛṣabha after being duly worshipped by them went as he pleased.

## \|\| 7.4.46 \|\|

*bhadrāyurapi rājarṣirjitvā ripugaṇānvidhe \|*  
*rājyaṃ cakāra dharmeṇa vivāhya kīrttimālinīm \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. O Brahmā, the saintly king Bhadrāyus conquered all his enemies, married Kīrtimālinī and virtuously ruled the kingdom.

## \|\| 7.4.47 \|\|

*itthaṃ prabhāvaṃ ṛṣabho'vatāraḥ śaṅkarasya me \|*  
*satāṃ gatirdīnabandhurnavamaḥ kathitastava \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. Thus the ninth Ṛṣabḥa incarnation of Śiva has been narrated to you. He is the goal of the good and kinsman of the indigent.

## \|\| 7.4.48 \|\|

*ṛṣabhasya caritraṃ hi paramaṃ pāvanaṃ mahat \|*  
*svargyaṃ yaśasyamāyuṣyaṃ śrotavyaṃ ca prayatnataḥ \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. The story of Ṛṣabḥa is great, highly sacred, and conducive to heaven, fame and longevity. It shall be listened to with devoted attention.

## Chapter 5 - The nineteen incarnations of Śiva

## \|\| 7.5.1 \|\|

*\|\| śiva uvāca \|*  
*daśame dvāpare vyāsastridhāmā nāmato muni \|*  
*himavacchikhare ramye bhṛgutuṃge nagottame \|\| 1 \|\|*

Śiva said:—

1\. In the tenth aeon of Dvāpara there will be Tridhāmā Vyāsa on the excellent Bhṛgutuṅga peak of Himavat.

## \|\| 7.5.2 \|\|

*tatrāpi mama putrāśca bhṛgvādyāḥ śrutisaṃmitāḥ \|*  
*balabandhurnarāmitraḥ ketuśṛṃgastapodhanaḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. There my sons well-versed in the Vedic lore Bhṛṅga and others i.e. Balabandhu, Nara, Mitra and the sage Ketuśṛṅga.

## \|\| 7.5.3 \|\|

*ekādaśe dvāpare tu vyāsaśca trivṛto yadā \|*  
*gaṃgādvāre kalau nāmnā tapo'haṃ bhavitā tadā \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. In the eleventh aeon of Dvāpara when there will be Trivṛta Vyāsa I shall be born as Kali at Gaṅgādvāra.

## \|\| 7.5.4 \|\|

*lambodaraśca lambākṣaḥ keśalamvaḥ pralambakaḥ \|*  
*tatrāpi putrāścatvāro bhaviṣyanti dṛḍhavratāḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. There also the four sons of steady rites viz. Lambodara, Lambākṣa, Keśalamba and Pralambaka will be born.

## \|\| 7.5.5-6 \|\|

*dvādaśe parivartte tu śatatejāśca vedakṛt \|*  
*tatrāpyahaṃ bhaviṣyāmi dvāparānte kalāviha \|\| 5 \|\|*

*hemakaṃcukamāsādya nāmnā hyatriḥ pariplutaḥ \|*  
*vyāsasyaiva sāhāyyārthaṃ nivṛttipatharoṣaṇaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

5-6. In the twelfth aeon, there will be Śatatejas Vyāsa. I will be born in the Kali age at the end of Dvāpara adopting golden bodice. I shall be wandering for rendering help to Vyāsa as the reviver of the path of renunciation.

## \|\| 7.5.7 \|\|

*sarvajñaḥ samabuddhiśca sādhyaḥ śarvasuyoginaḥ \|*  
*tatreti putrāścatvāro bhaviṣyanti mahāmune \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. O great sage, these four sons of good yogic skill will be born as my sons viz. Sarvajña, Samabuddhi, Sādhya and Śarva.

## \|\| 7.5.8 \|\|

*trayodaśe yuge tasmindharmo nārāyaṇaḥ sadā \|*  
*vyāsastadāhaṃ bhavitā balirnāma mahāmuniḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. In the thirteenth aeon, there will be Nārāyaṇa Vyāsa. Then I shall be born as the great sage Bali.

## \|\| 7.5.9 \|\|

*bālakhilyāśrame gaṃdhamādane parvatottame \|*  
*sudhāmā kāśyapaścaiva vasiṣṭho virajāḥ śubhāḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. In the hermitage of Bālakhilyas on the excellent mountain of Gandhamādana, the auspicious sons will be Sudhāmā, Kāśyapa, Varṣiṣṭha and Virajas.

## \|\| 7.5.10 \|\|

*yadā vyāsastu rakṣākhyaḥ paryāye tu caturdaśe \|*  
*vaṃśa āṅgirase tatra bhavitāhaṃ ca gautamaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. In the fourteenth aeon, there will be Rakṣa Vyāsa. I shall be born as Gautama, in the family of Aṅgiras.

## \|\| 7.5.11 \|\|

*tatrāpi mama te putrā bhaviṣyanti kalau tadā \|*  
*atrirdavaśadaścaiva śravaṇotha śraviṣkaṭaḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. In the Kali age my sons will be Atri, Devasada, Śravaṇa and Śraviṣkaṭa.

## \|\| 7.5.12-13 \|\|

*vyāsaḥ pañcadaśe trayyāruṇirvai dvāpare yadā \|*  
*tadāhaṃ bhavitā vedaśirā vedaśirastathā \|\| 12 \|\|*

*mahāvīryaṃ tadastraṃ ca vedaśīrṣaśca parvataḥ \|*  
*himavatpṛṣṭhamāsādya sarasvatyāstathottare \|\| 13 \|\|*

12-13. In the fifteenth aeon of Dvāpara, there will be Trayyāruṇi Vyāsa. I shall be born as Vedaśiras with a powerful weapon. Vedaśīrṣa is a mountain on the top of Himavat on the northern bank of Sarasvatī.

## \|\| 7.5.14 \|\|

*tatrāpi mama catvāro bhaviṣyanti sutā dṛḍhāḥ \|*  
*kuṇiśca kuṇibāhuśca kuśarīraḥ kunetrakaḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. There the four viz. Kuṇi, Kuṇibāhu, Kuśarīra and Kunetraka will be my sturdy sons.

## \|\| 7.5.15 \|\|

*vyāso yuge ṣoḍaśe tu yadā devo bhaviṣyati \|*  
*tadā yogapradānāya gokarṇo bhavitā hyaham \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. In the sixteenth aeon, there will be Deva Vyāsa; I shall be born as Gokarṇa for imparting instructions in Yoga.

## \|\| 7.5.16 \|\|

*tatraiva ca supuṇyaṃ ca gokarṇaṃ nāma tadvanam \|*  
*tatrāpi yoginaḥ putra bhaviṣyaṃtityambusaṃmitāḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. It is there itself that the holy forest Gokarṇa exists. There also four Yogin sons will be born of me.

## \|\| 7.5.17 \|\|

*kāśyapopyuśanāścaiva cyavano'tha bṛhaspatiḥ \|*  
*tepi tenaiva mārgeṇa gamiṣyanti śivālayam \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. They are Kāśyapa, Uśanas, Cyavana and Bṛhaspati. They too will go to the abode of Śiva through the same path.

## \|\| 7.5.18-19 \|\|

*parivartte saptadaśe vyāso devakṛtaṃjayaḥ \|*  
*guhāvāsīti nāmnāhaṃ himavacchikhare śubhe \|\| 18 \|\|*

*mahālaye mahottuṃge śivakṣetraṃ himāla yam \|*  
*utathyo vāmadevaśca mahāyogo mahābalaḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

18-19. In the seventeenth aeon, there will be Devakṛtañjaya Vyāsa. I shall be born in the name of Guhāvāsin. There is a snow-covered Śiva temple on the lofty summit of Himavat. The sons are Utathya, Vāmadeva, Mahāyoga and Mahābala.

## \|\| 7.5.20 \|\|

*parivartte'ṣṭādaśe tu yadā vyāsa ṛtaṃjayaḥ \|*  
*śikhāṇḍīnāmatohaṃ taddhimavacchikhare śubhe \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. In the eighteenth aeon, there will be Ṛtañjaya Vyāsa. I will be born as Śikhaṇḍin on the auspicious summit of Himavat.

## \|\| 7.5.21 \|\|

*siddhakṣetre mahāpuṇye śikhaṇḍī nāma parvataḥ \|*  
*śikhaṇḍino vanaṃ vāpi yatra siddhaniṣevitam \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. In the very sacred holy centre of achievements there is the mountain Śikhaṇḍin, where the forest is resorted to by the Siddhas.

## \|\| 7.5.22 \|\|

*vācaḥśravā rucīkaśca syāvāsyaśca yatīśvaraḥ \|*  
*ete putrā bhaviṣyanti tatrāpi ca tapodhanāḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. These four will be born as the sons, the sages Vācaśśravas, Rucīka, Śyāvāsya and Yatīśvara.

## \|\| 7.5.23-24 \|\|

*ekonaviṃśe vyāsastu bharadvājo mahāmuniḥ \|*  
*tadāpyahaṃ bhaviṣyāmi jaṭī mālī ca nāmataḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

*himavacchikhare tatra putrā me'mbudhisaṃhitāḥ \|*  
*hiraṇyanāmā kauśalyo lokākṣī pradhimistathā \|\| 24 \|\|*

23-24. In the nineteenth aeon, there will be Bharadvāja Vyāsa. Then I shall be born as Jaṭī and Mālin. On the summit of Himavat, my sons will be four in number named Hiraṇyanāmā, Kauśalya, Lokākṣin and Praghima.

## \|\| 7.5.25 \|\|

*parivartte viṃśatime bhavitā vyāsa gautamaḥ \|*  
*tatrāṭṭahāsanāmāhamaṭṭahāsapriyā narāḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. In the twentieth aeon, there will be Vyāsa Gautama. My name will be Aṭṭahāsa. Men are fond of Aṭṭahāsa (loud boisterous laughter).

## \|\| 7.5.26 \|\|

*tatraiva himavatpṛṣṭhe aṭṭahāso mahāgiriḥ \|*  
*devamānuṣayakṣendrasiddhacāraṇasevitaḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. On the tops of Himavat, Aṭṭahāsa a great mountain which is resorted to by Gods, men, Yakṣas, Indra, Siddhas and Cāraṇas.

## \|\| 7.5.27 \|\|

*tatrāpi mama te putrā bhavi ṣyanti suyoginaḥ \|*  
*sumanturbabarirvidvān kabaṃdhaḥ kuśikandharaḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. There will be born of me sons, who will be experts in Yoga and will be called Sumantu, Varvari, Kabandha and Kukṣikandhara.

## \|\| 7.5.28 \|\|

*ekaviṃśe yuge tasmin vyāso vācaḥśravā yadā \|*  
*tadāhaṃ dāruko nāma tasmāddāruvanaṃ śubham \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. In the twenty-first aeon when Vācaśśravas becomes Vyāsa, I shall be born as Dāruka. Hence the forest Dāruvana is auspicious.

## \|\| 7.5.29 \|\|

*tatrāpi mama te putrā bhaviṣyanti suyoginaḥ \|*  
*plakṣo dārbhāyaṇiścaiva ketumān gautamastathā \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. There too, experts in Yoga, will be my sons viz. Plakṣa, Dārbhāyaṇi, Ketumān and Gautama.

## \|\| 7.5.30 \|\|

*dvāviṃśe parivarte tu vyāsaḥ śuṣmāyaṇo yadā \|*  
*tadāpyahaṃ bhaviṣyāmi vārāṇasyāṃ mahāmuniḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. In the twenty-second aeon, there will be Śuṣmāyaṇa Vyāsa. I shall be born as a great sage in Vārāṇasī.

## \|\| 7.5.31 \|\|

*nāmnā vai lāṃgalī bhīmo yatra devāḥ savāsavāḥ \|*  
*drakṣyaṃti māṃ kalau tasminbhavaṃ caiva halāyudham \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. I shall be known as Lāṅgali Bhīma (having ploughshare) where the gods including Indra will see me in the Kali age as Bhava and Halāyudha.

## \|\| 7.5.32 \|\|

*tatrāpi mama te putrā bhaviṣyaṃti sudhārmikāḥ \|*  
*bhallavī madhupiṃgaśca śvetaketustathaiva ca \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. There too my sons will be virtuous viz. Bhallava, Madhu, Piṅga and Śvetaketu.

## \|\| 7.5.33 \|\|

*parivarte trayoviṃśe tṛṇabinduryadā muniḥ \|*  
*śveto nāma tadāhaṃ vai girau kālaṃjare śubhe \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. In the twenty-third aeon, there will be the Tṛṇabindu Vyāsa. I shall be born as Śveta in the auspicious Kālañjara mountain.

## \|\| 7.5.34 \|\|

*tatrāpi mama te putrā bhaviṣyanti tapasvinaḥ \|*  
*uśiko bṛhadaśvaśca devalaḥ kavireva ca \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. There too my sons will be the sages—Uśika, Bṛhadaśva, Devala and Kavi.

## \|\| 7.5.35 \|\|

*parivarte caturviṃśe vyāso yakṣo yadā vibhuḥ \|*  
*śūlī nāma mahāyogī tadyuge naimiṣe tadā \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. In the twenty-fourth aeon, there will be Yakṣa Vyāsa. I shall be born as Śūlin a great Yogin in the same Yuga in Naimiṣa.

## \|\| 7.5.36 \|\|

*tatrāpi mama te śiṣyā bhaviṣyanti tapasvinaḥ \|*  
*śālihotro'gniveśaśca yuvanāśvaḥ śaradvasuḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. There too my disciples will be the sages Śālihotra, Agniveśa, Yuvanāśva and Śaradvasu.

## \|\| 7.5.37 \|\|

*paṃcaviṃśe yadā vyāsaḥ śaktirnāmnā bhaviṣyati \|*  
*tadāpyahaṃ mahāyogī daṇḍī muṇḍīśvaraḥ prabhuḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. In the twenty-fifth aeon, there will be Śakti Vyāsa. I shall be the great Yogin, lord Muṇḍiśvara armed with a staff.

## \|\| 7.5.38 \|\|

*tatrāpi mama te śiṣyā bhaviṣyanti tapasvinaḥ \|*  
*chagalaḥ kuṇḍakarṇaśca kumbhāṇḍaśca pravāhakaḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. There too my disciples will be the sages Chāgala, Kuṇḍakarṇa, Kumbhāṇḍa and Pravāhaka.

## \|\| 7.5.39 \|\|

*vyāsaḥ parāśaro yarhi ṣaḍviṃśe bhavitāpyaham \|*  
*puraṃ bhadravaṭaṃ prāpya sahiṣṇurnāma nāmataḥ \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. In the twenty-sixth aeon, when Parāśara, becomes Vyāsa I will be born as Sahiṣṇu after reaching the city Bhadravaṭa.

## \|\| 7.5.40 \|\|

*tatrāpi mama te śiṣyā bhaviṣyanti tapasvinaḥ \|*  
*ulūko vidyutaścaiva śambūko hyāśvalāyanaḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. There too my disciples will be the sages Ulūka, Vidyuta, Śambūka and Āśvalāyana.

## \|\| 7.5.41 \|\|

*saptaviṃśe yadā vyāso jātūkarṇyo bhaviṣyati \|*  
*prabhāsatīrthamāśritya somaśarmā tadāpyaham \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. In the twenty-seventh aeon, there will be Jātūkarṇya Vyāsa. I shall be born as Somaśarman after resorting to the holy centre Prabhāsa.

## \|\| 7.5.42 \|\|

*tatrāpi mama te śiṣyā bhaviṣyaṃti tapasvinaḥ \|*  
*akṣapādaḥ kumāraścolūko vatsastathaiva ca \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. There too my disciples will be the sages Akṣapāda, Kumāra, Ulūka and Vatsa.

## \|\| 7.5.43-44 \|\|

*aṣṭāviṃśe drāpare tu parāśarasuto hariḥ \|*  
*yadā vyāso bhaviṣyāmi nāmnā dvaipāyanaḥ pramuḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

*tadā ṣaṣṭhena cāṃśena kṛṣṇaḥ puruṣasattamaḥ \|*  
*vasudevasutaḥ śreṣṭho vāsudevo bhaviṣyati \|\| 44 \|\|*

43-44. In the twenty-eighth aeon of Dvāpara, there will be Dvaipāyana Vyāsa, the son of Parāśara, and the most excellent of Puruṣas shall be born as Kṛṣṇa with his one sixth part, as the foremost of the sons of Vasudeva.

## \|\| 7.5.45 \|\|

*tadāpyahaṃ bhaviṣyāmi yogātmā yogamāyayā \|*  
*lokavismāpanārthāya brahmacāriśarīrakaḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. Then I too shall be born with the body of a Brahmacārin and the soul of a Yogin by means of Yogic Māyā to the great surprise of the worlds.

## \|\| 7.5.46-47 \|\|

*śmaśāne mṛtamutsṛjya dṛṣṭvā kāyamanāmayam \|*  
*brāhmaṇānāṃ hitārthāya praviṣṭo yogamāyayā \|\| 46 \|\|*

*divyāṃ meruguhāṃ puṇyāṃ tvayā sārddhaṃ ca viṣṇunā \|*  
*bhaviṣyāmi tadā brahmaṃllakulī nāmanāmataḥ \|\| 47 \|\|*

46-47. On seeing a dead body forsaken in the cremation ground I shall enter into it and make it free from ailments by means of Yogic Māyā for the welfare of the brahmins. Then I will enter the holy divine cavern of Meru along with you and Viṣṇu. O Brahmā, I shall then be known as Lakulin.

## \|\| 7.5.48 \|\|

*kāyāvatāra ityevaṃ siddhakṣetraṃ paraṃ tadā \|*  
*bhaviṣyati suvikhyātaṃ yāvadbhūmirdhariṣyati \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. The physical incarnation thus and the holy Siddha centre will be greatly renowned as long as the earth lasts.

## \|\| 7.5.49 \|\|

*tatrāpi mama te śiṣyā bhaviṣyanti tapasvinaḥ \|*  
*kuśikaścaiva gargaśca mitraḥ kauruṣya eva ca \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. There too my disciples will be the sages Kuśika, Garga, Mitra and Taurusya.

## \|\| 7.5.50 \|\|

*yogino brāhmaṇā vedapāragā ūrddhvaretasaḥ \|*  
*prāpya māheśvaraṃ yogaṃ gamiṣyaṃti śivaṃ puram \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. The Yogic brahmins well-versed in the Vedas, and of sublimated power will attain the Yoga of Maheśvara and reach the auspicious city.

## \|\| 7.5.51 \|\|

*vaivasvate'ntare samyak proktā hi paramātmanā \|*  
*yogeśvarāvatārāśca sarvāvarteṣu suvratāḥ \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. In the Vaivasvata manvantara, during all its recurring aeons, the Yogeśvara incarnations of the great Ātman of virtuous deeds have been explained to you.

## \|\| 7.5.52 \|\|

*vyāsāścaivāṣṭaviṃśatkā dvāparedvāpare vibho \|*  
*yogeśvarāvatāraśca prāraṃbhe ca kalau kalau \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. O great lord, the Vyāsas in every recurring Dvāpara are twenty-eight in number. The Yogeśvara incarnations at the beginning of every recurring Kali age are also so many.

## \|\| 7.5.53 \|\|

*yogeśvarāvatārāṇāṃ yogamārgapravarddhakāḥ \|*  
*mahāśaivāśca catvāraḥ śiṣyāḥ pratyekamavyayā \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. In each of the incarnations of Yogeśvara, there are four disciples who make the path of Yoga flourish and who are eternal as well as great devotees of Śiva.

## \|\| 7.5.54 \|\|

*ete pāśupatāḥ śiṣyā bhasmoddhūlitavigrahāḥ \|*  
*rudrākṣamālābharaṇāstripuṇḍrāṃkitamastakāḥ \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. These disciples of Śiva smear their bodies with holy ashes, use the necklaces of Rudrākṣa beads as their ornaments and mark their foreheads with Tripuṇḍra.

## \|\| 7.5.55 \|\|

*śiṣyā dharmaratāḥ sarve vedavedāṃgapāragāḥ \|*  
*liṃgārcanaratā nityaṃ bāhyābhyantarataḥ sthitāḥ \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. All the disciples are pious and well-versed in the Vedas and Vedāṅgas; they are devoted both externally and internally to the worship of the phallic image of Śiva.

## \|\| 7.5.56 \|\|

*bhaktyā mayi ca yogena dhyānaniṣṭhā jitendriyāḥ \|*  
*saṃkhyayā dvādaśādhikya śataṃ ca gaṇitā budhaiḥ \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. By their devotion to me and by means of Yoga they are engaged in meditation; they have perfect control over the sense-organs. They are considered one hundred and twelve in number by the learned.

## \|\| 7.5.57 \|\|

*ityetadvai mayā proktamavatāreṣu lakṣaṇam \|*  
*manvādikṛṣṇaparyantamaṣṭāviṃśadyugakramāt \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. Thus I have mentioned the characteristic features of the incarnations from Manu to Kṛṣṇa in the twenty-eighth recurring cycles of the Yoga in order.

## \|\| 7.5.58 \|\|

*tatra śrutisamūhānāṃ vidhānaṃ brahmalakṣaṇam \|*  
*bhaviṣyati tadā kalpe kṛṣṇadvaipāyano yadā \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. The classification of the Vedas was the characteristic sign of the Kalpa when Kṛṣṇa Dvaipāyana became the chief redactor.

## \|\| 7.5.59 \|\|

*ityevamuktvā brahmāṇamanugṛhya maheśvaraḥ \|*  
*punaḥ saṃprekṣya deveśastatraivāntaradhīyata \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. After saying this and blessing Brahmā, the great lord of the gods glanced at again and vanished there itself.

## Chapter 6 - The incarnation of Nandīśvara

## \|\| 7.6.1 \|\|

*atha nandīśvarāvatāramāha \|*  
*sanatkumāra uvāca \|*  
*bhavānkathamanuprāpto mahādevāṃśajaḥ śivam \|*  
*śrotumicchāmi tatsarvaṃ vaktumarhasi me prabho \|\| 1 \|\|*

Sanatktumāra said:—

1\. O lord, how did you happen to be born of Mahādeva and gain access to Śiva I wish to hear. Please narrate everything in detail.

## \|\| 7.6.2 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*sanatkumāra sarvajña sāvadhānatayā śṛṇu \|*  
*yathāhaṃ ca śivaṃ prāpto mahādevāṃśajo mune \|\| 2 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

2\. O sage Sanatkumāra, O omniscient one, listen with attention as to how I was born of Mahādeva and how I gained access to Śiva.

## \|\| 7.6.3-4 \|\|

*prajākāmaḥ śilādo'bhūduktaḥ pitṛbhirādarāt \|*  
*taduddhartumanā bhaktyā samuddhāramabhīpsubhiḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

*adhodṛṣṭiḥ sudharmātmā śilādo nāma vīryavān \|*  
*tasyāsīnmunikairvṛttiḥ śivaloke ca sogamat \|\| 4 \|\|*

3-4. Urged eagerly by the manes who desired their uplift Śilāda piously thought of raising them up. He was therefore desirous of a progeny. For that he of great potentiality performed a penance with his drooping eyes. He the same activities as the sages. He went to Śivaloka.

## \|\| 7.6.5 \|\|

*śakramuddiśya sa munistapastepe suduḥ saham \|*  
*niścalātmā śilādākhyo bahukālaṃ dṛḍhavrataḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. That sage Śilāda performed a severe penance with Indra in view, for a long time. His mind was steady and he was stead-fast in his activities.

## \|\| 7.6.6 \|\|

*tapatastasya tapasā saṃtuṣṭo'bhūcchatakratuḥ \|*  
*jagāma ca varaṃ dātuṃ sarvadevaprabhustadā \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Indra was delighted at his penance. He, the lord of gods, went to him in order to grant him the boon.

## \|\| 7.6.7 \|\|

*śilādamāha suprītyā śakrastuṣṭo'smi te'nagha \|*  
*tena tvaṃ muniśārdūla varayasva varāniti \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Indra lovingly spoke to Śilāda—“O excellent sage, O sinless one, I am delighted with you. Therefore choose your boons.”

## \|\| 7.6.8 \|\|

*tataḥ praṇamya deveśaṃ stutvā stutibhirādarāt \|*  
*śilādo muniśārdūlastamāha sukṛtāñjaliḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Bowing to the lord of the gods and eulogising him with holy hymns, Śilāda, the most excellent sage, told him with palms joined in reverence.

## \|\| 7.6.9 \|\|

*śilāda uvāca \|*  
*śatakrato sureśāna santuṣṭo yadi me prabho \|*  
*ayonijaṃ mutyuhīnaṃ putramicchāmi suvratam \|\| 9 \|\|*

Śilāda said:—

9\. O lord of gods, O Indra, if you are pleased with me, I wish to have a son of holy rites free of death and not born of a womb.

## \|\| 7.6.10 \|\|

*śakra uvāca \|*  
*putraṃ dāsyāmi putrārthinyonijaṃ mṛtyusaṃyutam \|*  
*anyathā te na dāsyāmi mṛtyuhīnā na santi vai \|\| 10 \|\|*

Indra said:—

10\. O desirer of a son, I can give you only a son born of a womb and sure to die. I cannot give it to you otherwise. There are no persons who can escape death.

## \|\| 7.6.11 \|\|

*na dāsyāmi sutaṃ te'haṃ mṛtyuhīnamayonijam \|*  
*harirvidhiśca bhagavānkimutānyo mahāmune \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. I shall not give you a son devoid of death and not born of a womb. O great sage, even lord Viṣṇu and Brahmā die. Not to speak of others then?

## \|\| 7.6.12 \|\|

*tāvapi tripurāryaṃgasambhāvau maraṇānvitau \|*  
*tayorapyāyuṣā mānaṃ kathitaṃ nigame pṛthak \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Even these two, born of Śiva, the destroyer of the Tripuras, die. The extent of their longevity has been mentioned separately in. the sacred lore.

## \|\| 7.6.13 \|\|

*tasmādayonije putre mṛtyuhīne prayatnataḥ \|*  
*parityajāśāṃ viprendra gṛhāṇātmakṣamaṃ sutam \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Hence O great brahmin, abandon your desire of deathless son who is not born of a womb. Accept a son befitting your mortal nature.

## \|\| 7.6.14 \|\|

*kintu deveśvaro rudraḥ prasīdati maheśvaraḥ \|*  
*sudurlabho mṛtyuhīnastava putro hyayonijaḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. But if Śiva, the lord of the gods, becomes delighted, even an immortal son not born of a womb, though hard to get, may be obtained.

## \|\| 7.6.15 \|\|

*ahaṃ ca viṣṇurbhagavāndruhiṇaśca mahāmune \|*  
*ayonijaṃ mṛtyuhīnaṃ putraṃ dātuṃ na śaknumaḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. Neither I, nor lord Viṣṇu, nor Brahmā, O, great sage, can give an immortal son not born of a womb.

## \|\| 7.6.16 \|\|

*ārādhaya mahādevaṃ tatputravinikāmyayā \|*  
*sarveśvaro mahāśaktaḥ sa te putraṃ pradāsyati \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Propitiate lord Śiva therefore, for tḥe fulfilment of your desire for such a son. The great lord of all is very powerful. He will give you a son.

## \|\| 7.6.17 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*evaṃ vyāhṛtya viprendramanugṛhya ca taṃ ghṛṇī \|*  
*devairvṛtaḥ sureśānassvalokaṃ samagānmune \|\| 17 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

17\. O sage, after speaking thus to the great brahmin and blessing him, the compassionate lord of the gods returned to his region surrounded by the gods.

## \|\| 7.6.18 \|\|

*gate tasmiṃśca varade sahasrākṣe śilāśanaḥ \|*  
*ārādhayanmahādevaṃ tapasātoṣayadbhavam \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. When the granter of boons, the thousand-eyed lord Indra had gone away, Śilāda propitiated lord Śiva. He delighted Śiva by means of his penance.

## \|\| 7.6.19 \|\|

*atha tasyaivamaniśantatparasya dvijasta vai \|*  
*divyamvarṣasahasraṃ tu gataṃ kṣaṇamivādbhutam \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. As the brahmin was engrossed in penance day and night, a thousand divine years elapsed like a moment.

## \|\| 7.6.20-21 \|\|

*valmīkena vṛtāṃgaśca lakṣakoṭagaṇairmuniḥ \|*  
*sūcīmukhaiścānyai raktabhugbhiśca sarvataḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

*nirmāṃsarudhira tvagvai bile tasminnavasthitaḥ \|*  
*asthiśeṣobhavatpaścācchilādo munisattamaḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

20-21. His body was covered with anthill. He was surrounded by a hundred thousand worms, with pointed beaks, as hard as adamant and other types of blood-sucking insects all round. He remained in the cavity with his flesh and blood drained up. He was left with a bare skeleton. The excellent sage Śilāda was reduced to such a state.

## \|\| 7.6.22 \|\|

*tuṣṭaḥ prabhustadā tasmai darśayāmāsa svāṃ tanum \|*  
*divyāṃ divyaguṇairyuktāmalabhyāṃ vāmabuddhibhiḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. The delighted lord showed him his divine body endowed with all divine qualities and inaccessible to people of crooked intellect.

## \|\| 7.6.23 \|\|

*divyavarṣasahasreṇa tapyamānāya śūladhṛk \|*  
*sarvadevādhipastasmai varadosmītyabhāṣata \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. As he continued the penance for a thousand divine years, the trident-bewaring lord of all the spoke to him “I am here, the granter of boons.”

## \|\| 7.6.24 \|\|

*mahāsamādhisaṃlīnassa śilādo mahāmuniḥ \|*  
*nāśṛṇottadgiraṃ śambhorbhaktyadhīnaratasya vai \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. The great sage Śilāda engrossed in the trance did not hear those words of Śiva who is subservient to the devotees.

## \|\| 7.6.25 \|\|

*yadā spṛṣṭo munistena kareṇa tripurāriṇā \|*  
*tadaiva muniśārdūla utsasarja tapaḥkramam \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Only when the great sage was touched by the slayer of the Tripuras did he leave off his penance.

## \|\| 7.6.26 \|\|

*athonmīlya munirnetre somaṃ śaṃbhuṃ vilokayan \|*  
*drutaṃ praṇamya sa mudā pādayornyapatanmune \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. O sage, on seeing Śiva accompanied by Pārvatī after opening his eyes, he immediately bowed to him and fell at his feet with joy.

## \|\| 7.6.27 \|\|

*harṣagadgadayā vācā nataskaṃdhaḥ kṛtāñjaliḥ \|*  
*prasannātmā śilādassa tuṣṭāva parameśvaram \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. The delighted Śilāda, drooping down his shoulders, with palms joined in reverence eulogised lord Śiva with words choked with pleasure.

## \|\| 7.6.28 \|\|

*tataḥ prasanno bhagavāndevadevastrilocanaḥ \|*  
*varado'smīti tamprāha śilādaṃ munipuṃgavam \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. Then the delighted lord of gods, the three-eyed deity told the great sage “I am the granter of boons.”

## \|\| 7.6.29 \|\|

*tapasānena kiṃ kāryaṃ bhavate hi mahā mate \|*  
*dadāmi putraṃ sarvajñaṃ sarvaśāstrārthapāragam \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. O intelligent one, what shall be done with this penance? I shall give you a son who will be omniscient and a master of all sacred lore.

## \|\| 7.6.30 \|\|

*tataḥ praṇamya deveśaṃ tacchrutvā ca śilāśanaḥ \|*  
*harṣagadgadayā vācovāca somavibhūṣaṇam \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. On hearing that, the sage Śilāda bowed to the moon-crested lord and spoke in words choked with pleasure.

## \|\| 7.6.31 \|\|

*śilāda uvāca \|*  
*maheśa yadi tuṣṭo'si yadi vā varadaśca me \|*  
*icchāmi tvatsamaṃ putraṃ mṛtyuhīnamayonijam \|\| 31 \|\|*

Śilāda said:—

31\. O lord Śiva, if you are delighted, if you are ready to grant boons, I wish for a son equal to you, devoid of death and not born of a womb.

## \|\| 7.6.32 \|\|

*naṃdīśvara uvāca \|*  
*evamuktastato devastryambakastena śaṅkaraḥ \|*  
*pratyuvāca prasannātmā śilādaṃ munisattamam \|\| 32 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

32\. When thus requested by him, the three-eyed lord Śiva delightedly replied to the excellent sage Śilāda.

## \|\| 7.6.33 \|\|

*\|\| śiva uvāca \|*  
*pūrvamārādhito vipra brahmaṇāhaṃ tapodhana \|*  
*tapasā cāvatārārthaṃ munibhiśca surottamaiḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

Śiva said:—

33\. O brahmin, O sage, formerly I have been propitiated by means of penance by Brahmā, by sages and by leading gods and urged to take an incarnation.

## \|\| 7.6.34 \|\|

*tava putro bhaviṣyāmi nandī nāmnā tvayonijaḥ \|*  
*pitā bhaviṣyasi mama piturvai jagatāṃ mune \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. O sage, I shall become your son by the name of Nandin. I shall not be born of a womb. Thus you shall be my father, of me who am the father of the worlds.

## \|\| 7.6.35 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*evamuktvā muniṃ prekṣya praṇipatyāsthitaṃ ghṛṇī \|*  
*somastūrṇaṃ tamādiśya tatraivāntardadhe haraḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

35\. After addressing these words to the sage who stood waiting after bowing, the merciful lord Śiva gave him his directions and vanished there itself along with Pārvatī.

## \|\| 7.6.36 \|\|

*gate tasminmahādeve sa śilādo mahāmuniḥ \|*  
*svamāśrayamupāgamya ṛṣibhyo'kathayattataḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. After lord Śiva had disappeared, the great sage Śilāda returned to his hermitage and mentioned the tidings to the sages.

## \|\| 7.6.37 \|\|

*kiyatā caiva kālena tadāsau janakaḥ sa me \|*  
*yajñāṃgaṇaṃ cakarṣāśu yajñārthaṃ yajñavittamaḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. After a short while, my father, the foremost among those who knew the sacrificial rites, ordered for the construction of the sacrificial altar for the performance of sacrifice.

## \|\| 7.6.38 \|\|

*tataḥ kṣaṇādahaṃ śaṃbhostanujastasya cājñayā \|*  
*sa jātaḥ pūrvvamevāhaṃ yugāntāgnisamaprabhaḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. But before the beginning of sacrifice I was born as Śiva’s son at his bidding, with a lustre as brilliant as the fire at the end of a Yuga.

## \|\| 7.6.39 \|\|

*avarṣaṃstadā puṣkarāvartakādyā jaguḥ khecarāḥ kinnarāḥ siddhasādhyā \|*  
*śilādātmajatvaṃ gate mayyṛṣīndrāssamantācca vṛṣṭiṃ vyadhuḥ kausumīṃ te \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. Then Puṣkarāvartaka and other clouds showered. When I was born as the son of Śilāda, the Kinnaras, Siddhas, Sādhyas and other beings walked through the sky and the great sages made showers of flowers all round.

## \|\| 7.6.40 \|\|

*atha brahmādayo devā devapalyaśca sarvaśaḥ \|*  
*tatrājagmuśca suprītyā hariścaiva śivo'mbikā \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. Then Brahmā and other gods together with their wives, Viṣṇu, Śiva and Pārvatī came there lovingly.

## \|\| 7.6.41 \|\|

*tadotsavo mahānāsīnnanṛtuścāpsarogaṇāḥ \|*  
*ādṛtya māṃ tathā liṃgaṃ tuṣṭuvurharṣitāśca te \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. There was great jubilation. The celestial damsels danced. After honouring me all of them delightedly eulogised my phallic image.

## \|\| 7.6.42 \|\|

*supraśasya śilādaṃ taṃ stutvā ca sustavaiḥ śivau \|*  
*sarve jagmuśca dhāmāni śivāvapyakhileśvarau \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. After praising Śilāda and eulogising Śiva and Pārvatī by reciting good hymn all of them returned to their respective abodes. Śiva and Śivā, the lord and goddess of all, went away too.

## \|\| 7.6.43-44 \|\|

*śilādo'pi ca māṃ dṛṣṭvā kālasūryyānalaprabham \|*  
*tryakṣaṃ caturbhujaṃ bālaṃ jaṭāmukuṭadhāriṇam \|\| 43 \|\|*

*triśūlādyāyudhaṃ dīptaṃ sarvathā rudrarūpiṇam \|*  
*mahānandabharaḥ prītyā praṇamyaṃ praṇanāma ca \|\| 44 \|\|*

43-44. On seeing me the boy with three eyes, four arms and lustre like that of Yama, sun and fire and clad in matted hair and coronet, Śilāda was highly delighted. I was in the form of Rudra in every respect with the trident and other weapons. He bowed to me lovingly.

## \|\| 7.6.45 \|\|

*śilāda uvāca \|*  
*tvayāhaṃ nandito yasmānnandī nāmnā sureśvara \|*  
*tasmāttvāṃ devamānandaṃ namāmi jagadīśvaram \|\| 45 \|\|*

Śilāda said:—

45\. O lord of gods, since I have been delighted by you, you will be named Nandin. Hence I bow to you, bliss (incarnate), the lord of the universe.

## \|\| 7.6.46 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*mayā saha pitā hṛṣṭaḥ supraṇamya maheśvaram \|*  
*uṭajaṃ svaṃ jagāmāśu nidhiṃ labdhveva nirdhanaḥ \|\| 46 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

46\. My delighted father after bowing to the great lord returned to his hut with me, as pleased as an indigent person who had struck a treasure-trove.

## \|\| 7.6.47 \|\|

*yadā gatohamuṭajaṃ śilādasya mahāmune \|*  
*tadāhaṃ tādṛśaṃ rūpaṃ tyaktvā mānuṣyamāsthitaḥ \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. O great sage, when I reached the hut I cast off my divine form and assumed the human one.

## \|\| 7.6.48 \|\|

*mānuṣyamāsthitaṃ dṛṣṭvā pitā me lokapūjitaḥ \|*  
*vilalāpātiduḥkhārttaḥ svajanaiśca samāvṛtaḥ \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. After seeing me assuming human form, my father, honoured by the world, was distressed and so lamented surrounded by his own people.

## \|\| 7.6.49 \|\|

*jātakarmādikānyeva sarvāṇyapi cakāra me \|*  
*śālaṃkāyanaputro vai śilādaḥ putravatsalaḥ \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. The son of Śālaṅkayana Śilāda fond of his son performed all the post-natal rites for me.

## \|\| 7.6.50 \|\|

*vedānadhyāpayāmāsa sāṃgopāṃgānaśeṣataḥ \|*  
*śāstrāṇyanyānyapi tathā pañcavarṣe pitā ca mām \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. Within five years my father taught me all the Vedas with their Aṅgas and Upāṅgas as well as other sacred texts.

## \|\| 7.6.51 \|\|

*sampūrṇe saptame varṣe mitrāvaruṇasaṃjñakau \|*  
*munī tasyāśramaṃ prāptau draṣṭuṃ māṃ cājñayā vibhoḥ \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. When I completed the age of seven, the two sages Mitra and Varuṇa came to the hermitage at the bidding of the lord in order to see me.

## \|\| 7.6.52 \|\|

*satkṛtau muninā tena sūpaviṣṭo mahāmunī \|*  
*ūcatuśca mahātmānau māṃ nirīkṣya muhurmuhuḥ \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. Duly welcomed by the sage and seated on their seats, the great sages of noble soul looked at me again and again and said.

## \|\| 7.6.53 \|\|

*mitrāvaruṇāvūcatuḥ \|*  
*tāta naṃdīstavālpāyuḥ sarvaśāstrārthapāragaḥ \|*  
*na dṛṣṭameva cāpaśyaṃ hyāyurvarṣādataḥ param \|\| 53 \|\|*

Mitra and Varuṇa said:—

53\. O dear sage, although he has mastered the sacred lore, your son Nandin will be short-lived I do not see his life for more than a year from now.

## \|\| 7.6.54 \|\|

*viprayorityuktavatoḥ śilādaḥ putravatsalaḥ \|*  
*tamāliṅgya ca duḥkhārtto rurodātīva visvaram \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. When the brahmins said this, Śilāda fond of his son was much distressed. He lamented loudly after embracing me.

## \|\| 7.6.55 \|\|

*mṛtavatpatitaṃ dṛṣṭvā pitaraṃ ca pitāmaham \|*  
*pratyavocatprasannātmā smṛtvā śivapadāmbujam \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. On seeing my father and grand-father fallen like a dead body I spoke after remembering the lotus-like feet of Śiva with a delightful mind.

## \|\| 7.6.56 \|\|

*kena tvaṃ tāta duḥkhena vepamānaśca rodiṣi \|*  
*duḥkhaṃ te kuta utpannaṃ jñātumicchāmi tattvataḥ \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. “O father, what is your distress for which you tremble and cry? I wish to know correctly whence this misery has come to you?”

## \|\| 7.6.57 \|\|

*\|\| pitovāca \|*  
*tavālpamṛtyuduḥkhena duḥkhito'tīva putraka \|*  
*ko me duḥkhaṃ haratu vai śaraṇaṃ prayāmi hi \|\| 57 \|\|*

The father said:—

57\. O son, I am much distressed at the thought of your early death. Who can remove my misery? I shall indeed seek refuge in him.

## \|\| 7.6.58-59 \|\|

*\|\| putra uvāca \|*  
*devo vā dānavo vāpi yamaḥ kālotha vāpi hi \|*  
*ṛdhyeyuryadyapi hyete māmanyepi janāstathā \|\| 58 \|\|*

*athāpi cālpamṛtyurme na bhaviṣyati māṃ tudaḥ \|*  
*satyaṃ bravīmi janakaṃ śapathante karomyaham \|\| 59 \|\|*

The son said:—

58-59. Even if the gods, demons, Yama or Kāla were to be with me or even if it be any other person, I will not be short-lived. Do not be grieved. O father, I speak the truth, I take a vow unto you.

## \|\| 7.6.60 \|\|

*\|\| pitovāca \|*  
*kiṃ tapaḥ kiṃ parijñānaṃ ko yogaśca prabhuśca te \|*  
*yena tvaṃ dāruṇaṃ duḥkhaṃ vañcayiṣyasi putra me \|\| 60 \|\|*

The father said:—

60\. What is your penance? What is your knowledge? What is your Yoga? Who is your lord? Whereby O son, you propose to ward off my terrible misery?

## \|\| 7.6.61 \|\|

*\|\| putra uvāca \|*  
*na tāta tapasā mṛtyuṃ vaṃcayiṣye na vidyayā \|*  
*mahādevasya bhajanānmṛtyuṃ jeṣyāmi nānyathā \|\| 61 \|\|*

The son said:—

61\. O father, I shall deceive death by means of my penance, not learning. By worshipping great god, I will conquer death, not otherwise.

## \|\| 7.6.62 \|\|

*\|\| nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityuktvāhaṃ pituḥ pādau praṇamya śirasā mune \|*  
*pradakṣiṇīkṛtya ca tamagacchaṃ vanamuttamam \|\| 62 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

62\. O sage, after saying this and bowing at his feet and circumambulating my father I went to the excellent forest.

## Chapter 7 - The coronation and the nuptials of Nandīśvara

## \|\| 7.7.1 \|\|

*\|\| nandikeśvara uvāca \|*  
*tatra gatvā mune'haṃ vai sthitvaikāntasthale sudhīḥ \|*  
*atapaṃ tapa ugraṃ sanmunīnāmapi duṣkaram \|\| 1 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

1\. O sage, after going there and staying in an isolated place with a steady mind I performed a severe penance very difficult even for good sages.

## \|\| 7.7.2-3 \|\|

*hṛtpuṇḍarīkasuṣire dhyātvā devaṃ triyambakam \|*  
*tryakṣaṃ daśabhujaṃ śāntaṃ pañcavaktraṃ sadā śivam \|\| 2 \|\|*

*rudrajāpyamakārṣaṃ vai paramadhyānamāsthitaḥ \|*  
*saritaścottare puṇye hyekacittaḥ samāhitaḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

2-3. Meditating in the lotus-like cavity of the heart the lord Tryambaka, Sadāśiva wtih five faces, ten arms and three eyes and quiet in features I performed the repetition of Rudra Japa. I was in great meditation on the northern bank of the river. My mind was concentrated and I had pure thoughts.

## \|\| 7.7.4 \|\|

*tasmiñjāpye'tha saṃprītaḥ sthitaṃ māṃ parameśvaraḥ \|*  
*tuṣṭo'bravīnmahādevaḥ somaḥ somārddhabhūṣaṇaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. Delighted with that Japa, lord Śiva, accompanied by Pārvatī and bedecked with the crescent moon, said:—

## \|\| 7.7.5 \|\|

*śiva uvāca \|*  
*śailāde varadohaṃ te tapasānena toṣitaḥ \|*  
*sādhu taptaṃ tvayā dhīman brūhi yatte manogatam \|\| 5 \|\|*

Śiva said:—

5\. “O Śailādi (son of Śilāda) I have been pleased by this penance of yours. O intelligent one, you have performed the penance very well. Tell me what you desire. I am here the granter of boons.”

## \|\| 7.7.6 \|\|

*sa evamukto devena śirasā pādayornataḥ \|*  
*astavaṃ parameśānaṃ jarāśokavināśanam \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. When thus addressed by the lord I bowed to his feet with my head and eulogised lord Śiva, the destroyer of the sorrow of old age.

## \|\| 7.7.7-8 \|\|

*atha māṃ nandinaṃ śambhurbhaktyā parāmayā yutam \|*  
*aśrupūrṇekṣaṇaṃ samyak pādayoḥ śirasā natam \|\| 7 \|\|*

*utthāya parameśānaḥ pasparśa paramārtihā \|*  
*karābhyāṃ saṃmukhābhyāntu saṃgṛhya vṛṣabhadhvajaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

7-8. I was endowed with great devotion. My eyes were filled with tears. I fell prostrate at his feet with my head resting there. Lord Śiva, the destroyer of great distress, lifted me up and stroked me with his hands. The bull-bannered lord clasped me with his hands and stood face to face with me.

## \|\| 7.7.9 \|\|

*nirīkṣya gaṇapāṃścaiva devīṃ himavataḥ sutām \|*  
*uvāca māṃ kṛpādṛṣṭyā samīkṣya jagatāmpatiḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. Glancing at the leaders of the Gaṇas and the goddess, the daughter of Himavat, the lord of the worlds, looked at me with sympathy and spoke.

## \|\| 7.7.10 \|\|

*vatsa nandinmahāprājña mṛtyorbhītiḥ kutastava \|*  
*mayaiva preṣitau viprau matsamastvaṃ na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. “Dear Nandin, O intelligent one, wherefore can you be afraid of death? The two brahmins were sent by me alone. There is no doubt. You are equal to me.

## \|\| 7.7.11 \|\|

*amaro jarayā tyakto'duḥkhī gaṇapatiḥ sadā \|*  
*avyayaścākṣayaśceṣṭaḥ sa pitā sa suhṛjjanaḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. You will be free from death and old age. You will not be unhappy. You will for ever be the leader of the Gaṇas. You will be unchanging and unwasting. You will be loved by all in the company of your father and friends.

## \|\| 7.7.12 \|\|

*madbalaḥ pārśvago nityaṃ mameṣṭo bhavitāniśam \|*  
*na jarā janma mṛtyurvai matprasādādbhaviṣyati \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. You will gain strength from me. You will always be at my sides. You will be for ever my favourite. Due to my favour you will have neither birth, nor death, nor old age.

## \|\| 7.7.13 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*evamuktvā śiromālāṃ kuśeśayamayīṃ nijām \|*  
*samunmucya babandhāśu mama kaṇṭhe kṛpānidhiḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

13\. After saying thus, the store-house of mercy took off his own lotus-garland round his head and tied it round my neck immediately.

## \|\| 7.7.14 \|\|

*tayāhaṃ mālayā vipra śubhayā kaṇṭhasaktayā\|\| \|*  
*tryakṣo daśabhujaścāsaṃ dvitīya iva śaṅkara \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. O brahmin, as soon as that garland adorned my neck I became three-eyed and ten-armed like a second Śiva.

## \|\| 7.7.15 \|\|

*tata eva samādāya hastena parameśvaraḥ \|*  
*uvāca brūhi kiṃ te'dya dadāmi varamuttamam \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. Then, taking me by the hand, lord Śiva said—“Tell me, what excellent boon shall I grant you?”

## \|\| 7.7.16 \|\|

*tato jaṭāśritaṃ vāri gṛhītvā hāra nirmalam \|*  
*uktvā nandī bhavetīha visasarja vṛṣadhvajaḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Then he took the water from his matted hair, water as pure as necklace, and said “Be Nandin.” Then the bull-bannered lord let off the water.

## \|\| 7.7.17 \|\|

*tataḥ pañcamitā nadyaḥ prāvartata śubhāvahāḥ \|*  
*sutoyāśca mahāvegā divya rūpā ca sundarī \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Then five auspicious rivers flowed. They had pure water. They were very rapid. They were beautiful and divine in form.

## \|\| 7.7.18 \|\|

*jaṭodakā trisrotāśca vṛṣadhvaniritīva hi \|*  
*svarṇodakā jambunadī pañcanadyaḥ prakīrtitāḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. The five rivers are glorified thus—Jaṭodakā, Trisrotas, Vṛṣadhvani, Svarṇodakā and Jambunadī.

## \|\| 7.7.19 \|\|

*etatpañcanadaṃ nāma śivapṛṣṭhatamaṃ śubham \|*  
*japeśvarasamīpe tu pavitraṃ paramaṃ mune \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. O sage, this basin called Pañcanada is a very auspicious spot of Śiva. It is near Japeśvara and is very holy.

## \|\| 7.7.20 \|\|

*yaḥ pañcanadamāsādya snātvā japtveśvareśvaram \|*  
*pūjayecchivasāyujyaṃ prayātyeva na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. He who goes to the holy centre of Pañcanada, takes his bath there, performs the Japa of the lord of lords and worships him, certainly attains the Sāyujya of Śiva.

## \|\| 7.7.21 \|\|

*atha śambhuruvācomāmabhiṣiñcāmi nandinam \|*  
*gaṇendraṃ vyāhariṣyāmi kiṃ vā tvaṃ manyase'vyaye \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. Then Śiva said to Pārvatī “I am going to coronate Nandin and proclaim him as the lord of Gaṇas. O unchanging goddess, what do you think?”

## \|\| 7.7.22 \|\|

*umovāca \|*  
*dātumarhasi deveśa nandine parameśvara \|*  
*mahāpriyatamo nātha śailādistanayo mama \|\| 22 \|\|*

Pārvatī said:—

22\. O lord of gods, it behoves you to give that to Nandin. O lord, Śailādi is my most beloved son.

## \|\| 7.7.23 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*tatassa śaṅkaraḥ svīyānsasmāra gaṇapānvarān \|*  
*svatantraḥ parameśānassarvado bhaktavatsalaḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

23\. Then Śiva the independent lord, the Supreme deity, the bestower of everything and favourably disposed towards his devotees remembered his excellent leaders of Gaṇas.

## \|\| 7.7.24 \|\|

*smaraṇādeva rudrasya samprāptāśca gaṇeśvarāḥ \|*  
*asaṅkhyātā mahāmodāśśaṅkarākṛtayo'khilāḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. Even as they were remembered, all the leading Gaṇas of Rudra came there. They were countless. They had the shape of Śiva. They were highly delighted.

## \|\| 7.7.25 \|\|

*te gaṇeśāśśivaṃ devīṃ praṇamyāhuḥ śubhaṃ vacaḥ \|*  
*te praṇamya karau baddhvā nataskandhā mahābalāḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Those leading Gaṇas bowed to lord Śiva and the goddess Pārvatī with joined palms and drooping shoulders. The powerful Gaṇas spoke.

## \|\| 7.7.26 \|\|

*gaṇeśā ūcuḥ \|*  
*kimarthaṃ ca smṛtā deva hyājñāpaya mahāprabho \|*  
*kiṅkarānnaḥ samāyātāṃstripurārdana kāmada \|\| 26 \|\|*

The leading Gaṇas said:—

26\. O great lord, the destroyer of Tripuras, why are we called? Please command us, O bestower of wishes. We arc your servants and we have come here.

## \|\| 7.7.27 \|\|

*kiṃ sāgarāñśoṣayāmo yamaṃ vā saha kiṃkaraiḥ \|*  
*hanmo mṛtyuṃ mahāmṛtyuṃ viśeṣaṃ vṛddhapadmajam \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Shall we dry up the oceans? Shall we kill Yama with his servants? Shall we slay great death or the aged Brahmā especially?

## \|\| 7.7.28 \|\|

*baddhvendraṃ saha devaiśca viṣṇuṃ vā pārṣadaiḥ saha \|*  
*ānayāmaḥ susaṃkuddhāndaityānvā dānavaiḥ saha \|\| 28 \|\|*
