28\. Shall we bind Indra with all the gods or Viṣṇu with all his Pārṣadas and bring them here? Or the Daityas and Dānavas?

## \|\| 7.7.29 \|\|

*kasyādya vyasanaṃ ghoraṃ kariṣyāmastavājñayā \|*  
*kasya vādyotsavo deva sarvakāmasamṛddhaye \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. To whom shall we cause great and terrible distress at your behest? O lord, who is to be extremely jubilant to-day for the increase of all cherished desires?

## \|\| 7.7.30 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityākarṇya vacasteṣāṃ gaṇānāṃ vīravādinām \|*  
*uvāca tānsa praśaṃsya gaṇeśānparameśvaraḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

30\. On hearing these heroic words of the Gaṇas the great lord spoke after praising them.

## \|\| 7.7.31 \|\|

*śiva uvāca \|*  
*nandīśvaroyaṃ putro me sarveṣāmīśvareśvaraḥ \|*  
*priyo gaṇāmagraṇīssarvaiḥ kriyatāṃ vacanaṃ mama \|\| 31 \|\|*

Śiva said:—

31\. Nandīśvara is my son. He is the lord of lords. He is the favourite leader of my Gaṇas. Let my behest be carried out by you.

## \|\| 7.7.32 \|\|

*sarve prītyābhiṣiñcadhvaṃ madgaṇānāṃ gatimpatim \|*  
*adyaprabhṛti yuṣmākamayaṃ nandīśvaraḥ prabhuḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. All of you, crown him lovingly as your leader and resort of all my Gaṇas. From today onwards, Nandīśvara is your lord.

## \|\| 7.7.33 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*evamuktāḥ śaṅkareṇa gaṇapāssarvva eva te \|*  
*evamastviti samprocya sambhārānāharaṃstataḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

33\. Thus ordered by Siva, the leading Gaṇas said “Let it be so”; they brought all the requisite things (for his consecration)

## \|\| 7.7.34 \|\|

*tato devāśca sendrāśca nārāyaṇamukhāstathā \|*  
*munayaḥ sarvato lokā ājagmurmuditānanāḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. Then Nārāyaṇa and other gods including Indra, and die sages came from all the worlds with pleased faces.

## \|\| 7.7.35 \|\|

*pitāmahopi bhagavanniyogācchaṅkarasya vai \|*  
*cakāra naṃdinassarvvamabhiṣekaṃ samāhitaḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. At the behest of Śiva, the holy Brahmā performed the coronation of Nandin with care and attention.

## \|\| 7.7.36 \|\|

*tato viṣṇustataśśakro lokapālāstathaiva ca \|*  
*ṛṣayastuṣṭuvuścaiva pitāmahapurogamāḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. Then Viṣṇu, Indra, the guardians of the quarters, Brahmā and the sages eulogised me.

## \|\| 7.7.37 \|\|

*stutimatsu tatasteṣu viṣṇuḥ sarvajagatpatiḥ \|*  
*śirasyañjalimādhāya tuṣṭāva ca samāhitaḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. While they were eulogising, Viṣṇu, the lord of the universe, eulogised me with devotion with palms joined together over the head.

## \|\| 7.7.38 \|\|

*prāñjaliḥ praṇato bhūtvā jayaśabdaṃ cakāra ca \|*  
*tato gaṇādhipāssarvve tato devāstato'surāḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. With palms joined in reverence he bowed to me and cried shouts of victory. Then all the leading Gaṇas, the gods and the Asuras did likewise in order.

## \|\| 7.7.39 \|\|

*evaṃ stutaścābhiṣikto devaissa brahmakaistadā \|*  
*nandīśvarohaṃ viprendra niyogātpa rameśituḥ \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. O great brahmin, thus at the instance of the great lord, I, Nandīśvara was coronated and eulogised by the gods including Brahmā.

## \|\| 7.7.40 \|\|

*udvāhaśca kṛtastatra niyogātparameṣṭhinaḥ \|*  
*mahotsavayutaḥ prītyā viṣṇubrahmādibhirmama \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. My nuptial ceremony was also conducted at the instance of Brahmā with great jubilation and pleasure by Viṣṇu, Brahmā and others.

## \|\| 7.7.41 \|\|

*marutāṃ ca sutā devī suyaśāstu manoharā \|*  
*patnī sā me'bhavaddivyā manonayananandinī \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. The daughter of the Maruts, the gentle and beautiful lady Suyaśā became my divine wife pleasing to my eyes and mind.

## \|\| 7.7.42 \|\|

*labdhaṃ śaśiprabhaṃ chatraṃ tayā tatra vibhūṣitam \|*  
*cāmaraiścāmarāsaktahastāgraiḥ strīgaṇairyutam \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. A well-decorated umbrella shedding moonlike lustre was held above her in the company of women holding the chowries in their hands.

## \|\| 7.7.43 \|\|

*siṃhāsanaṃ ca paramaṃ tayā cādhiṣṭhitaṃ mayā \|*  
*alaṃkṛto mahālakṣmyā mukuṭādyaissubhūṣaṇaiḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. A fine throne was occupied by me along with her. I was bedecked by the illustrious Lakṣmī with various ornaments like crown and others.

## \|\| 7.7.44-45 \|\|

*labdho hāraśca paramo devyāḥ kaṇṭhagatastathā \|*  
*vṛṣendraśca śito nāgassiṃhassiṃhadhvajastathā \|\| 44 \|\|*

*rathaśca hemahāraśca candrabiṃbasamaḥ śubhaḥ \|*  
*anyānyapi ca vastūni labdhāni hi mayā mune \|\| 45 \|\|*

44-45. A fine necklace worn by the goddess round her neck was given to me. I got a fine bull, a white elephant, a lion, the lion-banner, a chariot, gold necklace, like the disc of the moon and many other auspicious articles, O sage.

## \|\| 7.7.46 \|\|

*evaṃ kṛtavivāho'haṃ tayā patnyā mahāmune \|*  
*pādau vavande śambhośca śivāyā brahmaṇo hareḥ \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. O great sage, the marriage having been celebrated thus I, in the company of my wife, saluted the feet of Śiva, Brahmā and Viṣṇu for the sake of welfare.

## \|\| 7.7.47 \|\|

*tathāvidhaṃ trilokeśassapatnīkaṃ ca māmprabhuḥ \|*  
*provāca parayā prītyā sa śivo bhaktavatsalaḥ \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. Siva, lord of the three worlds, favourably disposed towards his devotees spoke these words with great love and pleasure to me accompanied by my wife.

## \|\| 7.7.48 \|\|

*īśvara uvāca \|*  
*śṛṇu satputra tātastvaṃ suyaśeyantava priyā \|*  
*dadāmi te varamprītyā yatte manasi vāñchitam \|\| 48 \|\|*

Lord Śiva said:—

48\. O dear son, listen. This Suyaśā is your beloved wife: Whatever is desired by you in your mind I shall grant you that boon.

## \|\| 7.7.49 \|\|

*sadāhantava nandīśa santuṣṭo'smi gaṇeśvara \|*  
*devyā ca sahito vatsa śṛṇu me paramaṃ vacaḥ \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. O Nandīśa, O lord of Gaṇas, I am always pleased with you in the company of the goddess. O dear boy, listen to my weighty words.

## \|\| 7.7.50-51 \|\|

*sadeṣṭaśca viśiṣṭaśca paramaiśvaryyasaṃyutaḥ \|*  
*mahāyogī maheṣvāsaḥ sa pitā sa pitāmahaḥ \|\| 50 \|\|*

*ajeyassarvajetā ca sadā pūjyo mahābalaḥ \|*  
*ahaṃ yatra bhavāṃstatra yatra tvaṃ tatra cāpyaham \|\| 51 \|\|*

50-51. You will be my favourite always. You will be endowed with all prosperity. You will be a great Yogin and a fine bowman along with your father and grandfather never to be defeated by anyone. You will be all-victorious and worthy of worship. You will be endowed with great strength. Wherever I am, you shall also be and wherever you are I shall also be.

## \|\| 7.7.52 \|\|

*ayaṃ ca te pitā putra paramaiśvaryyasaṃyutaḥ \|*  
*bhaviṣyati gaṇādhyakṣo mama bhakto mahābalaḥ \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. O son, your father will be endowed with all prosperity. He will become a presiding officer of the Gaṇas, a great devotee of mine and very powerful.

## \|\| 7.7.53 \|\|

*pitāmaho'pi te vatsa tathāstu niyamā ime \|*  
*matsamīpaṃ gamiṣyanti mayā dattavarāstathā \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. O dear son, your grandfather too shall be like-wise. This is my rule that whoever approaches me is certainly endowed with a boon.

## \|\| 7.7.54 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*tato devī mahābhāgā nandinaṃ varadābravīt \|*  
*varaṃ brūhīti māmputra sarvvānkāmānyathesitān \|\| 54 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

54\. Then the goddess, the granter of the boon and of great fortune told me—“Speak out what your desire is. Tell me all your wishes.”

## \|\| 7.7.55 \|\|

*tacchrutvā vacanaṃ devyāḥ prāvocatsāñjalistadā \|*  
*bhaktirbhavatu me devi pādayoste sadā varā \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. On hearing those words of the goddess, I spoke with palms joined in reverence “O goddess, let my excellent devotion be always towards your feet.”

## \|\| 7.7.56 \|\|

*śrutvā mama vaco devī hyevamastviti sābravīt \|*  
*suyaśāṃ tāñca suprītyā nandipriyatamāṃ śivām \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. On hearing my words the goddess said “Let it be so.” With great pleasure Śivā spoke to Suyaśā, the beloved wife of Nandin.

## \|\| 7.7.57 \|\|

*devyuvāca \|*  
*vatse varaṃ yatheṣṭaṃ hi trinetrā janmavarjitā \|*  
*putrapautrestu bhaktirme tathā ca bhartureva hi \|\| 57 \|\|*

The goddess said:—

57\. Dear daughter, as you desire I grant you the boon that you will be endowed with three eyes. You will be devoid of future births. You will have devotion to me along with your husband, sons and grandsons.

## \|\| 7.7.58 \|\|

*nandyuvāca \|*  
*tadā brahmā ca viṣṇuśca sarvve devagaṇāśca vai \|*  
*tābhyāṃ varāndaduḥ prītyā suprasannāśśivājñayā \|\| 58 \|\|*

Nandin said:—

58\. Then the delighted Brahmā, Viṣṇu, all the gods and the Gaṇas gave them boons with pleasure at the behest of Śiva.

## \|\| 7.7.59 \|\|

*sānvayaṃ māṃ gṛhītveśastatassambandhibāndhavaiḥ \|*  
*āruhya vṛṣamīśāno gato devyā nijaṃ gṛham \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. Taking me along with all the members of the family, kinsmen and relatives, lord Śiva mounted his bull and went to the abode of the goddess.

## \|\| 7.7.60 \|\|

*viṣṇvādayaḥ surāssarvve praśaṃsanto hyamī tadā \|*  
*svadhāmāni yayuḥ prītyā saṃstuvantaḥ śivaṃ śivam \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. Viṣṇu and all other gods praising these and eulogising Śiva and Śivā lovingly returned to their respective abodes.

## \|\| 7.7.61 \|\|

*iti te kathito vatsa svāvatāro mahāmune \|*  
*sadānandakaraḥ puṃsāṃ śivabhaktipravarddhanaḥ \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. O great sage, O dear one, thus my own incarnation has been narrated to you. It is always pleasing and heightening the power of Śiva.

## \|\| 7.7.62-63 \|\|

*ya idannandino janma varadānantathā mama \|*  
*abhiṣekaṃ vivāhaṃ ca śṛṇuyācchrāvayettathā \|\| 62 \|\|*

*paṭhedvā pāṭhayedvāpi śraddhāvānbhaktisaṃyutaḥ \|*  
*iha sarvvasukhambhuktvā paratra labhate gatim \|\| 63 \|\|*

62-63. He who listens to or narrates, reads or teaches the incarnation of Nandin, the granting of the boons, the coronation and the nuptial ceremony with faith and devotion enjoys all pleasures here and attains great goal hereafter.

## Chapter 8 - Bhairava incarnation

## \|\| 7.8.1 \|\|

*atha bhairavāvatāramāha \|*  
*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*sanatkumāra sarvvajña śṛṇu tvaṃ bhairavīṃ kathām \|*  
*yasyāḥ śravaṇamātreṇa śaivī bhaktirdṛḍhā bhavet \|\| 1 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

1\. O Sanatkumāra, O omniscient one, listen to the story of Bhairava by listening to which alone, devotion to Śiva becomes firm and steady.

## \|\| 7.8.2 \|\|

*bhairavaḥ pūrṇarūpo hi śaṃkarasya parātmanaḥ \|*  
*mūḍhāstaṃ vai na jānanti mohitāśśivamāyayā \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Bhairava is the prior form of Siva, the great soul. Fools who are deluded by Śiva’s Māyā do not know about it.

## \|\| 7.8.3 \|\|

*sanatkumāra no vetti mahimānaṃ maheśituḥ \|*  
*caturbhujo'pi viṣṇurvai caturvvaktro'pi vai vidhiḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. O Sanatkumāra, neither Viṣṇu of four arms nor Brahmā of four faces knows the greatness of lord Śiva.

## \|\| 7.8.4 \|\|

*citramatra na kiñcidvai durjñeyā khalu śāmbhavī \|*  
*tayā saṃmohitāssarve nārcayantyapi tamparam \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. There is nothing to be surprised at in this. Siva’s Māyā is inscrutable. Deluded by it people do not even worship him, the great lord.

## \|\| 7.8.5 \|\|

*veda cedyadi vātmānaṃ sa eva parameśvaraḥ \|*  
*tadā vihanti te sarvve svecchayā na hi ke'pi tam \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. If any one knows him the supreme soul, he alone is lord Śiva. But then, none of these people know him out of their own will.

## \|\| 7.8.6 \|\|

*sarvvago'pi maheśāno nekṣyate mūḍhabuddhibhiḥ \|*  
*devavad budhyate loke yo'tīto manasāṃ girām \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Although lord Śiva is omnipresent he is not seen by the people of deluded intellect. He is known as a mere lord, he who is beyond the reach of minds and words.

## \|\| 7.8.7 \|\|

*atretihāsaṃ vakṣye'haṃ paramarṣe purātanam \|*  
*śṛṇu taṃ śraddhayā tāta paramaṃ jñānakāraṇam \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. O great sage, in this connection I shall narrate an

ancient legend. O dear one, listen to it with faith. It is the great cause of perfect knowledge.

## \|\| 7.8.8 \|\|

*meruśṛṅge'dbhute ramye sthitambrahmāṇamīśvaram \|*  
*jagmurdevarṣayaḥ sarvve sutattvaṃ jñātumicchayā \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. As Brahmā was stationed on the wonderful and beautiful summit of Meru, all the celestial sages approached him with a desire to know the real truth.

## \|\| 7.8.9 \|\|

*tatrāgatya vidhinnatvā papracchuste mahādarāt \|*  
*kṛtāñjalipuṭāssarve nataskandhā munīśvarāḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. All the great sages with palms joined in reverence and shoulders drooping down came there and bowed to Brahmā. They asked him respectfully.

## \|\| 7.8.10 \|\|

*devarṣaya ūcuḥ \|*  
*devadeva prajānātha sṛṣṭikṛllokanāyaka \|*  
*tattvato vada cāsmabhyaṃ kimekaṃ tattvamavyayam \|\| 10 \|\|*

The celestial sages said:—

10\. O lord of gods, O lord of subjects, O Creator, O leader of the worlds, tell us the real fact. What is that sole unchanging entity?

## \|\| 7.8.11 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*sa māyayā maheśasya mohitaḥ padmasambhavaḥ \|*  
*avijñāya parambhāvaṃ saṃbhāvaṃ pratyuvāca ha \|\| 11 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

11\. Deluded by the Māyā of great lord and not knowing the great fact the lotus-born Brahmā replied haughtily.

## \|\| 7.8.12 \|\|

*brahmovāca \|*  
*he surā ṛṣayaḥ sarvve sumatyā śṛṇutādarāt \|*  
*vacmyahaṃ paramaṃ tattvamavyayaṃ vai yathārthataḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

Brahmā said:—

12\. O gods, O sages, all of you listen with attention and intelligence I shall describe the great unchanging entity as it really is.

## \|\| 7.8.13 \|\|

*jagadyonirahaṃ dhātā svayambhūraja īśvaraḥ \|*  
*anādibhāgahaṃ brahma hyeka ātmā nirañjanaḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. I am the womb (material cause) of the universe, the creator, the self-born, the unborn, the lord, the primordial being, the Brahman, the sole unsullied soul.

## \|\| 7.8.14 \|\|

*pravartako hi jagatāmahameva nivarttakaḥ \|*  
*saṃvartako madadhiko nānyaḥ kaścitsurottamāḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. O excellent gods, I am the instigator of the worlds, and the deterrent too. There is none greater than I or any other who causes their revolution.

## \|\| 7.8.15 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*tasyaivaṃ vadato dhāturviṣṇustatra sthito mune \|*  
*provāca prahasanvākyaṃ saṃkruddho mohito'jayā \|\| 15 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

15\. While the Creator was saying thus, O sage, Viṣṇu who was there became angry as he too was deluded by Māyā. He spoke these words derisively.

## \|\| 7.8.16 \|\|

*na caitaducitā brahmanyogayuktasya mūrkhatā \|*  
*avijñāya paraṃ tattvaṃ vṛthaitatte nigadyate \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. O Brahmā, this is not proper. It is sheer foolishness on the part of a practiser of Yoga. Without knowing the great reality, this is being mentioned in vain.

## \|\| 7.8.17 \|\|

*kartā vai sarvalokānāṃ paramātmā paraḥ pumān \|*  
*yajño nārāyaṇo devo māyādhīśaḥ parā gatiḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. I am the Creator of all the worlds, the great Śtman, the greatest Puruṣa, the sacrifice, the lord Nārāyaṇa, the master of Māyā and the greatest goal.

## \|\| 7.8.18 \|\|

*mamājñayā tvayā brahmansṛṣṭireṣā vidhī yate \|*  
*jagatāṃ jīvanaṃ naiva māmanādṛtya ceśvaram \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. O Brahmā, it is at my behest that creation is carried on by you. Without regard for me, the lord, no life can exist in the worlds.

## \|\| 7.8.19 \|\|

*evaṃ triprakṛtau mohātparasparajayaiṣiṇau \|*  
*procaturnigamāṃścātra pramāṇe sarvathā tanau \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. O sage, thus starting a mutual clash due to delusion and desiring to conquer each other they asked the Vedas about truth.

## \|\| 7.8.20 \|\|

*praṣṭavyāste viśeṣeṇa sthitā mūrtidharāśca te \|*  
*papracchatuḥ pramāṇajñānityuktvā caturo'pi tān \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. The Vedas were there in embodied form. They shall be specifically asked. With this idea in mind they asked the four Vedas about the truth.

## \|\| 7.8.21 \|\|

*vidhiviṣṇū ūcatuḥ \|*  
*vedāḥ pramāṇaṃ sarvvatra pratiṣṭhā paramāmitāḥ \|*  
*yūyaṃ vadata viśrabdhaṃ kimekaṃ tattvamavyayam \|\| 21 \|\|*

Brahmā and Viṣṇu said:—

21\. Vedas are the authorities everywhere. They have been honoured everywhere. Hence you please say unreservedly. What is the sole unchanging entity?

## \|\| 7.8.22 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityākarṇya tayorvācaṃ punaste hi ṛgādayaḥ \|*  
*avadaṃstattvataḥ sarvve saṃsmarato paraṃ prabhum \|\| 22 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

22\. On hearing their words, the Vedas spoke in reality after remembering the lord.

## \|\| 7.8.23 \|\|

*yadi mānyā vayandevau sṛṣṭisthitikarau vibhū \|*  
*tadā pramāṇaṃ vakṣyāmo bhavatsandehabhedakam \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. “If we are honoured, O deities as the causes of creation and maintenance, we shall cite authorities that will dispel your doubts.

## \|\| 7.8.24 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*śrutyuktavidhimākarṇya procatustau surau śrutīḥ \|*  
*yuṣmaduktaṃ pramāṇaṃ nau kintattvaṃ samyagucyatām \|\| 24 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

24\. On hearing the conditions mentioned by the Vedas the two deities told the Vedas—“What is mentioned by you is acceptable to us as authority. What is that entity? Please tell us that.

## \|\| 7.8.25 \|\|

*ṛgveda uvāca \|*  
*yadantassthāni bhūtāni yatassarvvampravarttate \|*  
*yadāhuḥ paramantattvaṃ sa rudrastveka eva hi \|\| 25 \|\|*

Ṛgveda said:—

25\. That in which the Bhūtas stay, that from which everything comes out, what they call as the greatest entity—that is Rudra alone.

## \|\| 7.8.26 \|\|

*yajurveda uvāca \|*  
*yo yajñairakhilairīśo yogena ca samijyate \|*  
*yena pramāṇaṃ khalu nassa ekaḥ sarvvadṛk chivaḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

Yajurveda said:—

26\. The lord who is worshipped by means of all sacrifices and meditation, whereby we have an authority—that sole seer of all is Śiva.

## \|\| 7.8.27 \|\|

*sāmaveda uvāca \|*  
*yenedambhramyate viśvaṃ yogibhiryo vicintyate \|*  
*yadbhāsā bhāsate viśvaṃ sa ekastryambakaḥ paraḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

Sāmaveda said:—

27\. That by which this universe whirls, that which is meditated upon by Yogins, the light whereby the universe Is illuminated—that Tryambaka alone is the greatest.

## \|\| 7.8.28 \|\|

*atharvaveda uvāca \|*  
*yaṃ prapaśyanti deveśambhaktyanugrahiṇo janāḥ \|*  
*tamāhurekaṃ kaivalyaṃ śaṃkaraṃ duḥkhataḥ param \|\| 28 \|\|*

Atharvaveda said:—

28\. They call that sole being Śiva who is beyond all misery who is the lord of gods whom the people who have the blessing of devotion see.

## \|\| 7.8.29 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*śrutyuktamidamākarṇyātīvamāyāvimohitau \|*  
*smitvāhaturvidhiharī nigamāṃstānvicetanau \|\| 29 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

29\. After hearing what was thus said by the Vedas, Brahmā and Viṣṇu, deluded very much by the Māyā (and hence) crooked in consciousness smiled and said to the Vedas.

## \|\| 7.8.30 \|\|

*vidhiharī ūcatuḥ \|*  
*he vedāḥ kimidaṃ yūyambhāṣante gatacetanāḥ \|*  
*kiñjātaṃ vo'dya sarvvaṃ hi naṣṭaṃ suvayunaṃ param \|\| 30 \|\|*

Brahmā and Viṣṇu said:—

30\. O Vedas, what is this that you all speak without sense? What has happened to you? All wisdom is utterly lost now.

## \|\| 7.8.31-32 \|\|

*kathampramathanātho'sau ramamāṇo nirantaram \|*  
*digambaraḥ pītavarṇo śivayā dhūlidhūsaraḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

*virūpaveṣo jaṭilo vṛṣago vyālabhūṣaṇaḥ \|*  
*paraṃ brahmatvamāpannaḥ kva ca tatsaṃgavarjitam \|\| 32 \|\|*

31-32. The lord of Pramathas incessantly sports with Śivā. He is naked and of yellow colour and tawny due to dust. His dress and features are ugly. He has matted hair and moves about on his bull. He wear? serpents as his ornaments. How can he be the supreme Brahman? Where is that Brahman free from any attachment?”

## \|\| 7.8.33 \|\|

*ityudīritamākarṇya praṇavaḥ sarvagastayoḥ \|*  
*amūrto mūrtimānprītyā jṛmbhamāṇa uvāca tau \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. On hearing their words, the all-pervasive Praṇava who though unembodied assumed an embodied, extensive form told them thus.

## \|\| 7.8.34 \|\|

*praṇava uvāca \|*  
*na hīśo bhagavāñchaktyā hyātmano vyatiriktayā \|*  
*kadācidramate rudro līlārūpadharo haraḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

Praṇava said:

34\. Lord Īśa, Rudra, Śiva indulging in sports and assuming various forms never sports about with the Śakti different from him.

## \|\| 7.8.35 \|\|

*asau hi parameśānassvayaṃjyotissanātanaḥ \|*  
*ānandarūpā tasyaiṣā śaktirnāgantukī śivā \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. The great lord, is self-luminous and eternal. Śivā is his Śakti in the form of Bliss, not a chance occurrence.

## \|\| 7.8.36 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityevamukto'pi tadā vidherviṣṇośca vai tadā \|*  
*nājñānamagamannāśaṃ śrīkaṇṭhasyaiva māyayā \|\| 36 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

36\. Even after this explanation the ignorance of Brahmā and Viṣṇu was not quelled. It was due to the Māyā of Śiva alone.

## \|\| 7.8.37 \|\|

*prādurāsīttato jyotirubhayorantare mahat \|*  
*pūrayannijayā bhāsā dyāvābhūmyoryadantaram \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. Then a great flame manifested itself in their midst. It filled the space between the heaven and the sarth by means of its splendour.

## \|\| 7.8.38 \|\|

*jyotirmaṇḍalamadhyastho dadṛśe puruṣākṛtiḥ \|*  
*vidhikratubhyāṃ tatraiva mahādbhutatanurmune \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. O sage, then Brahma and Viṣṇu saw a shape in the form of a man in the middle of the mass of flame. Its body was huge and wonderful.

## \|\| 7.8.39 \|\|

*prajajvālātha kopena brahmaṇaḥ pañcamaṃ śiraḥ \|*  
*āvayorantare ko'sau bibhṛyātpuruṣākṛtim \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. The fifth head of Brahmā burnt with rage (as. though saying) “who is this bearing a man’s outline and features in our midst?”

## \|\| 7.8.40-41 \|\|

*vidhiḥ saṃbhāvayedyāvattāvatsa trivilocanaḥ \|*  
*dṛṣṭaḥ kṣaṇena ca mahāpuruṣo nīlalohitaḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

*triśūlapāṇirbhālākṣo nāgoḍupavibhūṣaṇaḥ \|*  
*hiraṇyagarbhastaṃ dṛṣṭvā vihasanprāha mohitaḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

40-41. By the time Brahmā could reflect properly, the great Being was seen immediately as the three-eyed Nīlalohita with the trident in his hand and an eye in the forehead. Serpents and crescent moon constituted his ornaments. The deluded Brahmā on seeing him said laughingly.

## \|\| 7.8.42-43 \|\|

*brahmovāca \|*  
*nīlalohita jāne tvāṃ mā bhaiṣīścandraśekhara \|*  
*bhālasthalānmama purā rudraḥ prādurabhūdbhavān \|\| 42 \|\|*

*rodanādrudranāmāpi yojito'si mayā purā \|*  
*māmeva śaraṇaṃ yāhi putra rakṣāṅkaromi te \|\| 43 \|\|*

Brahmā said:—

42-43. “O Nīlalohita, I know you. Do not be afraid; O moon-crested one. You, Rudra, came out formerly from my forehead. Since you cried, the name Rudra was also assigned to you by me formerly. O son, seek refuge in me alone. I shall save you.”

## \|\| 7.8.44 \|\|

*\|\| nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*atheśvaraḥ padmayoneḥ śrutvā garvavatīṃ giram \|*  
*cukopātīva ca tadā kurvanniva layammune \|\| 44 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:

44\. O sage, on hearing the haughty words of the lotus-born Brahmā, Śiva became angry as if out for destruction.

## \|\| 7.8.45 \|\|

*sa kopatassamutpādya puruṣaṃ bhairavaṃ kvacit \|*  
*prajvalantaṃ sumahasā prītyā ca parameśvaraḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. Angrily, he created a Puruṣa Bhairava blazing in great brilliance. Then he spoke lovingly.

## \|\| 7.8.4-6 \|\|

*citramatra na kiñcidvai durjñeyā khalu śāmbhavī \|*  
*tayā saṃmohitāssarve nārcayantyapi tamparam \|\| 4 \|\|*

*veda cedyadi vātmānaṃ sa eva parameśvaraḥ \|*  
*tadā vihanti te sarvve svecchayā na hi ke'pi tam \|\| 5 \|\|*

*sarvvago'pi maheśāno nekṣyate mūḍhabuddhibhiḥ \|*  
*devavad budhyate loke yo'tīto manasāṃ girām \|\| 6 \|\|*

Śiva said:—

4-6. O Kālabhairava, at the outset this lotus-born Brahmā shall be chastised by you. You shine like god of death, hence you are Kālarāja.

## \|\| 7.8.47 \|\|

*viśvaṃ bhartuṃ samarthosi bhīṣaṇādbhairavaḥ smṛtaḥ \|*  
*tvatto bheṣyati kālo'pi tatastvaṃ kālabhairavaḥ \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. You are called Bhairava because you are of terrifying features and you are capable of supporting the universe. Since even Kāla is afraid of you, you are called Kālabhairava.

## \|\| 7.8.48 \|\|

*āmardayiṣyati bhavānruṣṭo duṣṭātmano yataḥ \|*  
*āmardaka iti khyātiṃ tatassarvatra yāsyasi \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. When you arc angry you will be suppressing the wicked souls. Hence you will be known everywhere as the suppressor of the wicked.

## \|\| 7.8.49 \|\|

*yataḥ pāpāni bhaktānāṃ bhakṣayiṣyasi tatkṣaṇāt \|*  
*pāpabhakṣaṇa ityeva tava nāma bhaviṣyati \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. Since you will be devouring the sins of devotees in a trice your name will be famous as sin-eater.

## \|\| 7.8.50 \|\|

*yā me muktipurī kāśī sarvvābhyo'hi garīyasī \|*  
*ādhipatyaṃ ca tasyāste kālarāja sadaiva hi \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. O Kālarāja, you will have forever the suzerainty over my city Kāśī, the city of liberation, which is greater than all other cities.

## \|\| 7.8.51 \|\|

*tatra ye pātakinarāsteṣāṃ śāstā tvameva hi \|*  
*śubhāśubhaṃ ca tatkarma citragupto likhiṣyati \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. You alone will be the chastiser of those who commit sins there. Citragupta will write down their auspicious and inauspicious activities.

## \|\| 7.8.52 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*etānvarānpragṛhyātha tatkṣaṇātkālabhairavaḥ \|*  
*vāmāṃgulinakhāgreṇa cakarta ca vidheśśiraḥ \|\| 52 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

52\. After receiving all these boons. Kāla-Bhairava, in a trice, cut off Brahmā’s head with the tip of the nails of the fingers of his left hand.

## \|\| 7.8.53 \|\|

*yadaṃgamaparādhnoti kāryaṃ tasyaiva śāsanam \|*  
*ato yena kṛtā nindā tacchinnampañcamaṃ śiraḥ \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. The limb that commits offence shall be chestísed. Hence the fifth head that censured was cut off.

## \|\| 7.8.54 \|\|

*atha cchinnaṃ vidhiśiro dṛṣṭvā bhītataro hariḥ \|*  
*śātarudriyamantraiśca bhaktyā tuṣṭāva śaṅkaram \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. On seeing Brahma’s (Brahmā’s?) head cut off, Viṣṇu became terribly afraid. Devotedly he repeated the Śatarudriya mantras and eulogised Śiva.

## \|\| 7.8.55 \|\|

*bhīto hiraṇyagarbho'pi jajāpa śatarudriyam \|*  
*itthaṃ tau gatagarvau hi saṃjātau tatkṣaṇānmune \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. The frightened golden-wombed Brahma (Brahmā?) too performed the Japa of Śatarudriya. O sage, thus, in an instant, both of them were divested of their pride.

## \|\| 7.8.56 \|\|

*parabrahma śivaḥ sākṣātsaccidānandalakṣaṇaḥ \|*  
*paramātmā guṇātīta iti jñānamavāpatuḥ \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. But they realised that Śiva is the Supreme Brahman with Sat (Existence) Cit (knowledge) and Ānanda (Bliss) as the characteristics. He is the great Ātman and transcends the attributes.

## \|\| 7.8.57 \|\|

*sanatkumāra sarvajña śṛṇu me paramaṃ śubham \|*  
*yāvadgarvo bhavettāvajjñānaguptirviśeṣataḥ \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. O omniscient Sanatkumāra, listen to my extremely auspicious advice. As long as there is pride, perfect knowledge lies excessively hidden.

## \|\| 7.8.58 \|\|

*tyaktvābhi mānaṃ puruṣo jānāti parameśvaram \|*  
*garviṇaṃ hanti viśveśo jāto garvāpahārakaḥ \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. Eschewing false prestige and pride, a man realises lord Śiva. Lord Śiva quells the arrogant because he is the destroyer of arrogance.

## \|\| 7.8.59 \|\|

*atha viṣṇuvidhī jñātvā vigarvau parameśvaraḥ \|*  
*prasanno'bhūnmahādevo'karottāvabhayau prabhuḥ \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. Coming to know that Brahmā and Viṣṇu had been deprived of their pride, lord Śiva the great god became delighted and the lord assured them of his protection.

## \|\| 7.8.60 \|\|

*āśvāsya tau mahādevaḥ prītaḥ praṇatavatsalaḥ \|*  
*prāha svāṃ mūrtimaparāṃ bhairavantaṃ kapardinam \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. After consoling both of them, the delighted Śiva, favourably disposed towards his devotees, spoke to Bhairava—his own other form.

## \|\| 7.8.61 \|\|

*mahādeva uvāca \|*  
*tvayā mānyo viṣṇurasau tathā śatadhṛtiḥ svayam \|*  
*kapālamvaidhasamvāpi nīlalohita dhāraya \|\| 61 \|\|*

Lord Siva said:—

61\. This Viṣṇu shall be honoured by you as well as by Brahmā, O Nīlalohita, hold the skull of Brahmā as an atonement.

## \|\| 7.8.62 \|\|

*brahmahatyāpanodāya vrataṃ lokāya darśaya \|*  
*cara tvaṃ satataṃ bhikṣāṃ kapālavratamāśritaḥ \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. Show to the world the rite of expiation for removing the sin of slaying brahmin. You beg for alms by resorting to “the rite of skull.”

## \|\| 7.8.63 \|\|

*ityuktvā paśyatastasya tejorūpaḥ śivo'bravīt \|*  
*utpādya caikāṃ kanyāntu brahmahatyābhiviśrutām \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. After saying this and creating a virgin renowned as “Brahmahatyā” even as he was watching, Śiva, in the form of mass of flame, spoke thus.

## \|\| 7.8.64 \|\|

*yāvadvārāṇasīndivyāmpurīmeṣāṃ gamiṣyati \|*  
*tāvattvaṃ bhīṣaṇe kālamanugacchograrūpiṇam \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. Until she goes to the divine city of Vārāṇasī you too shall follow her for a terribly long period.

## \|\| 7.8.65 \|\|

*sarvatra te praveśo'sti tyaktvā vārāṇasīṃ purīm \|*  
*vārāṇasīṃ yadā gacchettanmuktāṃ bhava tatkṣaṇāt \|\| 65 \|\|*

65\. You will have free access to everywhere except the city of Vārāṇasī. When she goes to Vārāṇasī you can be freed of her in a trice.

## \|\| 7.8.66 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*niyojya tāmiti tadā brahmahatyāṃ ca tāmprabhuḥ \|*  
*mahādbhutaśca sa śivo'pyantardhānamagāttataḥ \|\| 66 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

66\. Thus directing the Brahmahatyā, the wonderful lord Śiva, vanished from the scene.

## Chapter 9 - The sports of Bhairava

## \|\| 7.9.1 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*sanatkumāra sarvajña bhairavīmaparāṃ kathām \|*  
*śṛṇu prītyā mahādoṣasaṃhartrīmbhaktivarddhinīm \|\| 1 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

1\. O omniscient Sanatkumāra, listen lovingly to another story of Bhairava that destroys all defects and enhances devotion.

## \|\| 7.9.2 \|\|

*tatsānnidhyaṃ bhairavo'pi kālo'bhūtkālakālanaḥ \|*  
*sa devadevavākyena bibhratkāpālikaṃ vratam \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Due to Brahmahatyā’s presence Bhairava began to calculate the time for release. At the words of the lord of gods, he kept on the rite of Kāpālika.

## \|\| 7.9.3 \|\|

*kapālapāṇirviśvātmā cacāra bhuvanatrayam \|*  
*nātyākṣīccāpi taṃ devaṃ brahmahatyāpi dāruṇā \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. He, the soul of the universe, roamed over the three worlds with the skull in his hand. The terrible Brahmahatyā never left Bhairava.

## \|\| 7.9.4 \|\|

*pratitīrthaṃ bhramanvāpi vimukto brahmahatyayā \|*  
*ataḥ kāmārimahimā sarvopi hyavagamyatām \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. Although he went to every holy centre, he was not left off by Brahmahatyā. Let the greatness of Śiva, the enemy of Kāma, be understood thereby.

## \|\| 7.9.5 \|\|

*pramathaiḥ sevyamāno'pi hyekadā viharanharaḥ \|*  
*kāpāliko yayau svairī nārāyaṇaniketanam \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Once, while sporting about, being attended upon by Pramathas, Bhairava went as he pleased, to the abode of Viṣṇu.

## \|\| 7.9.6-7 \|\|

*athāyāntaṃ mahākālaṃ trinetraṃ sarpakuṇḍalam \|*  
*mahādevāṃśasambhūtaṃ pūrṇākāraṃ ca bhairavam \|\| 6 \|\|*

*papāta daṇḍavadbhūmau taṃ dṛṣṭvā garuḍadhvajaḥ \|*  
*devāśca munayaścaiva devanāryyaḥ samantataḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

6-7. On seeing the three-eyed Bhairava of full size, born partially of lord Śiva, with serpents for his ear-rings the Garuḍa-bannered Viṣṇu fell prostrate on the ground like a staff. The gods, the sages and the womenfolk of the gods too did the same.

## \|\| 7.9.8 \|\|

*atha viṣṇuḥ praṇamyainaṃ prayātaḥ kamalāpatiḥ \|*  
*śirasyañjalimādhāya tuṣṭāva vividhaiḥ stavaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. After bowing to him, pure in mind, and keeping the joined palms on his head, Viṣṇu, the consort of Lakṣmī, eulogised him with various hymns.

## \|\| 7.9.9 \|\|

*sānando'tha hariḥ prāha prasannātmā mahāmune \|*  
*kṣīrodamathanodbhūtāṃ padmāṃ padmālayāṃ mudā \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. The delighted Viṣṇu being highly pleased in his mind, O great sage, joyously spoke to Lakṣmī, born at the time of the churning of the milky ocean and residing in the lotus.

## \|\| 7.9.10 \|\|

*viṣṇuruvāca \|*  
*priye paśyābjanayane dhanyāsi subhage'naghe \|*  
*dhanyo'haṃ devi suśroṇi yatpaśyāvo jagatpatim \|\| 10 \|\|*

Viṣṇu said:—

10\. “O lotus-eyed beloved, you are blessed, O comely sinless lady. O gentle lady of beautiful hips, I am blessed, since we both see the lord of the universe.

## \|\| 7.9.11 \|\|

*ayandhātā vidhātā ca lokānāṃ prabhurīśvaraḥ \|*  
*anādiḥ śaraṇaḥ śāntaḥ puraḥ ṣaḍviṃśasaṃmitaḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. He is the creator, the maintainer and the lord of the worlds. He is the primordial being, the lord and the refuge of all. He is calm and higher than the twenty-six principles.

## \|\| 7.9.12 \|\|

*sarvajñaḥ sarvayogīśassarvabhūtaikanāyakaḥ \|*  
*sarvabhūtāntarātmāyaṃ sarveṣāṃ sarvadaḥ sadā \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. He is omniscient, the lord of all Yogins, the sole leader of all Beings, the inner Ātman of all living creatures and the bestower of everything to everyone always.

## \|\| 7.9.13 \|\|

*ye vinidrā viniśvāsāḥ śāntā dhyānaparāyaṇāḥ \|\| \|*  
*dhiyā paśyaṃti hṛdaye soyaṃ padme samīkṣatām \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. O Lakṣmī, see, he is that lord whom the quiet wakeful people, engrossed in breathless meditation, see in their heart of hearts through their mind.

## \|\| 7.9.14 \|\|

*yaṃ vidurvvedatattvajñā yogino yatamānasāḥ \|*  
*arūpo rūpavānbhūtvā so'yamāyāti sarvagaḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Here he comes, the all-pervasive who is without form but has assumed a form (now) and whom only the Yogins of restrained mind, the knowers of the principles of the Vedas, understand.

## \|\| 7.9.15 \|\|

*aho vicitraṃ devasya ceṣṭitamparameṣṭhinaḥ \|*  
*yasyākhyāṃ bruvato nityaṃ na dehaḥ so'pi dehabhṛt \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. Oh, wonderful indeed is the activity of the lord Śiva, for he who repeats his names is freed from the bondage of the body that he has taken then.

## \|\| 7.9.16 \|\|

*taṃ dṛṣṭvā na punarjanma labhyate mānavairbhuvi \|*  
*soyamāyāti bhagavāṃstryambakaśśaśibhūṣaṇaḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. On seeing him men do not obtain a rebirth in the world. Such a lord the moon-crested Tryambaka comes here.

## \|\| 7.9.17 \|\|

*puṇḍarīkadalāyāme dhanye me'dya vilocane \|*  
*yaddṛśyate mahādevo hyābhyāṃ lakṣmi maheśvaraḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. O Lakṣmī, blessed indeed are my eyes as wide as the petals of a lotus, since by them the great god lord Śiva is seen.

## \|\| 7.9.18 \|\|

*dhigdhikpadantu devānāṃ paraṃ dṛṣṭvā na śaṃkaram \|*  
*labhyate yatra nirvāṇaṃ sarva duḥkhāntakṛttu yat \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. Fie upon the position of the gods where emancipation is not obtained, but on seeing Śiva it is obtained and it puts an end to all miseries.

## \|\| 7.9.19 \|\|

*devatvādaśubhaṃ kiñciddevaloke na vidyate \|*  
*dṛṣṭvāpi sarve deveśaṃ yanmuktinna labhāmahe \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. There is nothing more inauspicious than the state of the gods in the heaven that here even after seeing the lord of the gods all of us do not obtain salvation.

## \|\| 7.9.20 \|\|

*evamuktvā hṛṣīkeśassaṃprahṛṣṭatanūruhaḥ \|*  
*praṇipatya mahādevamidamāha vṛṣadhvajam \|\| 20 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

20\. After saying this, Viṣṇu bowed to the great lord with the thrill of delight through pleasure and spoke this to the bull-bannered deity?

## \|\| 7.9.21 \|\|

*viṣṇuruvāca \|*  
*kimidandevadevena sarvajñena tvayā vibho \|*  
*kriyate jagatāṃ dhātrā sarvapāpaharāvyaya \|\| 21 \|\|*

Viṣṇu said:—

21\. O lord, O unchanging one, O queller of all sins, what is this being done by you the omniscient lord of the gods and the creator of the worlds?

## \|\| 7.9.22 \|\|

*krīḍeyantava deveśa trilocana mahāmate \|*  
*kiṅkāraṇaṃ virūpākṣa ceṣṭitante smarārdana \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. O lord of the gods, O three-eyed intelligent one this is only your sport. O Virūpākṣa, O suppresser of Kāma, what is this activity of yours motivated by?

## \|\| 7.9.23 \|\|

*kimarthaṃ bhagavañchambho bhikṣāñcarasi śaktipa \|*  
*saṃśayo me jagannātha eṣa trailokyarājyada \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. O lord Śiva, O lord of Śakti, why do you go a-begging? O lord of the universe, this is my doubt, O bestower of the kingdom of the three worlds!

## \|\| 7.9.24 \|\|

*\|\| nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*evamuktastataḥ śambhurviṣṇunā bhairavo haraḥ \|*  
*pratyuvācādbhutotissa viṣṇuṃ hi vihasanprabhuḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

24\. Thus addressed by Viṣṇu, lord Śiva, the destroyer of the worlds of ferocious form of wonderful sports, laughingly said to Viṣṇu.

## \|\| 7.9.25 \|\|

*\|\| bhairava uvāca \|*  
*brahmaṇastu śiraśchinnamaṃgulyāgranakhena ha \|*  
*tadaghampratihantuṃ hi carāmyetadvrataṃ śubham \|\| 25 \|\|*

Bhairava said:—

25\. Brahma's head was cut off with the top of the nail of my fingers. It is to remove that sin that I am observing this auspicious sacred rite.

## \|\| 7.9.26 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*evamukto maheśena bhairaveṇa ramāpatiḥ \|*  
*smṛtvā kiṃcinnataśirāḥ punarevamajijñapat \|\| 26 \|\|*

Nanīśvara said:—

26\. Thus addressed by Śiva, Bhairava, the lord of Lakṣmī remembered something aod bent down his head. He spoke again.

## \|\| 7.9.27 \|\|

*\|\| viṣṇuruvāca \|*  
*yathecchasi tathā krīḍa sarvavighnopanodaka \|*  
*māyayā māṃ mahādeva nācchādayitumarhasi \|\| 27 \|\|*

Viṣṇu said:—

27\. “O great god, O remover of all obstacles, sport about as you please. But it dors not behove you to envelop me with your Māyā.

## \|\| 7.9.28 \|\|

*nābhīkamalakośāttu koṭiśaḥ kamalāsanāḥ \|*  
*kalpe kalpe purā hyānsatyaṃ yogabalādvibho \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. O lord, it is true that from within the lotus from my navel, crores of lotus-seated Brahmās have come out in the different Kalpas through the power of Yoga.

## \|\| 7.9.29 \|\|

*tyaja māyāmimāndeva dustarāmakṛtātmabhiḥ \|*  
*brahmādayo mahādeva māyayā tava mohitāḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. O lord, please eschew this Māyā very difficult to be crossed by those who have no control over themselves. Brahmā and others are deluded by your Māyā.

## \|\| 7.9.30 \|\|

*yathāvadanugacchāmi ceṣṭitante śivāpate \|*  
*tavaivānugrahācchambho sarveśvara satāṃgate \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. O Śiva, O lord of all, O goal of the good, O lord of Śivā, it is due to your own blessing that I am able to follow your activity.

## \|\| 7.9.31-32 \|\|

*saṃhārakāle saṃprāpte sadevānnikhilānmunīn \|*  
*lokānvarṇāśramavato hariṣyasi yadā hara \|\| 31 \|\|*

*tadā kṛte mahādeva pāpaṃ brahmavadhādikam \|*  
*pāratantryaṃ na te śambho svairaṃ krīḍatyato bhavān \|\| 32 \|\|*

31-32. When the time of dissolution comes, O destroyer of the world, you annihilate all the gods, sages and people of all castes and stages of life. O great god, then, the sin of slaying brahmins and others does not affect you. You are not bound by these and are quite independent. You sport as you please.

## \|\| 7.9.33 \|\|

*arghīva brahmaṇo hyasthnāṃ srakkaṇṭhe tava bhāsate \|*  
*tathādyanugatā śambho brahmahatyā tavānagha \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. O Śiva, the garland of the bones of Brahmā shines around your neck. Similarly, O Śiva, O sinless one, the primordial Brahmahatyā has followed you.

## \|\| 7.9.34 \|\|

*kṛtvāpi sumahatpāpaṃ yastvāṃ smarati mānavaḥ \|*  
*ādhāraṃ jagatāmīśa tasya pāpaṃ vilīyate \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. Even after committing a great sin, if a person, remembers you who are the lord and support of the worlds, his sin is quelled.

## \|\| 7.9.35 \|\|

*yathā tamo na tiṣṭheta sannidhāvaṃśumālinaḥ \|*  
*tathaiva tava yo bhaktaḥ pāpantasya vrajetkṣayam \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. Just as darkness does not stand in the presence of the sun, similarly the sin of your devotee does not stand but is wasted away.

## \|\| 7.9.36 \|\|

*yaścintayati puṇyātmā tava pādāmbujadvayam \|*  
*brahmahatyākṛtamapi pāpantasya vrajetkṣayam \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. If a pious soul contemplates on the lotus-like pair of your feet, even his sin of brahmin-slaughter becomes wasted away.

## \|\| 7.9.37 \|\|

*tava nāmānuraktā vāgyasya puṃso jagatpate \|*  
*apyadrikūṭatulitaṃ nainastamanubādhate \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. O lord of the universe, if a person is devoted to uttering your name, his sin as weighty as the peak of a mountain does not overwhelm him.

## \|\| 7.9.38 \|\|

*paramātmanparandhāma svecchābhidhṛtavigraha \|*  
*kutūhalaṃ taveśedaṃ kṛpaṇādhīnateśvara \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. O great soul, O ultimate abode, O god that assumest a body at your own sweet will, this is only a fancy of yours, this miserliness anḍ dependence.

## \|\| 7.9.39 \|\|

*adya dhanyo'smi deveśa yatra paśyaṃti yoginaḥ \|*  
*paśyāmi taṃ jaganmūrtti parameśvaramavyayam \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. O lord of gods, I am blessed. I am seeing the eternal supreme god of cosmic form whom even Yogins do not see.

## \|\| 7.9.40 \|\|

*adya me paramo lābhastvadya me maṃgalaṃ param \|*  
*taṃ dṛṣṭvāmṛta tṛptasya tṛṇaṃ svargāpavargakam \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. I have the greatest of gains today. I have the most auspicious of signs today. Even heaven and emancipation are as insignificant as a blade of grass to me who am contented with the nectar of seeing him.”

## \|\| 7.9.41 \|\|

*itthaṃ vadati goviṃde vimalā padmayā tayā \|*  
*manorathavatī nāma bhikṣā pātre samarpitā \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. When Govinda was saying this, Padmā offered the desired alms into his begging bowl.

## \|\| 7.9.42 \|\|

*bhikṣāṭanāya devo'pi niragātparayā mudā \|*  
*anyatrāpi mahādevo bhairavaścāttavigrahaḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. Lord Śiva, who had assumed the body of Lord Bhairava, left the place joyously for begging elsewhere.

## \|\| 7.9.43 \|\|

*dṛṣṭvānuyāyinīṃ tāntu samāhūya janārdanaḥ \|*  
*saṃprārthayadbrahmahatyāṃ vimuṃca tvaṃ triśūlinam \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. On seeing Brahmahatyā following him, Viṣṇu called her and requested her “Please stop pursuing the Trident-bearing deity.”

## \|\| 7.9.44 \|\|

*\|\| brahmahatyovāca \|*  
*anenāpi miṣeṇāhaṃ saṃsevyāmuṃ vṛṣadhvajam \|*  
*ātmānampāvayiṣyāmi tvapunarbhavadarśanam \|\| 44 \|\|*

Brahmahatyā said:—

44\. Under this pretext, I am waiting upon the bull-bannered deity. Thus I shall sanctify myself for the annihilation of a rebirth.

## \|\| 7.9.45 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*sā tatyāja na tatpārśvaṃ vyāhṛtāpi murāriṇā \|*  
*tamūce'tha hariṃ śaṃbhuḥ smerāsyo bhairavo vacaḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

45\. Though urged by Viṣṇu she did not leave his side. The smiling Śiva of Bhairava form, spoke these words to Viṣṇu.

## \|\| 7.9.46 \|\|

*bhairava uvāca \|*  
*tvadvākpīyūṣapānena tṛpto'smi bahumānada \|*  
*svabhāvo'yaṃ hi sādhūnāṃ yattvaṃ vadasi māpate \|\| 46 \|\|*

Bkairava said:—

46\. O lord of Lakṣmī, O bestower of honour, I am satisfied by drinking the nectar of your words. Whatever you say is natural to good-natured persons.

## \|\| 7.9.47 \|\|

*varaṃ vṛṇīṣva goviṃda varado'smi tavānagha \|*  
*agraṇīrmama bhaktānāṃ tvaṃ hare nirvikāravān \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. O Viṣṇu, choose your boon, O sinless one, I am the granter of boons. O Viṣṇu, you are free from aberrations and are the foremost among my devotees.

## \|\| 7.9.48 \|\|

*no mādyanti tathā bhaikṣyairbhikṣavo'pyatisaṃskṛtaiḥ \|*  
*yathā mānasudhāpānairnanu bhikṣāṭanajvarāḥ \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. Mendicants suffering from the fever of begging alms do not turn mad with the good articles of alms so much as by the drinking in of the nectar of honour.

## \|\| 7.9.49 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityākarṇya vacaḥ śaṃbho bhairavasya parātmanaḥ \|*  
*suprasannataro bhūtvā samavocanmaheśvaram \|\| 49 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

49\. On hearing the words of high-souled Bhairava, Viṣṇu became delighted and spoke to him.

## \|\| 7.9.50 \|\|

*viṣṇuruvāca \|*  
*eṣa eva varaḥ ślāghyo yadahaṃ dektādhipam \|*  
*paśyāmi tvāndevadeva manovāṇī pathātigam \|\| 50 \|\|*

Viṣṇu said:—

50\. O lord of gods, this itself is a great favour that I see you who being the lord of the gods are inconceivable by mind and speech.

## \|\| 7.9.51 \|\|

*adabhreyaṃ sudhādṛṣṭiranayā me mahotsavaḥ \|*  
*ayattnanidhilābhoyaṃ vīkṣaṇaṃ hara te satām \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. O Śiva, this is like a shower of nectar without clouds. I am jubilant over this; thy benign glance to the good is a treasure easily acquired by them.

## \|\| 7.9.52 \|\|

*aviyogo'stu me deva tvadaṃghriyugalena vai \|*  
*eṣa eva varaḥ śaṃbho nānyaṃ kaścid vṛṇe varam \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. O lord Śiva, let me never be detached from your feet; this is the favour I seek, nothing else.

## \|\| 7.9.53 \|\|

*śrībhairavī uvāca \|*  
*evambhavatu te tāta yattvayoktaṃ mahāmate \|*  
*sarveṣāmapi devānāṃ varadastvaṃ bhaviṣyasi \|\| 53 \|\|*

Lord Bhairava said:—

53\. “O dear, O intelligent one, let what you have said occur. You will be the granter of boons to all the gods.”

## \|\| 7.9.54 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*anugṛhyeti daityāri keṃdrādribhuvanecaram \|*  
*bheje vimuktanagarīṃ nāmnā vārāṇasīṃ purīm \|\| 54 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

54\. Blessing Viṣṇu moving in the central mountain Meru and the world, the lord went to the city of the liberated, Vārāṇasī.

## \|\| 7.9.55 \|\|

*kṣetre praviṣṭamātre'tha bhairave bhīṣaṇākṛtau \|*  
*hāhetyuktvā brahmahatyā pātālaṃ cāviśattadā \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. As soon as Bhairava of terrible features entered the holy centre, Brahmahatyā felt aggrieved and entered the nether worlds.

## \|\| 7.9.56 \|\|

*kapālaṃ brāhmaṇaḥ sadyo bhairavasya karāṃbujāt \|*  
*papāta bhuvi tattīrthamabhūtkāpālamocanam \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. The skull of Brahmā fell on the ground suddenly from the lotus-like hand of Bhairava. The holy centre then became Kapālamocana.

## \|\| 7.9.57 \|\|

*kapālaṃ brahmaṇo rudrassarveṣāmeva paśyatām \|*  
*hastātpatantamālokya nanarta parayā mudā \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. On seeing the skull of Brahmā falling from his hand, even as everyone was watching it, Rudra danced with great joy.

## \|\| 7.9.58 \|\|

*vidheḥ kapālaṃ nāmuṃcatkaramatyantadussaham \|*  
*parasya bhramataḥ kvāpi tatkāśyāṃ kṣaṇato'patat \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. The unbearable skull of Brahmā did not leave his hand as he was roaming elsewhere but it fell down in a trice at Kāśī.

## \|\| 7.9.59 \|\|

*śūlino brahmaṇo hatyā nāpaiti sma ca yā kvacit \|*  
*sā kāśyāṃ kṣaṇato naṣṭā tasmātsevyā hi kāśikā \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. Brahmahatyā which did not leave Siva at any other place vanished in a moment at Kāśī. Hence Kāśī shall be resorted to for shedding off evil.

## \|\| 7.9.60 \|\|

*kapālamocanaṃ kāśyāṃ yaḥ smarettīrthamuttamam \|*  
*ihānyatrāpi yatpāpaṃ kṣipraṃ tasya praṇaśyati \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. If a person remembers the excellent holy centre Kapālamocana at Kāśī, his sin committed here and elsewhere becomes quelled immediately.

## \|\| 7.9.61 \|\|

*āgatya tīrthapravare snānaṃ kṛtvā vidhānataḥ \|*  
*tarpayitvā pitṝndevānmucyate brahmahatyayā \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. After coming to the foremost of holy centres, performing the ablution in accordance with the rules and by making the water-offering to the manes one is rid of Brahmahatyā.

## \|\| 7.9.62 \|\|

*kapālamocanaṃ tīrthaṃ puraskṛtvā tu bhairavaḥ \|*  
*tatraiva tasthau bhaktānāṃ bhakṣayannaghasantatim \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. Bhairava stood facing the holy centre of Kapālamocana, devouring the series of sins of devotees.

## \|\| 7.9.63 \|\|

*kṛṣṇāṣṭamyāntu mārgasya māsasya parameśvaraḥ \|*  
*āvirbabhūva sallīlo bhairavātmā satāmpriyaḥ \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. It was on the eighth day in the dark half of the month of Mārgaśīrṣa that lord Śiva, the favourite of the good and indulging in auspicious sports, manifested himself in the form of Bhairava.

## \|\| 7.9.64 \|\|

*mārgaśīrṣāsitāṣṭamyāṃ kālabhairavasannidhau \|*  
*upoṣya jāgaraṃ kurvanmahāpāpaiḥ pramucyate \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. The person who observes fast on the eighth day of the dark half of the month of Mārgaśīrṣa in front of Kālabhairava and also keeps awake at night is freed from great sins.

## \|\| 7.9.65 \|\|

*anyatrāpi naro bhaktyā tadvrataṃ yaḥ kariṣyati \|*  
*sa jāgaraṃ mahāpāpairmukto yāsyati sadgatim \|\| 65 \|\|*

65\. He who performs that rite with devotion even elsewhere and also keeps awake is rid of great sins and attains the goal of the good.

## \|\| 7.9.66 \|\|

*anekajanmaniyutairyatkṛtaṃ jantubhistvagham \|*  
*tatsarvaṃ vilayaṃ yāti kālabhairavadarśanāt \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. The sin committed by persons in the course of countless millions of births is quelled entirely on seeing Kālabhairava.

## \|\| 7.9.67 \|\|

*kālabhairavabhaktānāṃ pātakāni karoti yaḥ \|*  
*sa mūḍho duḥkhito bhūtvā punardurgatimāpnuyāt \|\| 67 \|\|*

67\. He who commits offence against the devotees of Kālabhairava is deluded and becomes miserable. He will attain the distress of the wicked people.

## \|\| 7.9.68 \|\|

*viśveśvare'pi ye bhaktā no bhaktāḥ kālabhairave \|*  
*te labhante mahāduḥkhaṃ kāśyāṃ caiva viśeṣataḥ \|\| 68 \|\|*

68\. The devotees of lord Śiva, who do not show devotion for Kālabhairava, attain great sorrow and especially so at Kāśī.

## \|\| 7.9.69 \|\|

*vārāṇasyāmuṣitvā yo bhairavaṃ na bhajennaraḥ \|*  
*tasya pāpāni varddhante śuklapakṣe yathā śaśī \|\| 69 \|\|*

69\. The man who, while staying at Vārāṇasī, neglects the worship of Bhairava acquires sins that multiply like the moon in the bright half.

## \|\| 7.9.70 \|\|

*kālarājaṃ na yaḥ kāśyāṃ pratibhūtāṣṭamīkujam \|*  
*bhajettasya kṣayaṃ puṇyaṃ kṛṣṇapakṣe yathā śaśī \|\| 70 \|\|*

70\. If a person neglects the worship of Kālarāja at Kāśī on every Tuesday and Bhūtāṣṭamī day, his merit decreases like the moon in the dark half.

## \|\| 7.9.71 \|\|

*śrutvākhyānamidampuṇyambrahmahatyāpanodakam \|*  
*bhairavotpattisaṃjñaṃ ca sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate \|\| 71 \|\|*

71\. A person is rid of all sins on hearing this narrative of “Bhairava’s nativity” which can quell even Brahmahatyā.

## \|\| 7.9.72 \|\|

*bandhanāgārasaṃstho'pi prāpto'pi vipadamparām \|*  
*prādurbhāvaṃ bhairavasya śrutvā mucyeta saṅkaṭāt \|\| 72 \|\|*

72\. Even a person put in a prison, or involved in adversity is freed from distress on hearing of the narrative of Bhairava.

## Chapter 10 - The narrative of Nṛsiṃha (man-lion)

## \|\| 7.10.1 \|\|

*naṃdīśvara uvāca \|*  
*vidhvaṃsī dakṣayajñasya vīrabhadrāhvayaḥ prabho \|*  
*avatāraśca vijñeyaḥ śivasya paramātmanaḥ \|\| 1 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said.

1\. The destroyer of Dakṣa’s sacrifice Vīrabhadra shall be known as an incarnation of lord Śiva, the great soul.

## \|\| 7.10.2 \|\|

*satīcaritre kathitaṃ caritaṃ tasya kṛtsnaśaḥ \|*  
*śrutaṃ tvayāpi bahudhā nātaḥ proktaṃ suvistarāt \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. His narrative has been mentioned in the episode of Satī. You too have heard it several times. Hence it is not mentioned in detail.

## \|\| 7.10.3 \|\|

*ataḥ paraṃ muniśreṣṭha bhavatsnehādbravīmi tat \|*  
*śārdūlākhyāvatāraṃ ca śaṅkarasya prabhoḥ śṛṇu \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. O excellent sage, now by my affection to you I shall narrate the Śārdūla incarnation of lord Śiva, Listen.

## \|\| 7.10.4 \|\|

*sadāśivena devānāṃ hitārthaṃ rūpamadbhutam \|*  
*śārabhaṃ ca dhṛtandivyaṃ jvalajjvālāsamaprabham \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. For the benefit of the gods, the wonderfully divine form of a Śarabha (the mythological animal with eight legs and capacity of killing lions) of blazing fire-like lustre was assumed by lord Śiva.

## \|\| 7.10.5 \|\|

*śivāvatārā amitā sadbhaktahitakārakāḥ \|*  
*saṅkhyā na śakyate kartuṃ teṣāṃ ca munisattamāḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. O excellent sages, Śiva’s incarnations are countless and conducive to the benefit of good devotees. Their number cannot be counted or specified.

## \|\| 7.10.6-7 \|\|

*ākāśasya ca tārāṇāṃ reṇukānāṃ kṣitestathā \|*  
*āsārāṇāṃ ca vṛddhena bahukalpaiḥ kadāpi hi \|\| 6 \|\|*

*saṅkhyā viśakyate kartuṃ suprājñairbahujanmabhiḥ \|*  
*śivāvatārāṇāṃ naiva satyaṃ jānīhi madvacaḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

6-7. The number of the stars in the sky, of the sand particles on the earth and of the bursting showers of heavy rain can be specified by intelligent persons at some time in the course of many births over many Kalpas; but not of the incarnations of Śiva; know my words to be true.

## \|\| 7.10.8 \|\|

*tathāpi ca yathābuddhyā kathayāmi kathāśrutam \|*  
*caritraṃ śārabhaṃ divyaṃ paramaiśvaryyasūcakam \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Still in accordance with my intelligence I shall mention, exactly as I had heard, the divine story of the Śarabha! portending great prosperity.

## \|\| 7.10.9 \|\|

*jayaśca vijayaścaiva bhavadbhiḥ śāpitau yadā \|*  
*tadā ditisutau dvau tāvabhūtāṃ kaśyapānmune \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. O sage, when Jaya and Vijaya were cursed by you, they became the sons of Diti and Kaśyapa.

## \|\| 7.10.10 \|\|

*hiraṇyakaśipuścādyo hiraṇyākṣo'nujo balī \|*  
*devarṣipārṣadau jātau tau dvāvapi ditessutau \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Those, two attendants; the celestial sages were born as Diti’s sons. The elder was Hiraṇyakaśipu and the younger was Hiraṇyākṣa the very powerful.

## \|\| 7.10.11 \|\|

*pṛthvyuddhāre vidhātrā va prārthito hi purā prabhuḥ \|*  
*hiraṇyākṣaṃ jaghānāsau viṣṇurvārāharūpadhṛk \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Formerly lord Viṣṇu was requested by Brahmā to uplift the Earth. He assumed the form of a boar and slew Hiraṇyākṣa.

## \|\| 7.10.12 \|\|

*taṃ śrutvā bhrātaraṃ vīraṃ nihataṃ prāṇasannibham \|*  
*cukopa haraye'tīva hiraṇyakaśipurmune \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. O sage, on hearing that his heroic brother, as dear to him as his very vital breath, was killed, Hiraṇyakaśipu became furious with Viṣṇu.

## \|\| 7.10.13 \|\|

*varṣāṇāmayutaṃ taptvā brahmaṇo varamāpa saḥ \|*  
*na kaścinmārayenmāṃ vai tvatsṛṣṭāviti tuṣṭataḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. He performed penance for ten thousand years and propitiated Brahmā from whom he obtained the desired boon. “None of your creations shall kill me.”

## \|\| 7.10.14 \|\|

*śoṇitākhyapuraṃ gatvā devānāhūya sarvataḥ \|*  
*trilokīṃ svavaśe kṛtvā cakre rājyamakaṇṭakam \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. He left for Śoṇita. He challenged the gods everywhere. He put the three worlds in his control and had an unrivalled rule over it.

## \|\| 7.10.15 \|\|

*devarṣikadanaṃ cakre sarvadharma vilopakaḥ \|*  
*dvijapīḍākaraḥ pāpī hiraṇyakaśipurmune \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. O sage. Hiraṇyakaśipu harassed the gods and the sages. He violated all virtuous rites. He oppressed brahmins and became a sinner.

## \|\| 7.10.16 \|\|

*prahlādena svaputreṇa haribhaktena daityarāṭ \|*  
*yadā vidveṣamakaroddharirvairaṃ viśeṣataḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. When the king of the Daityas hated his own son Prahlāda, Viṣṇu became his particular enemy.

## \|\| 7.10.17 \|\|

*sabhāstambhāttadā viṣṇurabhūdāvirdrutammune \|*  
*sandhyāyāṃ krodhamāpanno nṛsiṃhavapuṣā tataḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. O sage, Viṣṇu then came out from a pillar in the hall, at dusk in the body of a man-lion (Nṛsiṃha), with great fury.

## \|\| 7.10.18 \|\|

*sarvathā muniśārdūla karālaṃ nṛharervvapuḥ \|*  
*prajajvālātibhayadaṃ trāsayandaityasattamān \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. O great sage, the body of the man-lion was very terrible in every respect. It blazed frighteningly and terrified the leading Daityas.

## \|\| 7.10.19 \|\|

*nṛsiṃhena tadā daityā nihatāścaiva tatkṣaṇam \|*  
*hiraṇyakaśipuścātha yuddhañcakre sudāruṇam \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. The Daityas were killed in a trice by the man-lion. Hiraṇyakaśipu fought a terrible battle.

## \|\| 7.10.20 \|\|

*mahāyuddhaṃ tayorāsīnmuhūrttammunisattamāḥ \|*  
*vikarālaṃ ca bhayadaṃ sarveṣāṃ romaharṣaṇam \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. O excellent sages, a great fight ensued between them for some time. It was terrifying, frightening and caused horripilation to every one.

## \|\| 7.10.21 \|\|

*sāyaṃ cakarṣa deveśo dehalyāṃ daityapuṅgavam \|*  
*vyomni deveṣu paśyatsu nṛsiṃhaśca rameśvaraḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. The lord of gods, lord oí Lakṣmī, the man-lion dragged the leading Daitya to the threshold even as the gods were watching in the sky.

## \|\| 7.10.22 \|\|

*athotsaṃge ca taṃ kṛtvā nakhaistadudarandrutam \|*  
*vidārya mārayāmāsa paśyatāṃ tridivaukasām \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. While the gods were watching he placed the daitya on his lap and immediately tore open his stomach with his claws and killed him.

## \|\| 7.10.23 \|\|

*hate hiraṇyakaśipau nṛsiṃhe naiva viṣṇunā \|*  
*jagatsvāsthyantadā lebhe na vai devāviśeṣataḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. When Hiraṇyakaśipu was killed by the man-lion Viṣṇu, the whole universe attained normalcy and peace. But the gods did not derive any special comfort.

## \|\| 7.10.24 \|\|

*devadundubhayo neduḥ prahlādo vismayaṃ gataḥ \|*  
*lakṣmīśca vismayaṃ prāptā rūpaṃ dṛṣṭvā'dbhutaṃ hareḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. The celestial drums were sounded. On seeing the wonderful form of Viṣṇu, Prahlāda was surprised, Lakṣmī was in a state of suspense.

## \|\| 7.10.25 \|\|

*hato yadyapi daityendrastathāpi na puraṃ sukham \|*  
*yayurdevā nṛsiṃhasya jvālā sā na nivartitā \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Though the lord of daityas was killed, yet the gods did not derive any pleasure. The fiery fury of the man-lion (Nṛsiṃha) did not subside.

## \|\| 7.10.26-27 \|\|

*tayā ca vyākulaṃ jātaṃ sarvaṃ caiva jagatpunaḥ \|*  
*devāśca duḥkhamāpannāḥ kimbhaviṣyati vā punaḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

*ityevaṃ ca vadantaste bhayādūdūramupasthi tāḥ \|*  
*nṛsiṃhakrodhajajvālāvyākulāḥ padmabhūmukhāḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

26-27. The entire universe was again excited by that fiery splendour. The gods were miserable. Saying “What will happen now?” they kept themselves at a safe distance from fear. The lotus-born deity and others were excited by the fiery fury of the man-lion.

## \|\| 7.10.28 \|\|

*prahrādaṃ preṣayāmāsustacchāntyai nikaṭaṃ hareḥ \|*  
*sarvānmilitvā prahlādaḥ prārthito gatavāṃstadā \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. They sent Prahlāda near Viṣṇu in order to subside his anger. At the behest of all in a body Prahlāda approached the man-lion.

## \|\| 7.10.29 \|\|

*urasā''liṃgayāmāsa taṃ nṛsiṃhaḥ kṛpānidhiḥ \|*  
*hṛdayaṃ śītalaṃ jātaṃ ruḍjvālā na nivarttitā \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. The man-lion, the store-house of mercy, embraced him. The heart became cod, still the flame of fury did not subside.

## \|\| 7.10.30 \|\|

*tathāpi na nivṛtā ruḍjvālā narahareryadā \|*  
*iṣṭaṃ prāptāstato devāśśaṃkara śaraṇaṃ yayuḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. When it did not subside, the afflicted and miserable gods sought refuge in Śiva.

## \|\| 7.10.31 \|\|

*tatra gatvā surāssarve brahmādyā munaya stathā \|*  
*śaṃkaraṃ stavayāmāsurlokānāṃ sukhahetave \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. Going there, Brahmā, other gods and the sages eulogised Śiva for the happiness of all the worlds.

## \|\| 7.10.32 \|\|

*devā ūcuḥ \|*  
*devadeva mahādeva śaraṇāgatavatsala \|*  
*pāhi naḥ śaraṇāpannā nsarvāndevāñjaganti ca \|\| 32 \|\|*

The gods said:—

32\. O great lord of gods, favourably disposed cowards those who seek refuge, save us the gods who have sought refuge in you as well as all the worlds.

## \|\| 7.10.33 \|\|

*namaste'stu namaste'stu namaste'stu sadāśiva \|*  
*pūrvaṃ duḥkhaṃ yadā jātaṃ tadā te rakṣitā vayam \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. Obeisance be to you, O Sadāśiva. Whenever there had been misery formerly, it was you that saved us.

## \|\| 7.10.34 \|\|

*samudro mathitaścaiva ratnānāṃ ca vibhāgaśaḥ \|*  
*kṛte devaistadā śaṃbho gṛhītaṃ garalantvayā \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. The ocean was churned and the gems were shared by the gods. Then O Śiva, poison was taken by you.

## \|\| 7.10.35 \|\|

*rakṣitāḥ sma tadā nātha nīlakaṇṭha iti śrutaḥ \|*  
*viṣaṃ pāsyasi no cettvaṃ bhasmībhūtāstadākhilāḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. We were saved by you, O lord and you became famous as Nīlakaṇṭha (Blue-necked). If you had not drunk the poison then, every thing would have been reduced to ashes.

## \|\| 7.10.36 \|\|

*prasiddhaṃ ca yadā yasya duḥkhaṃ ca jāyate prabho \|*  
*tadā tvannāmamātreṇa sarvaduḥkhaṃ vilīyate \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. This is well known, O lord, that whenever a person is in misery, by repeating your very name the misery is quelled.

## \|\| 7.10.37 \|\|

*idānīṃ nṛharijvālāpīḍitānnassadāśiva \|*  
*tāṃ tvaṃ śamayituṃ deva śakto'sīti suniścitam \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. O Sadāśiva, we are now afflicted by the fiery fury of the man-lion (Nṛsiṃha); O lord, it is certain that you are competent to quell it.

## \|\| 7.10.38 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*iti stutastadā devaiśśaṃkaro bhaktavatsalaḥ \|*  
*pratyuvāca prasannātmā'bhayandattvā paraprabhuḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

38\. Thus eulogised by the gods, lord Śiva, favourably disposed to the devotees, assured them of protection and spoke delightedly.

## \|\| 7.10.39 \|\|

*śaṃkara uvāca \|*  
*svasthānaṃ gacchata surāssarvve brahmādayo'bhayāḥ \|*  
*śamayiṣyāmi yadduḥkhaṃ sarvathā hi vratammama \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. Śiva said:—O ye Brahmā and other gods, Return to your abodes fearlessly. My rite quells misery in every respect:

## \|\| 7.10.40 \|\|

*gato maccharaṇaṃ yastu tasya duḥkhaṃ kṣayaṃ gatam \|*  
*matpriyaḥ śaraṇāpannaḥ prāṇebhyo'pi na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. The misery of the person who seeks refuge in me vanishes. Undoubtedly the seeker of refuge is dearer to me than life.

## \|\| 7.10.41 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*iti śrutvā tadā devā hyānandamparamaṃ gatāḥ \|*  
*yathāgataṃ tathā jagmussmarantaśśaṃkaraṃ mudā \|\| 41 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

41\. On hearing this, the gods were delighted much. Remembering Śiva joyously they returned.

## Chapter 11 - Prologue to Śarabha incarnation

## \|\| 7.11.1 \|\|

*naṃdīśvara uvāca \|*  
*evamabhyarthito devairmatiṃ cakre kṛpā layaḥ \|*  
*mahātejo nṛsiṃhākhyaṃ saṃharttuṃ parameśvaraḥ \|\| 1 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

1\. Thus implored by the gods, lord Śiva, the abode of mercy, decided to quell that great fiery fury of man-lion (Nṛsiṃha).

## \|\| 7.11.2 \|\|

*tadūrddhvaṃ smṛtavānrudro vīrabhadrammahābalam \|*  
*ātmano bhairavaṃ rūpaṃ prāha pralayakārakam \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Thereafter Rudra remembered the powerful Vīrabhadra, the cause of dissolution and addressed Bhairava, his own form.

## \|\| 7.11.3-5 \|\|

*ājagāma tatassadyo gaṇānāmagraṇīrhasan \|*  
*sāṭṭahāsairgaṇavarairutpatadbhiritastataḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

*nṛsiṃharūpairatyugraiḥ koṭibhiḥ parivāritaḥ \|*  
*mādyadbhirabhito vīrairnṛtyadbhiśca mudānvitaiḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

*krīḍadbhiśca mahāvīrairbrahmādyaiḥ kandukairiva \|*  
*adṛṣṭapūrvairanyaiśca veṣṭito vīravanditaḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

3-5. Then suddenly there came the leader of the Gaṇas laughing loudly and accompanied by the leading Gaṇas laughing boisterously and jumping about here and there. He was surrounded by crores of fierce man-lion-formed Gaṇas, madly dancing heroes joyously leaping about. The warlike heroes Brahmā and others were playing about as if with balls; the leader of the Gaṇas was surrounded by others too who had remained unseen. He was respected by the heroes.

## \|\| 7.11.6 \|\|

*kalpāntajvalanajvālo vilasallocanatrayaḥ \|*  
*aśastro hi jaṭājūṭī jvaladbālendumaṇḍitaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. He had a fiery splendour as fierce as the fire at the end of Kalpa. His three eyes shone splendidly. He had no weapon. He had matted hair. He was bedecked by the refulgent crescent moon.

## \|\| 7.11.7 \|\|

*bālenduvalayā kāratīkṣṇadaṃṣṭrāṃkuradvayaḥ \|*  
*ākhaṇḍaladhanuḥkhaṇḍasaṃnibhabhrūlatānvitaḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. He had two sharp fangs curved like the crescent moon. His eyebrows were like the split pieces of Indra’s bow.

## \|\| 7.11.8 \|\|

*mahāpracaṇḍahuṅkārabadhirīkṛtadiṅmukhaḥ \|*  
*nīlameghāñjana śyāmo bhīṣaṇaḥ śmaśrulodbhutaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. With his fierce hissing whooping sound be deafened the faces of the quarters. He was as dark as the blue cloud and collyrium He was terrible and wondrous with full grown moustaches and beards.

## \|\| 7.11.9 \|\|

*vādyakhaṇḍamakhaṇḍābhyāṃ bhrāmayaṃstriśikhaṃ muhuḥ \|*  
*vīrabhadro'pi bhagavānvaraśaktivijṛmbhitaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. He was whirling his trident of unsplit lustre like a piece of musical instrument over and over again. The lord Vīrabhadra arrived with his heroic strength fully expanded.

## \|\| 7.11.10 \|\|

*svayaṃ vijñāpayāmāsa kimatra smṛtikāraṇam \|*  
*ājñāpaya jagatsvāmin prasādaḥ kriyatāmmayi \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. He said to Śiva—“O lord of the universe, why I have been remembered by you? Please do me a favour and command me.”

## \|\| 7.11.11 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityākarṇya maheśāno vīrabhadroktamādarāt \|*  
*vilokya vacanamprītyā provāca khala daṇḍadhṛk \|\| 11 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

11\. On hearing the words of Vīrabhadra, and glancing at his face respectfully lord Śiva, the chastiser of the wicked, said lovingly.

## \|\| 7.11.12 \|\|

*\|\| śaṃkara uvāca \|*  
*akāle bhayamutpannaṃ devānāmapi bhairavam \|*  
*jvalitasya nṛsiṃhāgniśśamayainaṃ durāsadam \|\| 12 \|\|*

Śiva said:—

12\. An untimely terrible fear has cropped up tor the gods. The fire of that man-lion is blazing You shall quell this inaccessible one.

## \|\| 7.11.13 \|\|

*sāntvayanbodhayādau taṃ tena kinnopaśāmyati \|*  
*tato matparamaṃ bhāvaṃ bhairavaṃ sampradarśaya \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. At the outset convince him in a consoling manner, If he does not subside thereby, show my great terrible form.

## \|\| 7.11.14 \|\|

*sūkṣmaṃ saṃhṛtya sūkṣmeṇa sthūlaṃ sthūlena tejasā \|*  
*vaktramānāya kṛttiṃ ca vīrabhadra mamājñayā \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. O Vīrabhadra, at my behest, bring me his head and hide after quelling his subtle fury with your subtle one and his gross splendour with your gross one.

## \|\| 7.11.15 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityādiṣṭo gaṇādhyakṣo praśāntaṃ vapurāsthitaḥ \|*  
*jagāma raṃhasā tatra yatrāste narakesarī \|\| 15 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

15\. Urged thus, the leader of the Gaṇas, assumed a calm body and features and hastened to the place where the man-lion was sitting.

## \|\| 7.11.16 \|\|

*tatastambodhayāmāsa vīrabhadro haro harim \|*  
*uvāca vākyamīśānaḥ pitāputramivaurasam \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Then Vīrabhadra, the destroyer addressed Viṣṇu. Śiva, then spoke to him as a father speaks to his own son.

## \|\| 7.11.17 \|\|

*vīrabhadra uvāca \|*  
*jagatsukhāya bhagavannavatīrṇosi mādhava \|*  
*sthityarthaṃ tvaṃ prayukto'si pareśaḥ parameṣṭhinā \|\| 17 \|\|*

Vīrabhadra said:—

17\. O lord Viṣṇu, you have taken the incarnation for the happiness of the universe. You, the great lord, have been entrusted with the task of maintaining the universe by Śiva.

## \|\| 7.11.18 \|\|

*jantucakraṃ bhagavatā pracchinnaṃ matsyarūpiṇā \|*  
*pucchenaiva samābadhya bhramannekārṇave purā \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. Formerly, multitudes of creatures moving about in the one vast ocean have been split by your lordship in the form of a fish by binding them with your tail.

## \|\| 7.11.19 \|\|

*bibharṣi karmarūpeṇa vārāheṇoddhṛtā mahī \|*  
*anena harirūpeṇa hiraṇyakaśipurhataḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. You support the earth in the form of a tortoise. You lifted it up in the form of a boar. In the form of a lion Hiraṇyakaśipu has been killed.

## \|\| 7.11.20 \|\|

*vāmanena balirbaddhastvayā vikramatā punaḥ \|*  
*tvameva sarvvabhūtānāṃ prabhavaḥ prabhuravyayaḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. Again in the form of a dwarf, you had bound Bali. You are the origin of all living beings. You are the eternal lord.

## \|\| 7.11.21 \|\|

*yadāyadā hi lokasya duḥkhaṃ kiñcitprajāyate \|*  
*tadātadāvatīrṇastvaṃ kariṣyasi nirāmayam \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. Whenever any misery torments the world you incarnate yourself and make it free from distress and ailment.

## \|\| 7.11.22 \|\|

*nādhikastvatsamo'pyasti hare śivaparāyaṇaḥ \|*  
*tvayā vedāśca dharmāśca śubhamārge pratiṣṭhitāḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. O Viṣṇu, there is none else devoted to Śiva more than you or even equal to you. The Vedas and sacred rites have been established by you towards an auspicious goal.

## \|\| 7.11.23 \|\|

*yadarthamavatāro'yaṃ nihatassa hi dānavaḥ \|*  
*hiraṇyakaśipuścaiva prahlādo'pi surakṣitaḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. That Dānava Hiraṇyakaśipu has been killed for which purpose you have taken this incarnation. Prahlāda too has been saved.

## \|\| 7.11.24 \|\|

*atīva ghoraṃ bhagavannarasiṃhavapustava \|*  
*upasaṃhara viśvātmaṃstvameva mama sannidhau \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. O lord Narasiṃha, O soul of the universe, subdue this extremely terrible body yourself in my presence.

## \|\| 7.11.25 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityukto vīrabhadreṇa nṛsiṃhaḥ śāntayā girā \|*  
*tato'dhikaṃ mahāghoraṃ kopañcakre mahāmadaḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

25\. Urged thus with quiet peaceful words of Vīrabhadra, the arrogant man-lion assumed a more terrible anger.

## \|\| 7.11.26 \|\|

*uvāca ca mahāghoraṃ kaṭhinaṃ vacanantadā \|*  
*vīrabhadrammahāvīraṃ daṃṣṭrābhirbhīṣayanmune \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. O sage, threatening the heroic Vīrabhadra with his curved fangs he spoke harsh and terrible words.

## \|\| 7.11.27 \|\|

*nṛsiṃha uvāca \|*  
*āgatosi yatastatra gaccha tvammā hitaṃ vada \|*  
*idānīṃ saṃhariṣyāmi jagadetaccarācaram \|\| 27 \|\|*

The Man-lion said:—

27\. Go back the way you have come from. Do not speak offending words. I am now going to annihilate the universe of the mobile and immobile beings.

## \|\| 7.11.28 \|\|

*saṃharturna hi saṃhāraḥ svato vā parato'pi vā \|*  
*śāsitammama sarvvatra śāstā ko'pi na vidyate \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. The annihilation is not the withdrawal of myself by myself or through others. I have my sway everywhere. There is none to rule over me.

## \|\| 7.11.29 \|\|

*matprasādena sakalamabhayaṃ hi pravarttate \|*  
*ahaṃ hi sarvaśaktīnāṃ pravartakanivartakaḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. Everything functions fearlessly by my favour, I am the instigator as well as the restrainer of all powers.

## \|\| 7.11.30 \|\|

*yadyadvibhūtimatsattvaṃ śrīmadūrjitameva vā \|*  
*tattadviddhi gaṇādhyakṣa mama tejovijṛmbhitam \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. O leader of the Gaṇas, whatever is endowed with magnificence, whatever is glorious and powerful, know that to be my expanded splendour,

## \|\| 7.11.31 \|\|

*devatāparamārthajñaṃ māmeva paramamviduḥ \|*  
*madaṃśāśśaktisampannā brahmaśakrādayassurāḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. They know me, the knower of the reality of the divinity, as the greatest. Brahmā, Indra and other gods are powerful in as much as they are my own parts.

## \|\| 7.11.32 \|\|

*mannābhikamalājjātaḥ purā brahmā jagatkaraḥ \|*  
*sarvādhikassvatantraśca kartā hartākhileśvaraḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. Brahmā, the creator of the universe who was formerly born of my umbilical lotus is greater than all, being independent, maker, remover and the lord of all.

## \|\| 7.11.33 \|\|

*idantu matparaṃ tejaḥ kiṃ punaḥ śrotumicchasi \|*  
*ato māṃ śaraṇamprāpya gaccha tvaṃ vigatajvaraḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. This is my greatest glory. What else do you wish to hear again? Hence seek refuge in me and return to your abodes being freed from feverish ailment.

## \|\| 7.11.34 \|\|

*avehi paramaṃ bhāvamidambhūtaṃ gaṇeśvara \|*  
*māmakaṃ sakalaṃ viśvaṃ sadevāsuramānuṣam \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. O lord of Gaṇas, know that the entire universe of this nature, is my own; this universe including the gods, Asuras and human beings is the manifestation of my greatness.

## \|\| 7.11.35 \|\|

*kālo'smyahaṃ lokavināśaheturlokānsamāhartumahampravṛttaḥ \|*  
*mṛtyormṛtyuṃ viddhi māṃ vīrabhadra jīvantyete matprasādena devāḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. I am Kāla, the cause of destroying the worlds. I am engaged in annihilating the worlds. O Vīrabhadra, know me to be the Death of Death. These gods are alive by my very favour.

## \|\| 7.11.36 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*sāhaṅkāraṃ vacaḥ śrutvā hareramitavikramaḥ \|*  
*vihasyovāca sāvajñantato visphuritādharaḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

36\. On hearing the arrogant words of Viṣṇu, Vīrabhadra of immeasurable exploits laughed and said contemptuously with throbbing lips.

## \|\| 7.11.37 \|\|

*vīrabhadra uvāca \|*  
*kinna jānāsi viśveśaṃ saṃhartārampinākinam \|*  
*asadvādo vivādaśca vināśastvayi kevalaḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

Vīrabhadra said:—

37\. Don’t you know the Pināka-bearing lord of the universe as the annihilator? Baseless argument and disputation will end in death for you.

## \|\| 7.11.38 \|\|

*tavānyonyāvatārāṇi kāni śeṣāṇi sāmpratam \|*  
*kṛtāni yena kenaiva kathāśeṣo bhaviṣyati \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. What other incarnation of yours is left still to be taken with that whatever it may be you will remain alive only in the story.

## \|\| 7.11.39 \|\|

*doṣaṃ taṃ vada yena tvamavasthāmīdṛśī gataḥ \|*  
*tena saṃhāradakṣeṇa dakṣiṇāśeṣameṣyasi \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. Tell me the defect which has led you to this plight. With that lord capable of annihilation you will get the balance of Dakṣiṇā.

## \|\| 7.11.40 \|\|

*prakṛtistvaṃ pumānrudrastvayi vīryyaṃ samāhitam \|*  
*tvannābhipaṅkajājjātaḥ pañca vaktraḥ pitāmahaḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. You are Prakṛti and Rudra is Puruṣa. The semen is deposited in you. The five-faced Brahmā was born of your umbilical lotus.

## \|\| 7.11.41 \|\|

*jagattrayīsarjanārthaṃ śaṃkaraṃ nīlalohitam \|*  
*lalāṭe'cintayatsoyantapasyugre ca saṃsthitaḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. Remaining in a severe penance, he contemplated on Śiva, the blue-necked, in his forehead for the creation of the three worlds.

## \|\| 7.11.42 \|\|

*tallalāṭādabhūcchambhuḥ sṛṣṭyarthe tena bhūṣaṇam \|*  
*ato'haṃ devadevasya tasya bhairavarūpiṇaḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. Śiva came out of his forehead. I am the ornament of the lord of gods in the form of Bhairava in the matter of creation.

## \|\| 7.11.43 \|\|

*tvatsaṃhāre niyukto'smi vinayena balena ca \|*  
*devadevena rudreṇa sakalaprabhuṇā hare \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. O Viṣṇu, I have been engaged in subduing you cither through humility or through force, by Rudra the lord of gods, the lord of all.

## \|\| 7.11.44 \|\|

*ekaṃ rakṣo vidāryaiva tacchaktikalayā yutaḥ \|*  
*ahaṃkārāvalepena garjasi tvamatandritaḥ \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. After tearing to pieces a single demon you roar by your arrogance. Actually you are free from slumber only because you are endowed with a digit of his power.

## \|\| 7.11.45 \|\|

*upakāro hi sādhūnāṃ sukhāya kila saṃmataḥ \|*  
*upakāro hyasādhūnāmapakārāya kevalam \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. Help rendered to good men is accepted as conducive to happiness. Help rendered to bad men is conclusive to offence.

## \|\| 7.11.46 \|\|

*yannṛsiṃha maheśānaṃ punarbhūtaṃ tu manyase \|*  
*tarhyajñānī mahāgarvī vikārī sarvathā bhavān \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. O Nrsiṃha, if you consider lord Śiva as a mere living being you are ignorant, very arrogant and full of aberrations in every respect.

## \|\| 7.11.47 \|\|

*na tvaṃ sraṣṭā na saṃhartā bhartāpi na nṛsiṃhaka \|*  
*paratantro vimūḍhātmā na svatantro hi kutracit \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. O base Nṛsiṃha, you are neither creator, nor sustainer, nor annihilator. You are subservient and deluded in mind. You are not independent anywhere.

## \|\| 7.11.48 \|\|

*kulālacakravacchaktyā preritosi pinākinā \|*  
*nānāvatārakartā tvaṃ tadadhīnassadā hare \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. O Viṣṇu, like the potter’s wheel you are forcibly induced by Śiva when you take the different incarnations. You are always dependent on him.

## \|\| 7.11.49-50 \|\|

*adyāpi tava nikṣiptaṃ kapālaṃ kūrmarūpiṇaḥ \|*  
*hara hāralatāmadhye dagdhaḥ kaścinna badhyate \|\| 49 \|\|*

*vismṛtiḥ kiṃ tadaṃśena daṃṣṭrotpātanapīḍitam \|*  
*vārāhavighnahaste'dya yākrośantārakāriṇā \|\| 50 \|\|*

49-50. Your skull, left by you in the midst of the necklace of Śiva, while you had assumed the form of a tortoise, is still there. None who is burnt is bound. Is it your partial forgetfulness that in your body as a Boar your fangs were felled by the slayer of Tāraka?

## \|\| 7.11.51 \|\|

*dagdhosi paśya śūlāgre viṣvaksenacchalādbhavān \|*  
*dakṣayajñe śiraśchinnaṃ mayā tejaḥsvarūpiṇā \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. You were burnt by the fire of the Trident, O Viṣvaksena. At the time of Dakṣa’s sacrifice your head was cut off by me brilliant in form.

## \|\| 7.11.52 \|\|

*adyāpi tava putrasya brahmaṇaḥ pañcamaṃ śiraḥ \|*  
*chinnaṃ na sajjitaṃ bhūyo hare tadvismṛtantvayā \|\| 52 \|\|*

52, O Viṣṇu, the fifth head of Brahmā your son, which was cut, has not been set right even now. It is forgotten by you.

## \|\| 7.11.53 \|\|

*nirjitastvaṃ dadhīcena saṃgrāme samarudgaṇaḥ \|*  
*kaṇḍūyamāne śirasi kathaṃ tadvismṛtantvayā \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. In a battle you were defeated by Dadhīca along with the gods. How can it be forgotten by you while scratching your head?

## \|\| 7.11.54 \|\|

*cakraṃ vikramato yasya cakrapāṇe tava priyam \|*  
*kutaḥ prāptaṃ kṛtaṃ kena tvayā tadapi vismṛtam \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. O discus-armed one, you are fond of your discus but you have forgotten whence you got it and by whom it was made.

## \|\| 7.11.55 \|\|

*ye mayā sakalā lokā gṛhītāstvaṃ payonidhau \|*  
*nidrāparavaśaśśeṣe sa kathaṃ sāttviko bhavān \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. Whatever worlds I have seized, in them, you lie down in the ocean, overcome by sleep. How can you then be a Sāttvika?

## \|\| 7.11.56 \|\|

*tvadādistambaparyantaṃ rudraśaktivijṛmbhitam \|*  
*śaktimānabhitastvaṃ ca hyanalāttvaṃ vimohitaḥ \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. From you to a blade of grass, every thing expands and develops only through Rudra’s power. Professing to possess power you have been deluded by the fire all round.

## \|\| 7.11.57 \|\|

*tattejaso hi māhātmyaṃ pumāndra ṣṭunna hi kṣamaḥ \|*  
*asthūlā ye prapaśyanti tadviṣṇoḥ paramampadam \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. Ordinary man is incapable of seeing the greatness of his splendour. That is the greatest of the regions of Viṣṇu which only the people of subtle intellect can perceive.

## \|\| 7.11.58 \|\|

*dyāvāpṛthivyā indrāgneryamasya varuṇasya ca \|*  
*dhvāntodare śaśāṃke ca janitvā parameśvaraḥ \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. Lord Śiva manifests himself in between heaven and earth, Indra and Agni, Yama and Varuṇa, in the heart of darkness and in the light of the moon.

## \|\| 7.11.59 \|\|

*kālosi tvaṃ mahākālaḥ kālakālo maheśvara \|*  
*atastvamugrakalayā mṛtyormṛtyurbhaviṣyasi \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. You are Kāla but lord Śiva is Mahākāla and Kālakāla. Hence you will be death of death only through Śiva’s digit.

## \|\| 7.11.60 \|\|

*sthirodya tvakṣaro vīro vīro viśvāvakaḥ prabhuḥ \|*  
*upahantā jvaraṃ bhīmo mṛgaḥ pakṣī hiraṇmayaḥ \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. But the steady one today is the imperishable hero, the lord protector of the universe. He is the destroyer of fever, a dreadful beast, a golden bird.

## \|\| 7.11.61 \|\|

*śāstā śeṣasya jagatastattvaṃ naiva caturmukhaḥ \|*  
*nānye ca kevalaṃ śambhussarvaśāstā na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. You and the four-faced Brahmā are not the rulers of the world. Nor any one else is the ruler. Śiva is certainly the ruler of all.

## \|\| 7.11.62 \|\|

*itthaṃ sarvaṃ samālokya saṃhārātmānamātmanā \|*  
*na vinaṣṭantvamātmānaṃ kuru he nṛhare'budha \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. Thus taking everything in consideration you subdue yourself by yourself. O Man-lion, O wise one, keep your-sélf safe.

## \|\| 7.11.63 \|\|

*no cedidānīṃ krodhasya mahābhairavarūpiṇaḥ \|*  
*vajrāśaniriva sthāṇau tvayi mṛtyuḥ patiṣyati \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. Otherwise death will befall you like a thunderbolt on a stump due to the fury of the lord in the form of Bhairava.

## \|\| 7.11.64 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityuktvā vīrabhadropi virarāmākutobhayaḥ \|*  
*dṛṣṭvā nṛsiṃhābhiprāyaṃ krodhamūrttiśśivasya saḥ \|\| 64 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

64\. After saying this and observing the view of the Man-lion, Vīrabhadra the furious form of Śiva, stopped. He was not afraid of anyone.

## Chapter 12 - The incarnation of Śarabha

## \|\| 7.12.1 \|\|

*sanatkumāra uvāca \|*  
*nandīśvara mahāprājña vijñātantadanantaram \|*  
*mamopari kṛpāṃ kṛtvā prītyā tvantadvadādhunā \|\| 1 \|\|*

Sanatkumāra said:—

1\. O Nandīśvara, O intelligent one, this is understood. Take pity on me and tell me now lovingly what happened thereafter.

## \|\| 7.12.2 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityukto vīrabhadreṇa nṛsiṃhaḥ krodhavihvalaḥ \|*  
*ninadannanu vegena taṃ grahītumpracakrame \|\| 2 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

2\. Thus admonished by Vīrabhadra, the Man-lion (Nṛsiṃha)be came furious. He roared and began to seize him with force.

## \|\| 7.12.3 \|\|

*atrāntare mahāghoraṃ pratyakṣabhayakāraṇam \|*  
*gaganavyāpi durdharṣaṃ śaivatejassamudbhavam \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. In the meantime, the splendour of Śiva rose up unbearably enveloping the sky all around. It was terrible and cause for imminent fear.

## \|\| 7.12.4-5 \|\|

*vīrabhadrasya tadrūmadṛśyantu tataḥ kṣaṇāt \|*  
*tadvai hiraṇmayaṃ saumyaṃ na saurannāgnisambhavam \|\| 4 \|\|*

*na taḍiccandrasadṛśamanaupamyammaheśvaram \|*  
*tadā tejāṃsi sarvāṇi tasmiṃllīnāni śaṃkare \|\| 5 \|\|*

4-5. Then in a trice the form of Vīrabhadra became invisible. The splendour of Lord Śiva was neither golden nor fiery, neither lunar nor solar. It resembled neither the lightning nor the moon. It was beyond comparison. The forms of brilliance were merged in Śiva.

## \|\| 7.12.6 \|\|

*na tadvyoma mahattejo vyaktāntaścābhavattataḥ \|*  
*rudrasādhāraṇaṃ caiva cihnitaṃ vikṛtākṛti \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. That great splendour was not of the sky too. Thereafter its middle became clearly manifested in the form characteristic of Rudra of deformed shape.

## \|\| 7.12.7 \|\|

*tatassaṃhārarūpeṇa suvyaktaṃ parameśvaraḥ \|*  
*paśyatāṃ sarvadevānāṃ jayaśabdādimaṃgalaiḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*
