7\. Even as all the gods stood watching with the auspicious shouts of victory, Lord Śiva became manifest in the form the Annihilator.

## \|\| 7.12.8 \|\|

*sahasrabāhurjaṭilaścandrārddhakṛtaśekharaḥ \|*  
*samṛddhograśarīreṇa pakṣābhyāñcañcunā dvijaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. He had a thousand hands and wore matted hair. His head was adorned by the crescent moon. He appeared like a bird with wings and beak. His body was fierce and fully developed.

## \|\| 7.12.9 \|\|

*atitīkṣṇo mahādaṃṣṭro vajratulyanakhāyudhaḥ \|*  
*kaṇṭhe kālo mahābāhuścatuṣpādvahnisannibhaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. His fangs were very sharp. Adamantine daws were his weapons. His neck was black in colour. He had huge arms and four legs. He was blazing like fire.

## \|\| 7.12.10 \|\|

*yugāntodyatajīmūtabhīmagambhīranisvanaḥ \|*  
*mahākupitakṛtyāgnivyāvṛttanayanatrayaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. His voice was resonant and terrible like the rumbling sound of the clouds that gather at the end of a Yuga. His three eyes were as wide and blazing as the fire of the evil spirit of great fury.

## \|\| 7.12.11 \|\|

*spaṣṭadaṃṣṭrādharoṣṭhaśca huṃkārasaṃyuto haraḥ \|*  
*īdṛgvidhasvarūpaśca hyugra āvirbabhūva ha \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. His fangs and lips were clearly visible. He was producing a hissing sound of Huṃkāra. Śiva of such a form appeared in sight.

## \|\| 7.12.12 \|\|

*aristaddarśanādeva vinaṣṭabalavikramaḥ \|*  
*bibhraddhāma sahasrāṃśoradhaḥ khadyotavibhramam \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. At his very vision Viṣṇu lost his strength and exploit. He assumed a lustre of the flickering glow-worm beneath the sun.

## \|\| 7.12.13-14 \|\|

*atha vibhramya pakṣābhyāṃ nābhipādānvidārayan \|*  
*pādānbabaṃdha pucchena bāhubhyāmbāhu maṇḍalam \|\| 13 \|\|*

*bhindannurasi bāhubhyānnijagrāha haro harim \|*  
*tato jagāma gaganandevaissaha maharṣibhiḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

13-14. Fluttering with his wings, and tearing the navel and legs he bound the legs of the Man-lion with his tail and his hands with his own hands. He struck his chest, caught hold of Viṣṇu. It expanded in the sky along with the gods and the sages.

## \|\| 7.12.15-16 \|\|

*sahasaivābhayādviṣṇuṃ sa hi śyena ivoragam \|*  
*utkṣipyotkṣipya saṃgṛhya nipātya ca nipātya ca \|\| 15 \|\|*

*uḍḍīyoḍḍīya bhagavānpakṣaghātavimohitam \|*  
*harīṃ harastaṃ vṛṣabhaṃ viveśānanta īśvaraḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

15-16. Like a vulture seizing a serpent he fearlessly caught hold of Viṣṇu, lifted him up and fell him to the ground. At the time of flight he was excessively oppressed, being hit with the wings. Then Lord Śiva took Viṣṇu and flew up.

## \|\| 7.12.17 \|\|

*anuyāntaṃ surāssarve namovākyena tuṣṭuvuḥ \|*  
*praṇemussādaraṃ prītyā brahmādyāśca munīśvarāḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. All the gods followed him eulogising him with the words of obeisance. Brahmā and other sages bowed to him with reverence and love.

## \|\| 7.12.18 \|\|

*nīyamānaḥ paravaśo dīnavaktraḥ kṛtāñjaliḥ \|*  
*tuṣṭāva parameśānaṃ haristaṃ lalitākṣaraiḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. Being led thus Viṣṇu was helpless. His face was pallid and sorrowful. With palms joined in reverence he eulogised Lord Śiva with prayers of simple words.

## \|\| 7.12.19 \|\|

*nāmnāmaṣṭaśatenaiva stutvā tāmmṛḍameva ca \|*  
*punaśca prārthayāmāsa nṛsiṃhaḥ śarabheśvaram \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. After eulogising Mṛḍa with the hundred and eight names of the lord, Nṛsiṃha requested lord Śarabha again.

## \|\| 7.12.20 \|\|

*yadāyadā mamājñeyaṃ matissyādgarvadūṣitā \|*  
*tadātadā'panetavyā tvayaiva parameśvara \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. “O great lord, whenever my ignorant mind becomes defiled by arrogance, it shall be removed by you alone.”

## \|\| 7.12.21 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*evaṃ vijñāpayanprītyā śaṅkaraṃ narakesarī \|*  
*natvā'śakto'bhavadviṣṇu jīvitānta parājitaḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

21 Thus saying with love to Śiva, the Man-lion turned submissive and bowed to him. Viṣṇu was utterly defeated and came to the end of his life.

## \|\| 7.12.22 \|\|

*tadvaktraṃ śeṣagātrāntaṃ kṛtvā sarvasvavigraham \|*  
*śaktiyuktaṃ tadīyāṃgaṃ vīrabhadraḥ kṣaṇāttataḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. Within a trice Vīrabhadra made his powerful body powerless and dead.

## \|\| 7.12.23 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*atha brahmādayo devāśśārabhaṃ rūpamāsthitam \|*  
*tuṣṭuvuḥ śaṃkaraṃ devaṃ sarvalokaikaśaṃkaram \|\| 23 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

23\. Then Brahmā and other gods eulogised lord Śiva who had assumed the form of a Śarabha and who was the sole benefactor of all the worlds.

## \|\| 7.12.24 \|\|

*\|\| devā ūcuḥ \|*  
*brahmaviṣṇvindracandrādisurāḥ sarve maharṣayaḥ \|*  
*ditijādyāḥ samprasūtāstvattassarve maheśvara \|\| 24 \|\|*

The gods said:—

24\. O lord Śiva, Brahmā, Viṣṇu, Indra, the Moon, the gods, the sages, and the Daityas are born of you.

## \|\| 7.12.25 \|\|

*brahmaviṣṇumahendrāśca sūryādyānasurānsurām \|*  
*tvaṃ vai sṛjasi pāsyatsi tvameva sakaleśvaraḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. O lord of all, you alone create and protect Brahmā, Viṣṇu and Indra, the sun and others, the gods and Asuras and you alone devour them.

## \|\| 7.12.26 \|\|

*yato harasi saṃsāraṃ hara ityucyate budhaiḥ \|*  
*nigṛhīto hariryasmāddhara ityucyate budhaiḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Since you annihilate the world, you are called Hara by the learned. Since Viṣṇu was curbed and caught hold of by you, you are called Hara by the learned.

## \|\| 7.12.27 \|\|

*yato bibharṣi sakalaṃ vibhajya tanumaṣṭadhā \|*  
*ato'smānpāhi bhagavan surādānairabhīpsitaiḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Since you support and hold every thing after dividing your body into eight, O lord, protect us, the gods, by means of the desired gifts.

## \|\| 7.12.28 \|\|

*tvaṃ mahāpuruṣaḥ śambhuḥ sarveśassuranāyakaḥ \|*  
*niḥsvātmā nirvikārātmā parabrahma satāṃ gatiḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. You are the great Puruṣa, Śiva, lord of all, leader of the gods, the soul of the indigent, of a mind without aberrations. You are the great Brahman, the goal of the good.

## \|\| 7.12.29 \|\|

*dīnabandhurdayā sindhu'radbhutotiḥ parātmadṛk \|*  
*prājño virāṭvibhussatyaḥ saccidānandalakṣaṇaḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. You are the kinsman of the distressed, the ocean of mercy, of wonderful sports, the vision of the great soul, the intelligent, the Immense Cosmic Being, the all-pervasive, the truth, the Existence, knowledge and Bliss as the characteristics.

## \|\| 7.12.30 \|\|

*\|\| nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityākarṇya vacaḥ śambhurdevānāṃ parameśvaraḥ \|*  
*uvāca tān surāndevamaharṣīṃśca purātanān \|\| 30 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

30\. On hearing these words of the gods, Śiva, the great God, spoke to the gods and the great ancient sages.

## \|\| 7.12.31 \|\|

*yathā jalaṃ jale kṣiptaṃ kṣīre kṣīraṃ ghṛte ghṛtam \|*  
*eka eva tadā viṣṇuḥ śive līno na cānyathā \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. Just as water poured into water, or milk poured into milk, or ghee poured into ghee becomes one with those things, so also Viṣṇu is merged into Śiva, not otherwise.

## \|\| 7.12.32 \|\|

*eko viṣṇurnṛsiṃhātmā sadarpaśca mahābalaḥ \|*  
*jagatsaṃhārakaraṇe pravṛtto narakesarī \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. It was Viṣṇu alone in the form of the Man-lion, haughty and strong, engaged in the activity of annihilating the universe.

## \|\| 7.12.33 \|\|

*prārthanīyo namastasmai madbhaktaissiddhikāribhiḥ \|*  
*madbhaktapravaraścaiva madbhaktavara dāyakaḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. He shall be prayed and bowed by my devotees aspiring achievements. He is the foremost of my devotees and the granter of boons.

## \|\| 7.12.34 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*etāvaduktvā bhagavān pakṣirājo mahābalaḥ \|*  
*paśyatāṃ sarvadevānāntatraivāntaradhīyata \|\| 34 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

34\. After saying this, the lordly king of birds of great strength, vanished there itself even as all the gods were watching.

## \|\| 7.12.35 \|\|

*vīrabhadro'pi bhagavāngaṇādhyakṣo mahābalaḥ \|*  
*nṛsiṃhakṛttiṃ niṣkṛṣya samādāya yayau girim \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. Lord Vīrabhadra, the leader of the Gaṇas, of great strength, peeled off the hide of the Man-lion and went to the mountain taking it with him.

## \|\| 7.12.36 \|\|

*nṛsiṃhakṛttivasanastadāprabhṛti śaṃkaraḥ \|*  
*tadvaktraṃ muṇḍamālāyāṃ nāyakatvena kalpitam \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. From that time Śiva began to wear the hide of the Man-lion. His head was the leading bead in his necklace of skulls.

## \|\| 7.12.37 \|\|

*tato devā nirātaṅkā kīrttayantaḥ kathāmimām \|*  
*vismayotphullanayanā jagmuḥ sarve yathāgatam \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. Then the gods freed from their distress began to sing the glory of this story. With their eyes blooming with wonder they returned the way they came.

## \|\| 7.12.38 \|\|

*ya idamparamākhyānaṃ puṇyaṃ vedarasānvitam \|*  
*paṭhati śṛṇuyāccaiva sarvvānkāmānavāpnuyāt \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. He who reads or listens to the great narrative, which being sacred has the flavour of the Vedas, will realise all his desires

## \|\| 7.12.39 \|\|

*dhanyaṃ yaśasyamāyuṣyamārogyampuṣṭivarddhanam \|*  
*sarvavighrapraśamanaṃ sarvavyādhivināśanam \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. This narrative is conducive to wealth, fame, longevity and health. It increases prosperity, subdues obstacles and quells ailments.

## \|\| 7.12.40 \|\|

*duḥkhapraśamanaṃ vāñchāsiddhidaṃ maṃgalālayam \|*  
*apamṛtyuharaṃ buddhipradaṃ śatruvināśanam \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. It subdues misery, yields the fruits of desires and is the abode of auspicious features. It removes premature death, confers intelligence and destroys enemies.

## \|\| 7.12.41 \|\|

*idantu śarabhākāraṃ paraṃ rūpampinākinaḥ \|*  
*prakāśanīyaṃ bhakteṣu śaṃkarasya careṣu vai \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. This great form of Śiva in the form of Śarabha shall be made public only among the leading devotees of Śiva.

## \|\| 7.12.42 \|\|

*taireva paṭhitavyaṃ ca śrotavyaṃ ca śivātmabhiḥ \|*  
*navadhā bhaktidaṃ divyamantaḥkaraṇabuddhidam \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. This shall be read and listened to only by them with their soul dedicated to Śiva. It then confers the ninefold devotion. It is divine. It purifies the inner mind and sense.

## \|\| 7.12.43 \|\|

*śivotsaveṣu sarveṣu caturdaśyaṣṭamīṣu ca \|*  
*paṭhetpratiṣṭhākāle tu śivasannidhikāraṇam \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. This shall be read during the festivals of Śiva, during the eighth and fourteenth Tithis, and at the installation of the idol. It then causes the presence of Śiva.

## \|\| 7.12.44-45 \|\|

*cauravyāghranṛsiṃhātmakṛta rājabhayeṣu ca \|*  
*anyeṣūtpātabhūkampadasyvādipāṃsuvṛṣṭiṣu \|\| 44 \|\|*

*ulkāpāte mahāvāte vināvṛṣṭyativṛṣṭiṣu \|*  
*paṭhedyaḥ prayato vidvāñ śivabhakto dṛḍhavrataḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

44-45. When there is danger from thieves, tigers, lions, one’s own or enemy kings, during commotions earthquakes and robberies, dust storms, when there is the fall of meteors, when there is draught or too much of raīn—during all these occasions he who reads this with purity becomes a great devotee of Śiva steady in his rites and very learned.

## \|\| 7.12.46 \|\|

*yaḥ paṭhecchṛṇuyādvāpi niṣkāmo vratamaiśvaram \|*  
*rudralokaṃ samāsādya rudrasyānucaro bhavet \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. He who reads or listens to this without any specific desire and performs the rites of Śiva attains Rudra’s region and becomes a follower of Rudra.

## \|\| 7.12.47 \|\|

*rudralokamanuprāpya rudreṇa saha modate \|*  
*tatassāyujyamāpnoti śivasya kṛpayā mune \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. O sage, after attaining Rudra’s region he rejoices with Rudra. Then he becomes one with Rudra by the mercy of Śiva.

## Chapter 13 - The incarnation of Gṛhapati

## \|\| 7.13.1 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*śṛṇu brahmasuta prītyā caritaṃ śaśimaulinaḥ \|*  
*so'vatīrṇo yathā prītyā viśvānaragṛhe śivaḥ \|\| 1 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

1\. O son of Brahmā, listen lovingly to the narrative of the moon-crested lord Śiva how he was born willingly in the house of Viśvānara.

## \|\| 7.13.2 \|\|

*nāmnā gṛhapatiḥ so'bhūdagnilokapatirmune \|*  
*agnirūpastejasaśca sarvvātmā paramaḥ prabhuḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. He was Gṛhapati by name. O sage, he became the lord of Agniloka. The great lord, the soul of all, was fiery in form and brilliant too.

## \|\| 7.13.3 \|\|

*narmadāyāstaṭe ramye pure narmapure purā \|*  
*purāribhaktaḥ puṇyātmā bhavadviśvānaro muniḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. On the beautiful banks of Narmadā in the city of Dharmapura, formerly there lived a devotee of Śiva, a soul, the sage Viśvānara.

## \|\| 7.13.4 \|\|

*brahmacaryyāśrame niṣṭho brahmayajñaratassadā \|*  
*śāṇḍilyagotraḥ śucimānbrahmatejo nidhirvvaśī \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. While in the stage of celibacy, he was devoted to Brahmayajña rites. He belonged to the Śāṇḍilya lineage. He was pure, the storehouse of Brahminical splendour and had self-control.

## \|\| 7.13.5 \|\|

*vijñātākhilaśāstrārthassadācāraratassadā \|*  
*śaivācārapravīṇo'ti laukikācāravidvaraḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. He understood the real meaning of sacred lord. He followed the conduct of the virtuous. He was highly skilled in Śiva ethics. He was the foremost among the knowers of worldly conventions.

## \|\| 7.13.6 \|\|

*citte vicāryya gṛhiṇīguṇānviśvānaraḥ śubhān \|*  
*uduvāha vidhānena svocitāṃ kālakanyakām \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. After pondering over the good qualities of an ideal housewife, Viśvānara married with due rites the daughter of Kāla who was really worthy of him.

## \|\| 7.13.7 \|\|

*agniśuśrūṣaṇarataḥ pañcayajñaparāyaṇaḥ \|*  
*ṣaṭkarmanirato nityaṃ devapitratithipriyaḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. He maintained the sacrificial fires. He was devoted to the performance of the five daily sacrifices.\[2\] He practised the six sacred rites every day. He was fond of serving gods, manes and guests.

## \|\| 7.13.8 \|\|

*evambahutithe kāle gate tasyāgrajanmanaḥ \|*  
*bhāryyā śuciṣmatī nāma bhartāramprāha suvratā \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. When a long time elapsed in that manner Śuciṣmatī the wife of that Brāhmaṇa who observed all rites spoke to her husband.

## \|\| 7.13.9 \|\|

*nātha bhogā mayā sarve bhuktā vai tvatprasādataḥ \|*  
*strīṇāṃ samucitā ye syustvāṃ sametya mudāvahāḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. “O Lord, by your favour, all pleasures which women could expect have been enjoyed by me in your company.

## \|\| 7.13.10 \|\|

*evamme prārthitannātha cirāya hṛdi saṃsthitam \|*  
*gṛhasthānāṃ samucitaṃ tvametaddātumarhasi \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. O Lord, still a desire has been lurking in my heart for a long time. It is proper to all householders. You shall grant it to me.

## \|\| 7.13.11 \|\|

*viśvānara uvāca \|\| \|*  
*kimadeyaṃ hi suśroṇi tava priyahitaiṣiṇī \|*  
*tatprārthaya mahābhāge prayacchāmyavilambitam \|\| 11 \|\|*

Viśvānara said:—

11\. O gentle lady of beautiful hips ever desirous of my welfare, what is there that cannot be granted to you? O lucky one, you may ask for it. I shall grant it to you without delay.

## \|\| 7.13.12 \|\|

*maheśituḥ prasādena mama kiñcinna durlabham \|*  
*ihāmutra ca kalyāṇi sarvakalyāṇakāriṇaḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. O good woman, by the favour of Lord Śiva, nothing is inaccessible to me either here or hereafter. Lord Śiva is the cause of all good.

## \|\| 7.13.13 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityākarṇya vacaḥ patyustasya sā patidevatā \|*  
*uvāca hṛṣyadvadanā karau baddhvā vinītikā \|\| 13 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

13\. On hearing these words of her husband she who thought her husband to be a deity was delighted. Joining her palms in reverence she humbly said.

## \|\| 7.13.14 \|\|

*śuciṣmatyuvāca \|*  
*varayogyāsmi cennātha yadi deyo varo mama \|*  
*maheśasadṛśamputrandehi nānyaṃ varaṃ vṛṇe \|\| 14 \|\|*

Śuciṣmatī said:—

14\. O Lord, if I deserve a boon, if a boon is to be granted to me, please give me a son equal to Lord Śiva. I am not choosing any other boon.

## \|\| 7.13.15 \|\|

*\|\| nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*iti tasyā vacaḥ śrutvā brāhmaṇassa śucivrataḥ \|*  
*kṣaṇaṃ samādhimādhāya hṛdyetatsamacintayat \|\| 15 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

15\. On hearing her words that Brahmin of pure rites entered into mystic trance for a short while and thought in his heart thus.

## \|\| 7.13.16 \|\|

*aho kiṃ me tayā tanvyā prārthitaṃ hyatidurlabham \|*  
*manorathapathāddūramastu vā sa hi sarvvakṛt \|\| 16 \|\|*

16 “What is this? This lady has wished for an extremely inaccessible thing. It is beyond the reach of wishes. Let it be. Only He does everything.

## \|\| 7.13.17 \|\|

*tenaivāsyā mukhe sthitvā vāksvarūpeṇa śambhunā \|*  
*vyāhṛtaṃ ko'nyathā karttumu tsaheta bhavedidam \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. It is by Śiva himself stationing himself in her mouth in the form of speech that this has been said. This alone is possible. Who else could have endeavoured to say so?”

## \|\| 7.13.18 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*iti sañciṃtya sa munirviśvānara udāradhīḥ \|*  
*tataḥ provāca tāmpatnīmekapatnīvrate sthitaḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. After thinking like this, the sage Viśvānara of liberal heart who strictly adhered to the principle of monogamy spoke to her.

## \|\| 7.13.19 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*itthamāśvāsya tāmpatnīñjagāma tapase muniḥ \|*  
*yatra viśveśvaraḥ sākṣātkāśīnātho'dhi tiṣṭhati \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. After consoling his wife, the sage set out for penance to the city of Vārāṇasī where Lord Śiva himself is stationed.

## \|\| 7.13.20 \|\|

*prāpya vārāṇasīṃ tūrṇaṃ dṛṣṭvā tāmmaṇikarṇikām \|*  
*tatyāja tāpatritayamapi janmaśatārjitam \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. Reaching Vārāṇasī immediately and seeing Maṇikarṇikā, he was freed from three distresses acquired in the course of hundreds of births.

## \|\| 7.13.21 \|\|

*dṛṣṭvā sarvāṇi liṃgāni viśveśapramukhāni ca \|*  
*snātvā sarveṣu kuṇḍeṣu vāpīkūpasarassu ca \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. He saw Viśveśvara and other phallic images. He performed ablutions in all holy ditches, wells, tanks and lakes.

## \|\| 7.13.22 \|\|

*natvā vināyakānsarvāngaurīṃ śarvāṃ praṇamya ca \|*  
*sampūjya kālarājañca bhairavampāpabhakṣaṇam \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. He bowed to all Vināyakas and Śarvā Gaurī. He worshipped Kālarāja Bhaīrava who devours sins.

## \|\| 7.13.23 \|\|

*daṇḍanāyakamukhyāṃśca gaṇānstutvā prayatnataḥ \|*  
*ādikeśavamukhyāṃśca keśavamparitoṣya ca \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Strenuously he eulogised Daṇḍanāyaka and other Gaṇas, Ādikeśava and others. He propitiated Keśava.

## \|\| 7.13.24 \|\|

*lokārkamukhasūryāṃśca praṇamya sa punaḥpunaḥ \|*  
*kṛtvā ca piṇḍadānāni sarvatīrtheṣvatandritaḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. He bowed to Lolārka and other suns again and again. Without lethargy he offered balls of rice in all the holy centres.

## \|\| 7.13.25 \|\|

*sahasrabhojanādyaiśca munīnviprānpratarpya ca \|*  
*mahāpūjopacāraiśca liṃgānyabhyarcya bhaktitaḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. He satiated sages and brahmins by means of “feeding thousand” and other rites. With devotion he propitiated the phallic images of Śiva by means of great worship and service.

## \|\| 7.13.26 \|\|

*asakṛccintayāmāsa kiṃ liṃgaṃ kṣiprasiddhidam \|*  
*yatra niścalatāmeti tapastanayakāmyayā \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. He thought frequently—“What is that phallic image that yields the result quickly? Where can the penance due to the longing for a son find ultimate rest.”

## \|\| 7.13.27 \|\|

*kṣaṇaṃ vicāryya sa muniriti viśvānarassudhīḥ \|*  
*kṣipramputrapradaṃ liṃgaṃ vīreśampraśaśaṃsa ha \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Thinking thus for a moment, the intelligent sage Viśvānara praised the Vīreśa form of Śiva which confers the blessings of a son immediately.

## \|\| 7.13.28 \|\|

*asaṃkhyātāssahasrāṇi siddhāḥ siddhiṃ gatāstataḥ \|*  
*siddhaliṃgamiti khyātantasmādvīreśvaramparam \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. Innumerable thousands of Siddhas have derived the fulfilment of their desire from the worship of Śiva. Hence Vīreśa is famous as the Fruit-yielding phallic image.

## \|\| 7.13.29 \|\|

*vīreśvarammahāliṃgamabdamabhyarcya bhaktitaḥ \|*  
*āyurmanorathaṃ sarvaṃ putrādikamanekaśaḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. If any one worships the Vīreśvara phallic image for a year with great devotion he obtains longevity, cherished desires, sons and other things.

## \|\| 7.13.30 \|\|

*ahamapyatra vīreśaṃ samārādhya trikālatāḥ \|*  
*āśu putramavāpsyāmi yathābhilaṣitaṃ striyā \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. I too shall worship Vīreśa here thrice a day and shall ere long acquire a son as desired by my wife.

## \|\| 7.13.31 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*iti kṛtvā matindhīro vipro viśvānaraḥ kṛtī \|*  
*candrakūpajale snātvā jagrāha niyamaṃ vratī \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. After thinking like this, the intelligent brahmin, the contented Viśvānara took a bath in the well Candrakūpa and took the vow of the sacred rite.

## \|\| 7.13.32-34 \|\|

*ekāhāro'bhavanmāsaṃ māsaṃ naktāśano'bhavat \|*  
*ayācitāśano māsammāsantyaktāśanaḥ punaḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

*payovrato'bhavanmāsammāsammāsaṃ śāka phalāśanaḥ \|*  
*māsammuṣṭitilāhāro māsaṃ pānīyabhojanaḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

*pañcagavyāśano māsammāsañcāndrāyaṇavratī \|*  
*māsaṃ kuśāgrajalabhugmāsaṃ śvasanabhakṣaṇaḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

32-34. For a month he lived on a single meal a day. For another month he took only the night-meal. He spent another month taking only the food he got without begging. He spent another month without any food. For another month he took only milk. For another month he took only fruits and vegetables. For another month he ate only a handful of gingelly seeds. For another month he took liquid diet. For another month he took only Pañcagavya. For another month he performed the Cāndrāyaṇa rite. For another month he drank water from the tip of Kuśa grass and for another month he had air alone as his food.

## \|\| 7.13.35 \|\|

*evamabdamitaṃ kālantatāpa sa tapo'dbhutam \|*  
*trikālamarcayadbhaktyā vīreśaṃ liṅgamuttamam \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. Thus far a full year he performed the wonderful penance. He worshipped the excellent Vīreśa thrice a day.

## \|\| 7.13.36-37 \|\|

*atha trayodaśe māsi snātvā tripathagāmbhasi \|*  
*pratyūṣa eva vīreśaṃ yāvadāyāti sa dvijaḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

*tāvadvilokayāñcakre madhye liṃgantapodhanaḥ \|*  
*vibhūtibhūṣaṇambālamaṣṭavarṣākṛtiṃ śiśum \|\| 37 \|\|*

36-37. On the thirteenth month while he was going to Vīreśa after taking bath in the Gaṅgā river very early in the morning, the sage saw a child of eight years smeared with ashes all over the body, in the middle of the phallic image.

## \|\| 7.13.38 \|\|

*ākarṇāyatanetrañca suraktadaśanacchadam \|*  
*cārupiṃgajaṭāmauli nnagnaprahasitānanam \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. The child had eyes as long as his ears. He had ruddy lips. He had matted hair of beautiful tawny colour. He was naked and had a smiling face.

## \|\| 7.13.39 \|\|

*śaiśavocitanepathyadhāriṇañcitidhāriṇam \|*  
*paṭhantaṃ śrutisūktāni hasantaṃ ca svalīlayā \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. He had all the dress and features proper to childhood. He seemed intelligent. He was reciting hymns from the Vedas. He was laughing sportively.

## \|\| 7.13.40 \|\|

*tamālokya mudamprāpya romakañcukito muniḥ \|*  
*proccacāra hṛdālāpānnamostviti punaḥ punaḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. On seeing him he was joyous. He was thrilled all over the body. He uttered the formula to you over and over again.

## \|\| 7.13.41 \|\|

*abhilāṣapradaiḥ padyairaṣṭabhirbālarūpiṇam \|*  
*tuṣṭāva paramānandaṃ śaṃbhuṃ viśvānaraḥ kṛtī \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. The contented Vaiśvānara eulogised Śiva, the great bliss in the form of child by means of eight verses that gave expression to his desire.

## \|\| 7.13.42 \|\|

*\|\| viśvānara uvāca \|*  
*ekambrahmaivādvitīyaṃ samastaṃ satyaṃsatyaṃ neha nānāsti kiñcit \|*  
*eko rudro na dvitīyo'vatasthe tasmādekantvāmprapadye maheśam \|\| 42 \|\|*

Viśvānara said:—

42\. The Brahman is alone without a second. It is true. Indeed there is no multitude of different things. There is only one Rudra. There is none second to him. Hence I seek refuge in you, the sole great lord.

## \|\| 7.13.43 \|\|

*kartā harttā tvaṃ hi sarvasya śambho nānārūpeṣvekarūpo'pyarūpaḥ \|*  
*yadvatpratyagdharma eko'pyanekastasmānnānyantvāṃ vineśamprapadye \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. O Śiva, you alone are the creator and annihilator of every thing. In the different forms you are of single form and formless. Just as the inner virtue is only one though it may seem to be many. Hence I do not resort to any one except you.

## \|\| 7.13.44 \|\|

*rajjo sarpaśśuktikāyāṃ ca raupyaṃ nairaḥ pūrastanmṛgākhye marīcau \|*  
*yadyatsadvadviṣvageva prapañco yasmiñjñāte tamprapadye maheśam \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. The snake in the rope, (he silver in the oyster shell, the water in the mirage—like that when he is known, the whole world vanishes and Sat alone remains. I resort to that great lord.

## \|\| 7.13.45 \|\|

*toye śaityaṃ dāhakatvaṃ ca vahnau tāpo bhānau śītabhānau prasādaḥ \|*  
*puṣpe gandho dugdhamadhye'pi sarpiryattacchaṃbho tvaṃ tatastvāmprapadye \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. Coolness in the water, heat in the fire, the scorching nature in the sun and the pleasing gemleness in the moon, fragrance in the flower and the ghee in the milk—just, in that manner, O Śiva, you are the essense in the world. Hence I resort to you.

## \|\| 7.13.46 \|\|

*śabdaṃ gṛhṇāsyaśravāstvaṃ hi jighrasyaprāṇastvaṃ tryaṃghrirāyāsi dūrāt \|*  
*tryakṣaḥ paśyestvaṃ rasajño'pyajihvaḥ kastvāṃ samyagvettyatastvāmprapadye \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. You the earless perceive sound; you the noseless perceive fragrance; you the footless come from far; you the eyeless see; you the tongueless perceive the taste. Who knows you really? Hence I seek refuge in you.

## \|\| 7.13.47 \|\|

*no veda tvāmīśa sākṣāddhi vedo no vā viṣṇurno vidhātākhilasya \|*  
*no yogīndrānendramukhyāśca devā bhakto vedastvāmatastvāmprapadye \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. O lord, even the Vedas do not know you directly, nor Viṣṇu, nor the Creator of every thing, nor the leading Yogins, nor Indra and other gods. Only a devotee knows. Hence I seek refuge in you.

## \|\| 7.13.48 \|\|

*no te gotraṃ no sajanmāpi nāśo no vā rūpaṃ naiva śīlanna deśaḥ \|*  
*itthambhūto'pīśvarastvaṃ trilokyāssarvānkāmānpūrayestvaṃ bhaje tvām \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. You have no spiritual lineage; you are unborn; you are immortal, formless without any prescribed conduct or native land. Still you are the lord of three worlds and you fulfil all our desires. I worship you.

## \|\| 7.13.49 \|\|

*tvattassarvaṃ tvaṃ hi sarvaṃ smarāre tvaṃ gaurīśastvaṃ ca nagno'tiśāntaḥ \|*  
*tvaṃ vai vṛddhastvaṃ yuvā tvaṃ ca bālastattvaṃ yatkiṃ nānyatastvāṃ nato'ham \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. Everything originates from you. You are all-in-all, O enemy of Kāma, you are the lord of Gaurī; you are naked and quiescent; you are old, youthful and the child. Whatever is, you are that. There is no source of your origin. I bow to you.

## \|\| 7.13.50 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*stutveti vipro nipapāta bhūmau saṃbaddhapāṇirbhavatīha yāvat \|*  
*tāvatsa bālo'ribalavṛddhavṛddhaḥ provāca bhūdevamatīva hṛṣṭaḥ \|\| 50 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

50\. After eulogising thus, when the brahmin fell on the ground with the joined hands, the boy became the aged of the aged and spoke delightedly to the brahmin.

## \|\| 7.13.51 \|\|

*bāla uvāca \|*  
*viśvānara muniśreṣṭha bhūdevāhaṃ tvayādya vai \|*  
*toṣitassuprasannātmā vṛṇīṣva varamuttamam \|\| 51 \|\|*

The boy said:—

51\. O Viśvānara, O excellent sage, O brahmin, propitiated by you I am very much delighted. Ask for the boon of your choice.

## \|\| 7.13.52 \|\|

*tata utthāya hṛṣṭātmā sunirviśvānaraḥ kṛtī \|*  
*pratyabravīnmuniśreṣṭhaḥ śaṃkarambālarūpiṇam \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. Then the delighted and contented sage Viśvānara stood up and replied to Śiva who had assumed the form of a boy.

## \|\| 7.13.53 \|\|

*viśvānara uvāca \|*  
*maheśvara kimajñātaṃ sarvajñasya tava prabho \|*  
*sarvāntarātmā bhagavāccharvassarvvaprado bhavān \|\| 53 \|\|*

Viśvānara said:—

53\. O lord Śiva, what is it that is not known to you who are omniscient? You are lord Śarva the granter of everything and the immanent soul of all.

## \|\| 7.13.54 \|\|

*yācñāmprati niyuktammāṃ kiṃ brūṣe dainyakāriṇīm \|*  
*iti jñātvā maheśāna yathecchasi tathā kuru \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. Why do you speak forcing me in the pity-provoking task of imploration? O lord Śiva, after knowing this, please do as you wish.

## \|\| 7.13.55 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*iti śrutvā vacastasya devo viśvānarasya hi \|*  
*śuciśśucivratasyātha śucismitvābravīcchiśuḥ \|\| 55 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

55\. On hearing the upright words of Viśvānara of pure rites, the lord in the form of a child, spoke smilingly.

## \|\| 7.13.56 \|\|

*tvayā śuce śuciṣmatyāṃ yo'bhilāṣaḥ kṛto hṛdi \|*  
*acireṇaiva kālena sa bhaviṣyatyasaṃśayam \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. O pious one, the desire that you have cherished in your heart regarding Śuciṣmatī will be certainly realised ere long.

## \|\| 7.13.57 \|\|

*tava putratvameṣyāmi śuciṣmatyāṃ mahāmate \|*  
*khyāto gṛhapatirnāmnā śucissarvvāmarapriyaḥ \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. O intelligent one, I shall become your son in Śuciṣmatī. I shall be known as Gṛhapati, pure and loved by all gods.

## \|\| 7.13.58 \|\|

*abhilāṣāṣṭakaṃ puṇyaṃ stotrametattvayeritam \|*  
*abdatrikālapaṭhanātkāmadaṃ śivasannidhau \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. The hymn of eight verses of desire—Abhilāṣāṣṭaka—that you recited now, when recited thrice a day for a year in the vicinity of Śiva will yield all desires.

## \|\| 7.13.59 \|\|

*etatstotraprapaṭhanaṃ putrapautradhanapradam \|*  
*sarvvaśāntikaraścāpi sarvvāpattivināśanam \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. The recitation of this hymn yields sons, grandsons and wealth. It is conducive to the mollification of everything and quells all mishaps.

## \|\| 7.13.60 \|\|

*svargāpavargasampattikārakannātra saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*sarvvastotrasamaṃ hyetatsarvvakāmapradaṃ sadā \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. It is the cause of acquisition of heaven, emancipation and prosperity. There is no doubt in this. This is on a par with all other hymns. It always yields every desire.

## \|\| 7.13.61 \|\|

*prātarutthāya susnāto liṃgamabhyarcya śāmbhavam \|*  
*varṣaṃ japannidaṃ stotramaputraḥ putravānbhavet \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. If an issueless man gets up early in the morning, takes his bath, worships Śiva’s phallic image, recites this hymn and continues the practice for a year, he will surely secure a son.

## \|\| 7.13.62 \|\|

*abhilāṣāṣṭakamidanna deyaṃ yasya kasyacit \|*  
*gopanīyaṃ prayatnena mahāvandhyāprasūtikṛt \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. The Abhilāṣāṣṭaka hymn shall not be taught to common people. It shall be preserved carefully as a secret mantra. It makes the most barren lady deliver of a child.

## \|\| 7.13.63 \|\|

*striyā vā puruṣeṇāpi niyamālliṃgasannidhau \|*  
*abdajaptamidaṃ stotramputradannātra saṃśayaḥ \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. This hymn recited by a woman or a man with due observances in the presence of a phallic image for a year undoubtedly yields the birth of a son.

## \|\| 7.13.64 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityuktvāntardadhe śambhurbālarūpaḥ satāṃ gatiḥ \|*  
*so'pi viśvānaro vipro hṛṣṭātmā svagṛhaṃ yayau \|\| 64 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

64\. After saying this, Śiva, the goal of the good and who assumed the form of a boy vanished. The brahmin Viśvānara returned to his house highly delighted.

## Chapter 14 - The incarnation of Gṛhapati (2)

## \|\| 7.14.1 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*sa vipro gṛhamāgatya mahāharṣasamanvitaḥ \|*  
*priyāyai kathayāmāsa tadvṛttāntamaśeṣataḥ \|\| 1 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

1\. With great delight the brahmin returned home and told his beloved wife all those details.

## \|\| 7.14.2 \|\|

*tacchrutvā viprapatnī sā mudamprāpa śuciṣmatī \|*  
*atīva premasaṃyuktā praśaśaṃsa vidhinnijam \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. On hearing it, Śuciṣmatī, the wife of the brahmin, rejoiced much and being full of love she praised her good fortune.

## \|\| 7.14.3 \|\|

*atha kālena tadyoṣidantarvatnī babhūva ha \|*  
*vidhivadvihite tena garbhādhānākhyakarmaṇi \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. She became pregnant in due course. The brahmin performed the rite of impregnation duly.

## \|\| 7.14.4 \|\|

*tataḥ puṃsavanantena syandanātprāgvipaścitā \|*  
*gṛhyoktavidhinā samyakkṛtampuṃstvavivṛddhaye \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. Before the stir and throb of the child in the womb was felt, the learned Brahmin performed the holy rite of Puṃsavana in the manner prescribed in the Gṛhyasūtras for the growth of embryo’s manliness.

## \|\| 7.14.5 \|\|

*sīmanto'thāṣṭame māse garbharūpasamṛddhikṛt \|*  
*sukhaprasavasiddhau ca tenākari kṛpāvidā \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Then in the eighth month he performed the holy rite of Sīmanta which is conducive to the growth of the embryo and which facilitates easy delivery of the child.

## \|\| 7.14.6-9 \|\|

*athātaśśubhatārāsu tārādhipavarānanaḥ \|*  
*kendre gurau śubhe lagne sugraheṣu yugeṣu ca \|\| 6 \|\|*

*ariṣṭadīpanirvāṇassarvāriṣṭavināśakṛt \|*  
*tanayo nāma tasyāntu śuciṣmatyāmbabhūva ha \|\| 7 \|\|*

*śarvassamastasukhado bhūrbhuvaḥ svarnnivāsinām \|*  
*gandhavāhanavāhāśca digvadhūrmukhavāsasaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

*iṣṭagandhaprasūnaughairvavṛṣuste ghanāghanāḥ \|*  
*devadundubhayo neduḥ prasedussarvvato diśaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

6-9. Then at the conjunction of stars when Jupiter was in the centre, when the Lagna was auspicious, when the good planets stood in pairs, Śiva, the giver of happiness to all, was born as the son of Śuciṣmatī. His face resembled the moon. The lamps in the lying-in-chamber were dimmed by his bodily lustre. The lord, the destroyer of misfortunes and the bestower of happiness to the dwellers of the Earth, the nether worlds and the heaven was born as the son. The smoke exuding therefrom became the perfume to sweeten the breath of the quarters. The immense clouds showered flowers of pleasing fragrance. The divine drums were sounded and the quarters were brightened up.

## \|\| 7.14.10 \|\|

*paritassaritassvacchā bhūtānāṃ mānasaissaha \|*  
*tamo'tāmyattu nitarāṃ rajo'pi virajo'bhavat \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. The rivers became transparent as also the minds of living beings. The darkness of evil dwindled and the Rajas was stripped of its quality or of its dust.

## \|\| 7.14.11 \|\|

*sattvāssattvasamāyuktāḥ sudhāvṛṣṭirbabhūva vai \|*  
*kalyāṇī sarvathā vāṇī prāṇinaḥ priyavatyabhūt \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. The living creatures were endowed with Sattva quality. There was a shower of nectar. People began to talk sweet and pleasant words.

## \|\| 7.14.12-13 \|\|

*raṃbhāmukhyā apsaraso maṅgaladravyapāṇayaḥ \|*  
*vidyādharyaśca kinnaryyastathā maryyassahasraśaḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

*gandharvoragayakṣāṇāṃ sumāniyaḥ śubhasvarāḥ \|*  
*gāyantyo maṃgalaṃ gītantatrājagmuranekaśaḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

12-13. Rambhā and other celestial damsels arrived with auspicious articles in their hands. Thousands of Vidyādharīs, Kinnarīs, Goddesses, the womenfolk of Gandharvas, serpents and Yakṣas of sweet voice flocked singing auspicious songs.

## \|\| 7.14.14-20 \|\|

*mārīciratriḥ pulahaḥ pulastyaḥ kraturaṅgirāḥ \|*  
*vasiṣṭhaḥ kaśyapo'gastyo vibhāṇḍo māṇḍavīsutaḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

*lomaśo romacaraṇo bharadvājo'tha gautamaḥ \|*  
*bhṛgustu gālavo gargo jātūkarṇyaḥ parāśaraḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

*āpastambo yājñavalkyo dakṣavālmīkimudgalāḥ \|*  
*śātātapaśca likhitaśśilādaḥ śaṃkha uñchabhuk \|\| 16 \|\|*

*jamadagniśca saṃvarto mataṃgo bharatoṃśumān \|*  
*vyāsaḥ kātyāyanaḥ kutsaḥ śaunakastu śrutaśśukaḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

*ṛṣyaśṛṅgo'tha durvvāsāśśucirnārada tumburuḥ \|*  
*uttaṃko vāmadevaśca pavano'sitadevalau \|\| 18 \|\|*

*sālaṃkāyanahārītau viśvāmitro'tha bhārgavaḥ \|*  
*mṛkaṇḍassaha putreṇa parvvato dārukastathā \|\| 19 \|\|*

*dhaumyopamanyuvatsādyā munayo munikanyakāḥ \|*  
*tacchāntyarthaṃ samājagmurdhanyaṃ viśvānarāśramam \|\| 20 \|\|*

14-20. The following sages came there—Marīci, Atri, Pulaha, Pulastya, Kratu, Aṅgiras, Vasiṣṭha, Kaśyapa, Agastya, Vibhāṇḍa son of Māṇḍavī, Lomaśa, Romacaraṇa, Bharadvāja, Gautama, Bhṛgu, Gālava, Garga, Jātūkarṇa, Parāśara, Āpastamba, Yājñavalkya, Dakṣa, Vālmīki, Mudgala, Śātātapa Likhita, Śilāda, Śaṅkha, Uñcchabhuk, Jamadagni, Saṃvarta, Mataṅga, Bharata, Aṃśumān, Vyāsa Kātyāyana, Kutsa, Śaunaka, Suśruta, Śuka, Ṛṣyaśṛṅga, Durvāsas, Śuci, Nārada, Tumburu, Uttaṅka, Vāmadeva, Pavana, Asīta, Devala, Sālaṅkāyana, Hārīta, Viśvāmitra, Bhārgava, Mṛkaṇḍu with his son, Parvata, Dāruka, Dhaumya, Upamanyu Vatsa and others came to the happy hermitage of Viśvānara for the auspicious Śānti ceremony. The daughters of the sages too came there.

## \|\| 7.14.21 \|\|

*brahmā bṛhaspatiyuto devo garuḍavāhanaḥ \|*  
*nandibhṛṅgi samāyukto gauryyā saha vṛṣadhvajaḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. Brahmā came there with Bṛhaspati. Lord Viṣṇu came riding on Garuḍa as vehicle. The bull-bannered lord came there with Gaurī, Nandin, Bhṛṅgī and others.

## \|\| 7.14.22 \|\|

*mahendramukhyā gīrvāṇā nāgāḥ pātālavāsinaḥ \|*  
*ratnānyādāya bahuśassasaritkā mahābdhayaḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. Mahendra and other gods, the Nāgas residing in the nether regions came there. Taking gems with them many great oceans and rivers came there.

## \|\| 7.14.23 \|\|

*sthāvarā jaṃgamaṃ rūpaṃ dhṛtvā yātāssahasraśaḥ \|*  
*mahāmahotsave tasminbabhūvākālakaumudī \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Thousands of immobile beings came there in the forms of the mobile beings. During the great festivities then there was an untimely flood of moonlight.

## \|\| 7.14.24-25 \|\|

*jātakarma svayaṃ tasya kṛtavānvidhirānataḥ \|*  
*śrutiṃ vicāryya tadrūpannāmnā gṛhapatistvayam \|\| 24 \|\|*

*iti nāma dadau tasmai deyamekādaśe'hani \|*  
*nāmakarmavidhānena tadarthaśrutimuccaran \|\| 25 \|\|*

24-25. Brahmā himself performed the postnatal rite\[4\] after bowing to the infant. In consonance with his form and beauty he gave him the name of Gṛhapati on the eleventh day in accordance with injunctions, repeating the Vedic mantras.

## \|\| 7.14.26 \|\|

*caturnigamamantroktairāśīrbhirabhinandya ca \|*  
*samayāddhaṃsamāruhya sarveṣāñca pitāmahaḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. After conferring blessings on everyone by reciting the mantras from the four Vedas, Brahmā left the place while riding on the swan.

## \|\| 7.14.27 \|\|

*kṛtvā bālocitāṃ rakṣāṃ laukikīṃ gatimāśritaḥ \|*  
*āruhya yānaṃ svandhāma haro'pi hariṇā yayau \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. After affording proper safeguards for the child in accordance with worldly conventions Śiva returned to his abode on his vehicle along with Viṣṇu.

## \|\| 7.14.28-30 \|\|

*aho rūpamaho tejastvaho sarvāṃgalakṣaṇam \|*  
*aho śuciṣmatī bhāgyamāvirāsītsvayaṃ haraḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

*athavā kimidaṃ citraṃ śarvabhaktajaneṣvaho \|*  
*svayamāvirabhūdrudro yayo rudrastadarcitaḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

*iti stuvantastenyonyaṃ samprahṛṣṭatanūruhaḥ \|*  
*viśvānaraṃ samāpṛcchya jagmuḥ sarve yathāgatam \|\| 30 \|\|*

28-30. The visitors praised the infant. They were at the height of their joy. They spoke in unison “O what a beauty! What a splendour! All the characteristic signs are there in all the limbs. How fortunate is Śuciṣmatī! Śiva himself is born. What is surprising in this? Śiva has incarnated in all devotees. Śiva has been worshipped by her.” Then they took leave of Viśvānara and departed.

## \|\| 7.14.31 \|\|

*ataḥ putraṃ samīhante gṛhasthāśramavāsinaḥ \|*  
*putreṇa lokāñjayati śrutireṣā sanātanī \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. The eternal Veda says—‘A person conquers the worlds through a son. Hence all householders wish for a son.

## \|\| 7.14.32 \|\|

*aputrasya gṛhaṃ śūnyamaputrasyārjanaṃ vṛthā \|*  
*aputrasya tapaśchinnaṃ no pavitratyaputrataḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. The house of a sonless person is a void. His earnings are futile. His penance is split. It is not holy due to the want of a son.

## \|\| 7.14.33 \|\|

*na putrātparamo lābho na putrātparamaṃ sukham \|*  
*na putrātparamaṃ mitramparatreha ca kutracit \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. Both here and hereafter there is no better acquisition than a son; there is no better happiness than a son; there is no greater friend than a son.

## \|\| 7.14.34 \|\|

*niṣkramo'tha caturthe'sya māsi pitrā kṛto gṛhāt \|*  
*annaprāśanamabdārddhe cūḍārddhe cārthavatkṛtā \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. In the fourth month the rite of “crossing the threshold” was performed by the father.\[6\] At the end of six months the rite of Annaprāśana\[7\] and at the end of a year the tonsure ceremony was performed.

## \|\| 7.14.35 \|\|

*karṇavedhantataḥ kṛtvā śravaṇarkṣe sa karmavit \|*  
*brahmatejobhivṛddhyarthaṃ pañcame'bde vratandadau \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. The knower rites performed the Karṇavedha rite\[9\] under the constellation of Sravaṇa. In order to enhance his brahminical splendour he made him perform an auspicious Vrata in his fifth year.

## \|\| 7.14.36 \|\|

*upākarmaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā vedānadhyāpayatsudhīḥ \|*  
*abdaṃ vedānsa vidhinā'dhyaiṣṭa sāṃgapadakramān \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. After celebrating the Upākarma rite, the intelligent sage taught him the Vedas. The boy learned the Vedas for a year with their ancillaries. He learnt the Padapāṭha and Kramapāṭha of the Vedas.

## \|\| 7.14.37 \|\|

*vidyājātaṃ samastaṃ ca sākṣimātraṃ guromukhāt \|*  
*vinayādiguṇānāviṣkurvañjagrāha śaktimān \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. Evincing his humility and other qualities, the competent youth grasped the lore almost by himself but formally through the preceptor just as a medium.

## \|\| 7.14.38 \|\|

*tato'tha navame varṣe pitrośśuśrūṣaṇe ratam \|*  
*vaiśvānaraṃ gṛhapatiṃ draṣṭumāyācca nāradaḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. In his ninth year, Nārada came there to see Gṛhapati, son of Viśvānara, and found him engaged in serving his parents.

## \|\| 7.14.39 \|\|

*viśvānaroṭajamprāpya devarṣistaṃ tu kautukī \|*  
*apṛcchatkuśalantatra gṛhītārdhāsanaḥ kramāt \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. The divine sage reached the hut of Viśvānara, received due hospitality and seated himself. Eagerly he enquired of his health.

## \|\| 7.14.40 \|\|

*tataḥ sarvaṃ ca tadbhāgyaṃ putradharmaṃ ca sammukhe \|*  
*vaiśvānaraṃ samavadatsmṛtvā śivapadāmbujam \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. He remembered the lotus-like feet of Śiva and mentioned to Viśvānara the future fortune and activities of his son.

## \|\| 7.14.41-42 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityukto muninā bālaḥ pitrorājñāmavāpya saḥ \|*  
*praṇamya nāradaṃ śrīmān bhaktyā prahva upāviśata \|\| 41 \|\|*

*vaiśvānara samabhyehi mamotsaṃge niṣīda bhoḥ \|*  
*lakṣaṇāni parīkṣe'haṃ pāṇindarśaya dakṣiṇam \|\| 42 \|\|*

41-42. Nārada said to the boy “O son of Viśvānara come on. Sit on my lap. I shall study your characteristics. Show me your right hand.” On being thus addressed, the boy bowed to Nārada after receiving the permission of his parents. With great devotion the glorious child sat there humbly.

## \|\| 7.14.43 \|\|

*tato dṛṣṭvā tu sarvaṃ hi tālujihvādi nāradaḥ \|*  
*viśvānaraṃ samavadacchivapreraṇayā sudhīḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

43\. Examining closely the palate, tongue etc. (of the boy) the intelligent Nārada spoke to Viśvānara, urged by Śiva.

## \|\| 7.14.44 \|\|

*nārada uvāca \|*  
*viśvānara mune vacmi śṛṇu putrāṃkamādarāt \|*  
*sarvāṃgasvaṃkavānputro mahālakṣaṇavānayam \|\| 44 \|\|*

Nārada said:—

44\. “O sage Viśvānara, listen. I shall mention the detailed characteristic marks of your son. Your son bears good marks on every limb. He possesses all good signs.

## \|\| 7.14.45 \|\|

*kintu sarvaguṇopetaṃ sarvalakṣaṇalakṣitam \|*  
*sampūrṇanirmalakalaṃ pālayedvidhuvadvidhiḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. But fate alone must guard this boy duly, this boy who has all good qualities, who is characterised by all good features and who is possessed of all pure digits in their entirety.

## \|\| 7.14.46 \|\|

*tasmātsarvaprayatnena rakṣaṇīyastvasau śiśu \|*  
*guṇo'pi doṣatāṃ yāti vakrībhūte vidhātari \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. This child should be protected with all efforts. When the fate is adverse, even good qualities assume the state of defects.

## \|\| 7.14.47 \|\|

*śaṃke'sya dvādaśe māsi pratyūho vidyudagnitaḥ \|*  
*ityuktvā nārado'gacchaddevalokaṃ yathāgatam \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. In the twelfth month from now I suspect a great danger to him from lightning and fire.” After saying this Nārada returned to heaven the way he had come.

## Chapter 15 - The incarnation of Gṛhapati (3)

## \|\| 7.15.1 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*viśvānarassapatnīkastacchrutvā nāraderitam \|*  
*tadevammanyamānobhūdvajrapātaṃ sudāruṇam \|\| 1 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

1\. On hearing the revelation of Nārada, Viśvānara and his wife considered it a terrible bolt from the blue.

## \|\| 7.15.2 \|\|

*hā hatosmīti vacasā hṛdayaṃ samatāḍayat \|*  
*mūrcchāmavāpa mahatīṃ putraśokasamākulaḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. He shouted “O I am doomed!” He beat his chest. Excited by the grief about his son he fell into a great swoon.

## \|\| 7.15.3 \|\|

*śuciṣmatyapi duḥkhārttā rurodātīva dussaham \|*  
*atisvareṇa hārāvairatyantaṃ vyākulendriyā \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. The grief-stricken Śuciṣmatī too lamented much. Her senses were benumbed and she. lamented aloud.

## \|\| 7.15.4 \|\|

*śrutvārttanādamiti viśvanaropi mohaṃ hitvotthitaḥ kimiti kiṃtviti kiṃ kimetat \|*  
*uccairvadan gṛhapatiḥ kva sa me bahisthaḥ prāṇontarātmanilayassakaleṃdriyeśaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. On hearing her loud lamentation, Viśvānara woke up from his swoon. He cried aloud “What is this? What is this? Where is Gṛhapati my vital breath who stands outside and who is the lord of all the senses lodged within me.

## \|\| 7.15.5 \|\|

*tato dṛṣṭvā sa pitarau bahuśokasamāvṛtau \|*  
*smitvovāca gṛhapassabālaśśaṃkarāṃśajaḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. On seeing his parents excessively grieved, the boy Gṛhapati, a part of Śiva, smiled and said.

## \|\| 7.15.6 \|\|

*gṛhapatiruvāca \|*  
*he mātastāta kiṃ jātaṃ kāraṇantadvadādhunā \|*  
*kimarthaṃ rudito'tyarthaṃ trāsastādṛkkuto hi vām \|\| 6 \|\|*

Gṛhapati said:—

6\. O Mother, O Father, what is the reason? Please tell me now. Why do both of you cry? What and whence is such a fright for you?

## \|\| 7.15.7 \|\|

*na māṃ kṛtavapustrāṇambhavaccaraṇareṇubhiḥ \|*  
*kālaḥ kalayituṃ śakto varākīṃ ciñcalālpikā \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Time and pitiable fickle existence cannot affect me, whose body is guarded by the dust-particles of your feet.

## \|\| 7.15.8 \|\|

*pratijñāṃ śṛṇutāntātau yadi vāntanayo hyaham \|*  
*kariṣyehaṃ tathā yena mṛtyustrasto bhaviṣyati \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. O my parents, please listen to my vow. If I am your true son, I will do that whereby death itself will be terrified.

## \|\| 7.15.9 \|\|

*mṛtyuṃjayaṃ samārādhya garvajñaṃ sarvadaṃ satām \|*  
*japiṣyāmi mahākālaṃ satyaṃ tātau vadāmyaham \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. I shall propitiate lord Śiva, the conqueror of death, the omniscient and the bestower of all to the good. I shall perform the Japa of Mahākāla. O parents, I am telling you the truth.

## \|\| 7.15.10 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*iti śrutvā vacastasya jāritau dvijadampatī \|*  
*akālamṛtavarṣaughairgatatāpau tadocatuḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

10\. On hearing his words the aged branmin couple were freed of their distress by the untimely showers of nectar. They then spoke.

## \|\| 7.15.11 \|\|

*dvijadampatī ūcatuḥ \|*  
*punarbrūhi punarbrūhi kīdṛkkīdṛk punarvada \|*  
*kālaḥ kalayitunnālaṃ varākī cañcalāsti kā \|\| 11 \|\|*

The brahmin couple said:—

11\. Say again. Say again. What is it? What is it? Speak again. Time and pitiable fickle existence will not affect you?

## \|\| 7.15.12 \|\|

*āvayostāpanāśāya mahopāyastvayeritaḥ \|*  
*mṛtyuṃjayākhyadevasya samārādhanalakṣaṇaḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. A great means has been suggested by you to dispel our distress, that of the propitiation of lord Śiva, the conqueror of death.

## \|\| 7.15.13 \|\|

*tadvacca śaraṇaṃ śambhornātaḥ parataraṃ hi tat \|*  
*manorathapathātīta kāriṇaḥ pāpahāriṇaḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. The same is the result for those who seek refuge in Śiva who dispels sins and does everything beyond the path of our desire. There is nothing better than this.

## \|\| 7.15.14 \|\|

*kinna śrutantvayā tāta śvetaketuṃ yathā purā \|*  
*pāśitaṃ kālapāśena rarakṣa tripurāntakaḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. O dear, haven’t you heard that the slayer of the Tripuras protected, in days of yore, Śvetaketu who was bound by the noose of Kāla?

## \|\| 7.15.15 \|\|

*śilādatanayaṃ mṛtyugrastamaṣṭābdamātrakam \|*  
*śivo nijajanañcakre nandinaṃ viśvanaṃdinam \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. When Śilāda’s son was threatened by death, when he was only eight, Śiva saved him and made him his own attendant as Nandin, the delighter of the whole universe.

## \|\| 7.15.16 \|\|

*kṣīrodamathanodbhūtaṃ pralayānalasannibham \|*  
*pītvā halāhalaṃ ghoramarakṣadbhuvanatrayam \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. He protected the three worlds by drinking off the terrible poison as fearful as the fire at the hour of dissolution, when it was produced at the churning of the milk-ocean.

## \|\| 7.15.17 \|\|

*jalaṃdharaṃ mahādarpaṃ hṛtatrailokyasampadam \|*  
*rucirāṃguṣṭharekhottha cakreṇa nijaghāna yaḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. With the wheel raised up by the line drawn with his beautiful toe he killed the haughty Jalandhara who had deprived the three worlds of their pleasure.

## \|\| 7.15.18 \|\|

*ya ekeṣu nipātotthajvalanaistripurampurā \|*  
*trailokyaiśvaryasammūḍhaṃ śoṣayāmāsa bhānunā \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. He burnt the Tripuras, who were haughty and deluded by their lordship of the three worlds, by means of the blazing fires emanating from the fall of a single arrow.

## \|\| 7.15.19 \|\|

*kāmaṃ dṛṣṭinipātena trailokyavijayorjitam \|*  
*nināyānaṃgapadavīṃ vīkṣyamāṇeṣvajādiṣu \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. Even as Brahmā and others were watching, he reduced Kāma to ashes by a single glance, Kāma whose strength had been enhanced by his conquest of the three worlds.

## \|\| 7.15.20 \|\|

*tambrahmādyaikakartārammeghavāhanamacyutam \|*  
*prayāhi putra śaraṇaṃ viśvarakṣāmaṇiṃ śivam \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. O son, seek refuge in that Śiva, the amulet of protection of the universe, the sole creator of Indra, Acyuta, Brahmā and others.

## \|\| 7.15.21 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*pitroranujñāmprāpyeti praṇamya caraṇau tayoḥ \|*  
*prādakṣiṇyamupāvṛtya bahvāśvāsya viniryayau \|\| 21 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

21\. Thus he secured the permission of his parents. He bowed at their feet and circumambulated them. He then pacified them and started.

## \|\| 7.15.22-25 \|\|

*samprāpya kāśīṃ duṣprāpāmbrahmanārāyaṇādibhiḥ \|*  
*mahāsaṃvarttasantāpahantrīṃ viśveśapālitām \|\| 22 \|\|*

*svardhunyā hārayaṣṭyeva rājitā kaṇṭhabhūmiṣu \|*  
*vicitraguṇaśālinyā harapatnyā virājitām \|\| 23 \|\|*

*tatra prāpya sa vipreśaḥ prāgyayau maṇikarṇikām \|*  
*tatra snātvā vidhānena dṛṣṭvā viśveśvaramprabhum \|\| 24 \|\|*

*sāñjalirnataśīrṣo'sau mahānandānvitassudhīḥ \|*  
*trailokyaprāṇasantrāṇakāriṇampraṇanāma ha \|\| 25 \|\|*

22-25. He reached Kāśī inaccessible even to Brahmā, Viṣṇu and others. He reached Kāśī that dispels all recurring distress, that is protected by Viśveśa, that shines with the celestial river Gaṅgā as with a necklace round its neck, that shines splendidly by the presence of Pārvatī, the consort of Śiva and of wonderful qualities. After reaching Kāśī he went to Maṇikarṇikā at the outset. After taking bath in accordance with the injunctions of the Śāstras he saw Lord Viśveśvara with palms joined and head bent in reverence. The intelligent brahmin was greatly delighted. He bowed to lord Śiva who instils life into and protects the three worlds.

## \|\| 7.15.26 \|\|

*ālokyālokya talliṃgaṃ tutoṣa hṛdaye muhuḥ \|*  
*paramānaṃdakaṃdāḍhyaṃ sphuṭametanna saṃśayaḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Seeing that phallic image of Śiva again and again he was delighted in his heart and thought—“Undoubtedly this is possessed of great bliss.”

## \|\| 7.15.27 \|\|

*aho na matto dhanyosti trailokye sacarācare \|*  
*yadadrākṣiṣamadyāhaṃ śrīmadviśveśvaraṃ vibhum \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Hā, there is none more blessed than I in the three worlds consisting of the mobile and immobile beings since today I have seen here the lord Viśveśvara.

## \|\| 7.15.28 \|\|

*mama bhāgyodayāyaiva nāradena maharṣiṇā \|*  
*purāgatya tathoktaṃ yatkṛtakṛtyosmyahantataḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. It is only to enhance my good fortune that sage Nārada formerly came and told me that. I am therefore very much contented.

## \|\| 7.15.29-31 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityānandāmṛtarasairvidhāya sa hi pāraṇam \|*  
*tataśśubhehni saṃsthāpya liṃgaṃ sarvvahitapradam \|\| 29 \|\|*

*jagrāha niyamānghorān duṣkarānakṛtātmabhiḥ \|*  
*aṣṭottaraśataiḥ kumbhaiḥ pūrṇairgaṃgāmbhasā śubhaiḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

*saṃsnāpya vāsasā pūtaḥ pūtātmā pratyahaṃ śivam \|*  
*nīlotpalamayīmmālāṃ samarpayati so'nvaham \|\| 31 \|\|*

29-31. By the nectarine juice of bliss he concluded the sacred rite. Then on an auspicious day he installed the phallic image, the bestower of all benefits. He adopted austere rites difficult to be performed by those who have no control over themselves. He filled pots with the water of the Gaṅgā strained and purified by means of a cloth. Every day he bathed the phallic image with hundred and eight potfulls of water. Then he put a garland of blue lotus round the deity.

## \|\| 7.15.32 \|\|

*aṣṭādhikasahasraistu sumanobhirvinirmitām \|*  
*sa pakṣe vātha vā māse kandamūlaphalāśanaḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. He wreathed the garlands with a thousand and eight flowers. He ate fruits, roots and bulbous roots once in a fortnight or a month.

## \|\| 7.15.33 \|\|

*śīrṇaparṇāśanairdhīraḥ ṣaṇmāsaṃ sambabhūva saḥ \|*  
*ṣaṇmāsaṃ vāyubhakṣo'bhūtṣaṇmāsaṃ jala bindubhuk \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. The courageous devotee spent six months by eating withered leaves. The remaining six months he spent drinking drops of water or simply breathing.

## \|\| 7.15.34 \|\|

*evaṃ varṣavayastasya vyatikrāntaṃ mahātmanaḥ \|*  
*śivaikamanaso viprāstapyamānasya nārada \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. O Brahmins, thus a year in the life of that noble soul passed by, performing the penance with the devoted mind resting only in Śiva.

## \|\| 7.15.35 \|\|

*janmato dvādaśe varṣe tadvaco nāraderitam \|*  
*satyaṃ kariṣyanniva tamabhyagātkuliśāyudhaḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. In the twelfth year of his life, the thunderbolt, armed Indra approached him as if fulfilling the utterance of Nārada.

## \|\| 7.15.36 \|\|

*uvāca ca varaṃ brūhi dadmi tvanmanasi sthitam \|*  
*ahaṃ śatakraturvipra prasannosmi śubhavrataiḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. He said—“O brahmin, I am Indra, I am delighted at your holy rites. Ask for the boon you wish to have. I shall give you whatever desire you have in your mind.

## \|\| 7.15.37 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityākarṇya mahendrasya vākyammunikumārakaḥ \|*  
*uvāca madhurandhīraḥ kīrtayanmadhurākṣaram \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. On hearing the words of lord Indra, the son of the sage spoke these words boldly and sweetly.

## \|\| 7.15.38 \|\|

*gṛhapatiruvāca \|*  
*maghavan vṛtraśatro tvāṃ jāne kuliśapāṇinam \|*  
*nāhaṃ vṛṇe varantvattaśśaṃkaro varado'sti me \|\| 38 \|\|*

Gṛhapati said:—

38\. “O Indra, O enemy of Vṛtra, I know you as armed with the thunderbolt. I do not want a boon from you. Śiva is the granter of boon to me.”

## \|\| 7.15.39 \|\|

*indra uvāca \|*  
*na mattaśśaṅkarastvanyo devadevo'smyahaṃ śiśo \|*  
*vihāya bāliśatvaṃ tvaṃ varaṃ yācasva mā ciram \|\| 39 \|\|*

Indra said:—

39\. O child, Śiva is not separate from me. I am the lord of gods. Give up your foolhardiness and ask for a boon from me. Do not delay.

## \|\| 7.15.40 \|\|

*gṛhapatiruvāca \|*  
*gacchāhalyāpate'sādho gotrāre pākaśāsana \|*  
*na prārthaye paśupateranyaṃ devāntaraṃ sphuṭam \|\| 40 \|\|*

Gṛhapati said:—

40\. O Paramour of Ahalyā, O wicked one, O enemy of mountains, O chastiser of Pāka, it is quite clear that I shall not ask any other god except Śiva for a boon.

## \|\| 7.15.41 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*iti tasya vacaḥ śrutvā krodha saṃraktalocanaḥ \|*  
*udyamya kuliśaṃ ghorambhīṣayāmāsa bālakam \|\| 41 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

41\. On hearing his words, lndra’s eyes turned red with fury. He raised his terrible thunderbolt and threatened the boy with dire consequences.

## \|\| 7.15.42 \|\|

*sa dṛṣṭvā bālako vajraṃ vidyujjvālā samākulam \|*  
*smarannārada vākyaṃ ca mumūrccha bhayavihvalaḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. On seeing the thunderbolt with the flames of lightning, the boy remembered the words of Nārada. He became frightened and fell into a swoon.

## \|\| 7.15.43 \|\|

*atha gaurīpatiśśambhurāvirāsīttaponudaḥ \|*  
*uttiṣṭhottiṣṭha bhadrante sparśaissaṃjīvayanniva \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. Then Śiva, the lord of Pārvatī, the dīspeller of darkness appeared in front. As though enlivening him with gentle strokings he said:—“Stand up. Stand up. May good come up to you.”

## \|\| 7.15.44 \|\|

*unmīlya netrakamale supte iva dinakṣaye \|*  
*apaśyadagre cotthāya śambhumarkaśatādhikam \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. Opening his eyes resembling the lotus that had gone to sleep at the end of the day and getting up, he saw Śiva shining more brilliantly than hundred suns.

## \|\| 7.15.45-49 \|\|

*bhāle locanamālokya kaṇṭhe kālaṃ vṛṣadhvajam \|*  
*vāmāṅgasanniviṣṭādritanayaṃ candraśekharam \|\| 45 \|\|*

*kaparddena virājantaṃ triśūlājagavāyudham \|*  
*sphuratkarpūragaurāṃgaṃ pariṇaddha gajājinam \|\| 46 \|\|*

*parijñāya mahādevaṃ guruvākyata āgamāt \|*  
*harṣabāṣpākulāsannakaṇṭharomāñcakañcukaḥ \|\| 47 \|\|*

*kṣaṇaṃ ca girivattasthau citrakūṭatriputrakaḥ \|*  
*yathā tathā susampanno vismṛtyātmānameva ca \|\| 48 \|\|*

*na stotuṃ na namaskartuṃ kiñcidvijñaptimeva ca \|*  
*yadā sa na śaśākālaṃ tadā smitvāha śaṅkaraḥ \|\| 49 \|\|*

45-49. Seeing the lord with the eyes in the fore-head, blue-necked, bull-bannered, moon-crested, with Pārvatī occupying the left side, shining with matted hair, armed with the trident and Ajagava bow, possessed of limbs shining white like camphor, clad in the elephant’s hide, he recognised the lord as the great God described in the Āgamas and statements of his preceptor. He was delighted and he evinced the thrill of hair. For a moment he stood motionless like the mountain Citrakūta. He forgot himself like a man suddenly becoming rich. He was unable to eulogise or bow down or plead for anything. Then Śiva smiled and said.

## \|\| 7.15.50 \|\|

*īśvara uvāca \|*  
*śiśo gṛhapate śakrādvajrodyatakarādaho \|*  
*jñāta bhīto'si mā bhaiṣīrjijñāsā te mayā kṛtā \|\| 50 \|\|*

Śiva said:—

50\. O child Gṛhapati, I know that you are frightened of Indra with the thunderbolt in his lifted hand. Don’t be afraid, I only wanted to know your nature.

## \|\| 7.15.51 \|\|

*mama bhaktasya no śakro na vajraṃ cāntako'pi ca \|*  
*prabhavedindrarūpeṇa mayaiva tvamvibhīṣitaḥ \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. Neither Indra, nor the thunderbolt, nor even the god of death is powerful to molest my devotee. You have been terrified by me alone in the form of Indra.

## \|\| 7.15.52 \|\|

*varandadāmi te bhadra tvamagnipadabhāgbhava \|*  
*sarveṣāmeva devānāṃ varadastvaṃ bhaviṣyasi \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. O gentle one, I shall grant you the boon. I confer the title of Agni on you. I authorise you to grant boons to whatever deity you think fit.

## \|\| 7.15.53 \|\|

*sarveṣāmeva bhūtānāṃ tvamagne'ntaścaro bhava \|*  
*dharmarājendrayormadhye digīśo rājyamāpnuhi \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. O Agni, you will be moving in the midst of all mortals. Acquire kingship as the guardian of the quarter in the south-east.

## \|\| 7.15.54 \|\|

*tvayedaṃ sthāpitaṃ liṃgaṃ tava nāmnā bhaviṣyati \|*  
*agnīśvara iti khyātaṃ sarvatejovibṛṃhaṇam \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. The phallic image installed by you will be known by your name. It will be known as Agnīśvara and it will be conducive to the enhancement of all splendour.

## \|\| 7.15.55 \|\|

*agnīśvarasya bhaktānāṃ na bhayaṃ vidyudagnibhiḥ \|*  
*agnimāṃdyabhayaṃ naiva nākālamaraṇaṃ kvacit \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. The devotees of Agnīśvara need not fear lightning and fire. They will never suffer from impaired digestion or premature death.

## \|\| 7.15.56 \|\|

*agnīśvaraṃ samabhyarcya kāśyāṃ sarvasamṛddhidam \|*  
*anyatrāpi mṛto daivādvahniloke mahīyate \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. A person who worships Agnīśvara, the bestower of prosperity, at Vārāṇasī, is honoured in the region of fire even if, by chance, he dies elsewhere.

## \|\| 7.15.57 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityuktānīya tadbandhūnpitrośca paripaśyatoḥ \|*  
*dikpatitve'bhiṣicyāgniṃ tatra liṃge śivo'viśat \|\| 57 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

57\. After saying this and fetching his kinsmen Śiva crowned him as the guardian of the quarter. Even as the parents watched it, Śiva entered his phallic image

## \|\| 7.15.58 \|\|

*itthamagnyavatāraste varṇito me janārdanaḥ \|*  
*nāmnā gṛhapatistāta śaṃkarasya parātmanaḥ \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. Thus, O dear, the incarnation of Śiva as fire has been explained to you. This incarnation of Śiva, the great soul, is famous as Gṛhapati also.

## \|\| 7.15.59 \|\|

*citrahotrapurī ramyā sukhadārciṣmatī varā \|*  
*jātavedasi ye bhaktā te tatra nivasanti vai \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. The city of Citrahotra is beautiful, pleasant and finely lustrous. Those who are devotees of fire stay there.

## \|\| 7.15.60 \|\|

*agnipraveśaṃ ye kuryyurdṛḍhasattvā jitendriyāḥ \|*  
*striyo vā sattvasampannāste sarvvepyagnitejasaḥ \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. People of steady Sāttvika quality and control over their sense-organs, who enter fire, or women endowed with Sattva quality become as lustrous as fire.

## \|\| 7.15.61 \|\|

*agnihotraratā viprāḥ sthāpitā brahmacāriṇaḥ \|*  
*paścānivarttino'pyevamagnilokegnivarcasaḥ \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. Brahmins performing Agnihotra regularly, Brahmacārins maintaining sacrificial fires, and those who stay amidst five fires become as splendrous as fire in the region of fire.

## \|\| 7.15.62 \|\|

*śīte śītāpanuttyai yastvedhobhārānprayacchati \|*  
*kuryyādagnīṣṭikāṃ vātha sa vasedagnisannidhau \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. He who makes gifts of bundles of fuel during winter for alleviating chillness or he who makes fire-bricks (of cow-dung) stays with fire.

## \|\| 7.15.63 \|\|

*anāthasyāgnisaṃskāraṃ yaḥ kuryyācchraddhayānvitaḥ \|*  
*aśaktaḥ prerayedanyaṃ sogniloke mahīyate \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. He who faithfully performs the rites of cremation for the unclaimed bodies or he who urges others to do so, himself being incapable, is honoured in the region of fire.

## \|\| 7.15.64 \|\|

*agnireko dvijātīnāṃ niśśreyasakaraḥ paraḥ \|*  
*gururdevo vrataṃ tīrthaṃ sarvamagnirviniścitam \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. Fire alone is the greatest means of salvation for the brahmins. He is the preceptor, the lord, the sacred rite, the holy centre. Certainly everything is fire.

## \|\| 7.15.65 \|\|

*apāvanāni sarvāṇi vahnisaṃsargataḥ kṣaṇāt \|*  
*pāvanāni bhavantyeva tasmādyaḥ pāvakaḥ smṛtaḥ \|\| 65 \|\|*

65\. All unholy things become pure the moment they come into contact with fire. Therefore fire is called Purifier,

## \|\| 7.15.66 \|\|

*antarātmā hyayaṃ sākṣānniścayo hyāśuśukṣaṇiḥ \|*  
*māṃsagrāsānpacetkukṣau strīṇāṃ no māṃsapeśikām \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. The fire is certainly the immanent soul of man. Even morsels of flesh it will digest in the stomach, but not the muscles of women.

## \|\| 7.15.67 \|\|

*taijasī śāmbhavī mūrttiḥ pratyakṣā dahanātmikā \|*  
*kartrī hartrī pālayitrī vinaitāṃ kiṃ vilokyate \|\| 67 \|\|*

67\. The fiery form of Śiva is in the form of the fire visible. Excepting this what else is seen as creative, protective and destructive?

## \|\| 7.15.68 \|\|

*citrabhānurayaṃ sākṣānnetrantribhuvaneśituḥ \|*  
*andhe tamomaye loke vinainaṃ kaḥ prakāśanaḥ \|\| 68 \|\|*

68\. Fire is the eye of the lord of the three worlds. In this world of pitch darkness what else than this becomes the illuminator?

## \|\| 7.15.69 \|\|

*dhūpapradīpanaivedyapayodadhighṛtaikṣavam \|*  
*etadbhuktaṃ niṣevante sarve divi divaukasaḥ \|\| 69 \|\|*

69\. The incense, the lights, the food offerings, milk, curd, ghee and sugarcane juice—all these go unto the dwellers of heaven when offered into the fire here.

## Chapter 16 - Śiva’s Incarnation as Yakṣeśvara

## \|\| 7.16.1 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*yakṣeśvarāvatāraṃ ca śṛṇu śaṃbhormunīśvara \|*  
*garviṇaṃ garvahantāraṃ satāmbhaktivivarddhanam \|\| 1 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

1\. O great sage, listen to the Yakṣeśvara incarnation of Śiva, that dispels the arrogance of the arrogant and lets the devotion of the good flourish.

## \|\| 7.16.2 \|\|

*purā devāśca daityāśca pīyūṣārthammahābalāḥ \|*  
*kṣīrodadhiṃ mamanthuste sukṛta svārtha sandhayaḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Formerly, the powerful gods and the Daityas motivated by the desire to gain their selfish ends churned the milk ocean for achieving nectar.

## \|\| 7.16.3 \|\|

*mathyamāne'mṛte pūrvaṃ kṣīrābdhessuradānavaiḥ \|*  
*agneḥ samutthitaṃ tasmādviṣaṃ kālānalaprabham \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. When the churning of the milk ocean started as the gods and demons wanted nectar, poison blazing as the fire of dissolution came out at first.

## \|\| 7.16.4 \|\|

*taṃ dṛṣṭvā nikhilā devā daityāśca bhayavihvalāḥ \|*  
*vidrutya tarasā tāta śaṃbhoste śaraṇaṃ yayuḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. O dear, at the sight of it, the gods and the daityas were highly agitated and frightened. They fled from there immediately and sought refuge in Śiva.

## \|\| 7.16.5 \|\|

*dṛṣṭvā taṃ śaṃkaraṃ sarve sarvadevaśikhāmaṇim \|*  
*praṇamya tuṣṭuvurbhaktyā sācyutā natamastakāḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. After seeing Śiva, the crestjewel of all the gods, all of them including Viṣṇu devoutly bent their heads and bowed to Viṣṇu with devotion.

## \|\| 7.16.6 \|\|

*tataḥ prasanno bhagavācchaṅkaro bhaktavatsalaḥ \|*  
*papau viṣaṃ mahāghoraṃ surāsuragaṇārdanam \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Then the delighted lord Śiva favourably disposed to the devotees, quaffed off the terrible poison afflicting gods and Asuras.

## \|\| 7.16.7 \|\|

*patiṃ taṃ viṣamaṃ kaṇṭhe nidadhe viṣamulbaṇam \|*  
*rejetenāti sa vibhurnīlakaṇṭho babhūva ha \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. The lord retained obliquely the acute poison that he drank, in his neck. He shone much thereby and became Blue-necked.

## \|\| 7.16.8 \|\|

*tataḥ surā suragaṇā mamanthuḥ punareva tam \|*  
*viṣadāhavinirmuktāḥ śivānugrahato'khilāḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Released from the burning sensation of the poison thanks to the blessings of Śiva, the gods and the Asuras began to churn again.

## \|\| 7.16.9 \|\|

*tāto bahūni ratnāni nissṛtāni tato mune \|*  
*amṛtaṃ ca padārthaṃ hi suradānavayormune \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. O sage, many gems came out of it, but O sage, it was the nectar that the gods and the Dānavas prized much.

## \|\| 7.16.10 \|\|

*tatpapuḥ kevalandevā nāsurāḥ kṛpayā hareḥ \|*  
*tato babhūva sumahadratnaṃ teṣāṃ mitho'kadam \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Not the Asuras, but the gods drank the nectar, thanks to the favour of Viṣṇu. Then a great battle ensued between them inflicting pain on both.

## \|\| 7.16.11 \|\|

*dvandvayuddhambabhūvātha devadānavayormune \|*  
*tatra rāhubhayāccandro vidudrāva tadarditaḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. A mutual clash occurred between the gods and the Dānavas, O sage. The moon was eclipsed by Rāhu and fled in terror of Rāhu.

## \|\| 7.16.12 \|\|

*jagāma sadanaṃ śaṃbhoḥ śaraṇambhaya vihvalaḥ \|*  
*supraṇamya ca tuṣṭāva pāhipāhīti saṃvadan \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. He went to the abode of Śiva extremely agitated by fear and sought Śiva’s protection. Saying “Protect me O Lord, protect me” he bowed to the lord and eulogised him.

## \|\| 7.16.13 \|\|

*tatassatāmabhayadaḥ śaṃkaro bhaktavatsalaḥ \|*  
*dadhre śirasi candraṃ sa vibhuśśaraṇamāgatam \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Lord Śiva, the bestower of protection to the good, and favourably disposed to his devotees, wore the moon on his head as the latter had sought refuge.

## \|\| 7.16.14 \|\|

*athāgatastadā rāhustuṣṭāva supraṇamya tam \|*  
*śaṃkaraṃ sakalādhīśaṃ vāgbhiriṣṭābhirādarāt \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Then Rāhu too, came there. He bowed to and eulogised lord Śiva with reverence by means of sweet words.

## \|\| 7.16.15 \|\|

*śaṃbhustanmatamājñāya tacchirāṃsyacyutena ha \|*  
*purā chinnāni vai ketusaṃjñāni nidadhe gale \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. Realising his desire, Śiva fixed on to his neck his head that had been formerly cut off by Viṣṇu, and that was known as Ketu.

## \|\| 7.16.16 \|\|

*tato yuddhe'surāḥ sarve devaiścaiva parājitāḥ \|*  
*pītvā'mṛtaṃ surāssarvve jayamprāpurmahābalāḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Then the Asuras were defeated by the gods in the battle. By drinking nectar the gods had become powerful and gained victory.

## \|\| 7.16.17 \|\|

*viṣṇuprabhṛtayaḥ sarvve babhūvuścātigarvitāḥ \|*  
*balāni cāṃkuraṃtontaśśivamāyāvimohitāḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Deluded by Śiva’s Māyā, Viṣṇu and others became very haughty. Their strength began to take root and sprout within them.

## \|\| 7.16.18 \|\|

*tatassa śaṃkaro devaḥ sarvādhīśotha garvahā \|*  
*yakṣo bhūtvā jagāmāśu yatra devāḥ sthitā mune \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. O sage, then Lord Śiva, the suppressor of arrogance, became a Yakṣa. He hastened to the place, O sage, where the gods were stationed.

## \|\| 7.16.19 \|\|

*sarvāndṛṣṭvācyutamukhāndevānyakṣapatissa vai \|*  
*mahāgarvāḍhyamanasā maheśāḥ prāha garvahā \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. On seeing Viṣṇu and all other gods arrogant in their minds, lord Śiva, the lord of Yakṣas and the suppressor of arrogance spoke.

## \|\| 7.16.20 \|\|

*yakṣeśvara uvāca \|*  
*kimarthaṃ saṃsthitā yūyamatra sarve surā mithaḥ \|*  
*kimu kāṣṭhākhilambrūta kāraṇaṃ menupṛcchate \|\| 20 \|\|*

Yakṣeśvara said:—

20\. O ye gods, why have you all assembled here. What is your goal? Tell me the reason as I ask unto you.

The gods said—

## \|\| 7.16.21 \|\|

*devā ūcuḥ \|*  
*abhūdatra mahāndeva raṇaḥ paramadāruṇaḥ \|*  
*asurā nāśitāssarve'vaśiṣṭā vidrutā gatāḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. O lord, a terrible battle took place here wherein all the Asuras were killed. The few that remained fled away.

## \|\| 7.16.22 \|\|

*vayaṃ sarve mahāvīrā daityaghnā balavattarāḥ \|*  
*agresmākaṃ kiyantaste daitya kṣudrabalāssadā \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. We are great heroes and powerful slayers of the Daityas. How insignificant in strength are those Daityas in front of us!

## \|\| 7.16.23 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*iti śrutvā vacasteṣāṃ surāṇāṃ garvagarbhitam \|*  
*garvahāsau mahādevo yakṣarūpo vaco'bravīt \|\| 23 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

23\. On hearing the arrogant words of those gods, lord Śiva, the suppressor of arrogance, in the form of Yakṣa said.

## \|\| 7.16.24 \|\|

*yakṣeśvara uvāca \|*  
*he surā nikhilā yūyaṃ madvacaśśṛṇutādarāt \|*  
*yathārthaṃ vacmi nāsatyaṃ sarvagarvāpahārakam \|\| 24 \|\|*

Yakṣeśvara said:—

24\. O ye gods, you listen to my words with attention. I shall tell you the truth that will dispel arrogance.

## \|\| 7.16.25 \|\|

*garvvamenaṃ na kuruta karttā harttā'paraḥ prabhuḥ \|*  
*vismṛtāśca maheśānaṃ kathayadhvamvṛthābalāḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Do not be arrogant. There is another lord who is the creator and annihilator. You have forgotten the great lord. You boast too much but you are devoid of strength.

## \|\| 7.16.26 \|\|

*yuṣmākañcetsa hi mado jānatāṃ svabalammahat \|*  
*matsthāpitaṃ tṛṇamidaṃ chinta svāstraiśca taissurāḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. If you are proud and think that you know your strength to be great, O gods, split this blade of grass placed by me, with your own weapons.

## \|\| 7.16.27 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityuktvaikatṛṇanteṣāṃ nicikṣepa purastataḥ \|*  
*jahre sarvamadaṃ yakṣarūpa īśassatāṃgatiḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

27\. Saying this, śiva in the form of Yakṣa, the goal of the good, placed a blade of grass before them and dispelled their arrogance.

## \|\| 7.16.28 \|\|

*atha sarve surā viṣṇupramukhā vīramāninaḥ \|*  
*kṛtvā svapauruṣantatra svāyudhāni vicikṣipuḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. Then Viṣṇu and other gods professing to be heroic exerted their manliness and hurled their respective weapons.

## \|\| 7.16.29 \|\|

*tatrāsan viphalānyāśu tānyastrāṇi divaukasām \|*  
*śivaprabhāvatasteṣāṃ mūḍhagarvāpahāriṇaḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. But the weapons of the gods, were rendered fruitless before the power of Śiva, the destroyer of the arrogance of the deluded.

## \|\| 7.16.30 \|\|

*athāsīttu nabhovāṇī devavismayahāriṇī \|*  
*yakṣo'yaṃ śaṃkaro devāḥ sarvagarvāpahārakaḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. Then a celestial voice arose that dispelled the surprise of the gods—“O gods this Yakṣa is Śiva who removes the arrogance of the arrogant.

## \|\| 7.16.31 \|\|

*kartā harttā tathā bharttā'yameva parameśvaraḥ \|*  
*etadbalena valino jīvāḥ sarve'nyathā na hi \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. The lord himself is the creator, sustainer and annihilator. The living beings are powerful thanks only to his power. Otherwise they are not powerful.

## \|\| 7.16.32 \|\|

*asya māyāprabhāvādvai mohitāḥ svaprabhuṃ śivam \|*  
*madato bubudhu naivādyāpi bodhatanumprabhum \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. Deluded by the power of his illusion you have become arrogant and have not realised lord śiva, who is the very knowledge embodied.

## \|\| 7.16.33 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*iti śrutvā nabhovāṇīṃ bubudhuste gatasmayāḥ \|*  
*yakṣeśvarampraṇemuśca tuṣṭuvuśca tamīśvaram \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. O hearing the celestial voice they became divested of their arrogance. They had realised Yakṣeśvara. They bowed to and eulogised him.

## \|\| 7.16.34 \|\|

*devā ūcuḥ \|*  
*devadeva mahādeva sarvagarvāpahāraka \|*  
*yakṣeśvaramahālīla māyā tetyadbhutā prabho \|\| 34 \|\|*

The gods said:—

34\. O lord of the gods, the dispeller of arrogance, O Yakṣeśvara of great sports, O lord, your Māyā is wonderful.

## \|\| 7.16.35 \|\|

*mohitā māyayādyāpi tava yakṣasvarūpiṇaḥ \|*  
*sagarvamabhibhāṣantastvatpuro hi pṛthaṅmayāḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. Even now we are deluded by this Māyā of yours in the form of Yakṣa. Hence, separated from you we began to speak arrogantly before you.

## \|\| 7.16.36 \|\|

*idānīṃ jñānamāyātantavaiva kṛpayā prabho \|*  
*kartā hartā ca bhartā ca tvamevānyo na śaṃkara \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. O lord, thanks to your grace, alone, knowledge has dawned on us. O Śiva, you alone are the creator, sustainer and annihilator, none else.

## \|\| 7.16.37 \|\|

*tvameva sarvaśaktīnāṃ sarveṣāṃ hi pravartakaḥ \|*  
*nivartakaśca sarveśaḥ paramātmāvyayo'dvayaḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

37, You alone are the initiator and the withdrawer of all powers. You are lord of all, the great soul, the unchanging, the unrivalled.

## \|\| 7.16.38 \|\|

*yakṣeśvarasvarūpeṇa sarveṣāṃ no mado hṛtaḥ \|*  
*ito manyāmahe tattenugraho hi kṛpālunā \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. In the form of Yakṣeśvara you have removed our arrogance. With sympathy and kindness we think we have been blessed by you.

## \|\| 7.16.39 \|\|

*atho sa yakṣanātho'nugṛhya vai sakalān surān \|*  
*vibodhya vividhairvākyaistatraivāntaradhīyata \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. Then the Yakṣanātha blessed the gods enlightened them with various instructions and vanished there itself.

## \|\| 7.16.40 \|\|

*itthaṃ sa varṇitaḥ śambhoravatāraḥ sukhāvahaḥ \|*  
*yakṣeśvarākhyassukhadassatāntuṣṭo'bhayaṃkaraḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. Thus has been described the pleasant incarnation of Śiva called Yakṣeśvara that bestows pleasure, satisfaction and protection of the good.

## \|\| 7.16.41 \|\|

*idamākhyānamamalaṃ sarvagarvāpahārakam \|*  
*satāṃ suśāntidannityaṃ bhuktimuktipradaṃ nṛṇām \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. The narrative is sacred; it dispels all arrogance; it bestows peace, worldly pleasure and salvation upon all good men.

## \|\| 7.16.42 \|\|

*ya idaṃ śṛṇuyādbhaktyā śrāvayedvā sudhīḥ pumān \|*  
*sarvakāmānavāpnoti tataśca labhate gatim \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. The intelligent man who listens to or narrates this with devotion attains all desires. Then he attains salvation.

## Chapter 17 - Ten incarnations of Śiva

## \|\| 7.17.1 \|\|

*śṛṇvatho giriśasyādyāvatārān daśasaṃkhyakān \|*  
*mahākalamukhān bhaktyopāsanākāṇḍasevitān \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Now listen to the first ten incarnations of Śiva beginning with that of Mahākāla. They are resorted to with devotion and explained in Upāsanākāṇḍa.

## \|\| 7.17.2 \|\|

*tatrādyo hi mahākālo bhuktimuktipradassatām \|*  
*śaktistatra mahākālī bhaktepsitaphalapradā \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. The first incarnation is that of Mahākāla, the bestower of worldly pleasures and liberation to the good. Śakti there, is Mahākālī who bestows the desired fruits on the devotees.

## \|\| 7.17.3 \|\|

*tāranāmā dvitīyaśca tārā śaktistathaiva sā \|*  
*bhuktimuktipradau cobhau svasevakasukhapradau \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. The second incarnation is called Tāra. Śakti there, is Tārā. Both of them confer worldly pleasures and salvation on their attendants.

## \|\| 7.17.4 \|\|

*bhuvaneśo hi bālāhvastṛtīyaḥ parikīrtitaḥ \|*  
*bhuvaneśī śivā tatra bālāhvā sukhadā satām \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. The lord of worlds called Bāla is glorified as the third incarnation. Śakti there, is Śivā that bestows pleasure on the good.

## \|\| 7.17.5 \|\|

*śrīvidyeśaḥ ṣoḍaśāhvaḥ śrīrvidyā ṣoḍaśī śivā \|*  
*caturtho bhakta sukhado bhuktimuktiphalapradaḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Śrīvidyeśa Ṣoḍaśa is the fourth incarnation and Śrīvidyā, Śivā Ṣoḍaśī is the Śakti. They confer pleasure on the devotees and yield worldly pleasures as well as salvation.

## \|\| 7.17.6 \|\|

*pañcamo bhairavaḥ khyātaḥ sarvadā bhaktakāmadaḥ \|*  
*bhairavī girijā tatra sadupāsakakāmadā \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. The fifth incarnation is famous as Bhairava, who bestows desires on the devotees. Śakti there is Bhairavī who bestows desires on good worshippers.

## \|\| 7.17.7 \|\|

*chinnamastakanāmāsau śivaḥ ṣaṣṭhaḥ prakīrtitaḥ \|*  
*bhaktakāmapradā caiva girijā chinnamastakā \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. The sixth incarnation of Śiva is glorified as Chinnamastaka who confers desires. Śakti there, is Dhūmāvatī who bestows all desires on the devotees.

## \|\| 7.17.8 \|\|

*dhūmavān saptamaḥ śambhussarvakāmaphalapradaḥ \|*  
*dhūmavatī śivā tatra sadupāsakakāmadā \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. The seventh incarnation of Śiva is Dhūmavān, the bestower of the fruits of all desires. Śaktit here, is Dhūmāvatī who bestows all desires on the good worshippers.

## \|\| 7.17.9 \|\|

*śivāvatāraḥ sukhado hyaṣṭamo bagalāmukhaḥ \|*  
*śaktistatra mahānandā vikhyātā bagalāmukhī \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. The eighth pleasant incarnation of Śiva is Bagalāmukha. Śakti there, is Bagalāmukhī who is also famous as Mahānandā.

## \|\| 7.17.10 \|\|

*śivāvatāro mātaṅgo navamaḥ parikīrtitaḥ \|*  
*mātaṃgī tatra śarvāṇī sarvakāmaphalapradā \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. The ninth incarnation of Śiva is glorified as Mātaṅga. Śakti there, is Mātaṅgī who bestows the fruits of all desires.

## \|\| 7.17.11 \|\|

*daśamaḥ kamalaḥ śambhurbhuktimuktiphalapradaḥ \|*  
*kamalā girijā tatra svabhaktaparipālinī \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. The tenth incarnation of Śiva is Kamala, the bestower of fruits of worldly pleasures and salvation. Śakti there, is Kamalā, the protectress of her own devotees.

## \|\| 7.17.12 \|\|

*ete daśamitāḥ śaivā avatārāssukhapradāḥ \|*  
*bhuktimuktipradāścaiva bhaktānāṃ sarvadāssatām \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. The ten incarnations of Śiva are pleasing as they confer worldly pleasures and salvation. They bestow everything on good devotees.

## \|\| 7.17.13 \|\|

*ete daśāvatārā hi śaṃkarasya mahātmanaḥ \|*  
*nānāsukhapradā nityaṃ sevatāṃ nirvikārataḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. These ten incarnations of Śiva the great soul bestow different kinds of pleasures on those who always resort to them without aberrations.

## \|\| 7.17.14 \|\|

*etaddaśāvatārāṇāṃ māhātmyaṃ varṇitaṃ mune \|*  
*sarvakāmapradaṃ jñeyaṃ taṃtraśāstrādigarbhitam \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. O sage, the glory of the ten incarnations of Śiva has thus been described. It bestows all desires as explained in the Tantraśāstra and other treatises.

## \|\| 7.17.15 \|\|

*etāsāmādiśaktīnāmadbhuto mahimā mune \|*  
*sarvakāmaprado jñeyastatraṃśāstrādigarbhitaḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. O sage, the greatness of these Śaktis too is wonderful. It bestows all desires and shall be known as explained in Tantraśāstra and other treatises.

## \|\| 7.17.16 \|\|

*śatrumāraṇakāryyādau tattacchaktiḥ parā matā \|*  
*khala daṇḍakarī nityambrahmatejovivarddhinī \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. The different Śaktis are considered in such activities as destroying enemies etc. They punish the wicked and enhance brahminical splendour.

## \|\| 7.17.17 \|\|

*ityuktāste mayā brahmannavatārā maheśituḥ \|*  
*saśaktikā daśamitā mahākālamukhāśśubhāḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. O Brahmin, thus I have explained to you the ten incarnations of Śiva along with his Śakti, beginning with Mahākāla. They are all auspicious.

## \|\| 7.17.18 \|\|

*śaivaparvasu sarveṣu yo'dhīte bhaktitatparaḥ \|*  
*etadākhyānamamalaṃ sotiśambhupriyo bhavet \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. He who reads this sacred narrative during the festivals of Śiva, with great devotion becomes a great favourite of Śiva.

## \|\| 7.17.19 \|\|

*brahmaṇo brahmavarcasvī kṣatriyo vijayī bhaveta \|*  
*dhanādhipo hi vaiśyaḥ syācchūdraḥ sukhamavāpnuyāt \|\| 19 \|\|*
