19\. A brahmin attains the sacred splendour of Brahman; Kṣatriya becomes victorious; a Vaiśya becomes lord of wealth and a Śūdra attains happiness.

## \|\| 7.17.20 \|\|

*śāṃkarā nijadharmasthāḥ śṛṇvantaścaritantvidam \|*  
*sukhinaḥ syurviśeṣeṇa śivabhaktā bhavantu ca \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. The devotees of Śiva maintaining their respective duties and listening to this narrative become especially happy and devoted to Śiva.

## Chapter 18 - Śiva’s Eleven Incarnations

## \|\| 7.18.1 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ekādaśavatārānvai śṛṇvatho śāṃkarānvarān \|*  
*yāñchrutvā na hi bādhyeta bādhāsatyādisambhavā \|\| 1 \|\|*

Śiva said:—

1\. Listen to the eleven excellent incarnations of Śiva on hearing which obstacles due to intimate contact with worldly objects etc. do not torment.

## \|\| 7.18.2 \|\|

*purā sarve surāśśakramukhā daityaparājitāḥ \|*  
*tyaktvāmarāvatīmbhītyā'palāyanta nijāmpurīm \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Formerly Indra and other gods defeated by the Asuras left their capital Amarāvatī due to fear.

## \|\| 7.18.3 \|\|

*daityaprapīḍitā devā jagmuste kaśyapā ntikam \|*  
*baddhvā karānnataskandhāḥ praṇemustaṃ suvihvalam \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. The gods harrassed by the Daityas went to Kaśyapa. Highly agitated, they bowed down to him with palms joined and shoulders drooping.

## \|\| 7.18.4 \|\|

*sunutvā taṃ surāssarvve kṛtvā vijñaptimādarāt \|*  
*sarvaṃ nivedayāmā ssvaduḥkhantatparājayam \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. After eulogising well, the gods made the submission respectfully. They intimated to him their misery due to that defeat.

## \|\| 7.18.5 \|\|

*tatassa kaśyapastāta tatpitā śivasaktadhīḥ \|*  
*tadākarṇyāmarākaṃ vai dukhitobhūnna cādhikam \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. O dear, on hearing the misery of the gods their father Kaśyapa whose mind was attached to Śiva, was much distressed.

## \|\| 7.18.6 \|\|

*tānāśvāsya munisso'tha dhairyamādhāya śāntadhīḥ \|*  
*kāśīṃ jagāma suprītyā viśveśvarapurīmmune \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. O sage, the sage consoled and encouraged them. With his mind regaining calmness he joyously went to Kāśī presided over by lord Viśveśvara.

## \|\| 7.18.7 \|\|

*gaṃgāmbhasi tataḥ snātvā kṛtvā taṃ vidhimādarāt \|*  
*viśveśvaraṃ samānarca sāmbaṃ sarveśvaramprabhum \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Taking his ceremonial bath in the waters of the Gaṅgā and performing due rites, he worshipped Lord Viśveśvara, lord of the universe, accompanied by Pārvatī.

## \|\| 7.18.8 \|\|

*śivaliṃgaṃ susaṃsthāpya cakāra vipulantapaḥ \|*  
*śambhumuddiśya suprītyā devānāṃ hitakāmyayā \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. With the desire for the benefit of the gods he joyously installed Śivaliṅga and performed an elaborate penance with Śiva as the object of pursuit.

## \|\| 7.18.9 \|\|

*mahānkālo vyatīyāya tapatastasya vai muneḥ \|*  
*śivapādāmbujāsaktamanaso dhairyyaśālinaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. A long time elapsed, O sage, as he boldly performed penance with his mind attached to the lotus-like feet of Śiva

## \|\| 7.18.10 \|\|

*atha prādurabhūcchambhurvarandātuntadarṣaye \|*  
*svapadāsaktamanase dīnabandhussatāṃgatiḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. In order to grant the boon to that sage whose mind was devoted to his feet, Śiva, kinsman of the distressed and goal of the good, appeared in front of him.

## \|\| 7.18.11 \|\|

*varambrūhīti covāca suprasanno maheśvaraḥ \|*  
*kaśyapaṃ muniśārdūlaṃ svabhaktaṃ bhaktavatsalaḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. The delighted Śiva who is favourably disposed towards his devotees said to Kaśyapa, the best of his devotees, to ask for the boon.

## \|\| 7.18.12 \|\|

*dṛṣṭvātha taṃ maheśānaṃ sa praṇamya kṛtāñjaliḥ \|*  
*tuṣṭāva kaśyapo hṛṣṭo devatātaḥ prasa nnadhīḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. On seeing lord Śiva, the delighted Kaśyapa bowed to him with palms joined in reverence. Kaśyapa, the progenitor of the gods, eulogised him with contentment in his mind.

Kaśyapa said

## \|\| 7.18.13 \|\|

*kaśyapa uvāca \|*  
*devadeva maheśāna śaraṇāgatavatsala \|*  
*sarveśvaraḥ parātmā tvaṃ dhyānagamyodvayo'vyayaḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. O lord Śiva, lord of the gods, favourably disposed towards those who seek refuge in you, you are the lord of all, the great soul, achievable only through meditation, the unequalled and unchanging.

## \|\| 7.18.14 \|\|

*balanigraha kartā tvaṃ maheśvara satāṃ gatiḥ \|*  
*dīnabandhurdayāsindhurbhaktarakṣaṇadakṣadhīḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. O lord Śiva, you are the restrainer of evil forces. You are the goal of the good, kinsman of the distressed, ocean of mercy and intellectually efficient in protecting your devotees.

## \|\| 7.18.15 \|\|

*ete surāstvadīyā hi tvadbhaktāśca viśeṣataḥ \|*  
*daityaiḥ parājitāścātha pāhi tānduḥkhitān prabho \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. O lord, these gods are your own. They are especially devoted to you. Now they are defeated by the Asuras. Save them from distress.

## \|\| 7.18.16 \|\|

*asamartho rameśopi duḥkhadaste muhurmuhuḥ \|*  
*ataḥ surā maccharaṇā vedayanto'sukhaṃ ca tat \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Even Viṣṇu cannot move in this matter. He has frequently pressed you for this job. The gods have sought refuge in me intimating their misfortune.

## \|\| 7.18.17 \|\|

*tadarthaṃ devadeveśa devaduḥkhavināśakaḥ \|*  
*tatpūrituṃ taponiṣṭhāṃ prasannārthaṃ tavāsadam \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. O lord of the chiefs of the gods, O destroyer of the miseries of the godi, it is for this that I have performed this penance and resorted to you. My purpose has now been fulfilled by you since you are pleased.

## \|\| 7.18.18 \|\|

*śaraṇaṃ te prapanno'smi sarvathāhaṃ maheśvara \|*  
*kāmaṃ me pūraya svāmindevaduḥkhaṃ vināśaya \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. O lord, I have sought refuge in you by all means O lord, please fulfill my desire. Please dispel the misery of the gods.

## \|\| 7.18.19 \|\|

*putraduḥkhaiśca deveśa duḥkhito'haṃ viśeṣataḥ \|*  
*sukhinaṃ kuru māmīśa sahāya stvandivaukasām \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. O lord of gods, I too am much distressed at the misfortune of my sons. O lord, make me happy. You are the source of succour to the heaven-dwellers.

## \|\| 7.18.20 \|\|

*bhūtvā mama suto nātha devā yakṣāḥ parājitāḥ \|*  
*daityairmahābalaiśśambho surānandaprado bhava \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. O lord Śiva, the gods and the Yakṣas, born as my sons, have been defeated by the powerful Asuras. Be the bestower of bliss on the gods.

## \|\| 7.18.21 \|\|

*sadaivāstu maheśāna sarvalekhasahāyakaḥ \|*  
*yathā daityakṛtā bādhā na bādheta surānprabho \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. O lord Śiva, do help the gods always lest the harassment by the Asuras should afflict them.

## \|\| 7.18.22 \|\|

*naṃdīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityuktassa tu sarveśastatheti procya śaṃkaraḥ \|*  
*paśyatastasya bhagavāṃstatraivāṃtardadhe haraḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

22\. On being thus requested, lord, Śiva said “So be it” and?ven as he was watching he vanished there.

## \|\| 7.18.23 \|\|

*kaśyapo'pi mahāhṛṣṭaḥ svasthānamagamaddrutam \|*  
*deveśaḥ kathayāmāsa sarvavṛttāntamādarāt \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Kaśyapa too was delighted. Immediately he returned to his abode. He eagerly intimated to the gods all the details.

## \|\| 7.18.24 \|\|

*tatassa śaṃkaraśśarvassatyaṃ kartuṃ svakaṃ vacaḥ \|*  
*surabhyāṃ kaśyapājjajñe ekādaśasvarūpavān \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. Then, in order to keep his promise Śiva was born of Surabhi assuming eleven forms.

## \|\| 7.18.25 \|\|

*mahotsavastadāsīdve sarvaṃ śivamayaṃ tvabhūt \|*  
*āsanhṛṣṭāḥ surāścātha muninā kaśyapena ca \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. There was great jubilation then. Everything became auspicious. The gods and the sage Kaśyapa became delighted.

## \|\| 7.18.26-27 \|\|

*kapālī 1 piṃgalo 2 bhīmo 3 virūpākṣo 4 vilohitaḥ \|*  
*śāstā' 6 japāda 7 hirbudhnya 8 śśaṃbhu 9 ścaṇḍo 10 bhavastathā 11 \|\| 26 \|\|*

*ekādaśaite rudrāstu surabhatinayāḥ smṛtāḥ \|*  
*devakāryyārthamutpannāśśivarūpāssukhāspadam \|\| 27 \|\|*

26-27. These are the eleven Rudras born of Surabhī (1) Kapālin (2) Piṅgala (3) Bhīma (4) Virūpākṣa (5) Vilohita (6) Śāstṛ (7) Ajapād (8) Ahirbudhnya (9) Śiva (10) Caṇḍa and (11) Bhava. They were born for the aid to the gods. They are identical with Śiva and the cause of happiness.

## \|\| 7.18.28 \|\|

*te rudrāḥ kāśyapā vīrā mahābalaparākramāḥ \|*  
*daityāñjaghnuśca saṃgrāme devasāhāyyakāriṇaḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. Those Rudras, born of Kaśyapa, rendering help to the gods, slew the Daityas in the battle. They were heroes of great strength and exploit.

## \|\| 7.18.29 \|\|

*tadrudrakṛpayā devā daityāñjitvā ca nirbhayāḥ \|*  
*cakrusvarājyaṃ sarve te śakrādyāssvasthamānasāḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. By the grace of those Rudras, the gods conquered the Asuras and were rid of fear. Indra and other gods had peace of mind and ruled over their own kingdom.

## \|\| 7.18.30 \|\|

*adyāpi te mahārudrāssarve śivasvarūpakāḥ \|*  
*devānāṃ rakṣaṇārthāya virājante sadā divi \|\| 30 \|\|*

30 Even today the great Rudras, identical with Śiva, shine always in the sky for the protection of the gods.

## \|\| 7.18.31 \|\|

*aiśānyāmpuri te vāsaṃ cakrire bhaktavatsalāḥ \|*  
*viramante sadā tatra nānālīlāviśāradāḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. Those Rudras favourably disposed towards their devotees took up their residence in the north-eastern city of Īśāna. Experts in different kinds of sports they sport about there always.

## \|\| 7.18.32 \|\|

*teṣāmanucarā rudrāḥ koṭiśaḥ parikīrtitāḥ \|*  
*sarvatra saṃsthitāstatra trilokeṣvabhibhāgaśaḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. The Rudras glorified as their followers and retainers are crores in number. They are stationed everywhere in the three worlds, undividedly.

## \|\| 7.18.33 \|\|

*iti te varṇitāstātāvatārāśśaṃkarasya vai \|*  
*ekādaśamitā rudrāssarvalokasukhāvahāḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. O dear, thus the eleven Rudra incarnations of Śiva, pleasing to all the worlds have been described to you.

## \|\| 7.18.34 \|\|

*idamākhyānamamalaṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśakam \|*  
*dhanyaṃ yaśasyamāyuṣyaṃ sarvakāmapradāyakam \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. This narrative is pure, it destroys all sins. It is conducive to wealth, fame and longevity. It yields all desires.

## \|\| 7.18.35 \|\|

*ya idaṃ śṛṇuyāttāta śrāvayedvai samāhitaḥ \|*  
*iha sarvasukhambhuktvā tato muktiṃ labheta saḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. He who listens to or reads this with pious heart enjoys all pleasures here and attains liberation hereafter.

## Chapter 19 - The Narrative of Durvāsas

## \|\| 7.19.1 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*athānyaccaritaṃ śambho śśṛṇu prītyā mahāmune \|*  
*yathā babhūva durvāsāśśaṃkaro dharmahetave \|\| 1 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

1\. O great sage, now listen lovingly to another story of Śiva, how Śiva was born as Durvāsas for the maintenance of virtue.

## \|\| 7.19.2 \|\|

*brahmaputro vabhūvātitapasvī brahmavitprabhuḥ \|*  
*anasūyāpati rdhīmānbrahmājñāpratipālakaḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Atri, son of Brahmā, was a great sage. He was holy and a knower of Brahman. He was the husband of Anasūyā. He was intelligent and he strictly followed the orders of Brahmā.

## \|\| 7.19.3 \|\|

*sunirdeśādbrahmaṇo hi sastrīkaḥ putrakāmyayā \|*  
*sa tryakṣakulanāmānaṃ yayau ca tapase girim \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. At the direction of Brahmā he went to the mountain Ṛkṣakula accompanied by his wife in order to perform penance there, with the desire to get son.

## \|\| 7.19.4 \|\|

*prāṇānāyamya vidhivannirvindhyātaṭinītaṭe \|*  
*tapaścacāra sumahadadvandvo'bdaśatammuniḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. On the banks of the river Nirvindhyā, the sage who had controlled his breath and had freed himself from mutually clashing emotions performed a great penance for a hundred years.

## \|\| 7.19.5 \|\|

*ya eka īśvaraḥ kaścidavikāro mahāprabhuḥ \|*  
*sa me putravaraṃ dadyāditi niścitamānasaḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. He decided in his mind thus—, “let that sole great lord, whoever he is, who is free from aberrations, grant me the boon of a son.”

## \|\| 7.19.6 \|\|

*bahukālo vyatīyāya tasmiṃstapati sattapaḥ \|*  
*āvirbabhūva tatkāttu śucirjvālā mahīyasī \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. A long time elapsed while he performed the holy penance. A holy flame of fire came out of him.

## \|\| 7.19.7 \|\|

*tayā sannikhilā lokā dagdhaprāyā munīśvarāḥ \|*  
*tathā surarṣayaḥ sarve pīḍitā vāsavādayaḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. O great sage, the worlds were almost burnt by that flame. Indra and other gods and the celestial sages too were afflicted by it.

## \|\| 7.19.8 \|\|

*atha sarve vāsavādyā surāśca munayo mune \|*  
*brahmasthānaṃ yayuśśīghraṃ tajjvālātiprapīḍitāḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Then, highly distressed by that flame, O sage, Indra and other gods and the sages hastened to the abode of Brahmā.

## \|\| 7.19.9 \|\|

*natvā nutvā vidhindevāstatsvaduḥkhannyavedayan \|*  
*brahmā saha suraistāta viṣṇulokaṃ yayāvaram \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. After bowing to and eulogising Brahmā the gods mentioned to him their grief. O dear, Brahmā immediately went to Viṣṇu’s region along with the god.

## \|\| 7.19.10 \|\|

*tatra gatvā ramānāthaṃ natvā nutvā vidhissuraiḥ \|*  
*svaduḥkhantatsamācakhyau viṣṇave'nantakaṃ mune \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. O sage, after reaching there along with the gods Brahmā bowed to and eulogised Viṣṇu and mentioned his endless grief to him.

## \|\| 7.19.11 \|\|

*viṣṇuśca vidhinā devai rudrasthānaṃ yayau drutam \|*  
*haraṃ praṇamya tatretya tuṣṭāva parameśvaram \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Viṣṇu too, accompanied by Brahmā and the gods hastened to Rudra’s abode. After reaching it he bowed to and eulogised lord Śiva.

## \|\| 7.19.12 \|\|

*stutvā bahutayā viṣṇuṃ svaduḥkhaṃ ca nyavedayat \|*  
*śarvaṃ jvālāsamudbhūtamatreśca tapasaḥ param \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. After eulogising him Viṣṇu mentioned to Śiva his grief caused by the flame due to the penance of Atri.

## \|\| 7.19.13 \|\|

*atha tatra samestāstu brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarāḥ \|*  
*mune saṃmantrayāñcakruranyonyaṃ jagatāṃ hitam \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. O sage, thus gathering there, together, Brahmā, Viṣṇu and Śiva consulted one another about the welfare of the worlds.

## \|\| 7.19.14 \|\|

*tadā brahmādayo devāstrayaste varadarṣabhāḥ \|*  
*jagmustadāśramaṃ śīghraṃ varandātuntadarṣaye \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. The three deities Brahmā and others, foremost among the bestowers of boons, went to the hermitage of Atri immediately to grant him the boon.

## \|\| 7.19.15 \|\|

*svacihnacihnitāṃstānsa dṛṣṭvātrirmunisattamaḥ \|*  
*praṇanāma ca tuṣṭāva vāgbhiriṣṭābhirādarāt \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. On seeing them marked by their respective characteristic signs, the excellent sage Atri bowed to and eulogised them respectfully by uttering pleasing words.

## \|\| 7.19.16 \|\|

*tatassa vismito viprastānuvāca kṛtāñjaliḥ \|*  
*brahmaputro vinītātmā brahmaviṣṇuharābhidhān \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. The surprised brahmin, the humble son of Brahmā spoke to those deities Brahmā, Viṣṇu and Śiva, with palms joined in reverence.

## \|\| 7.19.17 \|\|

*atriruvāca \|*  
*he brahman he hare rudra pūjyāstrijagatāmmatāḥ \|*  
*prabhavaśceśvarāḥ sṛṣṭirakṣāsaṃhārakārakāḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

Atri said:—

17\. O Brahmā, O Viṣṇu, O Śiva, you are worthy of being worshipped by the three worlds. You are lords and masters and the cause of creation, maintenance and annihilation.

## \|\| 7.19.18 \|\|

*eka eva mayā dhyāta īśvaraḥ putrahetave \|*  
*yaḥ kaścidīśvaraḥ khyāto jagatāṃ svastriyā saha \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. Accompanied by my wife, I meditated only on a single lord for obtaining a son, that lord whoever he may be, who is famous as Īśvara.

## \|\| 7.19.19 \|\|

*yūyaṃ trayasturāḥ kasmādāgatā varadarṣabhāḥ \|*  
*etanme saṃśayaṃ chittvā tato dattepsitaṃ varam \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. How is it that you three deities, the foremost among the bestowers of boons have come here? Please dispel my doubt and then grant me the desired boon.

## \|\| 7.19.20 \|\|

*iti śrutvā vacastasya pratyūcuste surāstrayaḥ \|*  
*yādṛkkṛtaste saṃkalpastathaivābhūnmunīśvara \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. On hearing his words, the three deities replied “O great sage, what has occurred is in accordance with what you have conceived.

## \|\| 7.19.21 \|\|

*vayaṃ trayo bhaveśānāssamānā varadarṣabhāḥ \|*  
*asmadaṃśabhavāstasmādbhaviṣyanti sutāstrayaḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. We three are great lords and equally leading among the granters of boons. Hence three sons originating from our parts will be born to you.

## \|\| 7.19.22 \|\|

*viditā bhuvane sarve pitroḥ kīrtivivarddhanāḥ \|*  
*ityuktāste trayo devāssvadhāmāni yayurmudā \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. They will be known in the world as enhancers of their parent’s reputation.” After saying this, the three deities returned to their abodes joyously.

## \|\| 7.19.23 \|\|

*varaṃ labdhvā munisso'tha jagāma svāśramaṃ mudā \|*  
*yuto'nusūyayā prīto brahmānaṃdaprado mune \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. After securing the boon, the sage joyously returned to his hermitage accompanied by Anasūyā. O sage, he was delighted and he experienced the bliss of the supreme Brahman.

## \|\| 7.19.24 \|\|

*atha brahmā hariśśambhuravateruḥ striyāṃ tataḥ \|*  
*putrarūpaiḥ prasannātmanānālīlā prakāśakāḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. Then Brahma, Viṣṇu and Śiva, the delighted exponents of different kinds of sports, took their incarnations as the sons of his wife.

## \|\| 7.19.25 \|\|

*vidheraṃśādvidhurjajñe'nasūyāyāṃ munīśvarāt \|*  
*āvirbabhūvodadhitaḥ śipto devessa eva hi \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. The part of Brahmā was born as the moon in Anasūyā from the great sage. Hurled by the gods into the ocean he alone could come out of it.

## \|\| 7.19.26 \|\|

*viṣṇoraṃśātstriyāntasyāmatrerdatto vyajāyata \|*  
*saṃnyāsapaddhatiryena varddhitā paramā mune \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. O sage, the part of Viṣṇu was born of that lady, wife of Atri, as Datta by whom the great path of renunciation was expanded.

## \|\| 7.19.27 \|\|

*durvāsā muniśārdūlaḥ śivāṃśānmunisattamaḥ \|*  
*jajñe tasyāṃ striyāmatrervaradharmapravartakaḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. The part of Śiva was born of that wife of Atri as the excellent and leading sage Durvāsas who propagated excellent virtue.

## \|\| 7.19.28 \|\|

*bhūtvā rudraśca durvāsā brahmatejovivarddhanaḥ \|*  
*cakre dharmaparīkṣāñca bahūnāṃ sa dayāparaḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. Siva by becoming Durvāsas, the enhancer of Brahminical splendour, tested the piety of many people. He was merciful.

## \|\| 7.19.29 \|\|

*sūryavaṃśe samutpanno yo'mbarīṣo nṛpo'bhavat \|*  
*tatparīkṣāmakārṣītsa tāṃ śṛṇu tvaṃ munīśvara \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. The king Ambarīṣa, a scion of the solar dynasty, was tested by him. O great sage, listen to that story.

## \|\| 7.19.30 \|\|

*so'mbarīṣo nṛpavaraḥ saptadvīparasāpatiḥ \|*  
*niyamaṃ hi cakārāsāvekādaśyā vrate dṛḍham \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. The king Ambarīṣa was the lord of the earth consisting of seven continents. He observed the rite of Ekādaśī regularly with steady restraint.

## \|\| 7.19.31 \|\|

*ekādaśyā vrataṃ kṛtvā dvādaśyāṃ caiva pāraṇām \|*  
*kariṣyāmīti sudṛḍhasaṃkalpastu narādhipaḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. The king had the firm resolve that he would perform the rite of breaking fast on the Dvādaśī day.

## \|\| 7.19.32 \|\|

*jñātvā tanniyamantasya durvāsā munisattamaḥ \|*  
*tadantikaṃ gataśśiṣyairbahubhiśśaṃkarāṃśajaḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. Coming to know of his regular observance the excellent sage Durvāsas, born of Śiva’s part approached him with many disciples.

## \|\| 7.19.33 \|\|

*pāraṇe dvādaśīṃ svalpāṃ jñātvā yāvatsa bhojanam \|*  
*karttuṃ vyavasitastāvadāgataṃ sa nyamantrayat \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. The king was about to take his breakfast realising that very little of Dvādaśī was left on that day when the sage came there. The king invited him for food.

## \|\| 7.19.34 \|\|

*tataḥ snānārthamagamaddurvāsāḥ śiṣyasaṃyutaḥ \|*  
*vilambaṃ kṛtavāṃstatra parīkṣārthaṃ munirbahu \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. Then the sage Durvāsas went away for his ceremonial ablution, accompanied by his disciples In order to test the king, the sage purposely delayed there.

## \|\| 7.19.35 \|\|

*dharmavighnaṃ tadā jñātvā sa nṛpaḥ śāstraśāsanāt \|*  
*jalamprāśyāsthitastatra tadāgamanakāṃkṣayā \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. Realising that there would be violation of sacred injunction otherwise, the king took in a small quantity of water and waited for the arrival of the sage.

## \|\| 7.19.36-37 \|\|

*etasminnantare tatra durvāsā munirāgataḥ \|*  
*kṛtāśanaṃ nṛpaṃ jñātvā parīkṣārthaṃ dhṛtākṛtiḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

*cukrodhāti nṛpe tasminparīkṣārthaṃ vṛṣasya saḥ \|*  
*provāca vacanantūgraṃ sa muniśśaṃkarāṃśajaḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

36-37. In the meantime the sage returned. Knowing that the king had already had his breakfast, the sage, born of Śiva’s part, who had assumed that form for the testing of piety, became very furious with the king. He spoke very harsh words for testing his piety.

## \|\| 7.19.38 \|\|

*durvāsā uvāca \|*  
*māṃ nimantrya nṛpābhojya jalaṃ pītantvayādhama \|*  
*darśayāmi phalaṃ tasya duṣṭadaṇḍadharo hyaham \|\| 38 \|\|*

Durvāsas said:—

38\. O base king, after inviting but without feeding me you have taken in water. I shall show you the fruit thereof. For I am the chastiser of the wicked.

## \|\| 7.19.39-40 \|\|

*ityuktvā krodhatāmrākṣo nṛpaṃ dagdhuṃ samudyataḥ \|*  
*samuttasthau drutaṃ cakraṃ tatsthaṃ rakṣārthamaiśvaram \|\| 39 \|\|*

*prajajvālāti taṃ cakraṃ muniṃ dagdhuṃ sudarśanam \|*  
*śivarūpaṃ tamajñātvā śivamāyāvimohitam \|\| 40 \|\|*

39-40. After saying this he stood up, his eyes turned red due to fury and attempted to burn him. Immediately, Sudarśana the discus of the lord blazed forth to protect the king anrd burn the sage unmindful of the fact that he was the part of Śiva. It had been deluded by the Māyā of Śiva.

## \|\| 7.19.41 \|\|

*etasminnantare vyomavāṇyuvācāśarīriṇī \|*  
*ambarīṣammahātmānaṃ brahmabhaktaṃ ca vaiṣṇavam \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. In the meantime an unembodied celestial voice spoke to Ambarīṣa, the noble soul, the devotee of Brahmā and Viṣṇu.

## \|\| 7.19.42 \|\|

*vyomavāṇyuvāca \|*  
*sudarśanamidaṃ cakraṃ haraye śambhunārpitam \|*  
*śāṃtaṃ kuru prajvalitamadya durvāsase nṛpa \|\| 42 \|\|*

The celestial voice said:—

42\. O king, this discus had been given to Viṣṇu by Śiva. It is now blazing against Durvāsas. Quieten it.

## \|\| 7.19.43 \|\|

*durvāsāyaṃ śivaḥ sākṣātsa cakraṃ haraye'rpitam \|*  
*evaṃ sādhāraṇamuniṃ na jānīhi nṛpottama \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. This sage Durvāsas is Śiva himself whose discus had been given to Viṣṇu. O excelent king, do not take him for an ordinary sage.

## \|\| 7.19.44 \|\|

*tava dharmaparīkṣārthamāgato'yaṃ munīśvaraḥ \|*  
*śaraṇaṃ yāhi tasyāśu bhaviṣyatyanyathā layaḥ \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. It was to test your piety that this great sage came. Seek refuge in him. Otherwise there will be a great destruction.

## \|\| 7.19.45 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityuktvā ca nabhovāṇī virarāma munīśvara \|*  
*astāvītsa harāṃśaṃ tamambarīṣo'pi cādarāt \|\| 45 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

45\. O great sage, after saying thus, the celestial voice stopped. Ambarīṣa too eulogised the sage who was the part of Śiva himself.

## \|\| 7.19.46 \|\|

*ambarīṣa uvāca \|*  
*yadyasti dattamiṣṭaṃ ca svadharmo vā svanuṣṭhitaḥ \|*  
*kulaṃ no vipradaivaṃ ceddharerastraṃ praśāmyatu \|\| 46 \|\|*

Ambarīṣa said:—

46\. If I possess the merit of charity and sacrifice, if my spiritual duty has been well performed by me, and if our family considers brahmins godly, let the weapon of Viṣṇu turn calm.

## \|\| 7.19.47 \|\|

*yadi no bhagavānprīto madbhakto bhaktavatsalaḥ \|*  
*sudarśanamidaṃ cāstraṃ praśāmyatu viśeṣataḥ \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. If our lord, favourably disposed to his devotees, is delighted with my devotion let this missile Sudarśana become quite calm.

## \|\| 7.19.48-49 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*iti stuvati rudrāgre śaivaṃ cakraṃ sudarśanam \|*  
*aśāmyatsarvathā jñātvā taṃ śivāṃśaṃ sulabdhadhīḥ \|\| 48 \|\|*

*athāmbarīṣassa nṛpaḥ praṇanāma ca taṃ munim \|*  
*śivāvatāraṃ saṃjñāya svaparīkṣārthamāgatam \|\| 49 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

48-49. While be eulogised in front of Durvāsas, the discus Sudarśana that originally belonged to Śiva became calm in all respects. Knowing him to be Śiva’s part, the king Ambarīṣa, who had regained the composure of mind bowed to the sage whom he realised as Śiva’s incarnation come there to test his vow.

## \|\| 7.19.50 \|\|

*suprasanno babhūvātha sa muniḥ śaṃkarāṃśajaḥ \|*  
*bhuktvā tasmai varaṃ dattvā svābhīṣṭaṃ svālayaṃ yayau \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. The sage, born of Śiva’s part, was much delighted. He took his meals and granted him the desired boon. Then he returned to his abode.

## \|\| 7.19.51 \|\|

*ambarīṣaparīkṣāyāṃ durvāsaścaritammune \|*  
*proktāmanyaccaritrantvaṃ śṛṇu tasya munīśvara \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. O sage, I have narrated the story of Durvāsas in the course of his test of Ambarīṣa. O great sage, listen to another story of his.

## \|\| 7.19.52 \|\|

*punardāśaratheścakre parīkṣāṃ niyamena vai \|*  
*munirūpeṇa kālena yaḥ kṛto niyamo mune \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. O sage, through the condition stipulated by Kāla in the form of a sage he conducted the test of Rāma, son of Daśaratha.

## \|\| 7.19.53 \|\|

*tadaiva muninā tena saumitriḥ preṣito haṭhāt \|*  
*tantatyāja drutaṃ rāmo bandhuṃ paṇavaśānmune \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. At the very same time Lakṣmaṇa was obstinately sent in by Durvāsas. O sage, Rāma immediately forsook his brother in accordance with the earlier agreement.

## \|\| 7.19.54 \|\|

*sā kathā vihitā loke munibhirbahudhoditā \|*  
*nāto me vistarātproktā jñātā yatsarvadhā budhaiḥ \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. The story has been diversely narrated and spread in the world by the sages. It is known by all means to the scholars. Hence it is not mentioned in detail by me.

## \|\| 7.19.55 \|\|

*niyamaṃ sudṛḍhaṃ dṛṣṭvā suprasanno'bhavanmuniḥ \|*  
*durvāsāssuprasannātmā varantasmai pradattavān \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. Knowing his promise steady and resolute, the sage was highly delighted. The delighted sage Durvāsas granted him the desired boons.

## \|\| 7.19.56 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇaniyamasyāpi parīkṣāṃ sa cakāra ha \|*  
*tāṃ śṛṇu tvaṃ muniśreṣṭha kathayāmi kathāṃ ca tām \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. He had tested the resoluteness of lord Kṛṣṇa too. O excellent sage, listen to that also. I shall tell you that story also.

## \|\| 7.19.57 \|\|

*brahmaprārthanayā viṣṇurvasudevasuto'bhavat \|*  
*dharābhārāvatārārthaṃ sādhūnāṃ rakṣaṇāya ca \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. At the request of Brahmā, Viṣṇu was born as the son of Vasudeva in order to ease the burden of the Earth as well as to protect the good.

## \|\| 7.19.58 \|\|

*hatvā duṣṭānmahāpāpān brahmadrohakarānmalān \|*  
*rarakṣa nikhilānsādhūnbrāhmaṇānkṛṣṇanāmabhāk \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. Lord Kṛṣṇa slew the wicked, the great sinners, those who harassed the brahmins and the knaves. He protected all good men and brahmins.

## \|\| 7.19.59 \|\|

*brahmabhaktiṃ cakārāti sa kṛṣṇo vasudevajaḥ \|*  
*nityaṃ hi bhojayāmāsa surasānbrāhmaṇānbahūn \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. Kṛṣṇa, son of Vasudeva, showed great devotion to Brahmins. Everyday he fed many brahmins with many juicy articles.

## \|\| 7.19.60 \|\|

*brahmabhakto viśeṣeṇa kṛṣṇaśceti prathāmagāta \|*  
*saṃdraṣṭukāmassa muniḥ kṛṣṇāntikamagānmune \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. The fame that Kṛṣṇa was especially devoted to the brahmins spread everywhere. O sage, desirous of seeing him, the sage approached Kṛṣṇa.

## \|\| 7.19.61 \|\|

*rukmaṇīsahitaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ sannaṃ kṛtvā rathe svayam \|*  
*saṃyojya saṃsthito vāhaṃ suprasanna uvāha tam \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. He tied Kṛṣṇa and his consort Rukmiṇī to the chariot and sat himself in the chariot. Kṛṣṇa bore the chariot delightfully.

## \|\| 7.19.62 \|\|

*munī rathātsamuttīrya dṛṣṭvā tāṃ dṛḍhatāmparām \|*  
*tasmai bhūtvā suprasanno vajrāṅgatvavarandadau \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. The sage descended the chariot. He was delighted at the steadfastness of Kṛṣṇa and blessed him with the boon that his body would become adamantine.

## \|\| 7.19.63 \|\|

*dyunadyāmekadā snānaṃ kurvannagno babhūva ha \|*  
*lajjitobhūnmuniśreṣṭho durvāsāḥ kautukī mune \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. O sage, once the excellent sage Durvāsas was taking bath in the celestial river Gaṅgā when he became naked. He became a curious object of sight and was ashamed.

## \|\| 7.19.64 \|\|

*tajjñātvā draupadī snānaṃ kurvatī tatra cādarāt \|*  
*tallajjāṃ chādayāmāsa bhinnasvāñcaladānataḥ \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. Draupadī who was taking bath there provided him with the cut-piece of her garment and thus saved him from dishonour.

## \|\| 7.19.65 \|\|

*tadādāya pravāhenāgataṃ svanikaṭaṃ muniḥ \|*  
*tenācchādya svaguhyaṃ ca tasyai tuṣṭo babhūva saḥ \|\| 65 \|\|*

65\. As the piece of cloth reached him being carried by the current, the sage covered his private parts with it. He was therefore pleased with her.

## \|\| 7.19.66 \|\|

*draupadyai ca varamprādāttadañcalavivarddhanam \|*  
*pāṇḍavānsukhinaścakre draupadī tadvarātpunaḥ \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. He granted her the boon of ever increasing folds of cloth. Later, Draupadī made the Pāṇḍavas happy due to that boon.

## \|\| 7.19.67 \|\|

*haṃsaḍimbhau nṛpau kaucitsāvamānakarau khalau \|*  
*dattvā nideśaṃ ca harernāśayāmāsa sa prabhuḥ \|\| 67 \|\|*

67\. That holy sage gave directive to Viṣṇu and destroyed two wicked kings who had insulted him in the form of two young ones of swans.

## \|\| 7.19.68 \|\|

*brahmatejoviśeṣeṇa sthāpayāmāsa bhūtale \|*  
*saṃnyāsapaddhatiñcaiva yathāśāstra vidhikramam \|\| 68 \|\|*

68\. By his excellent brahminical splendour he established the path of renunciation in the world in accordance with the order and injunctions of the sacred lore.

## \|\| 7.19.69 \|\|

*bahūnuddhārayāmāsa sūpadeśaṃ vibodhya ca \|*  
*jñānaṃ dattvā viśeṣeṇa bahūnmuktāṃścakāra saḥ \|\| 69 \|\|*

69\. By instructions and enlightenment he uplifted many. By imparting knowledge he made many liberated.

## \|\| 7.19.70 \|\|

*itthaṃ cakre sa durvāsā vicitraṃ caritambahu \|*  
*dhanyaṃ yaśasyamāyuṣyaṃ śṛṇvatassarvakāmadam \|\| 70 \|\|*

70\. Thus the sage Durvasas had a variegated and wonderful life, conducive to wealth, fame and longevity. To the listeners it yields all desires.

## \|\| 7.19.71 \|\|

*ya idaṃ śṛṇuyādbhaktyā durvāsaścaritammudā \|*  
*śrāvayedvā parāṃ yaśca sa sukhīha paratra ca \|\| 71 \|\|*

71\. He who hears or reads the story of Durvāsas with devotion and joy will be happy here and hereafter.

## Chapter 20 - The Incarnation of Hanūmat and his story

## \|\| 7.20.1 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ataḥ paraṃ śruṇu prītyā hanumaccaritammune \|*  
*yathā cakārāśu haro līlāstadrūpato varāḥ \|\| 1 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

1\. O sage, henceforth listen lovingly to the story of Hanūmat, how Śiva performed excellent sports in that form.

## \|\| 7.20.2 \|\|

*cakāra suhitaṃ prītyā rāmasya parameśvarāḥ \|*  
*tatsarvaṃ caritaṃ vipra śṛṇu sarvasukhāvaham \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Out of love, Lord Śiva rendered great help to Rāma. O brahmin, listen to the whole of that story which is pleasing to all.

## \|\| 7.20.3 \|\|

*ekasminsamaye śambhuradbhutotikaraḥ prabhuḥ \|*  
*dadarśa mohinīrūpaṃ viṣṇossa hi vasadguṇaḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Once lord Śiva of wonderful sports saw Viṣṇu in the form of an enchantress with glittering qualities.

## \|\| 7.20.4 \|\|

*cakre svaṃ kṣubhitaṃ śambhuḥ kāmabāṇahato yathā \|*  
*svamvīryampātayāmāsa rāmakāryārthamīśvaraḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. As if hit by the arrows of Cupid, Śiva let fall his semen dislodged from its seat, for Rāma’s work.

## \|\| 7.20.5 \|\|

*tadvīryaṃ sthāpayāmāsuḥ patre saptarṣayaśca te \|*  
*preritā manasā tena rāmakāryārtha mādarāta \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Eagerly urged by him mentally for Rāma’s work, the seven celestial sages retained that semen in a leaf.

## \|\| 7.20.6 \|\|

*tairgautamasutāyāṃ tadvīryaṃ śambhormaharṣibhiḥ \|*  
*karṇadvārā tathāṃjanyāṃ rāmakāryārthamāhitam \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. For Rāma’s work that semen was poured through the ears of Añjanī, the daughter of Gautama, by those sages.

## \|\| 7.20.7 \|\|

*tataśca samaye tasmāddhanūmā niti nāmabhāk \|*  
*śambhurjajñe kapitanurmahābalaparākramaḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. In due course Śiva was born of it in the form of a monkey named Hanūmat. He had great strength and exploit.

## \|\| 7.20.8 \|\|

*hanūmānsa kapīśānaḥ śiśureva mahābalaḥ \|*  
*ravibimbaṃ babhakṣāśu jñātvā laghuphalamprage \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Even as a child the lord of monkeys, Hanūmat was very powerful. Early in the morning he took the disc of the sun for a small fruit and wanted to eat it.

## \|\| 7.20.9 \|\|

*devaprārthanayā taṃ so'tyajajjñātvā mahābalam \|*  
*śivāvatāraṃ ca prāpa varāndattānsurarṣibhiḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. At the request of the gods he left it. Knowing him to be an incarnation of Śiva of great power the gods and the sages granted him boons.

## \|\| 7.20.10 \|\|

*svajananyantikamprāgādatha sotipraharṣitaḥ \|*  
*hanūmānsarvamācakhyau tasyai tadvṛttamādarāt \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Highly delighted he approached his mother and eagerly told her everything in detail.

## \|\| 7.20.11 \|\|

*tadājñayā tato dhīrassarvavidyāmayatnataḥ \|*  
*sūryātpapāṭha sa kapirgatvā nityaṃ tadāntikam \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. At her behest the bold monkey went to the sun every day and learnt all lore from the sun without any strain.

## \|\| 7.20.12 \|\|

*sūryājñayā tadaṃśatya sugrīvasyāntikaṃ yayau \|*  
*māturājñāmanuprāpya rudrāṃśaḥ kapisattamaḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. The excellent monkey, a part of Śiva approached Sugrīva, a part of the sun, at his behest, after getting the permission of his mother.

## \|\| 7.20.13 \|\|

*jyeṣṭhabhrātrā vālinā hi svastrībhoktrā tiraskṛtaḥ \|*  
*ṛṣyamṛkagirau tena nyavasatsa hanūmatā \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Sugrīva who had been exiled by his elder brother Bālī who enjoyed his (Sugrīva’s) wife, stayed on the mountain Ṛṣyamūka long with Hanūmat.

## \|\| 7.20.14 \|\|

*tato'bhūtsa sukaṇṭhasya mantrī kapivarassudhīḥ \|*  
*sarvathā suhitaṃ cakre sugrīvasya harāṃśajaḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. The intelligent monkey born of a part of Śiva became Sugrīva’s minister and did everything beneficial to him in every respect.

## \|\| 7.20.15 \|\|

*tatrāgatena sabhrātrā hṛtabhāryeṇa duḥkhinā \|*  
*kārayāmāsa rāmeṇa tasya sakhyaṃ sukhāvaham \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. He made him enter into a pleasing alliance with Rāma who came there along with his brother and who was sad because his wife had been abducted by Rāvaṇa.

## \|\| 7.20.16 \|\|

*ghātayāmāsa rāmaśca vālinaṃ kapikuñjaram \|*  
*bhrātṛpatnyāśca bhoktāraṃ pāpinamvīramāninam \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Rāma slew the powerful monkey Bālī who was a sinner as he enjoyed his brother’s wife and who professed to He a great hero.

## \|\| 7.20.17 \|\|

*tato rāmā''jñayā tāta hanūmānvānareśvaraḥ \|*  
*sa sītānveṣaṇañcakre bahubhirvānaraissudhīḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Then at the behest of Rāma, O dear, the intelligent and leading monkey Hanūmat went out in search of Sītā along with many monkeys.

## \|\| 7.20.18 \|\|

*jñātvā laṅkāgatāṃ sītāṃ gatastatra kapīśvaraḥ \|*  
*drutamullaṃghya siṃdhuntamanistīryyaṃ paraissa vai \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. On knowing that she was in Laṅkā. the leading monkey quickly jumped across the ocean, never before crossed by any and reached Laṅkā.

## \|\| 7.20.19 \|\|

*cakre'dbhutacaritraṃ sa tatra vikramasaṃyutam \|*  
*abhijñānandadau prītyā sītāyai svaprabhorvaram \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. There he performed wonderful exploits and handed over the excellent token of recognition of his lord Rāma to Sītā.

## \|\| 7.20.20 \|\|

*sītāśokaṃ jahārāśu sa vīraḥ kapināyakaḥ \|*  
*śrāvayitvā rāmavṛttaṃ tatprāṇāvanakārakam \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. Narrating the endeavours of Rāma for the protection of her life, the heroic leader of monkeys dispelled the sorrow of Sītā.

## \|\| 7.20.21 \|\|

*tadabhijñānamādāya nivṛtto rāmasannidhim \|*  
*rāvaṇā''rāmamāhatya jaghāna bahurākṣasān \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. Taking a token from her he turned back to go towards Rāma. On the way he broke Rāvaṇa’s park and killed many Rākṣasas.

## \|\| 7.20.22 \|\|

*tadeva rāvaṇasutaṃ hatvā sabahurākṣasam \|*  
*sa mahopadravaṃ cakre mahotistatra nirbhayaḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. At the same time he killed Rāvaṇa’s son also along with many Rākṣasas. That fearless monkey of great exploits wrought a havoc there.

## \|\| 7.20.23-24 \|\|

*yadā dagdho rāvaṇenāvaguṃṭhya vasanāni ca \|*  
*tailābhyaktāni sudṛḍhaṃ mahābalavatā mune \|\| 23 \|\|*

*utplutyotplutya ca tadā mahādevāṃśajaḥ kapiḥ \|*  
*dadāha laṃkāṃ nikhilāṃ kṛtvā vyājantameva hi \|\| 24 \|\|*

23-24. O sage, when the powerful Rāvaṇa bound him with oil-dripping cloths and set fire to them, the monkey, a part of Śiva himself, jumped about and making that a pretext burnt the city of Laṅkā.

## \|\| 7.20.25 \|\|

*dagdhvā laṃkāṃ vaṃcayitvā vibhīṣaṇagṛhaṃ tataḥ \|*  
*apatadvāridhau vīrastatassa kapikuñjaraḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. After burning Laṅkā except the mansion of Bibhīṣaṇa, the heroic leader of monkeys jumped into the ocean.

## \|\| 7.20.26 \|\|

*svapuccha tatra nirvāpya prāpa tasya parantaṭam \|*  
*akhinnassa yayau rāmasannidhiṃ giriśāṃśajaḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Thereby extinguishing the fire from his tail he reached the other shore. Not at all tired or grieved the monkey born of a part of Śiva approached Rāma.

## \|\| 7.20.27 \|\|

*avilaṃbena sujavo hanūmān kapisattamaḥ \|*  
*rāmopakaṇṭhamāgatya dadau sītāśiromaṇim \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. The rapid and excellent monkey Hanūmat reached Rāma’s side without delay and handed over the crest-jewel of Sītā.

## \|\| 7.20.28 \|\|

*tatastadājñayā vīrassindhau setumabandhayat \|*  
*vānarassa samānīya bahūngirivarānbalī \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. At his behest, the powerful hero, gathered many mountains through the hosts of monkeys and built a bridge in the ocean.

## \|\| 7.20.29 \|\|

*gatvā tatra tato rāmastartukāmo yathā tataḥ \|*  
*śivaliṃgaṃ samānarca pratiṣṭhāpya jayepsayā \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. At the time when he was about to cross the ocean, Rāma installed a phallic image of Śiva and worshipped it with a desire to be victorious.

## \|\| 7.20.30 \|\|

*tadvarātsa jayaṃ prāpya varaṃ tīrtvodadhiṃ tataḥ \|*  
*laṃkāmāvṛtya kapibhī raṇaṃ cakre sa rākṣasaiḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. Securing his boon of victory, he crossed the ocean, surrounded Laṅkā with the monkeys and fought against the Rākṣasas.

## \|\| 7.20.31 \|\|

*jaghānāthāsurānvīro rāmasainyaṃ rarakṣa saḥ \|*  
*śakti kṣataṃ lakṣmaṇaṃ ca saṃjīvinyā hyajīvayat \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. The heroic Hanūmat slew many Asuras and pro tected Rāma’s army. He enlivened Lakṣmaṇa by the medicinal herb Sañjīvanī when he was wounded by Meghanāda’s javelin.

## \|\| 7.20.32 \|\|

*sarvathā sukhinaṃ cakre sarāmaṃ lakṣmaṇaṃ hi saḥ \|*  
*sarvasainyaṃ rarakṣāsau mahādevātmajaḥ prabhuḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. The lordly son of Śiva made Rāma and Lakṣmaṇa happy in every respect. He protected the entire army.

## \|\| 7.20.33 \|\|

*rāvaṇaṃ parivārāḍhyaṃ nāśayāmāsa viśramaḥ \|*  
*sukhīcakāra devānsa mahābalagrahaḥ kapi \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. The untiring monkey destroyed Rāvaṇa, his family and his followers. The monkey, the storehouse of great strength made the gods happy.

## \|\| 7.20.34 \|\|

*mahīrāvaṇasaṃjñaṃ sa hatvā rāmaṃ salakṣmaṇam \|*  
*tatsthānādānayāmāsa svasthānamparipālya ca \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. He slew the demon Mahīrāvaṇa and brought Rāma and Lakṣmaṇa from his place to their own after guarding them well.

## \|\| 7.20.35 \|\|

*rāmakāryaṃ cakārāśu sarvathā kapipuṃgavaḥ \|*  
*asurānnamayāmāsa nānālīlāṃ cakāra ca \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. The leading monkey quickly carried out the tasks of Rāma by all means, made the Asuras bow down and submit and performed various sports.

## \|\| 7.20.36 \|\|

*sthāpayāmāsa bhūloke rāmabhaktiṃ kapīśvaraḥ \|*  
*svayaṃ bhaktavaro bhūtvā sītārāmasukhapradaḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. Himself a leader of devotees, he made Sītā and Rāma happy. He, the lord of monkeys, established the cult of devotion to Rāma in the world.

## \|\| 7.20.37 \|\|

*lakṣmaṇaprāṇadātā ca sarvadevamadāpahaḥ \|*  
*rudrāvatāro bhagavānbhaktoddhārakarassa vai \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. Indeed he was the Incarnation of Śiva the lord and uplifter of devotees. He was the life-saviour of Lakṣmaṇa and the destroyer of the arrogance of all Daityas.

## \|\| 7.20.38 \|\|

*hanumānsa mahāvīro rāmakāryakarassadā \|*  
*rāmadūtābhidho loke daityaghno bhaktavatsalaḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. He was the heroic Hanūmat who carried the tasks of Rāma always. In the world he is called Rāma’s messenger. He is favourably disposed to his devotees. He is the slayer of Daityas.

## \|\| 7.20.39 \|\|

*iti te kathitaṃ tāta hanumaccaritamvaram \|*  
*dhanyaṃ yaśasyamāyuṣyaṃ sarvakāmaphalapradam \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. Thus O dear, the excellent story of Hanūmat has been narrated to you. It is conducive to wealth, fame and longevity. It yields the fruits of all desires.

## \|\| 7.20.40 \|\|

*ya idaṃ śṛṇuyādbhaktyā śrāvayedvā samāhitaḥ \|*  
*sa bhuktvehākhilānkāmānante mokṣaṃ labhetparam \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. He who listens to this or narrates this with devotion and purity of mind enjoys all desires here and attains liberation hereafter.

## Chapter 21 - The Incarnation and the story of Maheśa

## \|\| 7.21.1 \|\|

*\|\|nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*atha prītyā śṛṇu mune'vatāraṃ paramaṃ prabhoḥ \|*  
*śaṃkarasyātmabhūputra śṛṇvatāṃ sarvakāmadam \|\| 1 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:

1\. O sage, O son of the self-born Brahmā, listen to the great incarnation of lord Śiva, which yields all desires to the hearer.

## \|\| 7.21.2 \|\|

*ekadā muniśārdūla girijāśaṃkarāvubhau \|*  
*vihartukāmau saṃjātau svecchayā parameśvarau \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. O great sage, once the supreme god Śiva and the great goddess Pārvatī became desirous of indulging in sports.

## \|\| 7.21.3 \|\|

*bhairavaṃ dvārapālaṃ ca kṛtvābhyantaramāgatau \|*  
*nānāsakhigaṇaiḥ prītyā sevitau naraśīlitau \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Keeping Bhairava as the watch-man they went inside. Acting like human beings they were lovingly served by many attendants.

## \|\| 7.21.4 \|\|

*ciraṃ vihṛtya tatra dvau svatantrau parameśvarau \|*  
*babhūvatuḥ prasanno tau nānālīlākarau mune \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. O sage, indulging in different sports of their choice for a long time, the lordly deities were highly delighted.

## \|\| 7.21.5 \|\|

*athonmattākṛtirdevī svatantrā līlayā śivā \|*  
*āgatā dvāri tadrūpā prabhorājñāmavāpa sā \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. The independent goddess Pārvatī sportively assumed the form of a mad woman and with the permission of her lord came near to the door in that form itself.

## \|\| 7.21.6 \|\|

*tāṃ devīṃ bhairavassotha nārīdṛṣṭyā vilokya ca \|*  
*niṣiṣedha bahirgantuṃ tadrūpeṇa vimohitaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Fascinated by her charming form Bhairava glanced at her as at a woman and prevented her from going out.

## \|\| 7.21.7 \|\|

*nārīdṛṣṭyā sudṛṣṭā sā bhairaveṇa yadā mune \|*  
*kuddhā'bhavacchivā devī taṃ śaśāpa tadāṃbikā \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. O sage, viewed at very closely by Bhairava, the goddess Pārvatī became furious and cursed him.

## \|\| 7.21.8 \|\|

*\|\| śivovāca \|*  
*nārīdṛṣṭyā paśyasi tvaṃ yato māṃ puruṣādhama \|*  
*ato bhava dharāyāṃ hi mānuṣastvaṃ ca bhairava \|\| 8 \|\|*

Śiva said:—

8\. O base man, since you glanced at me as at a woman, O Bhairava, be born as a human being on the earth.

## \|\| 7.21.9 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*itthaṃ yadā'bhavacchapto bhairavaśśivayā mune \|*  
*hāhākāro mahānāsīdduḥkhamāpa sa līlayā \|\| 9 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

9\. O sage, when Bhairava was thus cursed by her there was a hue and cry. He became very miserable due to the sport of the goddess.

## \|\| 7.21.10 \|\|

*tataśca śaṃkaraḥ śīghraṃ tamāgatya munīśvara \|*  
*aśvāsayadbhairavaṃ hi nānā'nunayakovidaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. O great sage, hastening to that place, Śiva consoled Bhairava. Indeed, the lord is very expert in the various forms of consolation.

## \|\| 7.21.11 \|\|

*tacchāpādbhairavassotha kṣitāvavataranmune \|*  
*manuṣyayonyāṃ vaitālasaṃjñakaśśaṃkarecchayā \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Due to her curse, and as a result of Śiva’s will, O sage, Bhairava descended to the earth in human birth under the name of Vaitāla.

## \|\| 7.21.12 \|\|

*tatsnehataḥ śivaḥ sopi kṣitāvavataradvibhuḥ \|*  
*śivayā saha sallīlo laukikīṅgatimāśritaḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Urged by his friendship lord Śiva also incarnated on the earth along with Śivā. He indulged in good sports and followed worldly conventions.

## \|\| 7.21.13 \|\|

*maheśāhvaḥ śivaścāsīcchāradā girijā mune \|*  
*sulīlāṃ cakratuḥ prītyā nānā līlā viśāradau \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. O sage, Śiva was known as Maheśa and Pārvatī as Śāradā. Both of them experts in different sports indulged in fine sports.

## \|\| 7.21.14 \|\|

*iti te kathitaṃ tāta maheśacaritaṃ varam \|*  
*dhanyaṃ yaśasyamāyuṣyaṃ sarvakāmaphalapradam \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. O dear, thus the story of Maheśa has been narrated to you. It is excellent, conducive to wealth, fame and longevity and yields all desires.

## \|\| 7.21.15 \|\|

*ya idaṃ śṛṇuyādbhaktyā śrāvayedvā samāhitaḥ \|*  
*sa bhuktvehākhilānbhogānante mokṣamavāpnuyāt \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. He who listens to and narrates this story with devotion and purity of mind enjoys all pleasures here and attains salvation hereafter.

## Chapter 22 - Harassment by Viṣṇu’s sons

## \|\| 7.22.1 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*śṛṇu brahmasuta prājña vṛṣeśākhyaṃ munīśvara \|*  
*śivāvatāraṃ sallīlaṃ harigarvaharaṃ varam \|\| 1 \|\|*

Summary: Harassment by Viṣṇu’s sons and the story of Śiva’s incarnation of the Bull.

Nandīśvara said:—

1\. O great sage, O intelligent son of Brahmā, listen to the excellent Vṛṣeśa incarnation of Śiva that performed good sports and that destroyed the arrogance of Viṣṇu.

## \|\| 7.22.2 \|\|

*purā devāsurāḥ sarve jarāmṛtyubhayārditāḥ \|*  
*parasparaṃ ca saṃdhāya ratnānyāditsavo'bhavan \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Formerly all the gods and Asuras, distressed by the fear of old age and death made alliance with each other and became desirous of taking gems from the ocean.

## \|\| 7.22.3 \|\|

*tataḥ surā'surāḥ sarve kṣīrodaṃ sāgarottamam \|*  
*udyatā mathituṃ taṃ ca babhūvurmuni nandana \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. O son of the sage, then the gods and the Asuras attempted to churn the excellent milky ocean.

## \|\| 7.22.4 \|\|

*āsañchucismitāssarve kenedaṃ manthanaṃ bhavet \|*  
*svakāryasiddhaye tasya brahmanniti surāsurāḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. “With what shall the churning be carried out lor the attainment of our object?” thus the gods and the Asuras were quite dismayed.

## \|\| 7.22.5 \|\|

*tadā nabhogatā vāṇī meghagambhīranisvanā \|*  
*uvāca devāndaityāṃśca śvāsayantīśvarājñayā \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Then a celestial voice with the rumbling sound of clouds spoke to the gods and the daityas as a result of Śiva’s wish and consoled them.

## \|\| 7.22.6 \|\|

*nabhovāṇyuvāca \|*  
*he devā asurāścaiva manthadhvaṃ kṣīrasāgaram \|*  
*bhavatāmbalabuddhirhi bhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

The celestial voice said:—

6\. O gods, O Asuras, churn the milk-ocean. Undoubtedly a factual test of your strength will take place.

## \|\| 7.22.7 \|\|

*mandaraṃ caiva manthānaṃ rajjuṃ kuruta vāsukim \|*  
*mithassarve militvā tu maṃthanaṃ kurutādarāt \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Make Mandara your churning rod and Vāsuki your churning rope. Unitedly you carry on the churning with firm devotion.

## \|\| 7.22.8 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*nabhogatā tadā vāṇīṃ niśamyātha surāsuraḥ \|*  
*udyogaṃ cakrire sarve tatkartuṃ munisattama \|\| 8 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

8\. O excellent sage, on hearing the celestial voice the gods and the Asuras exerted themselves in carrying out the plan.

## \|\| 7.22.9 \|\|

*susandhāyākhilāste vai mandaramparvatottamam \|*  
*kanakābhaṃ ca saralaṃ nānāśobhārcitaṃ yayuḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. Allying together they went straight to the excellent mountain Mandara of golden splendour and of diverse lustre.

## \|\| 7.22.10 \|\|

*suprasādya girīśaṃ taṃ tadājñaptāḥ surāsurāḥ \|*  
*balādutpāṭayāmāsurnetukāmāḥ payo'rṇavam \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. After propitiating Śiva and after obtaining his consent they tried to uproot the Mandara in their desire to take it to the milk-ocean.

## \|\| 7.22.11 \|\|

*bhujairutpāṭya te sarvve jagmuḥ kṣīrārṇava pure \|*  
*aśaktā abhavaṃstatra tamānetuṃ hataujasaḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. O sage, uprooting and lifting it by means of their arms they went to the milk-ocean. Their strength Tailing they became incapable of taking it up to the sea.

## \|\| 7.22.12 \|\|

*tadbhujaissa paribhraṣṭaḥ patito maṃdaro giriḥ \|*  
*sahasātigurussadyo devadaityopari dhruvam \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Slipping from their arms the Mandara became very heavy suddenly and fell over the gods and the Asuras.

## \|\| 7.22.13 \|\|

*evambhagnodyamā bhagnāḥ sambabhūvussurāsurā \|*  
*cetanāḥ prāpya ca tatastuṣṭuvurjagadīśvaram \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Failing in their effort the gods and the Asuras broke down. After regaining consciousness they eulogised the lord of the universe.

## \|\| 7.22.14 \|\|

*tadicchayodyatāssarve punarutthāya taṃ girim \|*  
*nicikṣipurjale nītvā kṣīrodasyottare taṭe \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. At his wish in their next attempt they lifted up the mountain. They took it to the northern shore of the ocean and hurled it into the waters.

## \|\| 7.22.15 \|\|

*tatassurāsuragaṇā rajjuṃ kṛtvā ca vāsukim \|*  
*ratnānyādātukāmāste mamaṃthuḥ kṣīrasāgaram \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. Then both the gods and the Asuras made Vāsuki the churning rope. Desirous of taking the excellent articles they churned the milk-ocean.

## \|\| 7.22.16 \|\|

*kṣīrode mathyamāne tu śrīssvarlokamaheśvarī \|*  
*samudbhūtā samudrācca bhṛguputrī haripriyā \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. When the milk-ocean was churned the goddess of heaven sprang up from the ocean as the daughter of Bhṛgu who later became Viṣṇu’s beloved.

## \|\| 7.22.17-18 \|\|

*dhanvantariḥ śaśāṃkaśca pārijāto mahādrumaḥ \|*  
*uccaiśśravāśca turago gaja airāvatastathā \|\| 17 \|\|*

*surā haridhanu śśaṅkho gāvaḥ kāmadughāstataḥ \|*  
*kaustubhākhyo maṇiścaiva tathā pīyūṣameva ca \|\| 18 \|\|*

17-18. There also emerged—Dhanvantari, the moon, the Pārijāta tree, the horse Uccaiśśravas, the elephant Airāvata, wine, the bow of Viṣṇu, the conch, the cow Kāmadhenu, the jewel Kaustubha and the nectar.

## \|\| 7.22.19 \|\|

*punaśca mathyamāne tu kālakūṭaṃ mahāviṣam \|*  
*yugāntānalabha jātaṃ surāsurabhayāvahama \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. When it was churned again, the great poison Kālakūṭa blazing like the fire at the dissolution of the Yugas and terrifying the gods and the Asuras, came out.

## \|\| 7.22.20 \|\|

*pīyūṣajanmakāle tu bindavo ye bahirgatāḥ \|*  
*tebhyaḥ kāntā samudbhūtā bahvayo hyadbhutadarśanāḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. From the sprays and drops that sprang up at the outcome of nectar, many damsels of wonderful beauty were born.

## \|\| 7.22.21 \|\|

*śaratpūrṇenduvadanāstaḍitsūryyānalaprabhāḥ \|*  
*hārakeyūrakaṭakairdivyaratnairalaṅkṛtāḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. Their faces resembled the full moon in the Autumn; their lustre was as glittering and dazzling as fire, lightning or the sun and they were bedecked in divine jewels, necklaces, bracelets and bangles.

## \|\| 7.22.22 \|\|

*lāvaṇyāmṛtatoyena tāssiṃcantyo diśo daśa \|*  
*jagadunmādayantyeva bhrūbhaṅgāyatavīkṣaṇāḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. Sprinkling the ten quarters by the water of nectarine beauty they seemed to madden the world by their beauteous glances.

## \|\| 7.22.23 \|\|

*koṭiśastāssamutpannāstvamṛtātkāmanissṛtāḥ \|*  
*tato'mṛtaṃ samutpannaṃ jarāmṛtyunivāraṇam \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. They emerged in crores from the nectar sprays. Then the nectar that wards off old age and death came out.

## \|\| 7.22.24 \|\|

*lakṣmīṃ śaṃkhaṃ kaustubhaṃ ca khaḍgaṃ jagrāha keśavaḥ \|*  
*jagrāhārko hayaṃ divyamuccaiḥśravasamādarāt \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. Viṣṇu kept for himself Lakṣmī, the conch, Kaustubha and the sword; the Sun chose the divine horse Uccaiśśravas for himself.

## \|\| 7.22.25 \|\|

*pārijātaṃ taruvaramairāvatamibheśvaram \|*  
*śacīpatiśca jagrāha nirjareśo mahādarāta \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. With great eagerness the husband of Śacī, the lord of the gods, took Pārijāta the excellent tree and Airāvata the lordly elephant.

## \|\| 7.22.26 \|\|

*kālakūṭaṃ śaśāṃkaṃ ca devatrāṇāya śaṃkaraḥ \|*  
*svakaṇṭhe dhṛtavāñchambhussvecchayā bhaktavatsalaḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. For the protection of the gods, Śiva, favourably disposed towards his devotees, voluntarily retained the Kālakūṭa in his neck and held the moon on his forehead.

## \|\| 7.22.27 \|\|

*daityāssurākhyāṃ ramaṇīmīśvarājñāvimohitāḥ \|*  
*jagṛhuḥ sakalā vyāsa sarve dhanvantariṃ janāḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. O Vyāsa, deluded by the illusion of Śiva, the Daityas accepted Surā (wine) and the common people took Dhanvantari (the physician).

## \|\| 7.22.28 \|\|

*jagṛhurmunayassarve kāmadhenummunīśvarāḥ \|*  
*sāmānyatastriyastāśca sthitā āsanvimohitāḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. The great sages took the cow Kāmadhenu. The damsels, the enchantresses became common to all.

## \|\| 7.22.29 \|\|

*amṛtārthaṃ mahāyuddhaṃ saṃbabhūva jayaiṣiṇām \|*  
*surāṇāmasurāṇāṃ ca mithaḥ saṃkṣubdhacetasām \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. A great battle for the possession of Amṛta ensued between the gods and the Asuras who desired to conquer each other and who were agitated in their minds.

## \|\| 7.22.30 \|\|

*hṛtaṃ somaṃ ca daiteyairbalāddevānvijitya ca \|*  
*baliprabhṛtibhirvyāsa yugāntāgnyarkasuprabhaiḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. The Amṛta was forcibly taken by the Daityas Bali and others, O Vyāsa, of fine lustre resembling that of the fire and the sun at the dissolution of the Yugas. They defeated the gods.

## \|\| 7.22.31 \|\|

*devāśśaṃkaramāpannā vihvalāśśivamāyayā \|*  
*sarve śakrādayastāta daiteyairarditā balāt \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. Indra and other gods, O dear, overwhelmed by Śiva’s illusion and harassed by the Daityas sought refuge in Śiva.

## \|\| 7.22.32 \|\|

*tatastadamṛtaṃ yatnātstrīsvarūpeṇa māyayā \|*  
*śivājñayā rameśena daityebhyaśca hṛtammune \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. O sage, then the Amṛta was strenuously snatched away from the Daityas by Viṣṇu at the behest of Śiva, after assuming the form of a woman.

## \|\| 7.22.33 \|\|

*apāyayatsurāṃstāṃśca mohinīstrīsvarūpadhṛk \|*  
*mohayitvā surānsarvānharirmāyāvināṃ varaḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. Viṣṇu, the most excellent of those who wield Māyā assuming the form of a woman Mohinī deluded the Asuras and made the gods drink it.

## \|\| 7.22.34 \|\|

*gatvā nikaṭametasyā ūcire daityapuṃgavāḥ \|*  
*pāyayasva sudhāmetāṃ mā bhūdbhedo'tra paṃktiṣu \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. The leading Daityas approached her and said—“Make us drink this nectar. Let there be no break in the lines.”

## \|\| 7.22.35 \|\|

*etaduktvā dadustasmai viṣṇave chalarūpiṇe \|*  
*te daityā dānavāḥ sarve śivamāyāvimohitāḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. All those Daityas and Dānavas deluded by Śiva’s Māyā said thus and gave the nectar to Viṣṇu in disguise.

## \|\| 7.22.36 \|\|

*etasminnantare dṛṣṭvā striyo dānavapuṃgavāḥ \|*  
*anayannamṛtodbhūtā yathāsthānaṃ yathāsukham \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. In the meantime on seeing the damsels born of Amṛta, the leading Dānavas took them to their abodes according to their convenience.

## \|\| 7.22.37 \|\|

*tāsāmpurāṇi divyāni svargācchaguṇānyapi \|*  
*ghorairyantraissuguptāni mayamāyākṛtāni ca \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. The cities built by the architect Maya for those women were divine, hundred times more beautiful than heaven. They were well fortified by terrible machines.

## \|\| 7.22.38 \|\|

*surakṣitāni sarvāṇi kṛtvā yuddhāya niryayuḥ \|*  
*aspṛṣṭavakṣaso daityāḥ kṛtvā samayameva hi \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. The daityas made those cities well guarded and set off for fighting after taking this pledge touching their chests.

## \|\| 7.22.39 \|\|

*na spṛśāmaḥ striyaścemā yadi devairvinirjitāḥ \|*  
*ityuktvā te mahāvīrā daityāssarve yuyutsavaḥ \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. “We will never touch these women if we were to be defeated by the gods.” After saying this, the heroic Daityas became eager to fight.

## \|\| 7.22.40 \|\|

*siṃhanādantataścakruśśaṃkhāndadhmuḥ pṛthakpṛthak \|*  
*pūrayanta ivākāśaṃ tarpayanto balāhakān \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. They roared like lions. Severally they blew their conches as if filling the sky and propitiating the clouds.

## \|\| 7.22.41 \|\|

*yuddhaṃ babhūva devānāmasuraissaha bhīkaram \|*  
*devāsurākhyamatulaṃ prasiddhaṃ bhuvanatraye \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. A terrible fight ensued between the gods and the Asuras. The unrivalled battle between the gods and Asuras is famous in the three worlds.

## \|\| 7.22.42 \|\|

*jayaṃ prāpussurāssarve viṣṇunā parirakṣitāḥ \|*  
*daityāḥ palāyitāstatra hatāḥ sāmaraviṣṇunā \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. Protected by Viṣṇu, the gods gained victory. The Daityas fled and those who remained were killed by Viṣṇu and his associate gods.

## \|\| 7.22.43 \|\|

*daityāḥ saṃmohitā devairviṣṇunā ca mahātmanā \|*  
*hatāvaśiṣṭāḥ pātālaṃ viviśurvivarāṇi ca \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. The Daityas were deluded by the gods and Viṣṇu the great soul. Those that survived entered the nether regions.

## \|\| 7.22.44 \|\|

*anuvavrāja tānviṣṇuścakrapāṇirmahābalaḥ \|*  
*pātālaṃ paramaṃ gatvā saṃsthitānbhītabhītavat \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. The powerful Viṣṇu armed with his discus chased them even after they had gone to the ultimate end of Pātāla excessively frightened.

## \|\| 7.22.45 \|\|

*etasminnantare viṣṇu dadarśāmṛtasambhavāḥ \|*  
*kāntāḥ pūrṇenduvadanā divyalāvaṇyagarvitāḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. In the meantime Viṣṇu saw those damsels born of nectar sprays who were haughty due to their divine beauty and whose faces resembled the full moon.

## \|\| 7.22.46 \|\|

*saṃmohitaḥ kāmabāṇairlebhe tatraiva nirvṛtim \|*  
*tābhiśca varanārībhiḥ krīḍamāno babhūva ha \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. Fascinated by the Cupid’s arrows Viṣṇu attained highest pleasure only there. He began to indulge in sexual dalliance with those women of exquisite beauty.

## \|\| 7.22.47 \|\|

*tābhya putrānajanayadviṣṇurvaraparākramān \|*  
*mahīṃ sarvāṃ kaṃpayato nānāyuddhaviśāradān \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. Viṣṇu begot of them sons of great exploits and valour, experts in various kinds of warfare, shaking the entire earth.

## \|\| 7.22.48 \|\|

*tato vai hariputrāste mahābalaparākramāḥ \|*  
*mahopadravamācerussvarge bhuvi ca duḥkhadam \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. Those sons of Viṣṇu of great strength and valour wrought great havoc both in heaven and earth causing misery to all.

## \|\| 7.22.49 \|\|

*lokopadravamālakṣya nirjarā munayo'tha vai \|*  
*cakrurnivedananteṣāṃ brahmaṇe praṇipatya ca \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. On seeing the great harm done to the worlds the gods and the sages approached Brahmā and informed him of this after bowing to him.

## \|\| 7.22.50 \|\|

*tacchrutvādāya tānbrahmā yayau kailāsaparvatam \|*  
*tatra dṛṣṭvā śivaṃ devaiḥ praṇanāma punaḥ punaḥ \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. On hearing it Brahmā took them to the mountain Kailāsa. On seeing Śiva there he bowed to him again and again along with the gods.

## \|\| 7.22.51 \|\|

*tuṣṭāva vividhaistotrairnata skandhaḥ kṛtāñjaliḥ \|*  
*jaya deva mahādeva sarvasvāminniti bruvan \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. Saying “O lord Śiva, be victorious, O Lord of all”, he eulogised him with various hymns with palms joined in reverence and shoulders drooping down.

## \|\| 7.22.52 \|\|

*brahmovāca \|*  
*devadeva mahādeva lokānrakṣākhilānprabho \|*  
*upadrutānviṣṇuputraiḥ pātālasthairvikāribhiḥ \|\| 52 \|\|*

Brahmā said:—

52\. O great God, O lord of the gods, save the worlds harassed by the passionate sons of Viṣṇu stationed in nether regions.

## \|\| 7.22.53 \|\|

*nārīṣvamṛtabhūtāsu saṃsaktātmā harirvibho \|*  
*pātāle tiṣṭhatīdānīṃ ramate hi vikāravān \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. O lord, Viṣṇu is now enamoured of the damsels born of the nectar sprays. He is in nether regions indulging in passionate sexual dalliance with them.

## \|\| 7.22.54 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*itthaṃ bahustutaḥ śambhurbrahmaṇā sarṣinirjaraiḥ \|*  
*lokasaṃrakṣaṇārthāya viṣṇorānayanāya ca \|\| 54 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

54\. For the protection of the worlds and for the redemption of Viṣṇu, Śiva was eulogised thus by Brahmā, the gods and the sages.

## \|\| 7.22.55 \|\|

*tatassa bhagavāñchambhuḥ kṛpāsiṃdhurmaheśvaraḥ \|*  
*tadupadravamājñāya vṛṣarūpo babhūva ha \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. Then lord Śiva, the ocean of mercy, realising the havoc caused by the sons of Viṣṇu assumed the form of a bull.

## Chapter 23 - The bull incarnation of Śiva (Vṛṣabha)

## \|\| 7.23.1 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*tato vṛṣabharūpeṇa garjamānaḥ pinākadhṛk \|*  
*praviṣṭo vivaraṃ tatra ninadanbhairavānravān \|\| 1 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said

1\. Then bellowing in the form of a Bull (Vṛṣabha), the Pināka-bearing lord entered the crevice and shouted terribly.

## \|\| 7.23.2 \|\|

*nipestutasya ninadaiḥ purāṇi nagarāṇi ca \|*  
*prakampo hi babhūvātha sarveṣāṃ puravāsinām \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. At his bellowing sound, cities and towns crumbled down and all the citizens trembled.

## \|\| 7.23.3 \|\|

*tato vṛṣo hareḥ putrānsaṃgrāmodyatakārmukān \|*  
*śivamāyāvimūḍhātmamahābalaparākramān \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Then the bull attacked Viṣṇu’s sons wielding their bows for fighting, deluded by Śiva’s Māyā and proud of their great strength and exploit.

## \|\| 7.23.4 \|\|

*hariputrāstataste'tha prākupyanmunisattama \|*  
*pradudruvuḥ pragarjyoccairvīrāśśaṃkarasanmukham \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. O excellent sage, the sons of Viṣṇu became furious. Those heroes roared loudly and rushed against Siva.

## \|\| 7.23.5 \|\|

*āyātāṃstānhareḥ putrānrudro vṛṣabharūpadhṛk \|*  
*prākupyadviṣṇuputrāṃśca khuraiśśṛṃgairvyadārayat \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Rudra in that form of a bull (Vṛṣabha) was furious at those sons of Viṣṇu who rushed at him. He kicked them with his hoofs and tore them with his horns.

## \|\| 7.23.6 \|\|

*vidāritāṃgā rudreṇa sarve harisutāśca te \|*  
*naṣṭā drutaṃ sanbabhūvurgataprāṇā vicetasaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. When their limbs were split by Rudra, they swooned and lost their lives. They were immediately destroyed.

## \|\| 7.23.7 \|\|

*hateṣu teṣu putreṣu viṣṇurbalavatāṃ varaḥ \|*  
*niṣkramyātha praṇamyoccairyayau śīghraṃ harāntikam \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. When they were killed Viṣṇu the foremost of the strong came out shouting loudly and hastened towards Śiva.

## \|\| 7.23.8 \|\|

*dṛṣṭvā rudraṃ pravrajaṃtaṃ hataviṣṇusutaṃ vṛṣam \|*  
*śaraissantāḍayāmāsa divyairastraiśca keśavaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. On seeing Śiva in the form of the bull going along after killing his sons, Viṣṇu hit him with divine arrows and missiles.

## \|\| 7.23.9 \|\|

*tataḥ kruddho mahādevo vṛṣarūpī madābalaḥ \|*  
*astrāṇi tāni viṣṇośca jagrāsa girigocaraḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. Then the infuriated Śiva, the highly powerful one in the form of a bull stood like a mountain and caught hold of all those missiles of Viṣṇu.

## \|\| 7.23.10 \|\|

*atha kṛtvā mahākopaṃ vṛṣātmā sa maheśvaraḥ \|*  
*vinanāda mahāghoraṃ kampayaṃstrijaganmune \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. O sage, shouting furiously, lord Śiva in the form of a bull shook the three worlds terribly.

## \|\| 7.23.11 \|\|

*tata viṣṇośca tarasā khuraiśśṛṃgairvyadārayat \|*  
*viṣṇuṃ krodhākulaṃ mūḍhamajānantaṃ nijaṃ harim \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. The lord leaped up suddenly and kicked the infuriated Viṣṇu who was deluded and could not realise him. The lord then tore him with his horns.

## \|\| 7.23.12 \|\|

*tatassa śithilātmā hi vyathitāṃgo babhūva ha \|*  
*tatprahāramasahyāśu harirmāyāvimohitaḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Unable to bear the onslaught, Viṣṇu deluded by Māyā was exhausted and utterly shattered.

## \|\| 7.23.13 \|\|

*gatagarvohariścaiva vicetā gatacetanaḥ \|*  
*jñātavānparameśānaṃ viharantaṃ vṛṣātmanā \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. When his pride fell off, Viṣṇu was dejected, and dispirited. He then realised that Śiva was sporting about in the form of a bull.

## \|\| 7.23.14 \|\|

*atha vijñāya gaurīśamāgataṃ vṛṣarūpataḥ \|*  
*prāha gambhīrayā vācā nataskandhaḥ kṛtāñjaliḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Then on realising that Śiva himself had come in the form of a Bull, Viṣṇu spoke in a shrill voice with drooping shoulders and palms joined in reverence.

## \|\| 7.23.15 \|\|

*hariruvāca \|*  
*devadeva mahādeva karuṇāsāgara prabho \|*  
*māyayā te maheśāna mohitohaṃ vimūḍhadhīḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

Viṣṇu said:—

15\. O lord of the gods, O ocean of mercy, O lord Śiva, I had been deluded by your Māyā and my mind had been confused.

## \|\| 7.23.16 \|\|

*kṛtaṃ yuddhaṃ tvayeśena svanāthena mayā prabho \|*  
*kṛpāṃ kṛtvā mayi svāminso'parādho hi sahyatām \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. O lord, I fought with you, Śiva. Taking pity on me, O lord, let this offence be excused.

## \|\| 7.23.17 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā harerdīnatayā mune \|*  
*bhagavāñchaṃkaraḥ prāha rameśaṃ bhaktavatsalaḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

17\. O sage, on hearing those piteous words of Viṣṇu, lord Śiva favourably disposed to his devotees spoke to Viṣṇu.

## \|\| 7.23.18 \|\|

*he viṣṇo he mahābuddhe kathaṃ māṃ jñātavānna hi \|*  
*yuddhaṃ kṛtaṃ kṛtaste'dya jñānaṃ sarvvaṃ ca vismṛtam \|\| 18 \|\|*

Śiva said:—

18\. O Viṣṇu, O intelligent one, how is it that you did not know me? Why did you fight? Your intellect has completely been depraved.

## \|\| 7.23.19 \|\|

*ātmānaṃ kinna jānāsi madadhīnaparākramam \|*  
*tvayā nātra ratiḥ kāryā nivartasva kucārataḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. Why don’t you realise yourself as one whose exploit is dependent on me. You should not have indulged in sexual dalliance there. You should have refrained from all misdeeds.

## \|\| 7.23.20 \|\|

*kāmādhīnaṃ kathaṃ jñānaṃ strīṣu sakto vihārakṛt \|*  
*nocitantava deveśa smaraṇaṃ viśvatāraṇam \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. Why should your intellect be subservient to lust? That you should indulge in dalliance with women does not behove you, O lord of gods. Remember the task of uplifting the universe.

## \|\| 7.23.21 \|\|

*tacchutvā śambhuvacanaṃ vijñānapradamādarāt \|*  
*vrīḍyansvamanasā viṣṇuḥ prāha vācaṃ maheśvaram \|\| 21 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

21\. On hearing those words of Śiva that imparted knowledge Viṣṇu was ashamed of himself and then he spoke to lord Śiva respectfully.

## \|\| 7.23.22 \|\|

*viṣṇuruvāca \|*  
*mamātra vidyate cakraṃ tadgṛhītve tadādarāt \|*  
*gamiṣyāmi svalokantantvadājñāparipālakaḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

Viṣṇu said:—

22\. My discus is here. I shall take the discus and return to my region abiding by your order with reverence.

Nandīśvara said

## \|\| 7.23.23 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*tadākarṇya maheśāno vacanaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ haraḥ \|*  
*pratyuvāca vṛṣātmā hi vṛṣarakṣaḥ punarharim \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. On hearing the words of Viṣṇu, lord Śiva the protector of virtue in the guise of a bull, replied to Viṣṇu again.

## \|\| 7.23.24 \|\|

*na vilambaḥ prakarttavyo gantavyamita āśu te \|*  
*macchāsanāddhare loke cakramatraiva tiṣṭhatām \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. You shall go away quickly from this place. You shall not delay, O Viṣṇu, this is my behest. Let your discus stay in this world itself.

## \|\| 7.23.25 \|\|

*santānādityasaṃsthānācchivatvavacanādapi \|*  
*ahaṃ ghorataraṃ tasmāccakramanyaddadāmi te \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. I shall give you a discus more terrible than the sun at the time of dissolution and more efficacious than the words of Śiva.

## \|\| 7.23.26 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*etaduktvā haro'lekhīddivyaṃ kālānalaprabham \|*  
*paraṃ cakraṃ pradīptaṃ hi sarvaduṣṭavināśanam \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. After saying this Śiva created another blazing discus, as lustrous as the Kāla fire, for the destruction of the wicked.

## \|\| 7.23.27 \|\|

*viṣṇave pradadau cakraṃ ghorārkāyutasuprabham \|*  
*sarvāmaramunīndrāṇāṃ rakṣakāya mahātmane \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. He gave the discus, as lustruous as ten thousand fierce suns, to Viṣṇu, the great Ātman, the protector of the gods and the leading sages.

## \|\| 7.23.28 \|\|

*labdhvā sudarśanaṃ cānyañcakraṃ paramadīptimit \|*  
*uvāca vibudhāṃstatra viṣṇurbuddhimatāṃ varaḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. After securing another Sudarśana discus of more brilliance, Viṣṇu the foremost of intelligent gods spoke to the gods.

## \|\| 7.23.29 \|\|

*sarvadevavarā yūyaṃ madvākyaṃ śṛṇutādarāt \|*  
*kartavyantattathā śīghraṃ tataśśaṃ vo bhaviṣyati \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. “O ye gods, you listen to my statement eagerly. You must act accordingly and immediately. It will be to your advantage.

## \|\| 7.23.30 \|\|

*divyā varāṃganāssanti pātāle yauvanānvitāḥ \|*  
*tābhiḥ sārddhaṃ mahākrīḍāṃ yaḥ karotu karotu saḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. There are many youthful maidens in the nether regions exquisitely dressed. Any one of you can go and indulge in sexual dalliance with them,”

## \|\| 7.23.31 \|\|

*tacchutvā keśavādvākyaṃ śūrāstridaśayonayaḥ \|*  
*praveṣṭukāmāḥ pātālambabhūvurviṣṇunā saha \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. On hearing these words of Viṣṇu the valorous gods became desirous of entering the nether regions along with Viṣṇu.

## \|\| 7.23.32 \|\|

*vicāramatha vijñāya tantadā bhagavānhara \|*  
*krodhācchāpandadau ghoraṃ devayonyaṣṭakasya ca \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. Knowing their intention then, lord Śiva angrily cursed all the eight types of the gods.

## \|\| 7.23.33 \|\|

*hara uvāca \|*  
*varjayitvā muniṃ śāntaṃ dānavānvā madaṃśajam \|*  
*idaṃ yaḥ praviśetsthānaṃ tasya syānnidhanaṃ kṣaṇāt \|\| 33 \|\|*

Śiva said:—

33\. “Excepting a quiescent sage and the Dānavas born of my parts whoever enters this place shall die in a trice.”

## \|\| 7.23.34 \|\|

*śrutvā vākyamidaṃ ghoraṃ manuṣyahitavardhanam \|*  
*pratyākhyātāstu rudreṇa devāssvagṛhamā yayuḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. After hearing these terrible words which are conducive to the increase of the benefit of human beings, the gods thus restrained by Rudra returned to their own abodes.

## \|\| 7.23.35 \|\|

*evaṃ strīlaḥ paro viṣṇuśśivena pratiśāsitaḥ \|*  
*svarlokamagamadvyāsa svāsthyaṃ prāpa jagacca tat \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. Thus Viṣṇu who was excessively indulgent in women was chastised by Śiva. O Vyāsa, he returned to heaven. The world attained normalcy.

## \|\| 7.23.36 \|\|

*vṛṣeśvaro'pi bhagavācchaṃkaro bhaktavatsalaḥ \|*  
*itthaṃ kṛtvā devakāryaṃ jagāma svagirīśvaram \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. Lord Śiva, favourably disposed towards his devotees, returned to his mountain after carrying out the task of the gods in the guise of a bull.

## \|\| 7.23.37 \|\|

*vṛṣeśvarāvatārastu varṇitaśśaṃkarasya ca \|*  
*viṣṇumohaharaśśarvastrailokyasukhakārakaḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. Thus the bull incarnation of Śiva that dispelled Viṣṇu’s delusion and caused happiness to the three worlds, has been narrated.

## \|\| 7.23.38 \|\|

*pavitramidamākhyānaṃ śatrubādhāharamparam \|*  
*svargyaṃ yaśasyamāyuṣyaṃ bhuktimuktipradaṃ satām \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. This narrative is sacred. It dispels the harassment from enemies. It is conducive to the attainment of heaven, fame and longevity. It confers worldly pleasures and salvation to the good.

## \|\| 7.23.39 \|\|

*ya idaṃ śṛṇuyādbhaktyā śrāvayedvai samāhitaḥ \|*  
*sa bhuktvā sakalānkāmānante mokṣa mavāpnuyāt \|*  
*tathā paṭhiti yo hīdaṃ pāṭhayetsudhiyo narān \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. He who listens to this with devotion and narrates this with purity enjoys all pleasures here and attains salvation hereafter. So also is the case with him who reads or teaches this to intelligent men.

## Chapter 24 - Pippalāda incarnation of Śiva

## \|\| 7.24.1 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*pippalādākhyaparamamavatāraṃ maheśituḥ \|*  
*śṛṇu prājña mahāprītyā bhaktivardhanamuttamam \|\| 1 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

1\. O intelligent one, listen with very great pleasure to the Pippalāda incarnation of Śiva that is excellent and that inspires devotion.

## \|\| 7.24.2 \|\|

*yaḥ purā gadito vipro dadhīcirmunisattamaḥ \|*  
*mahāśaivassupratāpī cyāvanirbhṛguvaṃśajaḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Formerly there was a great brahmin and an excellent sage Dadhīci. He was born of Cyavana of Bhṛgu family. He was a great devotee of Śiva and very powerful.

## \|\| 7.24.3 \|\|

*kṣuveṇa saha saṃgrāme yena viṣṇuḥ parājitaḥ \|*  
*sanirjaro'tha saṃśapto maheśvarasahāyinā \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. In the fight with Kṣuva, Viṣṇu and other gods were defeated by this sage who had the support of Śiva and they were cursed.

## \|\| 7.24.4 \|\|

*tasya patnī mahābhāgā suvarcā nāmanāmataḥ \|*  
*mahāpativratā sādhvī yayā śaptā divaukasaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. He had a fortunate wife Suvarcā, who was very noble and chaste. The gods were cursed by her.

## \|\| 7.24.5 \|\|

*tasmāttasyāṃ mahādevo nānālīlāviśāradaḥ \|*  
*prādurbabhūva tejasvī pippalādeti nāmataḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. The great lord, an expert in different kinds of sports and extremely brilliant was born of her in the name of Pippalāda.

## \|\| 7.24.6 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityākarṇya muniśreṣṭho nandīśvaravaco'dbhutam \|*  
*sanatkumāraḥ provāca nataskandhaḥ kṛtāñjaliḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

6\. On hearing these wonderful words of Nandīśvara, the excellent sage Sanatkumāra spoke with palms joined in reverence and shoulders drooping down.

## \|\| 7.24.7 \|\|

*sanatkumāra uvāca \|*  
*nandīśvara mahāprājña sākṣādrudrasvarūpadhṛk \|*  
*dhanyastvaṃ sadgurustāta śrāviteyaṃ kathādbhutā \|\| 7 \|\|*

Sanatkumāra said:—

7\. O Nandīśvara, O intelligent one, you are blessed in that you have assumed the form of Rudra himself. O dear, you are my excellent preceptor. This wonderful story has been narrated by you.

## \|\| 7.24.8 \|\|

*kṣuveṇa saha saṃgrāme śruto viṣṇupurā jayaḥ \|*  
*brahmaṇā me purā tāta tacchāpaśca śilādaja \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. O son of Śilāda, from my father Brahmā, the account of the battle with Kṣuva and the defeat of Viṣṇu has already been heard by me. So also the curse has been heard by me.

## \|\| 7.24.9 \|\|

*adhunā śrotumicchāmi devaśāpaṃ suvarcayā \|*  
*dattaṃ paścātpippalādacaritaṃ maṅgalāyanam \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. Now, I was to hear about the curse that Suvarcā pronounced on the gods. Afterwards, I wish to hear the auspicious story of Pippalāda.

## \|\| 7.24.10 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*iti śrutvātha śailādirvidhiputravacaśśubham \|*  
*pratyuvāca prasannātmā smṛtvā śivapadāmbujam \|\| 10 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

10\. On hearing these auspicious words of the son of Brahmā the delighted son of Śilāda remembered the lotus-like feet of Śiva and replied.

## \|\| 7.24.11 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ekadā nirjarāssarve vāsavādyā munīśvara \|*  
*vṛtrāsurasahāyaiśca daityairāsanparājitāḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

11\. O great sage, once Indra and other gods were defeated by the Daityas who had the support of the demon Vṛtra.

## \|\| 7.24.12 \|\|

*svāni svāni varāstrā ṇi dadhīcasyāśrame'khilāḥ \|*  
*nikṣipya sahasā sadyo'bhavandevāḥ parājitāḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. All the gods had formerly kept their respective weapons in the hermitage of Dadhīca. Hence they were immediately defeated.

## \|\| 7.24.13 \|\|

*tadā sarve surāssendrā vadhyamānāstatharṣayaḥ \|*  
*brahmalokagatāśśīghraṃ procuḥ svaṃ vyasanaṃ ca tat \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Then the harassed gods and the sages along with Indra immediately went to Brahmā’s region and narrated their distress to him.

## \|\| 7.24.14 \|\|

*tacchrutvā devavacanaṃ brahmā lokapitāmahaḥ \|*  
*sarvaṃ śaśaṃsa tattvena tvaṣṭuścaiva cikīrṣitam \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. On hearing the words of the gods Brahmā, the grandfather of the worlds, mentioned the details of what Tvaṣṭṛ was desirous of doing.

## \|\| 7.24.15 \|\|

*bhavadvadhārthaṃ janitastvaṣṭrāyaṃ tapasā surāḥ \|*  
*vṛtro nāma mahātejāḥ sarvadaityādhipo mahān \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. This Asura has been created by Tvaṣṭṛ by means of his penance in order to kill you. The name of that Asura is Vṛtra. He is of great brilliance and the leader of all Daityas.

## \|\| 7.24.16 \|\|

*atha prayatnaḥ kriyatāṃ bhavedasya vadho yathā \|*  
*tatropāyaṃ śṛṇu prājña dharmahetorvadāmi te \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. You shall make such efforts as would ensure his death. O intelligent one, listen to the means thereof. For the sake of virtue I shall tell you the same.

## \|\| 7.24.17 \|\|

*mahāmunirdadhīciryassa tapasvī jitendriyaḥ \|*  
*lebhe śivaṃ samārādhya vajrāsthitvavarampurā \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. There is a great sage Dadhīci of great penance and of full control of his sense-organs. After propitiating Śiva he secured the boon of adamantine bones formerly.

## \|\| 7.24.18 \|\|

*tasyāsthīnyeva yācadhvaṃ sa dāsyati na saṃśaya \|*  
*nirmāya tairdaṇḍavajraṃ vṛtraṃ jahi na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. You all request for his bones. He will undoubtedly give them to you. Make the thunderbolt staff therewith and kill Vṛtra without hesitation.

## \|\| 7.24.19 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*tacchrutvā brahmavacanaṃ śakro gurusamanvitaḥ \|*  
*āgacchatsāmaraḥ sadyo dadhīcyāśramamuttamam \|\| 19 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

19\. On hearing the words of Brahmā, Indra accompanied by his preceptor and other gods went immediately to the excellent hermitage of Dadhīci.

## \|\| 7.24.20 \|\|

*dṛṣṭvā tatra muniṃ śakraḥ suvarcānvitamādarāt \|*  
*nanāma sāñjalirnamraḥ saguruḥ sāmaraśca tam \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. There Indra saw the sage in the company of Suvarcā. Along with the preceptor and other gods he humbly and respectfully bowed to him with palms joined in reverence.

## \|\| 7.24.21 \|\|

*tadabhiprāyamājñāya sa munirbudhasattamaḥ \|*  
*svapatnīṃ preṣayāmāsa suvarcāṃ svāśramāntaram \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. On realising his intention the sage, the most excellent of scholars sent his wife Suvarcā to the inner apartments of the hermitage.

## \|\| 7.24.22 \|\|

*tatassa devarājaśca sāmaraḥ svārthasādhakaḥ \|*  
*arthaśāstraparo bhūtvā munīśaṃ vākyamabravīt \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. Then the king of gods, accompanied by the gods bent upon achieving his own object, and most efficient in polity spoke to the great sage.

## \|\| 7.24.23 \|\|

*śakra uvāca \|*  
*tvaṣṭrā viprakṛtāḥ sarve vayandevāstatharṣayaḥ \|*  
*śaraṇyaṃ tvāṃ mahāśaivaṃ dātāraṃ śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

Indra said:

23\. All of us, the gods and the sages are oppressed by Tvaṣṭṛ. We seek refuge in you, worthy of refuge, a great devotee of Śiva and a liberal donor.

## \|\| 7.24.24 \|\|

*svāsthīni dehi no vipra mahāvajramayāni hi \|*  
*asthnā te svapaviṃ kṛtvā haniṣyāmi suradruham \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. O brahmin, give us your bones of adamantine strength. I shall make my thunderbolt with your love and kill the demon.

## \|\| 7.24.25 \|\|

*ityuktastena sa muniḥ paropakaraṇe rataḥ \|*  
*dhyātvā śivaṃ svanāthaṃ hi visasarja kalevaram \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Thus requested, the sage fond of helping others meditated on lord Śiva and abandoned his body.

## \|\| 7.24.26 \|\|

*brahmalokaṃ gatassadyassa munirdhvastabandhanaḥ \|*  
*puṣpavṛṣṭirabhūttatra sarve vismayamāgatāḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Immediately he reached Brahma’s region with his bondage of actions quashed. There was a shower of flowers and the people were agreeably surprised.

## \|\| 7.24.27 \|\|

*atha gāṃ surabhiṃ śakra āhūyāśu hyalehayat \|*  
*astranirmitaye tvāṣṭraṃ nidi deśa tadasthibhiḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Indra then called the divine cow Surabhi to lick up the marrow etc. He directed Viśvakarman to make the weapon with the bones.

## \|\| 7.24.28 \|\|

*viśvakarmā tadājñaptaścaklṛpe'strāṇi kṛtsnaśaḥ \|*  
*tadasthibhirvajramayassudṛḍhaiśśivavarcasā \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. At his behest Viśvakarman made all the weapons with the sage’s bones—very firm and adamantine—that had the brilliance of Śiva.

## \|\| 7.24.29 \|\|

*vaṃśodbhavaṃ vajraṃ śaro brahmaśirastathā \|*  
*anyāsthibhirbahūni svaparāṇyastrāṇi nirmame \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. With his backbone he made the thunderbolt, arrow and the missile called Brahmaśiras. With the other bones he made many other weapons.

## \|\| 7.24.30 \|\|

*tamindro vajramudyamya varddhitaḥ śivavarcasā \|*  
*vṛtramabhyadravatkruddho mune rudra ivāntakam \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. O sage, flourishing with Śiva’s brilliance, the infuriated Indra lifted the thunderbolt and rushed against Vṛtra like Rudra rushing against the god of death.

## \|\| 7.24.31 \|\|

*tataḥ śakrassusannaddhastena vajreṇa sa drutam \|*  
*uccakarta śiro vārtraṃ giriśṛṃgamivaujasā \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. Indra who was well prepared, cut off the peaklike head of Vṛtra with the thunderbolt forcefully.

## \|\| 7.24.32 \|\|

*tadā samutsavastāta babhūva tridivaukasām \|*  
*tuṣṭuvurnirjarāśśakrampetuḥ kusumavṛṣṭayaḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. There was great jubilation among the heaven-dwellers. O dear, the gods eulogised Indra. Showers of flowers fell on him.

## \|\| 7.24.33 \|\|

*iti te kathitantāta prasaṃgāccari tantvidam \|*  
*pippalādāvatārambhe śṛṇu śambhormahādarāt \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. O dear, thus this story of Vṛtra’s slaughter is related to you as arising from the context. Now listen respectfully to the incarnation of Śiva as Pippalāda.

## \|\| 7.24.34 \|\|

*suvarcā sā muneḥ patnī dadhīcasya mahātmanaḥ \|*  
*yayau svamāśramābhyantastadājñaptā pativratā \|\| 34 \|\|*
