34\. At the behest of her lord Suvarcā the chaste wife of the sage Dadhīci, the noble soul went to the inner apartments of the hermitage.

## \|\| 7.24.35 \|\|

*āgatya tatra sā dṛṣṭvā na patiṃ svantapasvinī \|*  
*gṛhakāryaṃ ca sā kṛtvākhilampatinideśataḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. At the behest of her husband she finished her household duties and came there. The pitiable lady could not see her lord there.

## \|\| 7.24.36 \|\|

*ājagāma punastatra paśyantī bahvaśobhanam \|*  
*devāṃśca tānmuniśreṣṭha suvarcā vismitābhavat \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. After coming there and seeing the most inauspicious occurrence there, O excellent sage, the surprised Suvarcā spoke to the gods.

## \|\| 7.24.37 \|\|

*jñātvā ca tatsarvamidaṃ surāṇāṃ kṛtyaṃ tadānīñca cukopa sādhvī \|*  
*dadau tadā śāpamatīva ruṣṭā teṣāṃ suvarcā ṛṣivaryabhāryā \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. The chaste lady, on realising that all this was the action of the gods, became angry. The infuriated wife of the excellent sage, Suvarcā, cursed them.

## \|\| 7.24.38 \|\|

*suvarcovāca \|*  
*aho surā druṣṭatarāśca sarve svakāryadakṣā hyabudhāśca lubdhāḥ \|*  
*tasmācca sarve paśavo bhavantu sendrāśca me'dyaprabhṛtītyuvāca \|\| 38 \|\|*

Suvarcā said:—

38\. “Ah! the gods are very wicked. They are covetous, stupid but very skifīil in manipulating their own affairs. Let them all, inclusive of Indra, become beasts from today.”

## \|\| 7.24.39 \|\|

*evaṃ śāpandadau teṣāṃ surāṇāṃ saḥ tapasvinī \|*  
*saśakrāṇāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ suvarcā munikāminī \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. Suvarcā, a woman saint and the beloved wife of the sage cursed the gods inclusive of Indra thus.

## \|\| 7.24.40 \|\|

*anugantumpaterlokamathecchatsā pativratā \|*  
*citāṃ cakra samedhobhiḥ supavitrairmanasvinī \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. That chaste lady desired to go to the worlds of her husband. With the sacred twigs, the noble lady prepared the funeral pyre.

## \|\| 7.24.41 \|\|

*tato nabhogirā prāha suvarcāntāmmunipriyām \|*  
*āśvāsayantī giriśapreritā sukhadāyinī \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. Then a pleasant celestial voice urged by Śiva spoke to Suvarcā, consoling her.

## \|\| 7.24.42 \|\|

*ākāśavāṇyuvāca \|*  
*sāhasaṃ na kuru prājñe śṛṇu me paramaṃ vacaḥ \|*  
*munitejastvadudare tadutpādaya yatnataḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

The Celestial Voice said:—

42\. O intelligent lady, do not carry out this calamitous task. Listen to my weighty words. The semen of the sage is in your womb. Develop it carefully.

## \|\| 7.24.43 \|\|

*tataḥ svābhīṣṭacaraṇandevi kartuntvamarhasi \|*  
*sagarbhā na dahedgātramiti brahmanideśanam \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. O gentle lady, later on, you may do as you please. It is the command of Brahmā that a pregnant woman should not bum herself.

## \|\| 7.24.44 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityuktvā sā nabhovāṇī virarāma munīśvara \|*  
*tāṃ śrutvā sā muneḥ patnī vismitābhūtkṣaṇaṃ ca sā \|\| 44 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

44\. O great sage, after saying this the celestial voice stopped. On hearing that voice the wife of the sage was surprised for a moment.

## \|\| 7.24.45 \|\|

*suvarcā sā mahāsādhvī patilokamabhīpsatī \|*  
*upaviśyāśmanā bhūyaḥ sodaraṃ vidadāra ha \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. That great chaste lady, Suvarcā, desirous of attaining the world of her husband sat down and broke open her belly with a stone.

## \|\| 7.24.46 \|\|

*nirgato jaṭharāttasyā garbho munivarasya saḥ \|*  
*mahādivyatanurdīpto bhāsayaṃśca diśodaśa \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. The foetus of the child of the great sage! of brilliant divine body, came out of her belly illuminating all the ten quarters.

## \|\| 7.24.47 \|\|

*sākṣādrudrāvatāro'sau dadhīca varatejasaḥ \|*  
*prādurbhūtassvayantāta svalīlākaraṇe kṣamaḥ \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. O dear, it was the incarnation of Rudra himself born of the sage Dadhīci. He was competent to perform his divine sports. He therefore manifested himself.

## \|\| 7.24.48-49 \|\|

*tandṛṣṭvā svasutandivyaṃ svarūpammunikāminī \|*  
*suvarcājñāya manasā sākṣādrudrāvatārakam \|\| 48 \|\|*

*prahṛṣṭābhūnmahāsādhvī praṇamyāśu nunāva sā \|*  
*svahṛdi sthāpayāmāsa tatsvarūpammunīśvara \|\| 49 \|\|*

48-49. On seeing her son of divine form, the beloved wife of the sage Suvarcā realised in hef mind that it was the incarnation of Rudra himself. The great chaste lady became delighted, bowed to him and eulogised him. O great sage, that form of the child was impressed on her heart.

## \|\| 7.24.50 \|\|

*suvarcā tanayaṃ taṃ ca prahasya vimalekṣaṇā \|*  
*jananī prāha suprītyā patilokamabhīpsatī \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. Suvarcā of pure vision, the mother, desirous of going to her husband’s region, smilingly and lovingly spoke to her son.

## \|\| 7.24.51 \|\|

*suvarcovāca \|*  
*he tāta parameśāna cirantiṣṭhāsya sannidhau \|*  
*aśvatthasya mahābhāga sarveṣāṃ sukhado bhaveḥ \|\| 51 \|\|*

Suvarcā said:—

51\. O dear, O lord Śiva, O fortunate one, stay near this holy fig tree for a long time. Be the bestower of happiness to all.

## \|\| 7.24.52 \|\|

*māmājñāpaya suprītyā patilokāya cādhunā \|*  
*tatrasthāhaṃ ca patinā tvāṃ dhyāye rudrarūpiṇam \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. Out of affection allow me to go to the region of my husband. Staying there along with my husband I shall meditate on you in the form of Rudra.

## \|\| 7.24.53 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityevaṃ sā babhāṣe'tha suvarcā tanayamprati \|*  
*patimanvagamatsādhvī parameṇa samādhinā \|\| 53 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

53\. After speaking thus to her son that chaste lady Suvarcā followed her husband by means of the great transcendental meditation.

## \|\| 7.24.54 \|\|

*evandadhīcapatnī sā patinā saṃgatā mune \|*  
*śivalokaṃ samāsādya siṣeve śaṅkarammudā \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. O sage, thus the wife of Dadhīci was re-united with her husband. After reaching Śiva’s region she served Śiva devotedly.

## \|\| 7.24.55 \|\|

*etasminnantare devāssendrāśca munibhissaha \|*  
*tatrājagmustvarā tāta āhūtā iva harṣitāḥ \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. In the meantime the delighted gods inclusive of Indra, hurriedly came there along with Ac sages as though invited there.

## \|\| 7.24.56 \|\|

*harirbrahmā ca suprītyāvatīrṇaṃ śaṃkaraṃ bhuvi \|*  
*suvarcāyāṃ dadhīcādvā yayatussvagaṇaissaha \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. Viṣṇu and Brahmā too, accompanied by their attendants came over there to see Śiva who had taken incarnation in the world through Suvarcā and Dadhīci.

## \|\| 7.24.57 \|\|

*tatra dṛṣṭvāvatīrṇantaṃ muniputratvamāgatam \|*  
*rudraṃ sarve praṇemuśca tuṣṭuvurbaddhapāṇayaḥ \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. On seeing him there, incarnated as the sage’s son, they bowed to him with palms joined in reverence and made obeisances.

## \|\| 7.24.58 \|\|

*tadotsavo mahānāsīddevānāṃ munisattama \|*  
*nedurdundubhayastatra nartakyo nanṛturmudā \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. O excellent sage, there was great jubilation among the gods. The Dundubhis were sounded and dancing girls danced joyously.

## \|\| 7.24.59 \|\|

*jagurgandharvaputrāśca kinnarā vādyavādakāḥ \|*  
*vādayāmāsuramarāḥ puṣpa vṛṣṭiṃ ca cakrire \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. The sons of Gandharvas sang. Kinnaras and other players played on instruments. The gods showered flowers.

## \|\| 7.24.60 \|\|

*pippalasya śarvapiturvilasantaṃ sutaṃ ca tam \|*  
*saṃskṛtya vidhivatsarve viṣṇvādyāstuṣṭuvuḥ punaḥ \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. After consecrating the refulgent child and the fig tree who became the father of Rudra, Viṣṇu and other gods eulogised him again.

## \|\| 7.24.61 \|\|

*pippalādeti tannāma cakre brahmā prasannadhīḥ \|*  
*prasanno bhava deveśa ityūce hariṇā suraiḥ \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. The delighted Brahmā named him Pippalāda. Viṣṇu and the gods said “O lord of the gods be pleased”.

## \|\| 7.24.62 \|\|

*ityuktvā tamanujñāya brahmā viṣṇussurāstathā \|*  
*svaṃsvaṃ dhāma yayussarve vidhāya ca mahotsavam \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. After saying this and making great jubilation, Brahmā, Viṣṇu and the gods went to their respective abodes.

## \|\| 7.24.63 \|\|

*atha rudraḥ pippalādo'śvatthamūle mahāprabhuḥ \|*  
*tatāpa suciraṃ kālaṃ lokānāṃ hitakāmyayā \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. Then Rudra, the great lord in the form of Pippalāda, performed a penance for a long time at the foot of the fig tree, with a desire to bestow benefit on the worlds.

## \|\| 7.24.64 \|\|

*itthaṃ sutapatastasya pippalādasya sammukhe \|*  
*mahākālo vyatīyāya lokacaryānusāriṇaḥ \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. As Pippalāda, following the convention of the world, performed the penance, a long time passed by.

## Chapter 25 - Pippalāda incarnation of Śiva (2)

## \|\| 7.25.1 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*atha loke vyavasthāya dharmasya sthāpanecchayā \|*  
*mahālīlāṃ cakāreśastāmaho sanmune śṛṇu \|\| 1 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

1\. After arranging and establishing Dharma with a desire for its good foundation, lord Śiva performed great sports. O good sage, listen to that.

## \|\| 7.25.2 \|\|

*ekadā puṣpabhadrāyāṃ snātuṃ gacchanmunīśvaraḥ \|*  
*dadarśa padmāṃ yuvatīṃ śivāṃśāṃ sumanoharām \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Once while going to the river Puṣpabhadrā for his ablution, the great sage saw the beautiful youthful maiden Padmā, a part of Śivā herself.

## \|\| 7.25.3 \|\|

*tallipsustatpituḥ sthānamanaraṇyasya bhūpateḥ \|*  
*jagāma bhuvanācārī lokatattvavicakṣaṇaḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Desirous of getting her, the sage traversing the world and an expert in following the established conventions went to the abode of king Anaraṇya, her father.

## \|\| 7.25.4 \|\|

*rājā narāṇāṃ taṃ dṛṣṭvā praṇamya ca bhayākulaḥ \|*  
*madhuparkādikaṃ dattvā pūjayāmāsa bhaktitaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. On seeing him, the awe-struck king of men bowed to him. Offering him Madhuparka he worshipped him devotedly.

## \|\| 7.25.5 \|\|

*snehātsarvaṃ gṛhītvā sa yayāce kanyakāṃ muniḥ \|*  
*maunī babhūva nṛpatiḥ kiṃcinnirvaktumakṣamaḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. The sage accepted everything with regard and requested for the hand of his daughter. Thereupon the king was unable to say anything. He was silent.

## \|\| 7.25.6 \|\|

*muniḥ provāca nṛpatiṃ kanyāṃ me dehi bhaktitaḥ \|*  
*anyathā bhasmasātsarvaṃ kariṣyehaṃ tvayā saha \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. The sage repeated the request, saying “Give me your daughter devoutly. Otherwise I will reduce everything to ashes along with you.”

## \|\| 7.25.7 \|\|

*atho babhūvurācchannāḥ sarve rājajanāstadā \|*  
*tejasā pippalādasya dādhīcasya mahāmune \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. O great sage, then all the people of the king were enveloped by the brilliance of Pippalāda, son of Dadhīci.

## \|\| 7.25.8 \|\|

*atha rājā mahābhīto vilapya ca muhurmuhuḥ \|*  
*kanyāmalaṃkṛtāmpadmāṃ vṛddhāya munaye dadau \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Then the king was much frightened and he lamented again and again. He gave his daughter Padmā, fully bedecked in ornaments to the old sage.

## \|\| 7.25.9 \|\|

*padmāṃ vivāhya sa muniśśivāṃśāmbhūpateḥ sutām \|*  
*pippalādo gṛhītvā tāṃ muditaḥ svāśramaṃ yayau \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. After marrying Padmā, the daughter of the king and a part of Śivā, the sage Pippalāda joyously took her to his hermitage.

## \|\| 7.25.10 \|\|

*tatra gatvā munivaro vayasā jarjarodhikaḥ \|*  
*uvāca nāryā sa tayā tapasvīnātilampaṭaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. After going to the hermitage the aged excellent sage stayed there with that woman. The sage was not too indulgent.

## \|\| 7.25.11 \|\|

*atho'naraṇyakanyā sā siṣeve bhaktito munim \|*  
*karmaṇā manasā vācā lakṣmīrnārāyaṇaṃ yathā \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Then the daughter of Anaraṇya served the sage with devotion, in mind, word and deed, like Lakṣmī serving Viṣṇu.

## \|\| 7.25.12 \|\|

*itthaṃ sa pippalādo hi śivāṃśo munisattamaḥ \|*  
*reme tayā yuvatyā ca yuvābhūya svalīlayā \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Then the excellent sage Pippalāda, a part of Śiva, became a young man by means of his divine sport and sported with that young lady.

## \|\| 7.25.13 \|\|

*daśa putrā mahātmāno babhūvussutapasvinaḥ \|*  
*muneḥ pitussamāḥ sarve padmāyāḥ sukhavarddhanāḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Ten noble sons, all of them great sons, were born to the sage. They were equal to their father and increased the pleasure of Padmā.

## \|\| 7.25.14 \|\|

*evaṃ līlāvatāro hi śaṃkarasya mahāprabhoḥ \|*  
*pippalādo munivaro nānālīlākaraḥ prabhuḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. This is the sportive incarnation of Śiva the great lord. The excellent sage Pippalāda was the lord indulgent in different kinds of divine sports.

## \|\| 7.25.15 \|\|

*yena datto varaḥ prītyā lokebhyo hi dayālunā \|*  
*dṛṣṭvā loke śaneḥ pīḍāṃ sarveṣāmanivāriṇīm \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. On seeing the harassment by the planet Saturn of every one irremediably, the sympathetic lord lovingly granted a boon to all the world.

## \|\| 7.25.16 \|\|

*ṣoḍaśābdāvadhi nṛṇāṃ janmato na bhavecca sā \|*  
*tathā ca śivabhaktānāṃ satyametaddhi me vacaḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. “For sixteen years from birth no man will have that affliction. Similarly to the devotees of Śiva too. This word of mine is true.

## \|\| 7.25.17 \|\|

*athānādṛtya madvākyaṃ kuryātpīḍāṃ śaniḥ kvacit \|*  
*teṣāṃ nṛṇāṃ tadā sa syādbhasmasānna hi saṃśayaḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. If Saturn were to disregard my instruction and harass men, he will undoubtedly be reduced to ashes.”

## \|\| 7.25.18 \|\|

*iti tadbhayatastāta vikṛtopi śanaiścaraḥ \|*  
*teṣāṃ na kurute pīḍāṃ kadācidgrahasattamaḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. O dear, thus, afraid of him the excellent planet Saturn, though in aberration, does not afflict men at anytime.

## \|\| 7.25.19 \|\|

*iti līlāmanuṣyasya pippalādasya sanmune \|*  
*kathitaṃ sucaritrante sarvakāmaphalapradam \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. Thus I have narrated to you the good story of the great sage Pippalāda who bad assumed the form of a human being in the course of his divine sports. The story yields the fruit of all desires.

## \|\| 7.25.20 \|\|

*gādhiśca kauśikaścaiva pippalādo mahāmuniḥ \|*  
*śanaiścarakṛtāṃ pīḍāṃ nāśayanti smṛtāstrayaḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. These three—Gādhi, Kauśika and the great sage Pippalāda quell the affliction of Śani on being remembered.

## \|\| 7.25.21-22 \|\|

*pippalādasya caritaṃ padmācaritasaṃyutam \|*  
*yaḥ paṭhecchṛṇuyādvāpi subhaktyā bhuvi mānavaḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

*śanipīḍāvināśārthametaccaritamuttamam \|*  
*yaḥ paṭhecchaṇuyādvāpi sarvānkāmānavāpnuyāt \|\| 22 \|\|*

21-22. For the dissolution of the affliction of Saturn, the man who reads or listens devoutly to the story of Pippalāda along with that of Padmā attains all desires in this world.

## \|\| 7.25.23 \|\|

*dhanyo munivaro jñānī mahāśaivaḥ satāmpriyaḥ \|*  
*asya putro maheśānaḥ pippalādākhya ātmavān \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Blessed indeed is the great sage, who was wise and a great devotee of Śiva. He was a favourite of good men. Lord Śiva named Pippalāda was his noble son.

## \|\| 7.25.24 \|\|

*idamākhyānamanaghaṃ svargyaṃ kugrahapoṣahṛt \|*  
*sarvakāmapradantāta śivabhaktivivarddhanam \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. This pious narrative is conducive to the attainment of heaven. It quells the adverse effects of the evil planets. O dear, it yields all desires and enhances devotion to Śiva.

## Chapter 26 - Śiva’s incarnation as Vaiśyanātha

## \|\| 7.26.1 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*śṛṇu tāta pravakṣyāmi śivasya paramātmanaḥ \|*  
*avatāraṃ paramānandaṃ vaiśyanāthāhvayaṃ mune \|\| 1 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

1\. O dear sage, listen to me. I shall tell you about Vaiśyanātha, the incarnation of Śiva, the supreme soul that yields great bliss.

## \|\| 7.26.2 \|\|

*nandigrāme purā kācinmahānandeti viśrutā \|*  
*babhūva vāravanitā śivabhaktā susundarī \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Formerly, in Nandigrāma there was a prostitute known as Mahānandā. She was very beautiful and a devotee of Śiva.

## \|\| 7.26.3 \|\|

*mahāvibhavasampannā sudhanāḍhyā mahojjvalā \|*  
*nānāratnaparicchinna śṛṅgārarasanirbharā \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. She was very prosperous, rich and brilliant. She was bedecked in gems of different kinds. She was voluptuous in love.

## \|\| 7.26.4 \|\|

*sarvasaṃgīta vidyāsu nipuṇātimanoharā \|*  
*tasyā geyena hṛṣyanti rājñyo rājāna eva ca \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. She was an adept in all lores of music. She was delightful to all. Queens and kings were delighted by her songs.

## \|\| 7.26.5 \|\|

*samānarca sadā sāmbaṃ sā veśyā śaṃkaraṃ mudā \|*  
*śivanāmajapāsaktā bhasmarudrākṣabhūṣaṇā \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. She always wonhipped Śiva and Pārvatī with great joy. She was interested in recounting the names of Śiva. She used to wear ashes and Rudrākṣa.

## \|\| 7.26.6 \|\|

*śivaṃ sampūjya sā nityaṃ sevantī jagadīśvaram \|*  
*nanarta parayā bhaktyā gāyantī śivasadyaśaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. After worshipping Śiva, the lord of the universe she used to dance with great devotion and sing the great glory of Śiva.

## \|\| 7.26.7 \|\|

*rudrākṣairbhūṣayitvaikaṃ markaṭaṃ caiva kukkuṭam \|*  
*karatālaiśca gītaiśca sadā nartayati sma sā \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. She kept a monkey and a cock which she decorated with Rudrākṣas. She made them dance singing herself and clapping her hands.

## \|\| 7.26.8 \|\|

*nṛtyamānau ca tau dṛṣṭvā śivabhaktiratā ca sā \|*  
*veśyā sma vihasatyuccaiḥ premṇā sarvasakhīyutā \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. On seeing them dance, she, engrossed in devotion to Śiva, used to laugh lovingly along with her friends and attendants.

## \|\| 7.26.9 \|\|

*rudrākṣaiḥ kṛtakeyūrakarṇā bharaṇamaṇḍanaḥ \|*  
*markaṭaḥ śikṣayā tasyāḥ puro nṛtyati bālavat \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. The monkey, as a result of her instruction, danced before her, like a boy bedecked in Rudrākṣas as ear-rings, and shoulderlets.

## \|\| 7.26.10 \|\|

*śikhāsaṃbaddharudrākṣaḥ kukkuṭaḥ kapinā saha \|*  
*nityaṃ nanarta nṛtyajñaḥ paśyatāṃ hitamāvahan \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. The cock too was bedecked in Rudrākṣa tied round its tuft. It too danced along with the monkey delighting the onlookers.

## \|\| 7.26.11 \|\|

*evaṃ sā kurvatī veśyā kautukamparamādarāt \|*  
*śivabhaktiratā nityaṃ mahānandabharā'bhavat \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Thus that prostitute indulging in these delightful pranks and pursuing devotional service to Śiva was very happy always.

## \|\| 7.26.12 \|\|

*śivabhaktiṃ prakurvantyā veśyāyā munisattama \|*  
*bahukālo vyatīyāya tasyāḥ paramasaukhyataḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. O excellent sage, a long time thus elapsed when she was quite happy performing her devotional service to Śiva.

## \|\| 7.26.13 \|\|

*ekadā ca gṛhe tasyā vaiśyo bhūtvā śivassvayam \|*  
*parīkṣituṃ ca tadbhāvamājagāma śubho vratī \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Once Śiva assumed the form of a merchant and visited her house to test her. He had assumed the auspicious form of a devotee.

## \|\| 7.26.14 \|\|

*tripuṇḍravilasadbhālo rudrākṣābharaṇaḥ kṛtī \|*  
*śivanāmajapāsakto jaṭilaḥ śaivaveṣabhṛt \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. His forehead was marked with Tripuṇḍra. He wore Rudrākṣa as ornaments. He was interested in recounting the names of Śiva. He was clad as a devotee of Śiva. He wore matted locks of hair.

## \|\| 7.26.15 \|\|

*sa bibhradbhasmanicayaṃ prakoṣṭhe varakaṃkaṇam \|*  
*mahāratnaparistīrṇaṃ rājate parakautukī \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. He had smeared ashes all over his body. Round his wrist was tied an excellent bangle set with gems.

## \|\| 7.26.16 \|\|

*tamāgataṃ susaṃpūjya sā veśyā parayā mudā \|*  
*svasthāne sādaraṃ vaiśyaṃ sundarī hi nyaveśayat \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. The beautiful prostitute worshipped and welcomed the visitor with great joy. She made him sit with great respect.

## \|\| 7.26.17 \|\|

*tatprakoṣṭhe varaṃ vīkṣya kaṃkaṇaṃ sumanoharam \|*  
*tasmiñjātaspṛhā sā ca taṃ provāca suvismitā \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. On seeing the excellent bangle round his wrist she coveted the same. In great surprise she said.

## \|\| 7.26.18 \|\|

*mahānandovāca \|*  
*mahāratnamayaścāyaṃ kaṃkaṇastvatkare sthitaḥ \|*  
*mano harati me sadyo divyastrībhūṣaṇocitaḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

Mahānandā said:—

18\. This bangle set with great gems around your arm captivates my mind. It is worthy of being an ornament of a celestial damsel.

## \|\| 7.26.19 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*iti tāṃ navaratnāḍhye saspṛhāṃ karabhūṣaṇe \|*  
*vīkṣyodāramatirvaiśyaḥ sasmitaṃ samabhāṣata \|\| 19 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

19\. On seeing that she wished for the bangle set with nine gems, the liberal-minded merchant spoke smilingly.

## \|\| 7.26.20 \|\|

*vaiśyanātha uvāca \|*  
*asminratnavare divye saspṛhaṃ yadi te manaḥ \|*  
*tvamevādhatsva suprītyā maulyamasya dadāsi kim \|\| 20 \|\|*

Vaiśyanātha said:—

20\. If your mind wishes for this excellent gemset ornament you can have it with pleasure. But what is the price you are willing to pay?

## \|\| 7.26.21 \|\|

*veśyovāca \|*  
*vayaṃ hi svairacāriṇyo veśyāstu na pativratāḥ \|*  
*asmatkulocito dharmo vyabhicāro na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

The prostitute said:—

21\. We are prostitutes flirting here and there and not chaste ladies. Undoubtedly the regular practice observed in our family is harlotry.

## \|\| 7.26.22 \|\|

*yadyetadakhilaṃ cittaṃ gṛhṇāti karabhūṣaṇam \|*  
*dinatrayamahorātraṃ patnī tava bhavāmyaham \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. If I take the bangle from you I shall be your wife for three full days and nights.

## \|\| 7.26.23 \|\|

*vaiśya uvāca \|*  
*tathāstu yadi te satyaṃ vacanaṃ vīravallabhe \|*  
*dadāmi ratnavalayaṃ trirātraṃ bhava me vadhūḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

The merchant said:—

23\. O beloved harlot, if your words are true, let it be as you say. I shall give you the gemset bangle. Be my wife for three days and nights.

## \|\| 7.26.24 \|\|

*etasminvyavahāre tu pramāṇaṃ śaśibhāskarau \|*  
*trivāraṃ satyamityuktvā hṛdayaṃ me spṛśa priye \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. In this deal the witnesses are the moon and the sun. Repeat three times “True” and touch my heart, O beloved.

## \|\| 7.26.25 \|\|

*veśyovāca \|*  
*dinatrayamahorātraṃ patnī bhūtvā tava prabho \|*  
*sahadharmaṃ carāmīti satyaṃsatyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

The prostitute said:—

25\. O lord, I shall be your wife for three days and carry out all wifely duties. This is true. This is undoubtedly true.

## \|\| 7.26.26 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityuktvā hi mahānandā trivāraṃ śaśibhāskarau \|*  
*pramāṇīkṛtya suprītyā sā taddhṛdayamaspṛśat \|\| 26 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

26\. After repeating this for three times, with the moon and the sun as witnesses, Mahānandā touched his heart with pleasure.

## \|\| 7.26.27 \|\|

*atha tasyai sa vaiśyastu pradattvā ratnakaṃkaṇam \|*  
*liṃgaṃ ratnamayaṃ tasya haste dattvedamabravīt \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. After giving her the gemset bangle that merchant handed to her a gemset phallic image and spoke thus.

## \|\| 7.26.28 \|\|

*vaiśyanātha uvāca \|*  
*idaṃ ratnamayaṃ liṃgaṃ śaivaṃ matprāṇadavalla bham \|*  
*rakṣaṇīyaṃ tvayā kānte gopanīyaṃ prayatnataḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

The merchant said:—

28\. O my beloved, this gemset phallic image is very dear to me. It shall be well-preserved strenuously and in secret by you.

## \|\| 7.26.29 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*evamastviti sā procya liṃgamādāya ratnajam \|*  
*nāṭyamaṇḍapikā madhye nidhāya prāviśadgṛham \|\| 29 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

29\. She said “Let it be so” and took the gemset phallic image. She kept it in the middle of the dancing platform and entered the house.

## \|\| 7.26.30 \|\|

*sā tena saṃgatā rātrau vaiśyena viṭadharmiṇā \|*  
*sukhaṃ suṣvāpa paryaṃke mṛdutalpopaśo bhite \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. During the night she had her sexual union with the merchant who behaved like a libertine. She then went to sleep on the couch brilliant with a soft bed.

## \|\| 7.26.31 \|\|

*tato niśīthasamaye mune vaiśyapatīcchayā \|*  
*akasmādutthitā vāṇī nṛtyamaṇḍapikāntare \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. O sage, then at midnight, at the will of the lordly merchant, suddenly there arose a fire in the middle of the dancing platform.

## \|\| 7.26.32 \|\|

*mahāprajvalito vahniḥ susamīrasahāyavān \|*  
*nāṭyamaṇḍapikāṃ tāta tāmeva sahasāvṛṇot \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. The blazing fire helped by a forceful wind enveloped the dancing platform all of a sudden.

## \|\| 7.26.33 \|\|

*maṇḍape dahyamāne tu sahasotthāya saṃbhramāt \|*  
*markaṭaṃ mocayāmāsa sā veśyā tatra bandhanāt \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. When the platform was burning the prostitute got up suddenly in her bewilderment and let the monkey free untying it.

## \|\| 7.26.34 \|\|

*sa markaṭo muktabandhaḥ kukkuṭena sahāmunā \|*  
*bhiyā dūraṃ hi dudrāva vidhūyāgnikaṇānbahūn \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. Freed from the bondage the monkey in its fear ran here and there along with the cock and scattered sparks of fire.

## \|\| 7.26.35 \|\|

*stambhena saha nirdagdhaṃ talliṃgaṃ śakalīkṛtam \|*  
*dṛṣṭvā veśyā sa vaiśyaśca duraṃtaṃ duḥkhamāpatuḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. On seeing the phallic image burnt along with the pillar and smashed utterly, the prostitute and the merchant felt endless distress.

## \|\| 7.26.36 \|\|

*dṛṣṭvā hyātmasamaṃ liṃgaṃ dagdhaṃ vaiśyapatistadā \|*  
*jñātuntadbhāvamantaḥsthammaraṇāya matindadhe \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. On seeing the phallic image as it were his very soul burnt, the lordly merchant desired to end his life in order to know her mentality.

## \|\| 7.26.37 \|\|

*niviśyetitarāṃ khedādvaiśyastāmāha duḥkhitām \|*  
*nānālīlo maheśānaḥ kautukānnaradehavān \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. In great despair and sorrow the merchant told her in grief. Lord Śiva who had assumed a human body just for pleasure indulged in different sports.

## \|\| 7.26.38 \|\|

*vaiśyapatiruvāca \|*  
*śivaliṃge tu nirbhinne dagdhe mahatprāṇavallabhe \|*  
*satyaṃ vacmi na sandeho nāhaṃ jīvitumutsahe \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. “O my beloved, when the phallic image is smashed and burnt I do not desire to live. I am telling you the truth. There is no doubt in this.

## \|\| 7.26.39 \|\|

*citāṃ kāraya me bhadre svabhṛtyaistvaṃ varairlaghu \|*  
*śive manassamāveśya pravekṣyāmi hutāśanam \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. O gentle lady, prepare a pyre for me immediately through your servants. With my mind fixed in Śiva, I shall enter the fire.

## \|\| 7.26.40 \|\|

*yadi brahmendraviṣṇvādyā vārayeyuḥ sametya mām \|*  
*tathāpyasmin kṣaṇe bhadre praviśāmi tyajāmyasūn \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. Even if Brahmā, Indra, Viṣṇu and others were to prevent me, O gentle lady, I shall enter the fire this moment and shall forsake my life.

## \|\| 7.26.41 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*tamevaṃ dṛḍhanirbandhaṃ sā vijñāya suduḥkhitā \|*  
*svabhṛtyaiḥ kārayāmāsa citāṃ svabhavanādbahiḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

41\. On realising his tenacious decision the harlot was much distressed. Outside her house she caused the pyre to be made through her servants.

## \|\| 7.26.42 \|\|

*tatassa vaiśyaśśiva eka eva pradakṣiṇīkṛtya samiddhamagnim \|*  
*viveśa paśyatsu nareṣu dhīraḥ sukautukī saṃgatibhāvamicchuḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. Then the merchant who was only Śiva himself went round the blazing fire. Desirous of knowing her intentions he entered the fire firmly.

## \|\| 7.26.43 \|\|

*dṛṣṭvā sā tadgatiṃ veśyā mahānandātivismitā \|*  
*anutāpaṃ ca yuvatī prapede munisattama \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. O excellent sage, on seeing him enter the pyre, the harlot was dismayed. The youthful maiden sympathised with him.

## \|\| 7.26.44 \|\|

*atha sā duḥkhitā veśyā smṛtvā dharma sunirmalam \|*  
*sarvānbaṃdhujanānvīkṣya babhāṣe karuṇaṃ vacaḥ \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. The miserable harlot remembered the sacred vow and said piteously glancing at all her kinsmen.

## \|\| 7.26.45 \|\|

*mahānandovāca \|*  
*ratnakaṃkaṇamādāya mayā satyamudāhṛtam \|*  
*dinatrayamahaṃ patnī vaiśyasyāmuṣya saṃmatā \|\| 45 \|\|*

Mahānandā said:—

45\. Taking the gemset bangle I had taken the pledge that I shall be the avowed wife of this merchant for three days.

## \|\| 7.26.46 \|\|

*karmaṇā matkṛtenāyaṃ mṛto vaiśyaḥ śivavratī \|*  
*tasmādahaṃ pravekṣyāmi sahānena hutāśanam \|\| 46 \|\|*

46 This merchant, the avowed Śaivite, died of my activities. Hence I shall enter fire along with him.

## \|\| 7.26.47 \|\|

*svadharmacāriṇī tyaktamācāryyai satyavādibhiḥ \|*  
*evaṃ kṛte mama prītyā satyaṃ mayi na naśyatu \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. Truthfiil preceptors have ordained that a wife shall observe her duty. If I do likewise my truthfulness will not be marred.

## \|\| 7.26.48 \|\|

*satyāśrayaḥ paro dharma satyena paramā gatiḥ \|*  
*satyena svarga mokṣau ca satye sarvaṃ pratiṣṭhitam \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. Abiding by truth is a great virtue. Great goal can be attained through truth. Heaven and liberation can be achieved through truth. Everything is founded on truth.

## \|\| 7.26.49 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*iti sā dṛḍhanirvandhā vāryamāṇāpi bandhubhiḥ \|*  
*satyalokaparā nārī prāṇāṃstyaktuṃ mano dadhe \|\| 49 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

49\. Although prevented by her kinsmen the harlot was obstinate in her resolve. The woman eager in the attainment of Satyaloka decided on giving up her life.

## \|\| 7.26.50 \|\|

*sarvasvaṃ dvijamukhyebhyo dattvā dhyātvā sadāśivam \|*  
*tamagniṃ triḥ parikramya praveśābhimukhī hyabhūt \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. She gifted away her property to leading brahmins. She meditated on Sadāśiva. She circumambulated the fire thrice and stood ready to enter it.

## \|\| 7.26.51 \|\|

*tāṃ patantīṃ samiddhegnau svapadārpitamānasām \|*  
*vārayāmāsa viśvātmā prādurbhūtaḥ sa vai śivaḥ \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. As she was going to jump in the fire with her mind fixed in his feet, Śiva, the soul of the universe, appeared in front and stopped her.

## \|\| 7.26.52 \|\|

*sā taṃ vilokyākhiladevadevantrilocanaṃ candrakalāvataṃsam \|*  
*śaśāṃkasūryānalakoṭibhāsaṃ stabdheva bhīteva tathaiva tasthau \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. On seeing the three-eyed lord of the gods, decorated by the moon’s digit, and as brilliant as crores of moons, suns and fires she stood as If stunned and frightened.

## \|\| 7.26.53 \|\|

*tāṃ vihvalāṃ suvitrasvāṃ vepamānāṃ jaḍīkṛtām \|*  
*samāśvāsya galadbāṣpāṃ karau dhṛtvā'bravīdvacaḥ \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. The lord caught hold of the hands of the frightened and agitated lady, stunned, trembling and shedding (ears. He consoled her and spoke.

## \|\| 7.26.54 \|\|

*śiva uvāca \|*  
*satyaṃ dharmaṃ ca dhairyaṃ ca bhaktiṃ ca mayi niścalām \|*  
*parīkṣituṃ tvatsakāśaṃ vaiśyo bhūtvāhamāgataḥ \|\| 54 \|\|*

Śiva said:—

54\. It is to test your truthfulness, piety, courage and steady devotion to me that I came near you assuming the form of a merchant.

## \|\| 7.26.55 \|\|

*mā yayāgniṃ samuddīpya dagdhante nāṭyamaṇḍapam \|*  
*dagdhaṃ kṛtvā ratnaliṃgaṃ praviṣṭohaṃ hutāśanam \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. With my magic I produced the blazing fire and burnt your dancing platform. After burning the gemset phallic image I entered fire myself.

## \|\| 7.26.56 \|\|

*sā tvaṃ satyamanusmṛtya praviṣṭāgniṃ mayā sahā \|*  
*ataste saṃpradāsyāmi bhogāṃstridaśadurlabhān \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. But you remembered the promise and entered the fire along with me. Hence I shall give you such pleasures as are inaccessible to gods.

## \|\| 7.26.57 \|\|

*yadyadicchasi suśroṇi tadeva hi dadāmi te \|*  
*tvadbhaktyāhaṃ prasannosmi tavādeyaṃ na vidyate \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. O beautiful lady of good hips, whatever you wish I shall give you. I am delighted by your devotion. There is nothing which cannot be given to you.

## \|\| 7.26.58 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*iti bruvati gaurīśe śaṃkare bhaktavatsale \|*  
*mahānandā ca sā veśyā śaṃkarampratyabhāṣata \|\| 58 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

58\. When Śiva, lord of Pārvatī, favourably disposed to his devotees said thus, the harlot Mahānandā replied to Śiva.

## \|\| 7.26.59 \|\|

*veśyovāca \|*  
*na me vāñchāsti bhogeṣu bhūmau svarge rasātale \|*  
*tava pādāmbujasparśādanyatkiṃcinna kāmaye \|\| 59 \|\|*

This prostitute said:—

59\. I have no desire for enjoyment on the earth, in the heaven or in the nether worlds. I do not desire anything except the touch of your lotus-like feet.

## \|\| 7.26.60 \|\|

*ye me bhṛtyāśca dāsyaśca ye cānye mama bāndhavāḥ \|*  
*sarve tvaddarśanaparāstvayi sannyastavṛttayaḥ \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. My servants, my maids and my kinsmen are interested in seeing you. They have dedicated everything to you.

## \|\| 7.26.61 \|\|

*sarvānetānmayā sārddhaṃ ninīyātmaparampadam \|*  
*punarjanmabhayaṃ ghoraṃ vimocaya namo'stu te \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. Take them all along with me to your great region and quash the terrible fear of rebirth. Obeisance be to you.

## \|\| 7.26.62 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*tatassa tasyā vacanampratinandya maheśvaraḥ \|*  
*tāḥ sarvāśca tayā sārdhaṃ nināya svamparampadam \|\| 62 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

62\. Appreciating her words, the great Śiva took all of them to his great region.

## \|\| 7.26.63 \|\|

*vaiśyanāthāvatāraste varṇitaḥ paramo mayā \|*  
*mahānandāsukhakaro bhaktānandapradassadā \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. Thus I have described to you the great incarnation of Vaiśyanātha. It was pleasing to Mahānandā. It yields great bliss to devotees.

## \|\| 7.26.64 \|\|

*idaṃ caritraṃ paramaṃ pavitraṃ satāṃ ca sarvapradamāśu divyam \|*  
*śivāvatārasya viśāmpatermahānandāmahāsaukhyakaraṃ vicitram \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. This narrative is great holy and divine. It grants desires to good men immediately. This story of the incarnation of Śiva as the lordly merchant is the source of great happiness. It is wonderful.

## \|\| 7.26.65 \|\|

*idaṃ yaḥ śṛṇuyādbhaktyā śrāvayedvā samāhitaḥ \|*  
*cyavate na svadharmātsa paratra labhate gatim \|\| 65 \|\|*

65\. He who listens to this story with devotion and narrates it with purity never swerves from his duties. He attains salvation hereafter.

## Chapter 27 - The incarnation of Dvijeśvara

## \|\| 7.27.1 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*śṛṇu tāta pravakṣyāmi śivasya paramātmanaḥ \|*  
*dvijeśvarāvatāraṃ ca saśivaṃ sukhadaṃ satām \|\| 1 \|\|*

Note: This Chapter has a close resemblance with the second canto of Kālidāsa’s Raghuvaṃśa.

Nandīśvara said:—

1\. O dear, listen, I shall expound to you the incarnation of Śiva the great soul as Dvijeśvara (a leading Brahmin) along with Śiva. It is pleasing to the good.

## \|\| 7.27.2-3 \|\|

*yaḥ pūrvaṃ varṇitastāta bhadrāyurnṛpasattamaḥ \|*  
*yasminnṛṣabharūpeṇānugrahaṃ kṛtavāñchivaḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

*taddharmasya parīkṣārthaṃ punarāvirbabhūva saḥ \|*  
*dvijeśvarasvarūpeṇa tadeva kathayāmyaham \|\| 3 \|\|*

2-3. O dear, I have already described to you the excellent king Bhadrāyu whom Śiva blessed in the form of a bull. In order to test his steadfastness and piety the lord manifested himself again in the form of a leading brahmin. I shall mention that same story.

## \|\| 7.27.4 \|\|

*ṛṣabhasya prabhāveṇa śatrūñjitvā raṇe prabhuḥ \|*  
*prāptasiṃhāsanastāta bhadrāyuḥ saṃbabhūva ha \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. Conquering the enemies through the power of Ṛṣabha in battle, Bhadrāyu attained the throne, O dear.

## \|\| 7.27.5 \|\|

*candrāṃgadasya tanayā sīmantinyāḥ śubhāṃgajā \|*  
*patnī tasyābhavadbrahmansusādhvī kīrtimālinī \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. O brahmin, the chaste lady Kīrtimālinī, daughter of Candrāṅgada and Sīmantinī, became his wife.

## \|\| 7.27.6 \|\|

*sa bhadrāyuḥ kadācitsvapriyayā gahanaṃ vanam \|*  
*prāviśatsaṃvihārārthaṃ vasantasamaye mune \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. O sage, once after the advent of spring, the king Bhadrāyu entered a thick forest along with his beloved queen, for sport.

## \|\| 7.27.7 \|\|

*atha tasminvane ramye vijahāra sa bhūpatiḥ \|*  
*śaraṇāgatapālinyā tamāsyapriyayā saha \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. In that beautiful forest, the king sported about along with his beloved who protected those who sought refuge in her.

## \|\| 7.27.8 \|\|

*atha taddharmadṛḍhatāṃ pratīkṣanparameśvaraḥ \|*  
*līlāṃ cakāra tatraiva śivayā saha śaṅkaraḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Then, in order to test his steadfastness and piety, lord Śiva carried out his sports there itself along with Śivā.

## \|\| 7.27.9 \|\|

*śivā śivaśca bhūtvobhau tadvane dvijadampatī \|*  
*vyāghraṃ māyāmayaṃ kṛtvāvirbhūtau nijalīlayā \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. Śiva and Śivā in their own divine sports assumed the form of a brahmin couple and created an illusory tiger with their Māyā.

## \|\| 7.27.10 \|\|

*athāvidūre tasyaiva dravantau bhayavihvalau \|*  
*anvīyamānau vyāghreṇa rudantau tau babhūvatuḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Not far from the place where the king stood, they began running in great fright on being pursued by the tiger. They cried and shouted too.

## \|\| 7.27.11 \|\|

*atha viddhau ca tau tāta bhadrāyuḥ sa mahīpatiḥ \|*  
*dadarśa krandamānau hi śaraṇyaḥ kṣatriyarṣabhaḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. O dear, the king Bhadrāyu, the leading Kṣatriya worthy of being sought refuge in, saw both of them highly distressed and lamenting.

## \|\| 7.27.12 \|\|

*atha tau muniśārdūlaḥ svamāyādvijadampatī \|*  
*bhadrāyuṣaṃ mahārājamūcaturbhayavihvalau \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. O leading sage, the illusory brahmin couple, extremely terrified, spoke to the king Bhadrāyu.

## \|\| 7.27.13 \|\|

*dvijadampatī ūcatuḥ \|*  
*pāhi pāhi mahārāja nāvubhau dharmavittama \|*  
*eṣa āyāti śārdūlo jagdhumāvāṃ mahāprabho \|\| 13 \|\|*

The brahmin couple said:—

13\. O great king, O foremost of the knowers of virtue save us both. O great lord, this tiger is rushing on in order to devour us.

## \|\| 7.27.14 \|\|

*eṣa hiṃsraḥ kālasamaḥ sarvaprāṇibhayaṅkaraḥ \|*  
*yāvanna khādati prāpya tāvannau rakṣa dharmavit \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. This beast of prey is like the god of death. It is terrifying to all living beings. Knower of virtue that you are save us before it pounces on us and devours us.

## \|\| 7.27.15-16 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*itthamākranditaṃ śrutvā tayośca nṛpatīśvaraḥ \|*  
*ati śīghraṃ mahāvīraḥ sa yāvaddhanurādade \|\| 15 \|\|*

*tāvadabhyetya śārdūlastvaramāṇotimāyikaḥ \|*  
*sa tasya dvijavaryyasya madhye jagrāha tāṃ vadhūm \|\| 16 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

15-16. After hearing the lamentation of the pair, the great king of great heroism immediately took up his bow, but the rushing tiger of Māyic origin, seized the brahmin’s wife.

## \|\| 7.27.17 \|\|

*he nāthanātha he kānta hā śambho hā jagadguro \|*  
*iti rorūyamāṇāṃ tāṃ vyāghro jagrāsa bhīṣaṇaḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. The terrible tiger grasped the woman even as she was lamenting—“O lord, O husband, O Śiva, O, preceptor of the universe.”

## \|\| 7.27.18 \|\|

*tāvatsa rājā niśitairbhallairvyāghramatāḍayat \|*  
*na sa tairvivyathe kiṃcidgirīndra iva vṛṣṭibhiḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. The king struck the tiger with sharp arrows; but it was not at all affected by them. It stood like a lofty mountain, which is not affected by showers.

## \|\| 7.27.19 \|\|

*sa śārdūlo mahāsattvo rājñaḥ svairakṛtavyathaḥ \|*  
*balādākṛṣya tāṃ nārīmapākramata satvaraḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. The powerful tiger, not at all pained by the missiles of the king, seized the woman forcibly and ran away in a hurry.

## \|\| 7.27.20 \|\|

*vyāghreṇāpahṛtāṃ nārīṃ vīkṣya viprotivismitaḥ \|*  
*laukikīṃ gatimāśritya rurodāti muhurmuhuḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. On seeing his wife seized by the tiger, the brahmin was much bewildered and following the way of the world cried again and again.

## \|\| 7.27.21 \|\|

*ruditvā cirakālaṃ ca sa vipro māyayeśvaraḥ \|*  
*bhadrāyuṣaṃ mahīpālaṃ provāca madahārakaḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. After crying for some time that brahmin who was lord Himself wielding his Māyā, spoke to the king Bhadrāyu dispelling his arrogance.

## \|\| 7.27.22 \|\|

*dvijeśvara uvāca \|*  
*rājanka te mahāstrāṇi kva te trāṇaṃ mahaddhanuḥ \|*  
*kva te dvādaśasāhasramahānāgāyutambalam \|\| 22 \|\|*

Dvijeśvara said:—

22\. O king, where are your great weapons? Where is your great bow that professes to protect? Where is your strength of ten or twelve thousand great elephants?

## \|\| 7.27.23 \|\|

*kinte khaḍgena śaṅkhena kiṃ te maṃtrāstravidyayā \|*  
*kiṃ sattvena mahāstrāṇāṃ kiṃ prabhāveṇa bhūyasā \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Of what avail is your sword? Your conch? Of what avail is your learning in the lores of mantras and missiles? Of what avail is your might? Of what avail is the strength of your great missiles?

## \|\| 7.27.24 \|\|

*tatsarvaṃ viphalaṃ jātaṃ yaccānyattvayi tiṣṭhati \|*  
*yastvaṃ vanaukasāṃ ghātaṃ na nivārayituṃ kṣamaḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. All these have become futile, for you are incompetent to meet the onslaught of wild animals.

## \|\| 7.27.25 \|\|

*kṣatrasyāyaṃ paro dharmo kṣatācca parirakṣaṇam \|*  
*tasminkulocite dharme naṣṭe tvajjīvitena kim \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. The greatest duty of a Kṣatriya is the protection from injury. When that virtue hereditarily practised in your family is lost, of what avail is your life?

## \|\| 7.27.26 \|\|

*ārtānāṃ śaraṇāptānāṃ trāṇaṃ kurvanti pārthivāḥ \|*  
*prāṇairarthaiśca dharmajñāstadvinā ca mṛtopamā \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Kings protect the distressed people who seek refìige in them with their very lives and assets. They are the knowers of virtue. Without that they are like a dead body.

## \|\| 7.27.27 \|\|

*ārtatrāṇavihīnānāṃ jīvitānmaraṇaṃ varam \|*  
*dhaninānpānahīnānāṃ gārhasthyādbhikṣutā varam \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Better death than life that does not protect the distressed. A mendicant is better than a rich householder who does not possess charitable nature.

## \|\| 7.27.28 \|\|

*varaṃ viṣāśanaṃ prājñairvaramagnipraveśanam \|*  
*kṛpaṇānāmanāthānāṃ dīnānāmaparakṣaṇāt \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. Better to swallow poison or enter fire than cease to protect the oppressed, the helpless and the poor.

## \|\| 7.27.29 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*itthaṃ vilapitaṃ tasya svavīryyasya ca garhaṇam \|*  
*niśamya nṛpatiḥ śokādātmanyevamacintayat \|\| 29 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

29\. On hearing his lamentation and the decrial of his prowess, the king thought within himself thus—

## \|\| 7.27.30 \|\|

*aho me pauruṣaṃ naṣṭamadya devaviparyayāt \|*  
*adya kīrtiśca me naṣṭā pātakamprāptamutkaṭam \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. “Alas, my manliness is lost today due to the adverse fate. Today my fame has been quashed. Great sin has been acquired.

## \|\| 7.27.31 \|\|

*dharmaḥ kulocito naṣṭo mandabhāgyasya durmateḥ \|*  
*nūnaṃ me sampado rājyamāyuṣyaṃ kṣayameṣyati \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. The hereditary virtue has been smothered. I am unfortunate and confused in mind. Certainly my riches, my kingdom, my longevity will dwindle.

## \|\| 7.27.32 \|\|

*adya cainaṃ dvijanmānaṃ hatadāraṃ śucārditam \|*  
*hataśokaṃ kariṣyāmi dattvā prāṇānatipriyān \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. By surrendering my own life,, lovable though it is, I shall make this brahmin free from grief, this brahmin whose wife has been seized by the tiger and who is pained extremely.”

## \|\| 7.27.33 \|\|

*iti niścitya manasā sa bhadrāyurnṛpottamaḥ \|*  
*patitvā pādayostasya babhāṣe parisāntvayan \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. Having decided thus in his mind that excellent king Bhadrāyu fell at his feet and spoke to him in conciliatory tone.

## \|\| 7.27.34 \|\|

*bhadrāyuruvāca \|*  
*kṛpāṃ kṛtvā mayi brahman kṣatrabandhau hataujasi \|*  
*śokantyaja mahāprājña dāsyāmyadya tu vāñchitam \|\| 34 \|\|*

Bhadrāyu said:—

34\. O Brahmin, I am a base Kṣatriya bereft of strength. You take pity on me. O intelligent one, do not grieve. I shall give you what you desire.

## \|\| 7.27.35 \|\|

*idaṃ rājyamiyaṃ rājñī mamedañca kalevaram \|*  
*tvadadhīnamidaṃ sarvaṃ kinte'bhilaṣitaṃ varam \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. This kingdom, this queen, this body of mine, everything is subservient to you. What is the greatest thing that you desire?

## \|\| 7.27.36 \|\|

*brāhmaṇa uvāca \|*  
*kimādarśena cāndhasya kiṃ gṛherbhaikṣyajīvinaḥ \|*  
*kimpustakena mūḍhasya nistrīkasya dhanena kim \|\| 36 \|\|*

The Brahmin said:—

36\. What can a blind man do with a mirror? Of what avail is a house to him who lives on alms? What can a fool do with a book? Of what avail is wealth to him who is bereft of his wife?

## \|\| 7.27.37 \|\|

*ato'haṃ hatapatnīko bhuktabhogo na karhicit \|*  
*imāntavāgramahiṣīṃ kāmaye dīyatāmiti \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. Hence I who am not satiated with the enjoyment of pleasures but I whose wife is snatched away desire your crowned queen. Let her be given to me.

## \|\| 7.27.38 \|\|

*dātā rasāntavittasya rājyasya gajavājinām \|*  
*ātmadehasya yasyāpi kalatrasya na karhicit \|\| 38 \|\|*

Bhadrāyu said:—

38\. I can give all my assets to the extent of the earth, my kingdom, horses and elephants aiíd even my body to anyone but never can I give away my wife.

## \|\| 7.27.39 \|\|

*paradāropabhogena yatpāpaṃ samupārjitam \|*  
*na tatkṣālayituṃ śakyaṃ prāyaścittaśatairapi \|\| 39 \|\|*

39 The sin that accrues by indulging in sexual intercourse with another man’s wife cannot be wiped off even by hundreds of expiatory rites.

## \|\| 7.27.40 \|\|

*brāhmaṇa uvāca \|*  
*āstāṃ brahmavadhaṃ ghoramapi madyaniṣevaṇam \|*  
*tapasā vidhamiṣyāmi kiṃ punaḥ pāradārikam \|\| 40 \|\|*

The Brahmin said:—

40\. Let it be the terrible sin of the slaughter of a brahmin; let it be the sin of sipping wine, I shall quell it with my power of penance. What then the sin of enjoying another man’s wife?

## \|\| 7.27.41 \|\|

*tasmātprayaccha bhāryāṃ svāmiyāṃ kāmo na me'paraḥ \|*  
*arakṣaṇādbhayārtānāṃ gantāsi nirayandhruvam \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. Hence give me your wife. I have no other desire. Certainly you will go to hell by your inability to protect the oppressed.

## \|\| 7.27.42 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*iti vipragirā bhītaścintayāmāsa pārthivaḥ \|*  
*arakṣaṇānmahāpāpaṃ patnīdānantato varam \|\| 42 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

42\. At the words or the brahmin, the frightened king thought to himself, “It is a aw to withhold protection. It Is proper under the circumstance to give away my wife.”

## \|\| 7.27.43 \|\|

*ataḥ patnīṃ dvijāgryāya dattvā nirmuktakilviṣaḥ \|*  
*sadyo vahniṃ pravekṣyāmi kīrtiśca viditā bhavet \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. Hence I shall give my wife to this deserving brahmin and escape sin. Immediately thereafter I shall enter fire. My fame then shall be known.”

## \|\| 7.27.44 \|\|

*iti niścitya manasā samujjvālya hutāśanam \|*  
*tamāhūya dvijaṃ cakre patnīdānaṃ sahodakam \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. After deciding mentally like this, he kindled a blazing fire. He called the brahmin and gifted away his wife with water.

## \|\| 7.27.45 \|\|

*svayaṃ snātaḥ śucirbhūtvā praṇamya vibudheśvarān \|*  
*tamagniṃ triḥ parikramya śivaṃ dadhyau samāhitaḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. He took the ceremonial bath and became pure. He bowed to gods went round the fire thrice and meditated on Śiva with pure mind.

## \|\| 7.27.46 \|\|

*tamathāgniṃ patiṣyantaṃ svapadāsaktacetasam \|*  
*pratyaṣedhata viśveśaḥ prādurbhūto dvijeśvaraḥ \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. He was just to fall into the fire with his mind fixed at the feet of Śiva, the lord of the universe when Dvijeśvara revealed himself and stayed him.

## \|\| 7.27.47-49 \|\|

*tamīśvaraṃ pañcamukhaṃ trinetraṃ pinākinaṃ candrakalāvataṃsam \|*  
*pralambapiṃgāsujaṭākalāpaṃ madhyāhnasadbhāskarakoṭitejasam \|\| 47 \|\|*

*mṛṇālagauraṃ gajacarmavāsasaṃ gaṃgātaraṅgokṣitamaulideśakam \|*  
*nāgendrahārāvalikaṇṭhabhūṣaṇaṃ kirīṭakācyaṃgadakaṃkaṇojjvalam \|\| 48 \|\|*

*śūlāsikhaṭvāṃgakuṭhāracarmamṛgābhayāṣṭāṃgapinākahastam \|*  
*vṛṣoparisthaṃ śitikaṇṭhabhūṣaṇaṃ prodbhūtamagre sa nṛpo dadarśa \|\| 49 \|\|*

47-49. The king saw in front of him the five-faced, the three-eyed lord with the Pināka in his hand, bedecked by the digit of the moon, with hanging matted hair shedding tawny lustre, having the brilliance of a crore of midday blazing suns, white as the lotus stalk fibre, wearing the hide of the elephant, with his head drenched by the waves of Gaṅgā, having the necklaces of great serpents, bedecked in coronet, waistband, shoulderlet and shining bangles, holding in his hands, the trident, the sword, the missile Khaṭvāṅga, the dagger, the shield, the deer, the mystic sign of protection, the eight articles of worship and the Pināka, seated on his bull and blue-necked.

## \|\| 7.27.50 \|\|

*tatombarāddrutaṃ peturddivyāḥ kusumavṛṣṭayaḥ \|*  
*praṇedurdevatūryyāṇi devyaśca nanṛturjaguḥ \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. Then from the sky divine showers of flowers fell. The divine instruments were played. The celestial damsels sang and danced.

## \|\| 7.27.51 \|\|

*tatrājagmuḥ stūyamānā harirbrahmā tathāsurāḥ \|*  
*indrādayo nāradādyā munayaścāpare'pi ca \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. Viṣṇu, Brahmā, Indra and other gods excluding Nārada and other sages too came there eulogising.

## \|\| 7.27.52 \|\|

*tadotsavo mahānāsīttatra bhaktipravardhanaḥ \|*  
*sati paśyati bhūpāle bhaktinamrīkṛtāñjalau \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. While the king was watching with palms joined in humility and devotion, there was great jubilation that enhanced devotional feelings.

## \|\| 7.27.53 \|\|

*taddarśanānandavijṛmbhitāśayaḥ pravṛddhavāṣpāmbuviliptagātraḥ \|*  
*prahṛṣṭaromā sa hi gadgadākṣarastuṣṭāva gīrbhirmukulīkṛtāṃjaliḥ \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. The vision of the lord increased his pleasure. The incessant tear-drops smeared his body. He had horripilation and choked throat. With palms joined in reverence, he eulogised the lord.

## \|\| 7.27.54 \|\|

*tatassa bhagavānrājñā saṃstutaḥ parameśvaraḥ \|*  
*prasannaḥ saha pārvatyā tamuvāca dayānidhiḥ \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. The supreme lord, the storehouse of mercy and his consort Pārvatī were very much delighted. The lord then spoke to him thus.

## \|\| 7.27.55 \|\|

*rājaṃste parituṣṭohaṃ bhaktyā tvaddharmato'dhikam \|*  
*varaṃ brūhi sapatnīkamprayacchāmi na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. “O king, I am satisfied with your devotion more than your piety. Mention the boon that you as well as your wife wish to choose. I shall undoubtedly give it to you.

## \|\| 7.27.56 \|\|

*tava bhāvaparīkṣārthaṃ dvijo bhūtvāhamāgataḥ \|*  
*vyāghreṇa yā parigrastā sākṣāddevī śivā hi sā \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. It was to test your feeling? and emotions that I assumed the form of a brahmin and approached you. She who was seized by the tiger is the goddess Śivā herself.

## \|\| 7.27.57 \|\|

*vyāghro māyāmayo yaste śarairakṣata vigrahaḥ \|*  
*dhīratāndraṣṭukāmaste patnī yācitavānaham \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. It was an illusory tiger which could not be hit by your arrows. I wanted to test your courage by demanding your wife.

## \|\| 7.27.58 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityākarṇya prabhorvākyaṃ sa bhadrāyurmahīpatiḥ \|*  
*puna praṇamya saṃstūya svāminaṃ natako'bravīt \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. On hearing the words of the lord, the king Bhadrāyu eulogised and bowed to the lord with bent head.

## \|\| 7.27.59 \|\|

*bhadrāyuruvāca \|\| eka eva varo nātha yadbhavānparameśvaraḥ \|*  
*bhavatāpaprataptasya mama pratyakṣatāṃ gataḥ \|\| 59 \|\|*

Bhadrāyu said:—

59\. O lord, this alone is a great boon that you became visible to me searched by the sun of worldly existence.

## \|\| 7.27.60 \|\|

*yaddadāsi punarnātha varaṃ svakṛpayā prabho \|*  
*vṛṇehaṃ paramaṃ tyakto varaṃ hi varadarṣabhāt \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. O lord, since out of pity you wish to accord me a boon, I choose it as a great devotee does from his lord, the granter of boons.

## \|\| 7.27.61-62 \|\|

*vajrabāhuḥ pitā me hi sapa tnīko maheśvara \|*  
*sapatnīkastvahaṃ nātha sadā tvatpādasevakaḥ \|\| 61 \|\|*

*vaiśyaḥ padmākaro nāma tatputrassanayābhidhaḥ \|*  
*sarvānetānmaheśāna sadā tvaṃ pārśvagānkuru \|\| 62 \|\|*

61-62. O great god, my father Vajrabāhu with his wife, O lord, I a servant at your feet, along with my wife, the Vaiśya Padmākara and his son Sanaya, O great lord, make all of us residents near you.

## \|\| 7.27.63 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*atha rājñī ca tatpatnī pramattā kīrtimālinī \|*  
*bhaktyā prasādya giriśaṃ yayāce varamuttamam \|\| 63 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

63\. Then the queen, his wife, Kīrtimalinī propitiated Śiva with devotion and requested for an excellent boon.

## \|\| 7.27.64 \|\|

*satyuvāca \|*  
*candrāṃgado mama pitā mātā sīmantinī ca me \|*  
*tayoryāce mahādeva tvatpāśve sannidhiṃ mudā \|\| 64 \|\|*

The queen said:—

64\. O supreme God, Candrāṅgada my father and Sīmantinī my mother, I request the two to be joyously staying near you for ever.

## \|\| 7.27.65 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*evamastviti gaurīśaḥ prasanno bhaktavatsalaḥ \|*  
*tayoḥ kāmavarandattvā kṣaṇādantarhito'bhavat \|\| 65 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

65\. The delighted lord of Gaurī, favourably disposed to his devotees said—“Let it be so” and granted them the boon they desired and then vanished in a moment.

## \|\| 7.27.66 \|\|

*bhadrāyurapi suprītyā prasādamprāpya śūlinaḥ \|*  
*sahitaḥ kīrtimālinyā bubhuje viṣayānbahūn \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. Acquiring the grace of the trident-bearing lord, Bhadrāyu, enjoyed several pleasures lovingly in the company of Kīrtimālinī.

## \|\| 7.27.67 \|\|

*kṛtvā varṣāyutarājyamavyāhataparākramaḥ \|*  
*rājyaṃ vikṣipya tanaye jagāma śivasannidhim \|\| 67 \|\|*

67\. Unimpeded in the exercise of his power he ruled the kingdom for ten thousand years. Then he entrusted his son with the kingdom and went to Śiva’s presence.

## \|\| 7.27.68 \|\|

*candrāṃgadopi rājendro rājñī sīmantinī ca sā \|*  
*bhaktyā saṃpūjya giriśaṃ jagmatuḥ śāmbhavaṃ padam \|\| 68 \|\|*

68\. The king Candrāṅgada and the queen Sīmantinī devoutly worshipped Śiva and attained his feet.

## \|\| 7.27.69 \|\|

*dvijeśvarāvatāraste varṇitaḥ paramo mayā \|*  
*maheśvarasya bhadrāyuparamānandadaḥ prabho \|\| 69 \|\|*

69\. Thus the great incarnation of lord Śiva as Dvijeśvara has been described by me. It was the bestower of great bliss on Bhadrāyu.

## \|\| 7.27.70 \|\|

*idaṃ caritraṃ paramaṃ pavitraṃ śivāvatārasya pavitrakīrtteḥ \|*  
*dvijeśasaṃjñasya mahādbhutaṃ hi śṛṇvanpaṭhañśambhupadamprayāti \|\| 70 \|\|*

70\. He who reads or listens to this holy narrative of Dvijeśa incarnation of Śiva of pious fame, goes to Śiva’s region.

## \|\| 7.27.71 \|\|

*ya idaṃ śṛṇuyānnityaṃ śrāvayedvā samāhitaḥ \|*  
*na ścotati svadharmātsa paratra labhate gatim \|\| 71 \|\|*

71\. He who listens to this every day or narrates this with attentive mind does not swerve from his duty and attains salvation hereafter.

## Chapter 28 - Śiva’s incarnation as Yatinātha haṃsa (swan)

## \|\| 7.28.1 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*śṛṇu prājña pravakṣyāmi śivasya paramātmanaḥ \|*  
*avatāraṃ purānandaṃ yātināthāhvayaṃ mune \|\| 1 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

1\. O intelligent sage, listen. I shall explain the incarnation of great bliss named Yatinātha, of Śiva the supreme soul.

## \|\| 7.28.2 \|\|

*arbudācalasaṃjñe tu parvate bhillavaṃśajaḥ \|*  
*āhukaśca tadabhyāśe vasatisma munīśvara \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. O great sage, on the mountain Arbuda there lived a hunter Āhuka of Bhilla tribe.

## \|\| 7.28.3 \|\|

*tatpatnī hyāhukā nāma babhūva kila suvratā \|*  
*ubhāvapi mahāśaivāvāstāntau śivapūjakau \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. His wife Āhukā observed some good rites. Both of them were great devotees of Śiva and worshipped Śiva.

## \|\| 7.28.4 \|\|

*kasmiṃścitsamaye bhillaḥ śivabhaktirataḥ sadā \|*  
*āhārārthaṃ svapatnyāśca sudūraṃ sa gato mune \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. O sage, once this Bhilla who was always devoted to Śiva went far into the forest for fetching food for his wife.

## \|\| 7.28.5 \|\|

*etasminnantare tatra gehe bhillasya śaṅkaraḥ \|*  
*bhūtvā yativapuḥ sāyaṃ parīkṣārthaṃ samāyayau \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. In. the meantime towards the evening, Śiva assumed the guise of an ascetic and came to his house to test him

## \|\| 7.28.6 \|\|

*tasminnavasare tatrājagāma sa gṛhādhipaḥ \|*  
*pūjanaṃ ca yatīśasya cakāra premataḥ sudhīḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. The lord of the house that intelligent man too came back then and lovingly worshipped the leading ascetic.

## \|\| 7.28.7 \|\|

*tadbhāvasya parīkṣārthaṃ yatirūpassa śaṃkaraḥ \|*  
*mahālīlātaraḥ prītyā bhītaṃ provāca dīnagīḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. In order to test his feelings, Śiva in the guise of an ascetic, indulging in great sports lovingly said to him in a piteous tone.

## \|\| 7.28.8 \|\|

*yatinātha uvāca \|*  
*adya sthalaṃ nivāsārthaṃ dehi me prātareva hi \|*  
*yāsyāmi sarvathā bhilla svasti syāttava sarvadā \|\| 8 \|\|*

Yatinātha said:—

8\. O Bhilla, give me some place here to stay today. I shall by all means leave in the next morning, you will be blessed for ever.

## \|\| 7.28.9 \|\|

*bhilla uvāca \|*  
*tyamproktaṃ tvayā svāmiñśṛṇu madvacanaṃ ca te \|*  
*ati svalpaṃ sthalaṃ me hi syānnivāsaḥ kathantava \|\| 9 \|\|*

Bhilla said:—

9\. O lord, what you say is true. But please listen to me. The room is not spacious enough to hold us all together.

## \|\| 7.28.10 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityuktassa yatistena gamanāya matindadhe \|*  
*tāvadbhillyā vacaḥ proktaṃ svāminaṃ saṃvicāryya vai \|\| 10 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

10\. Thus addressed, the ascetic wanted to leave. By that time, considering the trend of thought of her husband the Bhillī said.

## \|\| 7.28.11 \|\|

*bhillyuvāca \|*  
*svāmindehi yateḥsthānaṃ vimukhaṃ kuru mātithim \|*  
*gṛhadharmaṃ vicāryya tvamanyathā dharmasaṃkṣayaḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. O lord, give him room to stay. Do not turn out a guest. Consider the duty of a householder or virtue will be affected.

## \|\| 7.28.12 \|\|

*sthīyatānte gṛhābhyaṃtaḥ sukhena yatinā saha \|*  
*ahaṃ bahiḥ sthitiṃ kuryyāmāyudhāni bṛhantyapi \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. You stay in the house along with the ascetic. I shall remain outside with immense weapons.

## \|\| 7.28.13 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*tasyāstadvacanaṃ śrutvā bhillyā dharmānvitaṃ śivam \|*  
*svapatnyā manasā tena bhillena ca vicāritam \|\| 13 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

13\. On hearing the virtuous and auspicious words of his wife, the Bhilla thought thus.

## \|\| 7.28.14 \|\|

*striyaṃ bahiśca niṣkāsya kathaṃ stheyaṃ mayā bahe \|*  
*yateranyatra gamanamadharmmakaramātmanaḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. “How can I stay in the house after keeping my wife out. It will be sinful on my part if the ascetic goes elsewhere.

## \|\| 7.28.15 \|\|

*dvayamapyucitaṃ naiva sarvathā gṛhamedhinaḥ \|*  
*yadbhāvi tadbhavedeva mayā stheyaṃ gṛhādbahiḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. Neither of the two courses is proper for a householder. What is destined to happen will surely happen. I shall stay outside.”

## \|\| 7.28.16 \|\|

*ityāgrahantadā kṛtvā gṛhāntaḥ sthāya tau mudā \|*  
*svāyudhāni ca saṃsthāpya bhillo'tiṣṭhadgṛhādbahiḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. After thinking thus and insisting on it, he kept both of them within the house joyously, himself stayed outside the house keeping all the weapons beside him.

## \|\| 7.28.17 \|\|

*rātrau tampaśavaḥ krūrā hiṃsakāḥ samapīḍayan \|*  
*tenāpi ca yathāśakti kṛto yatno mahāṃstadā \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. During the night the cruel beasts of prey tormented him. He too strenuously withstood them to the extent of his ability.

## \|\| 7.28.18 \|\|

*evaṃ yatnaṃ prakurvāṇa sa bhillo balavānapi \|*  
*prārabdhātpreritairhiṃsrairbalādāsīcca bhakṣitaḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. Thus fighting strenuously and although very strong, the Bhilla was devoured by the beasts of prey which seized him forcibly, as though indeed by his own adverse fate.

## \|\| 7.28.19 \|\|

*prātarutthāya sa yatirdṛṣṭvā hiṃsraiśca bhakṣitam \|*  
*bhillaṃ vane caraṃtaṃ vai duḥkhito'bhūdatīva hi \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. Getting up in the morning the ascetic saw the Bhilla of the forest devoured by the beasts of prey. He felt much dejected at it.

## \|\| 7.28.20 \|\|

*dukhitaṃ taṃ yatindṛṣṭvā bhillī sā duḥkhitāpi hi \|*  
*dhairyātsvaduḥkhaṃ saṃhṛtya vacanaṃ cedamabravīt \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. Although the Bhilli too was distressed, she suppressed her sorrow on seeing the ascetic miserable and spoke thus.

## \|\| 7.28.21 \|\|

*bhillyuvāca \|*  
*kimarthaṃ kriyate duḥkhaṃ bhadraṃ jātaṃ yate'dhunā \|*  
*dhanyoyaṃ kṛtakṛtyaśca yajjāto mṛtyurīdṛśaḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

Bhilli said:—

21\. O ascetic, why are you sorry? What has happened is for good. Blessed and contented is he since the death has happened in this manner.

## \|\| 7.28.22 \|\|

*ahaṃ cainaṃ gamiṣyāmi bhasma bhūtvānale yate \|*  
*citāṃ kāraya suprītyā strīṇāṃ dharmaḥ sanātanaḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. O ascetic, reducing myself to ashes, I too shall follow him. Be pleased to arrange for the pyre. This is the eternal duty of women.

## \|\| 7.28.23 \|\|

*iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvā hitaṃ matvā svayaṃ yatiḥ \|*  
*citāṃ vyaracayatsā hi praviveśa svadharmataḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. On hearing her words which he thought to be proper, the ascetic arranged the pyre. She entered the pyre dutifully.

## \|\| 7.28.24 \|\|

*etasminnantare sākṣātpuraḥ prādurabhūcchivaḥ \|*  
*dhanye dhanye iti prītyā praśaṃsastāṃ haro'bravīt \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. In the meantime Śiva himself appeared before her and spoke praising her lovingly “O blessed lady, O blessed lady.”

## \|\| 7.28.25 \|\|

*hara uvāca \|*  
*varaṃ brūhi prasannosmi tvadācaraṇato'naghe \|*  
*tavādeyaṃ na vai kiṃcidvaśyo'haṃ te viśeṣataḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

Śiva said:—

25\. O sinless lady, mention the boon you wish to have. I am pleased with your conduct. There is nothing which cannot be granted to you. I am utterly subservient to you.

## \|\| 7.28.26 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*tacchutvā śambhuvacanaṃ paramānandadāyakam \|*  
*sukhaṃ prāptaṃ viśeṣeṇa na kiṃcitsmaraṇaṃ yayau \|\| 26 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

26\. On hearing the words of Śiva yielding the greatest bliss she felt the highest pleasure. She did not remember anything.

## \|\| 7.28.27 \|\|

*tasyāstadgatimālakṣya suprasanno haro'bhavat \|*  
*uvāca ca punaḥ śambhurvaraṃ brūhīti tāmprabhuḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. On seeing her in that state, Śiva was much delighted. He asked her again to mention the boon she wished to have.

## \|\| 7.28.28 \|\|

*śiva uvāca \|*  
*ayaṃ yatiśca madrūpo haṃsarūpo bhaviṣyati \|*  
*parajanmani vāṃ prītyā saṃyoga kārayiṣyati \|\| 28 \|\|*

Śiva said:—

28\. This ascetic who is in my own form will assume the form of a swan in the next birth. He will lovingly unite both of you.

## \|\| 7.28.29 \|\|

*bhillaśca vīrasenasya naiṣadhe nagare vare \|*  
*mahānputro nalo nāma bhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. This Bhilla will be born as Nala, the son of Vīrasena, in the excellent city of Niṣadha. There is no doubt in this.

## \|\| 7.28.30 \|\|

*tvaṃ sutā bhīmarājasya vaidarbhe nagare'naghe \|*  
*damayantī ca vikhyātā bhaviṣyasi guṇānvitā \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. You will be born in the illustrious city of Vidarbha as the daughter of king Bhīma. You will be famous as Damayantī endowed with all good qualities.

## \|\| 7.28.31 \|\|

*yuvāṃ cobhau militvā ca rājabhogaṃ suvistaram \|*  
*bhuktvā muktiṃ ca yogīndrerlapsyethe durlabhāṃ dhruvam \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. After coming together you will enjoy royal pleasures. You will attain salvation too, inaccessible even to leading Yogins.

## \|\| 7.28.32 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityuktvā ca svayaṃ śambhurliṅgarūpo'bhavattadā \|*  
*tasmānna calito dharmādacaleśa iti smṛtaḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

32\. After saying this, Śiva assumed the form of phallic image. He became stationary. He is thereafter remembered as Acaleśa.

## \|\| 7.28.33 \|\|

*sa bhilla āhukaścāpi vīrasenasuto'bhavat \|*  
*naiṣadhe nagare tāta nalanāmā mahānṛpaḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. O dear, the Bhilla Āhuka was born as the son of Vīrasena in the city of Niṣadha. He was the great king Nala.

## \|\| 7.28.34 \|\|

*āhukā sā mahābhillī bhīmasya tanayā'bhavat \|*  
*vaidarbhe nagare rājño damayantīti viśrutā \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. That great Bhillī, Āhukā was born as the daughter of king Bhīma in the city of Vidarbha. She became famous as Damayantī.

## \|\| 7.28.35 \|\|

*yatināthāhvayassopi haṃsarūpo'bhavacchivaḥ \|*  
*vivāhaṃ kārayāmāsa damayantyā nalena vai \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. Śiva in the form of Yatinātha was born as a swan. He managed the marriage of Damayantī with Nala.

## \|\| 7.28.36 \|\|

*pūrvasatkārarūpeṇa mahāpuṇyena śaṃkaraḥ \|*  
*haṃsarūpaṃ vidhāyaiva tābhyāṃ sukhamadātprabhuḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. It was due to the merit accruing from hospitality that lord Śiva gave them pleasure after assuming the form of a swan.

## \|\| 7.28.37 \|\|

*śivo haṃsāvatāro hi nānāvārtāvicakṣaṇaḥ \|*  
*damayantyā nalasyāpi paramānandadāyakaḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. The incarnation of Śiva as the swan adept in the art of intelligence, was the bestower of great bliss to Damayantī as well as to Nala.

## \|\| 7.28.38 \|\|

*idaṃ caritaṃ paramaṃ pavitraṃ śivāvatārasya pavitrakīrteḥ \|*  
*yatīśasaṃjñasya mahādbhutaṃ hi haṃsāhvayasyāpi vimuktidaṃ hi \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. This great and holy narrative of Śiva’s incarnation of illustrious fame as swan is wonderful and indeed the bestower of salvation

## \|\| 7.28.39 \|\|

*yatīśabrahmahaṃsākhyāvatāracaritaṃ śubham \|*  
*śṛṇuyācchrāvayedyo hi sa labheta parāṃ gatim \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. He who narrates or listens to the auspicious story of incarnation of Yatinatha haṃsa, attains the greatest goal.

## \|\| 7.28.40 \|\|

*idamākhyānamanaghaṃ sarvakāmaphalapradam \|*  
*svargyaṃ yaśasyamāyuṣyaṃ bhaktivardhanamuttamam \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. The story is faultless, bestower of the fruits of all desires and very excellent. It is conducive to the attainment of heaven, fame and longevity. It enhances devotion.

## \|\| 7.28.41 \|\|

*śrutvaitaccacaritaṃ śambhoryatihaṃsasvarūpayoḥ \|*  
*iha sarvasukhambhuktvā so'nte śivapuraṃ vrajet \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. After hearing this narrative of Śiva in the forms of an ascetic and a swan, the hearer enjoys all pleasures here and goes to Śiva’s abode hereafter.

## Chapter 29 - The incarnation of Śiva as Kṛṣṇadarśana

## \|\| 7.29.1 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*sanatkumāra śambhostvavatāraṃ paramaṃ śṛṇu \|*  
*nabhagajñānadaṃ kṛṣṇadarśanāhvayamuttamam \|\| 1 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

1\. O Sanatkumāra, listen to the description of the great incarnation of Śiva, the excellent Kṛṣṇadarśana who gave perfect wisdom to Nabhaga.

## \|\| 7.29.2 \|\|

*ikṣvākupramukhā āsanśrāddhadevasutāśca ye \|*  
*nabhagastatra navamo nābhagastatsutaḥ smṛtaḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Ikṣvāku and others were the sons of Śrāddhadeva of whom Nabhaga, was the ninth. His son was Nābhaga.

## \|\| 7.29.3 \|\|

*ambarīṣassutastasya viṣṇubhakto babhūva saḥ \|*  
*yasyopari prasannobhūddurvāsā brahmabhaktitaḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Ambarīṣa was his son who was a devotee of Viṣṇu with whom the sage Durvāsas was much pleased due to his devotion for Brahmins.

## \|\| 7.29.4 \|\|

*pitāmaho'mbarīṣasya nabhago yaḥ prakīrtitaḥ \|*  
*taccaritaṃ śṛṇu mune yasmai jñānamadācchivaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. O sage, the grandfather of Ambarīṣa was Nabhaga to whom Śiva imparted perfect knowledge. Listen to his story.

## \|\| 7.29.5 \|\|

*nabhago manuputrastu paṭhanārthaṃ subuddhi mān \|*  
*cakre gurukule vāsaṃ bahukālaṃ jitendriyaḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Nabhaga the son of Manu, highly intelligent, and with perfect control of his sense-organs stayed in the abode of his preceptors for studies for a long time.

## \|\| 7.29.6 \|\|

*etasminsamaye te vā ikṣvākupramukhāssutāḥ \|*  
*tasmai bhāgamakalpyaiva bhejurbhāgānnijānkramāt \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. During this interval Ikṣvāku and other sons had their due shares in the property without leaving any share for Nabhaga.

## \|\| 7.29.7 \|\|

*svaṃsvaṃ bhāgaṃ gṛhītvā te bubhujū rājyamuttamam \|*  
*aviṣādaṃ mahābhāgā pitrādeśātsubuddhayaḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. At the behest of their father, the shrewd sons took their shares and enjoyed the kingdom with out any misery. They were fortunate.

## \|\| 7.29.8 \|\|

*sa paścādāgatastatra brahmacārī gurusthalāt \|*  
*nabhago'dhītya sarvāśca sāṃgopāṃgāḥ śrutīḥ kramāt \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. After learning the Vedas with their sections and sub-sections, in due course, the Brahmacārin Nabhga (Nabhaga?) returned.

## \|\| 7.29.9 \|\|

*bhrātṛnvilokya nabhago vibhaktānsakalānnijān \|*  
*dāyārthī prāha tānsnehādikṣvākupramukhānmune \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. O sage, on seeing his brothers Ikṣvāku and others having taken their shares, Nabhaga desirous of his share told them affectionately.

## \|\| 7.29.10 \|\|

*nabhaga uvāca \|*  
*bhrātarobhaktakaṃ mahyaṃ dāyaṃ kṛtvā yathātatham \|*  
*sarve vibhaktāssuprītyā svadāyārthāgatāya ca \|\| 10 \|\|*

Nabhaga said:—

10\. O my brothers you have taken your shares duly without leaving anything for me. I have now come for my share. Please give me mine.

## \|\| 7.29.11 \|\|

*tadā vismṛtamasmābhiridānīṃ pitaraṃ tava \|*  
*vibhajāmo vayaṃ bhāgaṃ taṃ gṛhāṇa na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Then we had forgotten to allow your share. We shall certainly do it now. Our father is your share. Take him.

## \|\| 7.29.12 \|\|

*tacchutvā bhrātṛvacanaṃ nabhagaḥ paravismṛtaḥ \|*  
*tadopakaṇṭhamāgatya pitaraṃ samabhāṣata \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. On bearing the words of his brothers Nabhaga was surprised. He approached his father and said.

## \|\| 7.29.13 \|\|

*nabhaga uvāca \|*  
*he tāta bhrātaraḥ sarve tyaktvā māṃ nyabhajaṃśca te \|*  
*paṭhanārthaṃ gataścāhaṃ brahmacārī guroḥ kule \|\| 13 \|\|*

Nabhaga said:—

13\. O father, my brothers have taken their shares leaving me out. I had been to the preceptor’s abode for study as a student.

## \|\| 7.29.14 \|\|

*tata āgatya me pṛṣṭā dāyadānārthamādarāt \|*  
*te tvāmūcurvibhāgaṃ me tadarthamahamāgataḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. When I returned I asked them to allot my share; they replied that you are my share. I have now come for that purpose.

## \|\| 7.29.15 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*tadākarṇya vacastasya pitā taṃ prāha vismitaḥ \|*  
*āśvāsya śrāddhadevassa satyadharmarataṃ mune \|\| 15 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

15\. O sage, on hearing his words, his father Śrāddhadeva was surprised. He consoled his son who was devoted to truth and virtue.

## \|\| 7.29.16 \|\|

*manuruvāca \|*  
*taduktaṃ mādṛthāstāta pratāraṇakaraṃ hi tat \|*  
*na hyahaṃ paramaṃ dāyaṃ sarvathā bhogasādhanam \|\| 16 \|\|*

Manu said:—

16\. O dear, do not pay heed to what they said. It was only to deceive you. I am not a share by any means, an instrument of enjoyment.

## \|\| 7.29.17 \|\|

*tathāpi dāyabhāvena datto'haṃ taiḥ pratāribhiḥ \|*  
*tava vai jīvanopāya vadāmi śṛṇu tattvataḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Still since I have been given as a share by the deceitful persons I shall tell you the means of livelihood. Hear attentively.

## \|\| 7.29.18 \|\|

*satramāṃgirasā viprāḥ kurvaṃtyadya sumedhasaḥ \|*  
*tatra karmaṇi muhyanti ṣaṣṭhaṃ ṣaṣṭhamahaḥ prati \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. The intelligent Āṅgirasa. brahmins are performing a sacrifice. Every sixth day the rites are marred.

## \|\| 7.29.19 \|\|

*tatra tvaṃ gaccha nabhaga tān suśaṃsa mahākave \|*  
*sūkte dve vaiśvadeve hi satraṃ śuddhaṃ hi tadbhavet \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. O wise Nabhaga, you go there and praise them. Recite two Vaiśvadeva sūkatas there. That sacrifice will then become perfect.

## \|\| 7.29.20 \|\|

*tatkarmaṇi samāpte hi svayānto brāhmaṇāśca te \|*  
*dhanaṃ dāsyanti te tuṣṭāssvasatrapariśeṣitam \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. As soon as the sacrifice is concluded those Brahmins going heavenwards will give you delightedly the wealth left over in the sacrifice.

## \|\| 7.29.21 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*tadākarṇya piturvākyaṃ nabhagaḥ satyasāravān \|*  
*jagāma tatra suprītyā yatra tatsatramuttamam \|\| 21 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

21\. On hearing the words of his father, Nabhaga who considered truth as virtue went eagerly where the excellent sacrifice was on.

## \|\| 7.29.22 \|\|

*tadāhaḥ karmaṇi mune satre tasminsa mānavaḥ \|*  
*sūkte dve vaiśvadeve hi provāca spaṣṭatassudhīḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. O sage, in that sacrifice during the diurnal rites the intelligent son of Manu recited two Vaiśvadeva sūktas clearly.

## \|\| 7.29.23 \|\|

*samāpte karmaṇi tato viprā āṃgirasāśca te \|*  
*tasmai dattvā yayuḥ svargaṃ svaṃsvaṃ satrāvaśeṣitam \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. When the sacrifice was concluded the Āṅgirasa brahmins gave him the wealth left after the sacrifice.

## \|\| 7.29.24 \|\|

*tattadā svīkariṣyaṃtaṃ susatrapariśeṣitam \|*  
*vijñāya giriśaḥ sadya āvirbhūta sadūtikṛt \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. Realising that he was taking the wealth left after the sacrifice, Śiva of divine sports manifested himself all of a sudden.

## \|\| 7.29.25 \|\|

*sarvāṃgasundaraḥ śrīmānpuruṣaḥ kṛṣṇadarśanaḥ \|*  
*bhāvaṃ samīkṣituṃ bhāgaṃ dātuṃ jñānaṃ paraṃ ca tat \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. He was beautiful in every limb, a glorious Puruṣa Kṛṣṇadarśana. It was to survey his emotional reaction and to bestow on him the perfect knowledge as his share that the lord manifested himself.

## \|\| 7.29.26 \|\|

*atho sa śaṃkaraḥ śambhuḥ parīkṣākara īśvaraḥ \|*  
*uvācottarato'bhyetya nabhagaṃ taṃ hi mānavam \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Lord Śiva desirous of testing Nabhaga the son of Manu approached from north and said.

## \|\| 7.29.27 \|\|

*īśvara uvāca \|*  
*kastvaṃ gṛhṇāsi puruṣa mamedaṃ vāstukaṃ vasu \|*  
*preṣitaḥ kena tatsarvaṃ satyaṃ vada mamāgrataḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

Śiva said:—

27\. O man, who are you that take away my assets and wealth? Who has sent you here? Tell me the whole truth.

## \|\| 7.29.28 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*tacchutvā tadvacastāta mānavo nabhagaḥ kavi \|*  
*pratyuvāca vinītātmā puruṣaṃ kṛṣṇadarśanam \|\| 28 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

28\. On heaving his words, O dear, the poet Nabhaga, son of Manu humbly replied to the Puruṣa Kṛṣṇadarśana.

## \|\| 7.29.29 \|\|

*nabhagaḥ uvāca \|*  
*mamedamṛṣibhirdattaṃ vasu yajñagataṃ khalu \|*  
*kathaṃ vārayase māṃ tvaṃ gṛhṇataṃ kṛṣṇadarśanam \|\| 29 \|\|*

Nabhaga said:—

29\. This wealth of the sacrifice has been given to me by the sages. Why do you, O Kṛṣṇadarśana prevent me from taking it.

## \|\| 7.29.30 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ākarṇya nābhagaṃ vākyamidaṃ satyamudīritam \|*  
*pratyuvāca prasannātmā puruṣaḥ kṛṣṇadarśanaḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

30\. On hearing these truthful words uttered by Nabhaga, the delighted puruṣa Kṛṣṇadarśana spoke.

Kṛṣṇadarśana said

## \|\| 7.29.31 \|\|

*kṛṣṇadarśana uvāca \|*  
*vivāde'sminhi nau tāta pramāṇaṃ janakastava \|*  
*yāhi tampṛccha sa brūyāttatpramāṇantu satyataḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. O dear, in our dispute the ultimate authority is your father. Go to him and ask him. What he says will be true and final.

## \|\| 7.29.32 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*tadākarṇya vacastasya nabhago mānavaḥ kaviḥ \|*  
*āgacchatpitaraṃ prītyā taduktaṃ pṛṣṭavānmune \|\| 32 \|\|*

Nāndīśvata said:—

32\. On hearing his words, the wise Nabhaga son of Manu, approached his father anxiously, O sage, and asked him about what he said.

## \|\| 7.29.33 \|\|

*putroditaṃ samākarṇya śrāddhadevassa vai manuḥ \|*  
*smṛtvā śivapadāmbhojaṃ prāptasmṛtiruvāca tam \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. On hearing what his son said, Manu Śrāddhadeva remembered the lotus-like feet of Śiva and after recollecting spoke to him.

## \|\| 7.29.34 \|\|

*manuruvāca \|*  
*he tāta śṛṇu madvākyaṃ sa devaḥ puruṣaḥ śivaḥ \|*  
*tasyaiva sakalaṃ vastu yajñaprāptaṃ viśeṣataḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

Manu said:—

34\. O dear, listen to my words: that Puruṣa is lord Śiva. Every object is his and particularly what is obtained from the sacrifice.

## \|\| 7.29.35 \|\|

*adhvarorvaritaṃ vastu rudrabhāgaḥ prakīrtitaḥ \|*  
*ityapi prājñavādo hi kvacijjātastadicchayā \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. That what is left over after the sacrifice is Śiva’s share. This argument is current among some intelligent people due to his will.

## \|\| 7.29.36 \|\|

*sa deva īśvaraḥ sarvaṃ vastvarhati na saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*yajñāvaśiṣṭaṃ kimuta pare tasyecchayā vibhoḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. Undoubtedly lord Śiva deserves everything. What then of the wealth left over in a sacrifice? More depends on the lord’s wish.

## \|\| 7.29.37 \|\|

*anugrahārthamāyātastava tadrūpataḥ prabhuḥ \|*  
*tatra tvaṃ gaccha nabhaga prasannaṃ kuru satyataḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. O Nabhaga, the lord has come in that form in order to bless you. Go there. Propitiate him properly.

## \|\| 7.29.38 \|\|

*kṣamāpaya svāparādhaṃ supraṇamya stutiṃ kuru \|*  
*sarvaprabhussa eveśo yajñādhīśo'khileśvaraḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Request him to forgive your fault. Bow to him well and eulogise him. That Śiva alone is the lord of all, the lord of sacrifice, the lord of everything.

## \|\| 7.29.39 \|\|

*viṣṇubrahmādayo devāḥ siddhāssarvarṣayo'pi hi \|*  
*tadanugrahatastāta samarthaḥ sarvakarmaṇi \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. Viṣṇu, Brahmā and other gods, Siddhas and the sages, O dear, are competent in their respective duties only due to his blessings.

## \|\| 7.29.40 \|\|

*kimbahūktyātmajaśreṣṭha gaccha tatrāśu māciram \|*  
*prasādaya mahādevaṃ sarvathā sakaleśvaram \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. O foremost of my sons, go there quickly. Do not delay. Propitiate the great god, the lord of all by every means.

## \|\| 7.29.41-42 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityuktvā sa manuḥ śrāddhadevaśca tanayaṃ drutam \|*  
*preṣayāmāsa nikaṭaṃ śambhosso'pi sametya tam \|\| 41 \|\|*

*nabhagaśca praṇamyāśu sāñjalirnatamastakaḥ \|*  
*provāca suprasannātmā vinayena mahāmatiḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

41-42. After saying this, Manu Śrāddhadeva, sent his son immediately to Śiva. After approaching him and bowing to him with palms joined in reverence and head bent down, the delighted Nabhaga of great intellect spoke to him humbly.

## \|\| 7.29.43 \|\|

*nabhaga uvāca \|*  
*idaṃ taveśa sarvaṃ hi vastu tribhuvane hi yat \|*  
*ityāha me pitā nūnaṃ kimutādhvaraśeṣitam \|\| 43 \|\|*

Nabhaga said:—

43\. O lord, my father says that whatever there is in the three worlds is yours to be sure. What then about the wealth left over in a sacrifice?

## \|\| 7.29.44 \|\|

*ajānatā mayā nātha yaduktantadvaco bhramāt \|*  
*aparādhantvaṃ kṣamasva śirasā tvāṃ prasādaye \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. O lord, what I said before was done in error and ignorance. Please forgive the same. I propitiate you with my head bent down.

## \|\| 7.29.45 \|\|

*ityuktvā nabhagassotidīnadhīstu kṛtāñjaliḥ \|*  
*tuṣṭāva taṃ maheśānaṃ kṛṣṇadarśanamānataḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. After saying this, Nabhaga of dejected mind, eulogised lord Śiva Kṛṣṇadarśana with palms joined in reverence and with his head bent down.

## \|\| 7.29.46 \|\|

*śrāddhadevo'pi śuddhātmā natakassāñjalissudhīḥ \|*  
*tuṣṭāva taṃ prabhuṃ natvā svāparādhaṃ kṣamāpayat \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. The pure-souled Śrāddhadeva too with head bent

and palms joined eulogised the lord and requested him to forgive his fault.

## \|\| 7.29.47 \|\|

*etasminnantare tatra viṣṇurbrahmākhilaḥ sudhīḥ \|*  
*vāsavādyāḥ samājagmuḥ siddhāśca munayo'pi hi \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. In the meantime, Viṣṇu, Brahmā, Indra and the gods, Siddhas and the sages came there.

## \|\| 7.29.48 \|\|

*mahotsavaṃ prakurvantaḥ sukṛtālayo'khilāḥ \|*  
*tuṣṭuvurnatakā bhaktyā supraṇamya pṛthakpṛthak \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. They were jubilant. They joined their palms in reverence. With great devotion they severally bowed down and eulogised.

## \|\| 7.29.49 \|\|

*atha rudraḥ prasannātmā kṛpādṛṣṭyā vilokya tān \|*  
*uvāca nabhagaṃ prītyā sasmitaṃ kṛṣṇadarśanaḥ \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. Then the delighted Rudra, Kṛṣṇadarśana glanced at them with sympathetic eyes and smilingly spoke to Nabhaga.

## \|\| 7.29.50 \|\|

*kṛṣṇadarśana uvāca \|*  
*yatte pitāvadaddharmyaṃ vākyantattu tathaiva hi \|*  
*tvayāpi satyamuktaṃ tatsādhustvannātra saṃśayaḥ \|\| 50 \|\|*

Kṛṣṇadarśana said:—

50\. What your father said is virtuous and true. You too spoke the truth. Hence you are good. There is no doubt in this.

## \|\| 7.29.51 \|\|

*ato'haṃ suprasanno'smi sarvathā suvratena te \|*  
*dadāmi kṛpayā te hi jñānambrahma sanātanam \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. Hence I am delighted with your holy rites. Sympathetically I am conferring on you the eternal Brahman, the perfect knowledge.

## \|\| 7.29.52 \|\|

*mahājñānī bhava tvaṃ hi savipro nabhagaṃ drutam \|*  
*gṛhāṇa vastvidaṃ sarvaṃ maddattaṃ kṛpayādhunā \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. Be great and wise, O Nabhaga, along with the brahmins immediately. Take all this wealth granted by me out of compassion.

## \|\| 7.29.53 \|\|

*iha sarvasukhaṃ bhuṅkṣva nirvikāraṃ mahāmate \|*  
*sugatiṃ prāpsyasi tvaṃ hi savipraḥ kṛpayā mama \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. O intelligent one, enjoy all pleasures without any aberration. By my grace you will attain the good goal along with the brahmin sacrifices.

## \|\| 7.29.54 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityuktvā tāta bhagavānsa rudraḥ satyavatsalaḥ \|*  
*sarveṣāmpaśyatānteṣāntatraivāntardadhe haraḥ \|\| 54 \|\|*

Nanāiśvara said:—

54\. O dear, after saying this, lord Śiva, favourably disposed to truth, vanished them itself even as all were watching.

## \|\| 7.29.55 \|\|

*viṣṇurbrahmāpi devādyāssarve te munisattama \|*  
*svaṃsvaṃ dhāma yayuḥ prītyā tasyai natvā diśe mudā \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. Viṣṇu, Brahmā, the gods and all others, O excellent sage, went to their respective abodes after joyously bowing to that direction.

## \|\| 7.29.56 \|\|

*saputraḥ śrāddhadevo'pi svasthānamagamanmudā \|*  
*bhuktvā bhogānsuvipulānso'nte śivapura yayau \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. Śrāddhadeva too, accompanied by his son, went joyously to his abode. After enjoying extensive pleasures he went to Śiva’s city in the end.

## \|\| 7.29.57 \|\|
