*itthante kīrtito brahmannavatāraḥ śivasya hi \|*  
*kṛṣṇadarśananāmā vai nabhagānandadāyakaḥ \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. Thus, O Brahmin, the incarnation of Śiva, named Kṛṣṇadarśana, bestower of bliss on Nabhaga, has been narrated to you.

## \|\| 7.29.58 \|\|

*idamākhyānamanaghaṃ bhuktimuktipradaṃ satām \|*  
*paṭhatāṃ śṛṇvatāṃ vāpi sarva kāmaphalapradam \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. This narrative is holy and conducive to worldly enjoyment. It grants salvation to the good. It yields the fruits of desires to those who read and listen to it.

## \|\| 7.29.59 \|\|

*ya etaccaritamprātassāyaṃ ca smarate sudhīḥ \|*  
*kavirbhavati mantrajño gatimante labhetparām \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. The intelligent man who remembers this narrative both in the morning and in the evening becomes wise and knower of mantras. He attains the greatest goal in the end.

## Chapter 30 - The incarnation of Śiva as Avadhūteśvara

## \|\| 7.30.1 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*śṛṇu tvaṃ brahmaputrādyāvatāraṃ parameśituḥ \|*  
*avadhūteśvarāhvaṃ vai śakragarvāpahārakam \|\| 1 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

1\. O son of Brahmā, listen to the first incarnation of the great lord, named Avadhūteśvara, who dispelled the arrogance of Indra.

## \|\| 7.30.2 \|\|

*śakraḥ purā hi saguruḥ sarvadevasamanvitaḥ \|*  
*darśanaṃ kartumīśasya kailāsamagamanmune \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. O sage, formerly Indra accompanied by Bṛhaspati and the other gods went to Kailāsa in order to see Śiva.

## \|\| 7.30.3-4 \|\|

*atha gurvindrayorjñātvāgamanaṃ śaṃkarastayoḥ \|*  
*parīkṣituṃ ca tadbhāvaṃ svadarśanaratātmanoḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

*avadhūtasvarūpo'bhūnnānālīlākaraḥ prabhuḥ \|*  
*digaṃbaro mahābhīmo jvaladagnisamaprabhaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

3-4. On knowing that Bṛhaspati and Indra were coming and in order to test their mind in their eagerness to see him, the lord indulging in different kinds of sports assumed the form of an Avadhūta. He was naked. He looked fierce and had the lustre of blazing fire.

## \|\| 7.30.5 \|\|

*so'vadhūtasvarūpo hi mārgamāruddhya sadgatiḥ \|*  
*laṃbamānapaṭaḥ śaṃbhuratiṣṭhacchobhitākṛtiḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. The Avadhūta, the goal of the good, blocked their way. He stood there with matted hair hanging loosely. and the whole feature shining brilliantly.

## \|\| 7.30.6 \|\|

*atha tau guruśakrau ca gacchantau śiva sannidhim \|*  
*adrāṣṭāṃpuruṣaṃ bhīmaṃ mārgamadhye'dbhutākṛtim \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Passing that way towards Śiva, Bṛhaspati and Indra saw the gigantic figure of wonderful features on the way.

## \|\| 7.30.7 \|\|

*atha śakro mune'pṛcchatsvādhikāreṇa durmadaḥ \|*  
*puruṣaṃ taṃ svamārgāntaḥ sthitamajñāya śaṃkaram \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Without Knowing mat the Puruṣa was Śiva himself, O sage, Indra who was arrogant due to his authority asked him as he stood in his way.

## \|\| 7.30.8 \|\|

*śakra uvāca \|*  
*kastvaṃ digaṃbarākārāvadhūtaḥ kuta āgataḥ \|*  
*kinnāma tava vikhyātaṃ tattvato vada me'ciram \|\| 8 \|\|*

Indra said:—

8\. Who are you? Whence have you come in this form of a naked Avadhūta? What is your true name? Tell me quīckly and factually.

## \|\| 7.30.9 \|\|

*svasthāne saṃsthitaḥ śaṃbhuḥ kimvānyatra gato'dhunā \|*  
*darśanārthaṃ hi tasyāhaṃ gacchāmi sagurussuraiḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. Is Śiva in his abode? Or has he gone out? I am now going to see him along with my preceptor and the gods.

## \|\| 7.30.10 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*śakreṇetthaṃ sa pṛṣṭaśca kiṃcinnovāca pūruṣaḥ \|*  
*līlāgṛhītadehassa śaṅkaro madahā prabhuḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

10\. When asked by Indra that Puruṣa who was lord Śiva himself who had taken that body sportively in order to quell his arrogance did not say anything.

## \|\| 7.30.11 \|\|

*śakraḥ punarapṛcchattaṃ novāca sa digaṃbaraḥ \|*  
*avijñātagatiśśambhurmahākautukakārakaḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Indra asked him again; but Śiva whose ways are unknown, who was in the naked form and who was the creator of fanciful things did not say anything.

## \|\| 7.30.12 \|\|

*punaḥ purandaro'pṛcchttrailokyādhipatissvarāṭ \|*  
*tūṣṇīmāsa mahāyogī mahālīlākarassa vai \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Again Indra, the lord of heaven and the master of the three worlds, asked him. The great Yogin of great sports remained silent.

## \|\| 7.30.13 \|\|

*itthaṃ punaḥ punaḥ pṛṣṭaḥ śakreṇa sa digambaraḥ \|*  
*novāca kiṃcidbhagavāñśakradarppajighāṃsayā \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Though asked by Indra again and again thus, lord Śiva remained silent desirous of quelling the arrogance of Indra.

## \|\| 7.30.14 \|\|

*atha cukrodha deveśastrailokyaiśvaryyagarvitaḥ \|*  
*uvāca vacanaṃ krodhāttaṃ nirbhartsya jaṭādharam \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Then the lord of the gods proud of his suzerainty over the three worlds became furious and said rebuking the lord having matted hair.

## \|\| 7.30.15 \|\|

*indra uvāca \|*  
*pṛcchamāno'pi re mūḍha nottaraṃ dattavānasi \|*  
*atastvāṃ hanmi vajreṇa kaste trātāsti durmate \|\| 15 \|\|*

Indra said:—

15\. O you fool, even though I am asking, you are not giving me any answer. Hence I am going so kill you with my thunderbolt. O wicked fellow, who is there to protect you?

## \|\| 7.30.16 \|\|

*ityudīryya tato vajrī saṃnirīkṣya krudhā hi tam \|*  
*hantundigambaraṃ vajramudyataṃ sa cakāra ha \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. After saying this and staring at him in fury Indra raised his thunderbolt in order to kill the naked person.

## \|\| 7.30.17 \|\|

*vajrahastaṃ ca taṃ dṛṣṭvā śakraṃ śīghraṃ sadāśivaḥ \|*  
*cakāra stambhanaṃ tasya vajrapātasya śaṃkaraḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. On seeing Indra lifting up the thunderbolt, Śiva made his hand stunned.

## \|\| 7.30.18 \|\|

*tataḥ sa puruṣaḥ kuddhaḥ karālākṣo bhayaṃkaraḥ \|*  
*drutameva prajajvāla tejasā pradahanniva \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. Then that Puruṣa, furious and terrific, with terrible eyes, blazed with his brilliance as though he would burn everything.

## \|\| 7.30.19 \|\|

*bāhupratiṣṭambhabhuvā manyunāntaśśacīpatiḥ \|*  
*samadahyata bhogīva maṃtraruddhaparākramaḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. The lord of Śacī burnt within himself due to the anger resulting from the benumbed state of his hand like a serpent whose fury had been curbed by a magical formula.

## \|\| 7.30.20 \|\|

*dṛṣṭvā vṛhaspatistvenamprajvalantaṃ svatejasā \|*  
*puruṣaṃ taṃ dhiyāmāsa praṇanāma haraṃ drutam \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. On seeing the Puruṣa blazing thus in his own brilliance Bṛhaspati knew him to be Śiva and bowed to him soon.

## \|\| 7.30.21 \|\|

*kṛtāñjalipuṭo bhūtvā tato gururudāradhīḥ \|*  
*daṇḍavatkau punarnatvā prabhuṃ tuṣṭāva bhaktitaḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. Bṛhaspati of liberal mind kept his palms joined together and prostrated on the ground like a staff and eulogised him with devotion.

## \|\| 7.30.22 \|\|

*gururuvāca \|*  
*devadeva mahādeva śaraṇāgatavatsala \|*  
*prasanno bhava gaurīśa sarveśvara namo'stu te \|\| 22 \|\|*

Bṛhaspati said: -

22\. O great god, lord of the gods, favourably disposed towards those who seek refuge in you, O lord of Gaurī, be pleased. O lord of all, obeisance be to you.

## \|\| 7.30.23 \|\|

*māyayā mohitāssarve brahmaviṣṇvādayopi te \|*  
*tvāṃ na jānanti tattvena jānanti tvadanugrahāt \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Even Brahmā, Viṣṇu and others are all deluded by your Māyā. They do not know you really. If at all they know, it is only by your blessings.

## \|\| 7.30.24 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*bṛhaspatiriti stutvā sa tadā śaṃkaramprabhum \|*  
*pādayoḥ pātayāmāsa tasyeśasya purandaram \|\| 24 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

24\. After eulogising lord Śiva, Bṛhaspati made Indra fell at his feet.

## \|\| 7.30.25 \|\|

*tatastāta surācāryyaḥ kṛtāñjalirudāradhīḥ \|*  
*bṛhaspatiruvācedaṃ praśrayāvanataḥ sudhīḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. O dear, then the liberal-minded preceptor of the gods, Bṛhaspati, the intelligent one, bowed humbly and spoke thus.

## \|\| 7.30.26 \|\|

*bṛhaspatiruvāca \|*  
*dīnanātha mahādeva praṇatantava pādayoḥ \|*  
*samuddhara ca māṃ tattvaṃ krodhaṃ na praṇayaṃ kuru \|\| 26 \|\|*

Bṛhaspati said:—

26\. O great god, lord of the distressed, please raise me up, who have fallen at your feet. Do not be furious. Be kind and loving.

## \|\| 7.30.27 \|\|

*tuṣṭo bhava mahādeva pāhīndraṃ śaraṇāgatam \|*  
*vahnireṣa samāyāti bhālanetrasamudbhavaḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. O great god, be satisfied. Save Indra who has sought refuge in you. Fire originating from your eye in the forehead is coming out.

## \|\| 7.30.28 \|\|

*\|\| nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityākarṇya gurorvākyamavadhūtākṛtiḥ prabhuḥ \|*  
*uvāca karuṇāsiṃdhurvihasansa sadūtikṛt \|\| 28 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

28\. On hearing the words of Bṛhaspati, the lord in the form of Avadhūta, the ocean of mercy, indulging in good sports, laughingly said.

## \|\| 7.30.29 \|\|

*avadhūta uvāca \|*  
*krodhācca nissṛtantejo dhārayāmi svanetrataḥ \|*  
*kathaṃ hi kaṃcukīṃ sarpassaṃdhatte cojjñitāṃ punaḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

Avadhūta said:—

29\. How can I withhold the fire that has come out of my eye due to anger? How can a serpent take up a slough that has been cast off.

## \|\| 7.30.30 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*iti śrutvā vacastasya śaṃkarasya bṛhaspatiḥ \|*  
*uvāca sāñjalirbhūyo bhayavyākulamānasaḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

30\. On hearing these words of Śiva, Bṛhaspati with his mind agitated due to fright spoke again with palms joined in revercncc.

## \|\| 7.30.31 \|\|

*\|\| bṛhaspatiruvāca \|*  
*he deva bhagavanbhaktā anukampyāḥ sadaiva hi \|*  
*bhaktavatsalanāmeti svaṃ satyaṃ kuru śaṃkara \|\| 31 \|\|*

Bṛhaspati said:—

31\. O lord, devotees are always to be pitied. O Śiva, thus make your name Bhaktavatsala true.

## \|\| 7.30.32 \|\|

*kṣeptumanyatra deveśa svatejo'tyugramarhasi \|*  
*uddhartā sarvabhaktānāṃ samuddhara purandaram \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. O lord of the gods, it behoves you to cast off this fire elsewhere. Please lift up Indra since you are the up-lifter of all devotees.

## \|\| 7.30.33 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityukto guruṇā rudro bhaktavatsalanāmabhāk \|*  
*pratyuvāca prasannātmā surejyampraṇatārtihā \|\| 33 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

33\. Thus requested by Bṛhaspati, Śiva who bore the name of Bhaktavatsala, the delighted soul and the dispeller of the distress of those who bow down, replied.

## \|\| 7.30.34 \|\|

*rudra uvāca \|*  
*prītastehaṃ surācāryya dadāmi varamuttamam \|*  
*indrasya jīvadānena jīveti tvaṃ prathāmvraja \|\| 34 \|\|*

Śiva said:—

34\. O preceptor of the gods, I am pleased with you. I shall grant you excellent boon. You will be famous as ‘Jīva’ because you have gifted life to Indra.

## \|\| 7.30.35 \|\|

*samudbhūto'nalo yoyaṃ bhālanetrātsurā sahaḥ \|*  
*enantyakṣyāmyahaṃ dūre yathendraṃ naiva pīḍayet \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. I shall cast off this fire which has come out of the eye in my forehead, and which is unbearable to the gods, so that it will not afflict Indra.

## \|\| 7.30.36 \|\|

*\|\| nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityuktvā sa kare dhṛtvā svatejo'nalamadbhutam \|*  
*bhālanetrasamudbhūtaṃ prākṣipallavaṇāmbhasi \|\| 36 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

36\. After saying this he took the wonderful fire, originating from his eye in the forehead in his hand and cast it off into the briny sea.

## \|\| 7.30.37 \|\|

*atho śivasya tattejo bhālanetrasamudbhavam \|*  
*kṣiptaṃ ca lavaṇāmbhodhau sadyo bālo babhūva ha \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. Then that fire originating from the eye in the forehead of Śiva and cast off into the briny sea became a boy immediately.

## \|\| 7.30.38 \|\|

*sa jalandharanāmābhūtsindhuputro'sureśvaraḥ \|*  
*taṃ jaghāna maheśāno devaprārthanayā prabhuḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. He became the lord of Asuras known as Jalandhara and Sindhuputra (Son of the Sea). At the request of the gods, lord Śiva slew him.

## \|\| 7.30.39 \|\|

*itthaṃ kṛtvā sucaritaṃ śaṃkaro lokaśaṃkaraḥ \|*  
*avadhūtasvarūpeṇa tataścāntarhito'bhavat \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. After carrying out this task Śiva, the benefactor of the worlds in the form of Avadhūta, vanished from there.

## \|\| 7.30.40 \|\|

*babhūvuḥ sakalā devāḥ sukhinaścātinirbhayāḥ \|*  
*guruśakrau bhayānmuktau jagmatuḥ sukhamuttamam \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. The gods became happy and fearless. Bṛhaspati and Indra became free from terror and attained supreme happiness.

## \|\| 7.30.41 \|\|

*yadarthe gamanodyuktau darśanaṃ prāpya tasya tau \|*  
*kṛtārthau guruśakrau hi svasthānaṃ jagmaturmudā \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. After seeing the lord for which they had come there, Bṛhaspati and Indra returned to their respective abodes joyous and contented.

## \|\| 7.30.42 \|\|

*avadhūteśvarāhvo'vatāraste kathito mayā \|*  
*parameśasya paramānandadaḥ khaladaṇḍadaḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. Thus the incarnation Avadhūteśvara of lord Śiva has been narrated by me. It yields the highest bliss and punishes the wicked.

## \|\| 7.30.43 \|\|

*idamākhyānamanaghaṃ yaśasyaṃ svargyameva ca \|*  
*bhuktimuktipradaṃ divyaṃ sarvakāmaphalapradam \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. This narrative is faultless and conducive to heaven and fame. It yields worldly pleasures and salvation. It is divine and it gives the fruits of all desires.

## \|\| 7.30.44 \|\|

*ya idaṃ śṛṇuyānnityaṃ śrāvayedvā samāhitaḥ \|*  
*iha sarvasukhaṃ bhuktvā sonte śivagatiṃ labhet \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. He who listens to this or narrates this with attentive mind enjoys all pleasures and attains the goal of Śiva in the end.

## Chapter 31 - The incarnation of Śiva as Bhikṣuvarya

## \|\| 7.31.1 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*atha vakṣye muniśreṣṭha śambhoḥ śṛṇvavatārakam \|*  
*svabhaktadayayā vipra nārīsandehabhaṃjakam \|\| 1 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

1\. O excellent sage, I shall now narrate the incarnation of Śiva in which he dispelled the suspicion of a brahmin woman.

## \|\| 7.31.2 \|\|

*āsītsatyaratho nāmnā vidarbhaviṣaye nṛpaḥ \|*  
*dharmmātmā satyaśīlaśca mahāśaivajanapriyaḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. There was a king in the land of Vidarbha, named Satyaratha. He was virtuous, regularly observing truthfulness and a favourite of great devotees of Śiva.

## \|\| 7.31.3 \|\|

*tasya rājñassudharmeṇa mahīṃ pālayato mune \|*  
*mahānkālo vyatīyāya sukhena śivadharmmataḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. O sage, as the king ruled over the kingdom virtuously observing piety to Śiva, a long time elapsed very happily.

## \|\| 7.31.4 \|\|

*kadācittasya rājñastu śālvaiśca purarodhibhiḥ \|*  
*mahānraṇo babhūvātha bahusainyairbaloddhataiḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. Once a great fight ensued between that king and the Śālvas who laid siege to his city with many armies puffed up with their strength.

## \|\| 7.31.5 \|\|

*sa vidarbhanṛpaḥ kṛtvā sārdhaṃ tairdāruṇaṃ raṇam \|*  
*pranaṣṭorubalaḥ śālvairnihato daivayogataḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. The king of Vidarbha had a terrible fight with them. But his armies were destroyed and he was killed by Śālvas due to adverse fate.

## \|\| 7.31.6 \|\|

*tasminnṛpe hate yuddhe śālvaistu bhayavihvalāḥ \|*  
*sainikā hataśeṣāśca mantribhissaha dudruvuḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. When the king was. killed in the battle by the Śālvas, the terrified soldiers who survived death fled along with the ministers.

## \|\| 7.31.7 \|\|

*atha tasya mahārājñī rātrau svapurato mune \|*  
*saṃruddhā ripubhiryatnādantarvatnī bahiryayau \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. His crowned queen who was pregnant and who had been imprisoned by the enemies escaped from the city, O sage, during the night,

## \|\| 7.31.8 \|\|

*nirgatā śokasaṃtaptā sā rājamahiṣī śanaiḥ \|*  
*prācīṃ diśaṃ yayau dūraṃ smarantīśapadāmbujam \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Coming out of the city the grief-stricken crowned queen slowly went very far in the eastern direction with her heart set on the lotus-like feet of Śiva.

## \|\| 7.31.9 \|\|

*atha prabhāte sā rājñī dadarśa vimalaṃ saraḥ \|*  
*atītā dūramadhvānaṃ dayayā śaṅkarasya hi \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. Early in the morning next day after she had traversed a long way, the queen saw a pure lake, thanks to the mercy of Siva.

## \|\| 7.31.10 \|\|

*tatrāgatya priyā rājñassaṃtaptā sukumāriṇī \|*  
*nivāsārthaṃ sarastīre chāyāvṛkṣamupāśrayat \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. After reaching the banks of the lake the distressed beloved of the king, of tender body, resorted to a shady tree for rest and stay.

## \|\| 7.31.11 \|\|

*tatra daivavaśādrājñī muhūrtte sadguṇānvite \|*  
*asūta tanayaṃ divyaṃ sarvalakṣaṇalakṣitam \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Fortunately in an auspicious and meritorious hour the queen gave birth to a son endowed with all divine characteristics.

## \|\| 7.31.12 \|\|

*atha tajjananī daivāttṛṣitāti nṛpāṅganā \|*  
*sarovatīrṇā pānārthaṃ grastā grāheṇa pāthasi \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. The thirsty mother, the wife of the king, entered the lake for drinking water but as willed by fate she was devoured by a crocodile in the water.

## \|\| 7.31.13 \|\|

*sa suto jātamātrastu kṣutpipāsārddito bhṛśam \|*  
*ruroda ca sarastīre vinaṣṭa pitṛmātṛkaḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. The son bereft of father and mother, distressed by hunger and thirst at the time of birth, cried out.

## \|\| 7.31.14 \|\|

*tasminvane krandamāne jātamātre sute mune \|*  
*kṛpānvito maheśo'bhūdantaryāmī sa rakṣaka \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. O sage, when the new born babe was weeping and wailing in the forest, lord Śiva, the immanent soul and protector, was moved to pity.

## \|\| 7.31.15 \|\|

*preritā manasā kācidīśena trāsahāriṇā \|*  
*akasmādāgatā tatra bhramantī bhaikṣyajīvinī \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. A wandering beggar woman came there accidentally, urged mentally by Śiva, the dispeller of fear.

## \|\| 7.31.16 \|\|

*sā tvekahāyanaṃ bālaṃ vahantī vidhavā nijam \|*  
*anāthamekaṃ kraṃdantaṃ śiśuntatra dadarśa ha \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. She was a widow and was herself carrying her one year old son. She saw an orphaned child weeping there.

## \|\| 7.31.17 \|\|

*sā dṛṣṭvā tatra tambālaṃ vane nirmanuje mune \|*  
*vismitāti dvijastrī sā cicintaṃ hṛdaye bahu \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. O sage, on seeing an infant boy in the desolate forest that brahmin woman was surprised and pondered over the situation in diverse ways.

## \|\| 7.31.18 \|\|

*aho sumahadāścaryyamidaṃ dṛṣṭammayādhunā \|*  
*asaṃbhāvyamakathyaṃ ca sarvathā manasā girā \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. “An extremely wonderful thing has been seen by me now. It cannot be conceived by the mind or explained in words.

## \|\| 7.31.19 \|\|

*acchinnanābhināloyaṃ rasāyāṃ kevalaṃ śiśuḥ \|*  
*śete mātṛvihīnaśca krandaṃstejasvināṃ varaḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. This infant whose umbilical cord has not been cut lies on the bare ground. He has no mother. He is weeping but he is very brilliant

## \|\| 7.31.20 \|\|

*asya pitrādayaḥ ke'pi na santīha sahāyinaḥ \|*  
*kāraṇaṃ kiṃ babhūvātha hyaho daivabalaṃ mahat \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. He has no parents or persons to help him. What might have caused this? Alas, how strong is power of fate l

## \|\| 7.31.21 \|\|

*na jāne kasya putro'yamasya jñātātra kopi na \|*  
*yataḥ pṛcchāmyasya janya jātā ca karuṇā mayi \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. I do not know his parentage. There is none who knows him. Whom can I enquire about the details of his birth? But I feel drawn towards him.

## \|\| 7.31.22 \|\|

*icchāmyenaṃ poṣituṃ hi bālamaurasaputravat \|*  
*saṃpraṣṭuṃ notsahe'jñātvā kulajanmādi cāsya vai \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. Without knowing his birth and parentage I shall not touch him although I wish to nourish him and bring him up as a child born of me.”

## \|\| 7.31.23 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*iti saṃcintyamānāyāṃ tasyāṃ vipravarastriyām \|*  
*kṛpāṃ cakāra mahatīṃ śaṃkaro bhaktavatsala \|\| 23 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

23\. While the excellent brahmin lady was musing thus, Śiva favourably disposed to his devotees, took pity.

## \|\| 7.31.24 \|\|

*dadhre bhikṣusvarūpaṃ hi mahālīlo maheśvaraḥ \|*  
*sarvathā bhaktasukhado nirupādhiḥ svayaṃ sadā \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. Lord Śiva of great sports, bestower of happiness on devotees in every respect, unconditionally for ever, by himself assumed the form of a mendicant.

## \|\| 7.31.25 \|\|

*tatrājagāma sahasā sa bhikṣuḥ parameśvaraḥ \|*  
*yatrāsti saṃdehavatī dvijastrī jñātumicchatī \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. The mendicant who was lord Śiva himself suddenly came to the place where the doubting brahmin lady, desirous of knowing the details, was standing.

## \|\| 7.31.26 \|\|

*bhikṣuvaryyasvarūpo'sāvavijñātagatiḥ prabhuḥ \|*  
*tāmāha vipravanitāṃ vihasya karuṇānidhiḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. The lord whose ways are unscrutable, who is the storehouse of mercy and who assumed the form of the leading mendicant, laughed and told the brahmin lady.

## \|\| 7.31.27 \|\|

*bhikṣuvaryya uvāca \|*  
*sandehaṃ kuru no citte viprabhāmini mā khida \|*  
*rakṣainambālakaṃ prītyā supavitraṃ svaputrakam \|\| 27 \|\|*

The mendicant said:—

27\. O brahmin lady, do not entertain any doubt in your mind. Do not be sorry. Protect this pure boy, your adopted son, lovingly.

## \|\| 7.31.28 \|\|

*anena śiśunā śreyaḥ prāpsyase na cirātparam \|*  
*puṣṇīhi sarvathā hyenaṃ mahātejasvinaṃ śiśum \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. Ere long you will attain a great glory through this infant. Nourish this extremely brilliant infant by all means.

## \|\| 7.31.29 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityuktavantaṃ taṃ bhikṣusvarūpaṃ karuṇānidhim \|*  
*sā vipravanitā śambhuṃ prītyā papraccha sādaram \|\| 29 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

29\. That brahmin lady respectfully and lovingly asked Śiva, the storehouse of mercy, in the form of a mendicant who spoke thus.

## \|\| 7.31.30 \|\|

*vipravanitovāca \|*  
*tvadājñayainaṃ bālaṃ hi rakṣiṣyāmi svaputravat \|*  
*paukṣyāmi nātra sandeho madbhāgyāttvamihāgataḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

The brahmin lady said:—

30\. At your behest I shall nourish and bring up this boy as though he is my own son. There shall be no; doubt in this. You have come here by my good fortune.

## \|\| 7.31.31 \|\|

*tathāpi jñātumicchāmi viśeṣeṇa tu tattvataḥ \|*  
*koyaṃ kasya sutaścāyaṃ kastvamatra samāgataḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. Still I wish to know the particular details and facts about him. Whose son is he? Who are you, that have come here?

## \|\| 7.31.32 \|\|

*muhurmama samāyāti jñānaṃ bhikṣuvara prabho \|*  
*tvaṃ śivaḥ karuṇāsindhustvadbhaktoyaṃ śiśuḥ purā \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. O lord, O excellent mendicant, again and again the thought occurs to me that you are Śiva, the ocean of mercy and this infant had been your devotee formerly.

## \|\| 7.31.33 \|\|

*kenacitkarmadoṣeṇa samprāptoyaṃ daśāmimām \|*  
*tadbhuktvā paramaṃ śreyaḥ prāpsyate tvadanugrahāt \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. Through some defect in his past actions he has attained this plight. After experiencing it he will surely attain glory, thanks to your bliss.

## \|\| 7.31.34 \|\|

*tvanmāyayaiva sāhaṃ vai mārgabhraṣṭā vimohitā \|*  
*āgatā preṣitā tvatto hyasya rakṣaṇahetutaḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. It is by your Māyā that I have been deluded. I had lost my way. In order to protect him I have been urged by you to come over here.

## \|\| 7.31.35 \|\|

*\|\| nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*iti taddarśanaprāptavijñānāṃ viprakāminīm \|*  
*jñātukāmāṃ viśeṣeṇa proce bhikṣutanuśśivaḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

35\. Śiva in the guise of a mendicant spoke to the brahmin lady who had attained perfect knowledge by seeing him and who evinced the desire to know the details.

## \|\| 7.31.36 \|\|

*\|\| bhikṣuvaryya uvāca \|*  
*śṛṇu prītyā viprapatni bālasyāsya purehitam \|*  
*sarvamanyasya suprītyā vakṣyate tattvato'naghe \|\| 36 \|\|*

The mendicant said:—

36\. O brahmin lady, listen with pleasure to the antecedents of this boy. O sinless one, I shall mention all the details to you.

## \|\| 7.31.37 \|\|

*suto vidarbharājasya śivabhaktasya dhīmataḥ \|*  
*ayaṃ satyarathasyaiva svadharmaniratasya hi \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. This boy is the son of Satyaratha, king of Vidarbha a great devotee of Śiva and devoted to his duty.

## \|\| 7.31.38 \|\|

*śṛṇu satyaratho rājā hataḥ śālve raṇe paraiḥ \|*  
*tatpatnī niśi suvyagrā niryayau svagṛhāddrutam \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Listen, the king Satyaratha was killed by his enemies in the battle. His excited and distressed wife set out from the palace at night.

## \|\| 7.31.39 \|\|

*asūta tanayaṃ cainaṃ samāyātā prage'tra hi \|*  
*sarovatīrṇā tṛṣayā grastā grāheṇa daivataḥ \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. She came here in the morning and gave birth to this boy. Due to thirst she got into the lake and as fate would have it, was seized by the crocodile.

## \|\| 7.31.40 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*iti tasya samutpattiṃ tatpituḥ saṃgare mṛtim \|*  
*tanmātṛmaraṇaṃ grāhātsarvaṃ tasya nyavedayat \|\| 40 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

40\. Thus he narrated to her all the details, the manner of his birth, the death of his father in the battle and that of his mother due to the crocodile.

## \|\| 7.31.41 \|\|

*atha sā brahmāṇī sā hi vismitāti munīśvara \|*  
*punaḥ papraccha taṃ bhikṣuṃ jñāninaṃ siddharūpakam \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. O great sage, then the brahmin lady who was surprised asked the mendicant of perfect knowledge in the form of a Siddha, again.

The brahmin lady said

## \|\| 7.31.42 \|\|

*brāhmaṇyuvāca \|*  
*sa rājo'sya pitā bhikṣo varabhogāntareva hi \|*  
*kasmācchālvaissvaripubhissvalpehaiśca vighātitaḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. O mendicant, how was his father, the king, killed in the midst of his enjoyment of excellent pleasures, by his enemies the Śālvas in a few days?

## \|\| 7.31.43 \|\|

*kasmādasya śiśormātā grāheṇāśu subhakṣitā \|*  
*yasmādanāthoyaṃ jāto vibandhuścaiva janmataḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. How was the mother of this infant devoured by the crocodile so suddenly? How did he happen to be orphaned and helpless ever since his birth?

## \|\| 7.31.44 \|\|

*kasmātsuto mamāpīha sudaridro hi bhikṣukaḥ \|*  
*bhavetkathaṃ sukhaṃ bhikṣo putrayoranayorvada \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. How is it that my son too is very poor and a beggar? O mendicant, how will these two sons attain happiness? Please tell me.

## \|\| 7.31.45 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*iti tasyā vacaḥ śrutvā sa bhikṣuḥ parameśvaraḥ \|*  
*viprapatnyāḥ prasannātmā provāca vihasaṃśca tām \|\| 45 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

45\. On hearing these words of the brahmin wife, the delighted mendicant, lord Śiva himself, said laughingly.

## \|\| 7.31.46 \|\|

*bhikṣuvaryya uvāca \|*  
*viprapatni viśeṣeṇa sarvapraśnānvadāmi te \|*  
*śṛṇu tvaṃ sāvadhānena caritramidamuttamam \|\| 46 \|\|*

The mendicant said:—

46\. O brahmin lady, I shall answer all questions in detail. Listen with attention to this excellent narrative.

## \|\| 7.31.47 \|\|

*amuṣya bālasya pitā sa vidarbhamahīpatiḥ \|*  
*pūrvajanmani pāṇḍyo'sau babhūva nṛpasattamaḥ \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. The father of this boy, the king of Vidarbha, was the excellent king Pāṇḍya in his previous birth.

## \|\| 7.31.48 \|\|

*sa śaivanṛpatirdharmātpālayannikhilā mahīm \|*  
*svaprajāṃ raṃjayāmāsa sarvopadravanāśanaḥ \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. That king was a devotee of Śiva. He ruled over the entire earth virtuously. Quelling all disturbances he made all the subjects happy and contented.

## \|\| 7.31.49 \|\|

*kadācitsa hi sarveśaṃ pradoṣe paryapūjayat \|*  
*trayodaśyāṃ nirāhāro divānaktavratī śivam \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. Observing fast and other rites in the day and night on a Trayodaśī (thirteenth) day, once he worshipped Śiva, the lord of all, at dusk.

## \|\| 7.31.50 \|\|

*tasya pūjayataḥ śambhuṃ pradoṣe giriśaṃ rate \|*  
*mahāñchabdo babhūvātha vikaṭassarvathā pure \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. As he was worshipping Śiva at dusk in the course of his holy rites there arose a great noise, in the city, hideous in every respect.

## \|\| 7.31.51 \|\|

*tamākarṇya ravaṃ so'tha rājā tyaktaśivārcanaḥ \|*  
*ripvāgamanaśaṃkāto niryayau bhavanādbahiḥ \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. On hearing that noise, the king left the worship of Śiva unfinished and went out of his palace suspecting the arrival of enemies.

## \|\| 7.31.52 \|\|

*etasminneva kāle tu tasyāmātyo mahābalī \|*  
*gṛhītaśastrasāmanto rājāntikamupāyayau \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. In the meantime his powerful minister came near the king catching hold of the enemy king.

## \|\| 7.31.53 \|\|

*tandṛṣṭvā śatrusāmantaṃ mahākrodhena vihvalaḥ \|*  
*avicārya vṛṣantasya śiraśchedamakārayat \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. On seeing the enemy king, he was extremely agitated by anger. Disregardful of the right course he caused him to be beheaded.

## \|\| 7.31.54 \|\|

*asamāpye śapūjāntāmaśucirnaṣṭadhīrnṛpaḥ \|*  
*rātrau cakāra suprītyā bhojanannaṣṭamaṃgalaḥ \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. Without concluding the worship of Śiva, the defiled king, confused in mind and bereft of auspiciousness, took his food at night with pleasure.

## \|\| 7.31.55 \|\|

*vidarbhe sobhavadrājā janmanīha śivavratī \|*  
*śivārcanāntarāyeṇa parairbhogāṃntare hataḥ \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. He was born as the king of Vidarbha. He observed holy rites of Śiva during this birth also, since there was a hindrance to his worship of Śiva (during the previous birth) he was now killed in the midst of his enjoyment.

## \|\| 7.31.56 \|\|

*tatputro yaḥ pūrvabhave so'smiñjanmani tatsutaḥ \|*  
*ahameva hataiśvaryyaḥ śivapūjā vyatikramāt \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. His son in the previous birth was born as his son in this birth too but he was bereft of riches due to the break in the worship of Śiva.

## \|\| 7.31.57 \|\|

*asya mātā pūrvabhave sapatnīṃ chadmanāharat \|*  
*bhakṣitā tena pāpena grāheṇā'sminbhave hi sā \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. The mother of this boy had killed her co-wife in the previous birth. Hence due to that sin, she was devoured by the crocodile in this birth.

## \|\| 7.31.58 \|\|

*eṣā pravṛttireteṣāṃ bhavatyai parikīrtitā \|*  
*anarcitā śivā bhaktyā prāpnuvanti daridratām \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. Thus their activities have been narrated to you. People having no devotion to Śiva and not worshipping him attain poverty.

## \|\| 7.31.59 \|\|

*eṣa te tanayaḥ pūrvajanmani brāhmaṇottamaḥ \|*  
*pratigrahairvayo ninye na yajñādyaissukarmabhiḥ \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. This son of yours was an excellent brahmin in his previous birth. He spent his life in accepting gifts but he did not perform holy rites, like sacrifice etc.

## \|\| 7.31.60 \|\|

*ato dāridryamāpannaḥ putraste dvijabhāmini \|*  
*taddoṣaparihārārthaṃ śaraṇaṃ śaṃkaraṃ vraja \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. O brahmin lady, hence your son has incurred poverty. Seek refuge in Śiva in order to ward off that sin.

## \|\| 7.31.61 \|\|

*etābhyāṃ khalu bālābhyāṃ śivapūjāvidhīyatām \|*  
*upavītānantaraṃ hi śivaḥ śreyaḥ kariṣyati \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. Let the worship of Śiva be performed by these two sons. Only after the investiture with the sacred thread will Śiva bestow glory.

## \|\| 7.31.62 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*iti tāmupadiśyātha bhikṣuvarṇyatanuḥ śivaḥ \|*  
*svarūpaṃ darśayāmāsa paramaṃ bhaktavatsalaḥ \|\| 62 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

62\. After instructing her thus, Śiva in the form of a mendicant, favourably disposed towards his devotees revealed his real self to her.

## \|\| 7.31.63 \|\|

*atha sā vipravanitā jñātvā taṃ śaṃkaramprabhum \|*  
*supraṇamya hi tuṣṭāva premṇā gadgadayā girā \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. On coming to know that he was lord Śiva the brahmin lady bowed to and eulogised him with choking words full of devotion.

## \|\| 7.31.64 \|\|

*tatassa bhagavāñcchambhurdhṛtabhikṣutanurdrutam \|*  
*paśyantyā viprapantyāstu tatraivāntaradhīyata \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. Even as the brahmin lady stood watching, Śiva who had taken the form of a mendicant vanished there itself.

## \|\| 7.31.65 \|\|

*atha tasmin gate bhikṣau viśrabdhā brāhmaṇī ca sā \|*  
*tamarbhakaṃ samādāya sasvaputrā gṛhaṃ yayau \|\| 65 \|\|*

65\. When the mendicant disappeared, the care-free brahmin woman took that boy and her son home.

## \|\| 7.31.66 \|\|

*ekacakrāhvaye ramye grāmye kṛta niketanā \|*  
*svaputraṃ rājaputraṃ ca varānnaiśca vyavarddhayat \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. She took up residence in the beautiful city Ekacakrā and nourished the prince and her own son with excellent food.

## \|\| 7.31.67 \|\|

*brāhmaṇai kṛtasaṃskārau kṛtopanayanau ca tau \|*  
*vavṛdhāte svagehe ca śivapūjanatatparau \|\| 67 \|\|*

67\. Their sacred rites and the investiture with the sacred thread were performed by the brahmins. They grew up thus devoted to the worship of Śiva in their own house.

## \|\| 7.31.68 \|\|

*tau śāṇḍilyamunestāta nideśānniyama sthitau \|*  
*pradoṣe cakratuḥ śambhoḥ pūjāṃ kṛtvā vrataṃ śubham \|\| 68 \|\|*

68\. O dear, at the behest of the sage Śāṇḍilya they observed vows and restraints and performed the worship of Śiva during dusk.

## \|\| 7.31.69 \|\|

*kadāciddvijaputreṇa vinā'sau rājanandanaḥ \|*  
*nadyāṃ snātuṃ gataḥ prāpa nidhānakalaśaṃ varam \|\| 69 \|\|*

69\. Once when the prince went to the river for his bath without the brahmin boy, he found an excellent pot of treasure.

## \|\| 7.31.70 \|\|

*evaṃ pūjayatoḥ śambhuṃ rājadvijakumārayoḥ \|*  
*sukhenaiva vyatīyāya tayormāsacatuṣṭayam \|\| 70 \|\|*

70\. Thus four months elapsed as the two boys continued their worship of Śiva.

## \|\| 7.31.71 \|\|

*evamarcayatoḥ śambhuṃ bhūyopi parayā mudā \|*  
*samvatsaro vyatīyāya tasminneva tayorgṛhe \|\| 71 \|\|*

71\. As they continued the worship of Śiva again with great joy, a year elapsed in the very same house.

## \|\| 7.31.72-73 \|\|

*samvatsare vyatikrānte sa rājatanayo mune \|*  
*gatvā vanānte vipreṇa śivasyānugrahādvibhoḥ \|\| 72 \|\|*

*akasmādāgatāṃ tatra dattāṃ tajjanakena ha \|*  
*vivāhya gandharvasutāṃ cakre rājyamakaṇṭakam \|\| 73 \|\|*

72-73. O sage, after a year had elapsed, the prince once went to a forest along with the brahmin boy. There a Gandharva came by chance and gave his daughter in marriage to the prince. The prince ruled over the kingdom without any check or hindrance.

## \|\| 7.31.74 \|\|

*yā vipravanitā pūrvaṃtamapuṣṇātsvaputravat \|*  
*saiva mātābhavattasya sa bhrātā dvijanandanaḥ \|\| 74 \|\|*

74\. He considered the brahman lady who brought him up as his mother and the brahmin boy as his brother.

## \|\| 7.31.75 \|\|

*itthamārādhya deveśaṃ dharmaguptāhvayassa vai \|*  
*vidarbha viṣaye rājñyā tayā bhogaṃ cakāra ha \|\| 75 \|\|*

75\. In name of Dharmagupta he continued to propitiate the lord of gods and enjoyed pleasures along with the princess in the kingdom of Vidarbha.

## \|\| 7.31.76 \|\|

*bhikṣuvaryyāvatāraste varṇitaśca mayādhunā \|*  
*śivasya dharmaguptāhva nṛpabālasukhapradaḥ \|\| 76 \|\|*

76\. Thus the incarnation of Śiva as the Bhikṣuvarya bestowing happiness on the prince Dharmagupta has been narrated by me now.

## \|\| 7.31.77 \|\|

*etadākhyānamanaghaṃ pavitraṃ pāvanaṃ mahat \|*  
*dharmārthakāmamokṣāṇāṃ sādhanaṃ sarvakāmadam \|\| 77 \|\|*

77\. This narrative is pious, extremely sanctifying and holy. It is an instrument for attaining virtue, wealth, love and liberation. It yields all pleasures.

## \|\| 7.31.78 \|\|

*ya etacchrṛṇuyānnityaṃ śrāvayedvā samāhitaḥ \|*  
*sa bhuktvehākhilānkāmānsonte śivapuramvrajet \|\| 78 \|\|*

78\. He who listens to this tale or narrates this to others with pure mind enjoys all pleasures here and goes to the region of Śiva hereafter.

## Chapter 32 - The incarnation of Śiva named Sureśvara

## \|\| 7.32.1 \|\|

*śṛṇu tāta pravakṣyāmi śivasya paramātmanaḥ \|*  
*sureśvarāvatāraste dhaumyāgraja hitāvaham \|\| 1 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

1\. O dear, listen, I shall narrate the incarnation of śiva, the great soul, as Indra in which he was beneficial to Dhaumya’s elder brother.

## \|\| 7.32.2 \|\|

*vyāghrapādasuto dhīmānupamanyussatāmpriyaḥ \|*  
*janmāntareṇa saṃsiddhaḥ prāpto munikumāratām \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. The intelligent son of Vyāghrapāda, Upamanyu, the beloved of the good, became the son of a sage in another birth. He achieved great attainments then.

## \|\| 7.32.3 \|\|

*uvāsa mātulagṛhe sa mātrā śiśure va hi \|*  
*upamanyurvyāghrapādissyāddaridraśca daivataḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Upamanyu, the son of Vyāghrapāda, while yet a small child stayed in his uncle’s house along with his mother. Due to adverse fate he was very poor.

## \|\| 7.32.4 \|\|

*kadācitkṣīramatyalpampītavānmātulāśrame \|*  
*yayāce mātaramprītyā bahuśo dugdha lālasaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. Once he tasted a little milk in his uncle’s hermitage. Approaching his mother he implored for milk, yearning for it.

## \|\| 7.32.5 \|\|

*tacchrutvā putravacanaṃ tanmātā ca tapasvinī \|*  
*sāṃtaḥ praviśyātha tadā śubhopāyamarīracat \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. On hearing the words of her son the pitiable mother went in and thought of a proper means to satisfy the child.

## \|\| 7.32.6 \|\|

*uñchavṛttyarjitānbījānpiṣṭvāloḍya jalena tān \|*  
*upalālya sutantasmai sā dadau kṛtrimampayaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. She ground some seeds and grains she got by gleaning, into a paste with water. She gave this artificial milk to her son after fondling and coaxing him.

## \|\| 7.32.7 \|\|

*pītvā ca kṛtrimaṃ dugdhaṃ mātrā dattaṃ sa bālakaḥ \|*  
*naitatkṣīramiti prāha mātaraṃ cārudatpunaḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. After drinking the artificial milk given by his mother the boy said “O, this is not milk” and cried again.

## \|\| 7.32.8 \|\|

*śrutvā sutasya ruditaṃ prāha sā duḥkhitā sutama \|*  
*saṃmārjya netre putrasya karābhyāṃ kamalākṛtiḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. On hearing the cry of her son, she wiped off his lotus-like eyes and said in grief.

## \|\| 7.32.9 \|\|

*mātovāca\|\|kṣīramatra kuto'smākaṃ vane nivasatāṃ sadā \|*  
*prasādena vinā śambhoḥ payaḥ prāptirbhavennahi \|\| 9 \|\|*

The mother said:—

9\. How can we the dwellers of the forest for ever have milk? Without the grace of Śiva we can never have milk.

## \|\| 7.32.10 \|\|

*pūrvajanmani yatkṛtyaṃ śivamu ddiśya he suta \|*  
*tadeva labhyate nūnannātra kāryā vicāraṇā \|*  
*iti mātṛvacaśśrunvā vyāghrapādissa bālakaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. O son, only that can be attained what we had dedicated to Śiva in our previous birth. We need not worry in this matter.

## \|\| 7.32.11 \|\|

*pratyuvāca viśokātmā mātaraṃ mātṛvatsalaḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. On hearing the words of his mother, the son of Vyāghrapāda excessively fond of his mother, freed of his grief, replied to her,

## \|\| 7.32.12 \|\|

*śokenālamimaṃ mātaḥ śaṃbhuryadyasti śaṅkaraḥ \|*  
*tyaja śokaṃ mahābhāge sarvaṃ bhadrambhaviṣyati \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. “O blessed mother, do not grieve. Cast off this sorrow. If Śiva is the benefactor, everything will result in welfare.

## \|\| 7.32.13 \|\|

*śṛṇu mātarvaco me'yamahādevo'sti cetkvacit \|*  
*cirādvā hyacirādvāpi kṣīrodaṃ sādhayāmyaham \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. O mother, listen to me. If there is lord Śiva anywhere, sooner or later I shall achieve, the ocean of milk”.

## \|\| 7.32.14 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityuktvā sa śiśuḥ prītyā śivaṃ me'stvityudīryya ca \|*  
*visṛjya tāṃ supraṇamya tapaḥ karttuṃ pracakrame \|\| 14 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

14\. After saying thus with pleasure and repeating “Let there be a welfare”, he bowed to his mother and left her. He began to perform penance.

## \|\| 7.32.15-16 \|\|

*himavatparvatagataḥ vāyubhakṣassamāhitaḥ \|*  
*aṣṭeṣṭakābhiḥ prāsādaṃ kṛtvā liṃgaṃ ca mṛnmayam \|\| 15 \|\|*

*tatrāvāhya śivaṃ sāmbaṃ bhaktyā pañcākṣareṇa ha \|*  
*patrapuṣpādibhirvanyaissamānarca śiśuḥ sa vai \|\| 16 \|\|*

15-16. It was on the mountain Himavat that he went to perform his penance with pure mind taking nothing in except air. He installed Śiva’s phallic image of clay after making a pedestal for it with eight bricks. He invoked Śiva along with Pārvatī into the phallic image with devotion. He repeated the five-syllabled mantra. The boy worshipped Śiva with leaves, flowers and other things.

## \|\| 7.32.17 \|\|

*dhyātvā śivaṃ ca taṃ sāmbaṃ japanpañcākṣarammanum \|*  
*samabhyarcya ciraṃ kālaṃ cacāra paramantapaḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. He meditated on Śiva along with Pārvatī repeating the five-syllabled mantra. Worshipping thus he performed great penance for a long time.

## \|\| 7.32.18 \|\|

*tapasā tasya bālasya hyupamanyormahātmanaḥ \|*  
*carācaraṃ ca bhuvanaṃ pradīpitamabhūnmune \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. O sage, due to the penance of the boy Upamanyu, the great soul, the whole world consisting of the mobile and immobile beings blazed as it were.

## \|\| 7.32.19 \|\|

*etasminnantare śaṃbhurviṣṇvādyaiḥ prārthitaḥ prabhuḥ \|*  
*parīkṣituṃ ca tadbhaktiṃ śakrarūpo'bhavattadā \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. In the meantime, requested by Viṣṇu aud others, Śiva assumed the form of Indra in order to test his devotion.

## \|\| 7.32.20 \|\|

*śivā śacīsvarūpābhūdgaṇāḥ sarve'bhavansurāḥ \|*  
*airāvatagajo nandī sarvameva ca tanmayam \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. Pārvatī took the form of Śacī. The Gaṇas became the gods, Nandin became the elephant Airāvata. Everything belonging to Śiva became that of Indra.

## \|\| 7.32.21 \|\|

*tataḥ sāmbaḥ śivaḥ śakrasvarūpassagaṇo drutam \|*  
*jagāmānugrahaṃ kartumupamanyostadāśramam \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. Then Śiva accompanied by Pārvatī and the Gaṇas, and assuming the form of Indra went to the hermitage of Upamanyu in order to bless him.

## \|\| 7.32.22 \|\|

*parīkṣituṃ ca tadbhaktiṃ śakrarūpadharo haraḥ \|*  
*prāha gaṃbhīrayā vācā bālakantaṃ munīśvara \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. O great sage, in order to test his devotion, Śiva in the form of Indra, spoke to the boy in a grave tone.

## \|\| 7.32.23 \|\|

*sureśvara uvāca \|*  
*tuṣṭo'smi te varaṃ brūhi tapasānena suvrata \|*  
*dadāmi cecchitānkāmānsarvānnātrāsti saṃśayaḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

Indra said:—

Note: The dialogue between Indra and Upamanyu is reminiscent of the talk between Pārvatī and the student ascetic. Cp. Kālidāsa’s Kumāra. Canto V.

23\. “O boy of good rites, I am pleased with you and your penance. Mention the boon you wish to have. I shall give you whatever you desire. There is no doubt about this.”

## \|\| 7.32.24 \|\|

*evamuktaḥ sa vai tena śakrarūpeṇa śambhunā \|*  
*varayāmi śive bhaktimityuvāca kṛtāñjali \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. Thus addressed by Śiva in the form of Indra, the boy replied with palms joined in reverence, “I choose devotion into Śiva.”

## \|\| 7.32.25 \|\|

*tanniśamya hariḥ prāha māṃ na jānāsi lekhapam \|*  
*trailokyādhipatiṃ śakraṃ sarvadevanamaskṛtam \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. On hearing it Indra said—“You do not know me the lord of the gods, the lord of the three worlds, Indra, bowed to by all the gods.

## \|\| 7.32.26 \|\|

*madbhakto bhava viprarṣe māmevārcaya sarvadā \|*  
*dadāmi sarvaṃ bhadrante tyaja rudraṃ ca nirguṇam \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. O brahmin sage, be my devotee. Worship me alone on every occasion. I shall grant you every benefit. Leave off Rudra devoid of attributes.

## \|\| 7.32.27 \|\|

*rudreṇa nirguṇenālaṃ kinte kāryaṃ bhaviṣyati \|*  
*devajātibahirbhūto yaḥ piśācatvamāgataḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Enough of Rudra devoid of attributes. What purpose of yours can be achieved by his worship? He is out of the caste of the gods. He has become a Piśāca.

## \|\| 7.32.28 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*tacchrutvā sa muneḥ putro japanpañcākṣarammanum \|*  
*manyamāno dharmavighnamprāha taṃ kartumāgatam \|\| 28 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

28\. On hearing these words, the son of the sage considered them an obstacle to his austerities. Repeating the five-syllabled mantra he told him who had come to create obstacles.

## \|\| 7.32.29 \|\|

*upamanyuruvāca \|*  
*tvayaivaṃ kathitaṃ sarvaṃ bhavanindā ratena vaiḥ \|*  
*prasaṃgāddevadevasya nirguṇatvaṃ piśācatā \|\| 29 \|\|*

Upamanyu said:—

29\. All this has been mentioned by you indulging in insult of Śiva. Incidentally you have mentioned that Śiva, the lord of the gods, is attributeless as well as Piśāca.

## \|\| 7.32.30 \|\|

*tvaṃ na jānāsi vai rudraṃ sarvadeveśvareśvaram \|*  
*brahmaviṣṇumaheśānāṃ janakamprakṛteḥ param \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. You do not know Śiva, lord of the chiefs of gods, the father and progenitor of Brahmā, Viṣṇu and Maheśa and greater than Prakṛti.

## \|\| 7.32.31 \|\|

*sadasadvyaktamavyaktaṃ yamāhurbrahmavādinaḥ \|*  
*nityamekamanekaṃ ca varaṃ tasmādvṛṇomyaham \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. I choose the boon from that deity, whom the philosophers call eternal, the single, the multitude, separate from the existent and non-existent and the unmanifest.

## \|\| 7.32.32 \|\|

*hetuvādavinirmuktaṃ sāṃkhyayogārthadamparam \|*  
*yamuśanti hi tattvajñā varantasmādvṛṇomyaham \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. I choose the boon from that deity whom the knowers of reality consider free from causations, disputes and the bestower of the principles of Sāṅkhya and Yoga.

## \|\| 7.32.33 \|\|

*nāsti śambhoḥ parantattvaṃ sarvakāraṇakāraṇāt \|*  
*brahmaviṣṇvādi devānāṃ śreṣṭhādgaṇaparādvibhoḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. There is no greater entity than lord Śiva who is beyond the attributes. He is the cause of all causes. He is the most excellent of Brahmā, Viṣṇu and other gods.

## \|\| 7.32.34 \|\|

*nāhaṃ vṛṇe varaṃ tvatto na viṣṇorbrahmaṇo'pi vā \|*  
*nānyasmādamarādvāpi śaṅkaro varado'stu me \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. I do not wish to take a boon from you or Brahma or Viṣṇu. Nor do I want any boon from any other god. Let Śiva be the bestower of boons on me.

## \|\| 7.32.35 \|\|

*bahunātra kimuktena vacmi tattvaṃ mataṃ svakam \|*  
*na prārthaye paśupateranyaṃ devādikaṃ sphuṭam \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. Of what avail is speaking much? I tell you the truth and my decision. I do not request any other god except Śiva.

## \|\| 7.32.36-37 \|\|

*madbhāvaṃ śṛṇu gotrāre mayādyānumitantvidam \|*  
*bhavāntare kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ śrutā nindā bhavasya cet \|\| 36 \|\|*

*śrutvā nindāmbhavasyātha tatkṣaṇādeva saṃtyajet \|*  
*svadehaṃ tannihatyāśu śivalokaṃ sa gacchati \|\| 37 \|\|*

36-37. O enemy of mountains, listen to my feeling in this matter. I infer that I have committed sins in the previous birth since I am forced to hear the insult to Śiva, on hearing which one shall shed his own mortal body at the very same moment. He goes to Śiva’s region thereby.

## \|\| 7.32.38 \|\|

*āstāṃ tāvanmameccheyaṃ kṣīramprati surādhama \|*  
*nihatya tvāṃ śivāstreṇa tyajāmyetatkalevaram \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. O base god, let it be that I had yearned for milk. I shall kill you by Śiva’s missile and then leave off my mortal frame.

## \|\| 7.32.39 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*evamuktvopamanyustaṃ martuṃ vyavasitaḥ svayam \|*  
*kṣīre vācchāmapi tyaktvā nihantuṃ śakramudyataḥ \|\| 39 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

39\. After saying thus, Upamanyu who was prepared to die, cast off his desire for milk and got ready to kill Indra.

## \|\| 7.32.40 \|\|

*bhasmādāya tadādhārādaghostrābhimantritam \|*  
*visṛjya śakramuddiśya nanāda sa munistadā \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. He took some ashes from the receptacle and invoked it with the mantra Aghorāstra. The sage, thereafter discharged it against Indra and roared.

## \|\| 7.32.41 \|\|

*smṛtvā sveṣṭapadadvandvaṃ svadehaṃ dagdhumudyataḥ \|*  
*āgneyīṃ dhāraṇāṃ bibhradupamanyuravasthitaḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. Remembering the feet of Śiva, his favourite deity, he got ready to burn himself. He stood there bearing the fiery pose.

## \|\| 7.32.42 \|\|

*evaṃ vyavasite vipre bhagavāñchakrarūpavān \|*  
*vārayāmāsa saumyena dhāraṇāntasya yoginaḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. When the brahmin was engaged thus, the lord in the form of Indra warded off the fiery pose of the Yogin by gentle means.

## \|\| 7.32.43 \|\|

*tadvisṛṣṭamaghorāstraṃ nandīśvaraniyo gataḥ \|*  
*jagṛhe manyataḥ kṣiptaṃ nandī śaṃkaravallabham \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. At the behest of Śiva, Nandin the favourite of Śiva delightedly caught hold of the Aghorāstra discharged by him, in the middle.

## \|\| 7.32.44 \|\|

*svarūpameva bhagavānāsthāya parameśvaraḥ \|*  
*darśayāmāsa viprāya bālendu kṛtaśekharam \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. Assuming his own form with the moon for his crest, lord Śiva revealed it to the brahmin.

## \|\| 7.32.45 \|\|

*kṣīrārṇavasahasrṃ ca dadhyādevararṇavantathā \|*  
*bhakṣyabhojyārṇavantasmai darśayāmāsa sa prabhuḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. The lord then showed him a thousand milk oceans, the ocean of curds and the ocean of different foodstuffs.

## \|\| 7.32.46 \|\|

*evaṃ sa dadṛśe śambhudevyā sārddhaṃ vṛṣopari \|*  
*gaṇeśvaraistriśūlādyairdivyāstrairapi saṃvṛtaḥ \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. Thus Śiva was seen seated on his bull accompained by the goddess. He was surrounded by the leading Gaṇas and the divine weapons, the trident and others.

## \|\| 7.32.47 \|\|

*divi dundubhayo nedu puṣpavṛṣṭiḥ papāta ha \|*  
*viṣṇubrahmendrapramukhairdevaiśchannā diśo daśa \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. The drums were sounded in the heaven. There was a shower of flowers. AH the ten quarters were covered by Brahmā, Viṣṇu and other gods.

## \|\| 7.32.48 \|\|

*athopamanyurānandasamudrormibhirāvṛtaḥ \|*  
*papāta daṇḍavadbhūmau bhaktinamreṇa cetasā \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. Then Upamanyu was swept by the waves of the ocean of delight and he prostrated on the ground like a staff, with his mind humble with devotion.

## \|\| 7.32.49 \|\|

*etasminsamaye tatra sasmito bhagavānbhavaḥ \|*  
*ehyehīti samāhūya mūrdhnyāghrāya dadau varān \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. In the meantime the smiling lord Śiva called him saying “Come on, Come on” and kissed him on the head. He granted him boons.

## \|\| 7.32.50 \|\|

*śiva uvāca \|*  
*vatsopamanyo tuṣṭo'smi tvadācaraṇato varāt \|*  
*dṛḍhabhakto'si viprarṣe mayā jijñāsito'dhunā \|\| 50 \|\|*

Śiva said:—

50\. “Dear Upamanyu, I am pleased with your excellent behaviour. O brahmin sage, you are very firm in your devotion. I just desired to know you thoroughly.

## \|\| 7.32.51 \|\|

*bhakṣyabhogānyathākāmaṃ bāndhavairbhuṃkṣva sarvadā \|*  
*sukhī bhava sadā duḥkhanirmukto bhaktimānmama \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. Enjoy the pleasure of eating sumptuously forever along with your kinsmen. Be ever happy and free from misery. Be devoted to me.

## \|\| 7.32.52 \|\|

*upamanyo mahābhāga tavāmbaiṣā hi pārvatī \|*  
*mayā putrīkṛto hyadya kumāratvaṃ sanātanam \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. O fortunate Upamanyu, you have been made eternally my son today. Pārvatī, here, is your mother.

## \|\| 7.32.53-54 \|\|

*dugdhadadhyājyamadhunāmarṇavāśca sahasraśaḥ \|*  
*bhakṣyabhojyādivastūnāmarṇavāścākhilā stathā \|\| 53 \|\|*

*tubhyaṃ dattā mayā prītyā tvaṃ gṛhṇīṣva mahāmune \|*  
*amaratvantathā dakṣa gāṇapatyaṃ ca śāśvatam \|\| 54 \|\|*

53-54. O great sage, thousands of oceans of milk, card, ghee and honey, and of different foodstuffs I grant you lovingly. Accept them. I confer on you the state of a god and the eternal chieftainship of Gaṇas.

## \|\| 7.32.55 \|\|

*pitāhante mahādevo mātā te jagadambikā \|*  
*varānvaraya suprītyā manobhilaṣitānparān \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. I, Śiva, am your father and she, Pārvatī, is your mother. Choose your boons whatever you desire.

## \|\| 7.32.56 \|\|

*ajaraścāmaraścaiva bhava tvaṃ duḥkhavarjita \|*  
*yaśasvī varatejasvī dittvajñānī mahāprabhuḥ \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. Be unaging and undying and free from misery. You will be famous, brilliant and endowed with divine knowledge. You will be a great lord.”

## \|\| 7.32.57-58 \|\|

*atha śambhuḥ prasannātmā smṛtvā tasya tapo mahat \|*  
*punardaśa varāndivyānmunaye hayūpamanyave \|\| 57 \|\|*

*vrataṃ pāśupataṃ jñānaṃ vratayogaṃ ca tattvataḥ \|*  
*dadau tasmai pravaktṛtvaṃ pāṭavaṃ ca nijaṃ padamṃ \|\| 58 \|\|*

57-58. Then considering his great penance, the delighted Śiva gave ten boons to the sage Upamanyu. He bestowed on him the knowledge of Śiva and his rites. He instructed him in the vows of Śiva. He gave him fluency of speech and efficiency. He bestowed his own region on him.

## \|\| 7.32.59 \|\|

*evandattvā mahādevaḥ karābhyāmupagṛhya tam \|*  
*mūrdhnyāghrāya sutaste'yamiti devyai nyavedayat \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. After granting him the boons, lord Śiva took him with both the hands, sniffed at his head and showed him to the goddess saying: “This is your son.”

## \|\| 7.32.60 \|\|

*devī ca śṛṇvatī prītyā mūrdhnideśe karāmbujam \|*  
*vinyasya pradadau tasmai kumārapadamakṣayam \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. Listening with pleasure and placing her hands on his head she granted him the everlasting status of a son.

## \|\| 7.32.61-62 \|\|

*kṣīrābdhimapi sākāraṃ kṣīrasvādukarodadhiḥ \|*  
*upāsthāya dadau tasmai piṇḍībhūtamanaśvaram \|\| 61 \|\|*

*yogaiśvaryyaṃ sadā tuṣṭambrahmavidyāmanaśvarām \|*  
*samṛddhiṃ paramāntasmai dadau santuṣṭamānasaḥ \|\| 62 \|\|*

61-62. She gave him undecaying, compressed ocean of milk, the prosperity and power of yoga, the undying knowledge of Brahman and the great assets.

## \|\| 7.32.63 \|\|

*so'pi labdhvā varāndivyānkumāratvaṃ ca sarvadā \|*  
*tasmācchivācca tasyāśca śivāyā mudito'bhavat \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. After receiving from Śiva and Śivā the excellent boons, and the eternal status of their son, he rejoiced much.

## \|\| 7.32.64 \|\|

*tataḥ prasannacetaskaḥ supraṇamya kṛtāñjaliḥ \|*  
*yayāce sa varaṃ prītyā devadevānmahe śvarāt \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. Then the delighted sage requested the lord of the gods after bowing to him with palms joined in reverence.

## \|\| 7.32.65 \|\|

*upamanyuruvāca \|*  
*prasīda devadeveśa prasīda parameśvara \|*  
*svabhaktindehi paramāṃ divyāmavyabhicāriṇīm \|\| 65 \|\|*

Upamanyu said:—

65\. O lord of gods, be pleased. O supreme lord, be pleased. Grant me the greatest devotion unto you, divine and unswerving.

## \|\| 7.32.66 \|\|

*śraddhāndehi mahādeva svasaṃbandhiṣu me sadā \|*  
*svadāsyaṃ paramaṃ snehaṃ svasānnidhyaṃ ca sarvadā \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. O great god, give roe great faith in persons devoted to you, the state of being your servant, your great affection and your constant presence.

## \|\| 7.32.67 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*evamuktvā prasannātmā harṣagadgadayā girā \|*  
*tuṣṭāva sa mahādevamupamanyurdvijottamaḥ \|\| 67 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

67\. After saying this, the delighted excellent brahmin Upamanyu eulogised great lord with words choked in delight.

## \|\| 7.32.68 \|\|

*evamuktaśśivastena sarveṣāṃ śṛṇvatāmprabhuḥ \|*  
*pratyuvāca prasannātmopamanyuṃ sakaleśvaraḥ \|\| 68 \|\|*

68\. Thus requested, Śiva, the lord of all, replied to Upamanyu, delightedly while everyone listened.

## \|\| 7.32.69 \|\|

*śiva uvāca \|*  
*vatsopamanyo dhanyastvaṃ mama bhakto viśeṣataḥ \|*  
*sarvandattammayā te hi yadvṛ kttambhavatānagha \|\| 69 \|\|*

Śiva said:—

69\. O dear Upamanyu, O sinless one, you are blessed. You are especially my devotee. Whatever has been chosen by you has been given to you.

## \|\| 7.32.70 \|\|

*ajaraścāmaraśca tvaṃ sarvadā duḥkhavarjita \|*  
*sarvapūjyo nirvikārī bhaktānāmpravaro bhava \|\| 70 \|\|*

70\. Be unaging and undying and free from misery. Be respectable to all, free from aberrations and the foremost among devotees.

## \|\| 7.32.71 \|\|

*akṣayā bāndhavāścaiva kulaṃ gotraṃ ca te sadā \|*  
*bhaviṣyati dvijaśreṣṭha mayi bhaktiśca śāśvatī \|\| 71 \|\|*

71\. O excellent brahmin, yon will have undwindling kinsmen, family and race. Your devotion to me will be permanent.

## \|\| 7.32.72 \|\|

*sānnidhyaṃ cāśraye nityaṃ kariṣyāmi mune tava \|*  
*tiṣṭha vatsa yathā kāmaṃ notkaṇṭhāṃ ca kariṣyasi \|\| 72 \|\|*

72\. O sage, I shall be ever present in your hermitage. Stay at ease. Do not be anxious.

## \|\| 7.32.73 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*evamuktvā sa bhagavāṃstasmai dattvā varānvarān \|*  
*sāṃbaśca sagaṇassadyastatraivāntardadhe prabhuḥ \|\| 73 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

73\. After saying this and granting him all excellent boons, the lord vanished from there, accompanied by Pārvatī and the Gaṇas.

## \|\| 7.32.74 \|\|

*upamanyuḥ prasannātmā prāpya śambhorvarānvarān \|*  
*jagāma jananīsthānaṃ mātre sarvama varṇayat \|\| 74 \|\|*

74\. After receiving excellent boons, the delighted Upamanyu went to the abode of his mother and described everything to her,

## \|\| 7.32.75 \|\|

*tacchrutvā tasya jananī mahāharṣamavāpa sā \|*  
*sarvapūjvo'bhavatso'pi sukhaṃ prāpādhikaṃ sadā \|\| 75 \|\|*

75\. On hearing it, his mother was highly delighted. He became worthy of honour and respect of all. He attained great happiness.

## \|\| 7.32.76 \|\|

*itthante varṇitastāta śivasya paramātmanaḥ \|*  
*sureśvarāvatāro hi sarvadā sukhadaḥ satām \|\| 76 \|\|*

76\. O dear, thus, the incarnation of Śiva the great soul, as Indra has been explained to you. It bestows happiness to the good always.

## \|\| 7.32.77 \|\|

*idamākhyānamanaghaṃ sarvakāmaphalapradam \|*  
*svargyaṃ yaśasyamāyuṣyaṃ bhuktimuktipradaṃ satām \|\| 77 \|\|*

77\. This story is pious and it yields the fruits of all desires. It is conducive to heaven, fame and longevity. It yields worldly pleasures and salvation to the good.

## \|\| 7.32.78 \|\|

*ya etacchṛṇuyādbhaktyā śrāvayedvā samāhitaḥ \|*  
*iha sarvasukhaṃ bhuktvā so'nte śivagatiṃ labhet \|\| 78 \|\|*

78\. He who listens to this with devotion and narrates this with purity of mind enjoys all pleasures here and attains Śiva’s goal hereafter.

## Chapter 33 - Śiva’s incarnation as the student-ascetic (Jaṭila)

## \|\| 7.33.1 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*sanatkumāra suprītyā śivasya paramātmanaḥ \|*  
*avatāraṃ śṛṇu vibhorjaṭilāhvaṃ supāvanam \|\| 1 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

1\. O Sanatkumāra, listen to the incarnation of Śiva the great soul. It is named Jaṭila and is very sanctifying.

## \|\| 7.33.2 \|\|

*purā satī dakṣakanyā tyaktvā dehaṃ piturmakhe \|*  
*svapitrā'nādṛtā jajñe menāyāṃ himabhūdharāt \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. After abandoning her body when she was insulted by her own father in the sacrificial hall, Satī, the daughter of Dakṣa, was born of Menā and the mountain Himavat.

## \|\| 7.33.3 \|\|

*sā gatvā gahane'raṇye tepe suvi malaṃ tapaḥ \|*  
*śaṃkarampatimicchantī sakhībhyāṃ saṃyutā śivā \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Desiring to attain Śiva as her husband, she went to the dense forest and performed a pure penance. She was attended upon by two of her friends.

## \|\| 7.33.4 \|\|

*tattapaḥsuparīkṣārthaṃ saptarṣīnpraiṣayacchivaḥ \|*  
*tapaḥsthānaṃ tu pārvatyā nānālīlāviśāradaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. In order to test her penance, Śiva an expert in various kinds of sports sent the seven sages to the penance grove of Pārvatī.

## \|\| 7.33.5 \|\|

*te gatvā tatra munayaḥ parīkṣāṃ cakrurādarāt \|*  
*tasyāḥ suyatnato naiva samarthā hyabhavaṃśca te \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. The sages went there and thoroughly tested her. As she was strenuous in her effort they could not dissuade her from her penance.

## \|\| 7.33.6 \|\|

*tatrāgatya śivaṃ natvā vṛttāntaṃ ca nivedya tat \|*  
*tadājñāṃ samanuprāpya svarlokaṃ jagmurādarāt \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. They returned and bowed to Śiva. After mentioning all the details to him and obtaining his permission they returned to heaven eagerly.

## \|\| 7.33.7 \|\|

*gateṣu muniṣu svasthānaṃ śaṃkaraḥ svayam \|*  
*parīkṣituṃ śivāvṛttamaicchatsūtikaraḥ prabhuḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. When they had gone to their abodes, lord Śiva, the cause of excellent sports, desired to test Śiva’s activity himself.

## \|\| 7.33.8 \|\|

*suprasannastapasvīcchāśamanādayamīśvaraḥ \|*  
*brahmacaryyasvarūpo'bhūttadādbhutataraḥ prabhuḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Then the lord assumed the pleasant, self-controlled and wonderful form of a student-ascetic.

## \|\| 7.33.9 \|\|

*atīva sthaviro vipradehadhārī svatejasā \|*  
*prajvalanmanasā hṛṣṭo daṇḍī chatrī mahojjalaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. He looked very old and had assumed the body of a brahmin. He was shining with brilliance. He had a staff and an umbrella. He was mentally delighted.

## \|\| 7.33.10 \|\|

*dhṛtvaivaṃ jaṭilaṃ rūpaṃ jagāma girijāvanam \|*  
*atiprītiyutaḥ śambhuśśaṅkaro bhaktavatsalaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Assuming this form of a Jaṭila (one with matted hair), the delighted Śiva, favourably disposed to his devotees went to the penance-grove of Pārvatī.

## \|\| 7.33.11 \|\|

*tatrāpaśyastthitāndevīṃ sakhībhiḥ parivāritām \|*  
*vedikopari śuddhāntāṃ śivāmiva vidhoḥ kalām \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. There he saw the goddess Pārvatī, seated on the dais surrounded by her friends. She appeared like the pure digit of the moon.

## \|\| 7.33.12 \|\|

*śaṃbhurnirīkṣya tāndevīṃ brahmacārisvarūpavān \|*  
*upakaṇṭhaṃ yayau prītyā cotsukī bhaktavatsalaḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. On seeing the goddess, Śiva who is favourably disposed to his devotees and who had assumed the form of a student-ascetic approached her eagerly and lovingly.

## \|\| 7.33.13-14 \|\|

*āgataṃ sā tadā dṛṣṭvā brāhmaṇaṃ tejasādbhutam \|*  
*aṃgeṣu lomaśaṃ śāntaṃ daṇḍacarmasamanvitam \|\| 13 \|\|*

*brahmacaryyadharaṃ vṛddhaṃ jaṭilaṃ sakamaṇḍalum \|*  
*apūjayatparaprītyā sarvapūjopahārakaiḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

13-14. On seeing the brahmin visitor of wonderful splendour, quiet with hairy limbs, bearing the staff and hide of deer, the aged bachelor with matted hair and the water-pot, she welcomed and worshipped him with all the articles of worship.

## \|\| 7.33.15 \|\|

*tatastā pārvatīdevī pūjitaṃ parayā mudā \|*  
*kuśalaṃ paryapṛcchattaṃ brahmacāriṇamādarāt \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. The goddess Pārvatī enquired after the health of that aged bachelor respectfully after worshipping him with great joy.

## \|\| 7.33.16 \|\|

*brahmacārisvarūpeṇa kastvaṃ hi kuta āgataḥ \|*  
*idṃ vanaṃ bhāsayasi vada vedavidāṃ vara \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. “O most excellent one among the knowers of the Vedas, who are you, please, in the guise of a student-ascetic and whence have you come illuminating this forest?

## \|\| 7.33.17 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*iti pṛṣṭastu pārvatyā brahmacārī sa vai dvijaḥ \|*  
*pratyuvāca drutamprītyā śivābhāvaparīkṣayā \|\| 17 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

17\. On being asked thus by Pārvatī, that brahmin replied immediately with great pleasure in order to test the feelings of Śivā.

## \|\| 7.33.18 \|\|

*brahmacāryyuvāca \|*  
*ahamicchābhigāmī ca brahmacārī dvijosmi vai \|*  
*tapasvī sukhado'nyeṣāmupakārī na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

The student-ascetic said:

18\. I am a brahmin bachelor going about here and there as I please. I am a saint pleasing and helping others undoubtedly.

## \|\| 7.33.19 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityuktvā brahmacārī sa śaṃkaro bhaktavatsalaḥ \|\| \|*  
*tasthivānupakaṇṭhaṃ sa gopāyanrūpamātmanaḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

19\. After saying this, the bachelor Śiva, who is favourably disposed to his devotees, stood near her concealing his real form.

## \|\| 7.33.20 \|\|

*brahmacāryyuvāca \|*  
*kimbravīmi mahādevi kathanīyanna vidyate \|*  
*mahānarthakaraṃ vṛttaṃ dṛśyate vikṛtaṃ mahat \|\| 20 \|\|*

The student-ascetic said:

20\. What shall I say, O great lady? There is nothing to be mentioned. A great calamity seems to have happened, extremely confusing.

## \|\| 7.33.21 \|\|

*nave vayasi sadbhogasādhane sukhakāraṇe \|*  
*mahopacārasadbhogairvṛthaiva tvaṃ tapasyasi \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. In your fresh youth, the means of good enjoyment and the cause of happiness through all requisite articles of pleasure, you are performing this penance in vain.

## \|\| 7.33.22 \|\|

*kā tvaṃ kasyāsi tanayā kimarthaṃ vijane vane \|*  
*tapaścarasi durdharṣaṃ munibhiḥ prayatātmabhiḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. Who are you? Whose daughter are you? Why are you performing a penance in this desolate forest? Your penance is such as cannot be outdone even by sages of elevated souls.

## \|\| 7.33.23 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvā prahasya parameśvarī \|*  
*uvāca vacanaṃ prītyā brahmacāriṇamuttamam \|\| 23 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

23\. On hearing his words, Pārvatī laughed and spoke to the excellent student ascetic with reverence.

## \|\| 7.33.24 \|\|

*pārvatyuvāca \|*  
*śṛṇu vipra brahmacārinmadavṛttamakhilaṃ mune \|*  
*janma me bhārate varṣe sāmprataṃ himavadgṛhe \|\| 24 \|\|*

Pārvatī said:—

24\. O sage, O brahmin bachelor, listen to the details of my wife. I am born in Bhārata in the house of Himavat.

## \|\| 7.33.25 \|\|

*pūrvaṃ dakṣagṛhe janma satī śaṅkarakāminī \|*  
*yogena tyaktadehāhaṃ tātena patinindinā \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Formerly my birth was in the house of Dakṣa. I was then Satī, the wife of Śiva. On account of my father’s insulting my husband I cast off my body by means of Yoga.

## \|\| 7.33.26 \|\|

*atra janmani saṃprāpya supuṇyena śivo dvija \|*  
*māṃ tyaktvā bhasmasātkṛtvā manmathaṃ sa jagāma ha \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. O brahmin, in this birth too, Śiva came my way due to merit but he reduced Kāma to ashes, abandoned me and went away.

## \|\| 7.33.27 \|\|

*prayāte śaṅkare tāpādvrīḍitāhaṃ piturgṛhāt \|*  
*āgacchamatra tapase guruvākyena saṃyatā \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. When Śiva went away I was overwhelmed with grief. A bit ashamed, I came away from my house to this place for performing penance after being initiated by the statement of my preceptor.

## \|\| 7.33.28 \|\|

*manasā vacasā sākṣātkarmaṇā patibhāvataḥ \|*  
*satyambravīmi no'satyaṃ saṃvṛtaḥ śaṅkaro mayā \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. Mentally, verbally and through activities too, Śiva has been chosen by me as my husband. I am telling you the truth and not falsehood.

## \|\| 7.33.29 \|\|

*jānāmi durlabhaṃ vastu kathamprāpyaṃ mayā bhavet \|*  
*tathāpi manasautsukyāttapyate me tapo'dhunā \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. I know that this is an extremely inaccessible object. How can I achieve it? Still, thanks to the great zeal, this penance is being pursued by me now.

## \|\| 7.33.30 \|\|

*hitvendrapramukhāndevānviṣṇumbrahmāṇamapyaham \|*  
*patimpinākapāṇiṃ vai prāptumicchāmi satyataḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. Leaving off Indra and other gods, Viṣṇu and Brahmā I wish to obtain Śiva as my husband really.

## \|\| 7.33.31 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityevaṃ vacanaṃ śrutvā pārvatyā hi suniścitam \|*  
*mune sa jaṭilo rudro vihasanvākyamabravīt \|\| 31 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

31\. O sage, on hearing these firm words of Pārvatī that Jaṭila Rudra laughingly spoke again.

## \|\| 7.33.32 \|\|

*jaṭila uvāca \|*  
*himācalasute devi kā buddhiḥ svīkṛtā tvayā \|*  
*rudrārthaṃ vibudhānhitvā karoṣi vipulantapaḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

Jaṭila said:—

32\. O gentle lady, O daughter of Himavat, what is this idea that has struck you? You are performing this great penance for obtaining Śiva after leaving off all the gods.

## \|\| 7.33.33 \|\|

*jānāmyahaṃ ca taṃ rudraṃ śṛṇu tvampravadāmi te \|*  
*vṛṣadhvajassa rudro hi vikṛtātmā jaṭādharaḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. I know that Śiva. Listen. I shall tell you that bull-bannered Śiva wears matted hair and is ill-mannered.

## \|\| 7.33.34 \|\|

*ekākī ca sadā nityaṃ virāgī ca viśeṣataḥ \|*  
*tasmāttvaṃ tena rudreṇa mano yoktuṃ na cārhasi \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. He is an isolationist for ever. Particularly he is a detached fellow. Hence it does not behove you to get mentally entangled with him.

## \|\| 7.33.35 \|\|

*sarvaṃ viruddhaṃ rūpādi tava devi harasya ca \|*  
*mahyaṃ na rocate hyetadyadīcchasi tathā kuru \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. O gentle lady, your features etc. and those of Śiva are ill-matched. I do not like this. But do as you please.

## \|\| 7.33.36 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityuktvā ca punā rudro brahmacārisvarūpavān \|*  
*nininda bahudhātmānaṃ tadagre tāṃ parīkṣitum \|\| 36 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

36\. After saying this, Śiva in the guise of a student-ascetic rebuked himself in her presence in order to test her.

## \|\| 7.33.37 \|\|

*tacchrutvā pārvatī devī vipravākyaṃ durāsadam \|*  
*pratyuvāca mahākruddhā śivanindāparaṃ ca tam \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. On hearing his intolerable words, Pārvatī replied extremely infuriated at him who was engrossed in insulting Śiva.

## \|\| 7.33.38 \|\|

*etāvaddhi mayā jñātaṃ kaściddhanyo bhaviṣyati \|*  
*parantu sakalaṃ jñātamavadhyo dṛśyate'dhunā \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Till now I thought that you would be some one fit to be killed. But now it is quite clear that you are not to be killed.

## \|\| 7.33.39 \|\|

*brahmacārisvarūpeṇa kaścittvaṃ dhūrta āgataḥ \|*  
*śivanindā kṛtā mūḍha tvayā manyurabhūnmama \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. You are some foolish knave in the guise of a student-ascetic. O fool, Śiva has been insulted by you. Hence, I am infuriated.

## \|\| 7.33.40 \|\|

*śivaṃ tvaṃ ca na jānāsi śivāttvaṃ hi bahirmukhaḥ \|*  
*tvatpūjā ca kṛtā yanme tasmāttāpayutā'bhavam \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. You do not know Śiva. You arc averse to Śiva. Since I had already welcomed and honoured you I am now distressed.

## \|\| 7.33.41 \|\|

*śivanindāṃ karotīha tattvamajñāya yaḥ pumān \|*  
*ājanmasaṃcitaṃ puṇyaṃ tasya bhasmībhavatyuta \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. The accumulated merits of the person who insults Śiva without knowing reality are reduced to ashes.

## \|\| 7.33.42 \|\|

*śivavidveṣiṇaṃ spṛṣṭvā prāyaścittaṃ samācaret \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. One should perform expiation after touching a Śiva-hater.

## \|\| 7.33.43 \|\|

*re re duṣṭa tvayā proktamahaṃ jānāmi śaṃkaram \|*  
*niścayena na vijñātaḥ śiva eva paraḥ prabhuḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. O wicked fellow, you said I know Śiva. Certainly he is not known to you. Śiva alone is the great lord.

## \|\| 7.33.44 \|\|

*yathā tathā bhavedrudro māyayā bahurūpavān \|*  
*mamābhīṣṭaprado'tyantaṃ nirvikāraḥ satāmpriyaḥ \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. Assuming many guises through his Māyā, Rudra may appear in any form. But the beloved of the good, having no aberrations, lavishly bestows all desired things on me.

## \|\| 7.33.45 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityuktvā taṃ śivā devī śivatattvaṃ jagāda sā \|*  
*yatra brahmatayā rudraḥ kathyate nirguṇo'vyayaḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

45\. After saying this, Pārvatī expatiated on Śiva’s principle, where Śiva was explained as attributeless and unchanging performing the penance of Brahman.

## \|\| 7.33.46-47 \|\|

*tadākarṇya vaco devyā brahmacārī sa vai dvijaḥ \|*  
*punarvacanamādātuṃ yāvadeva pracakrame \|\| 46 \|\|*

*provāca girijā tāvatsvasakhīṃ vijayāndrutam \|*  
*śivāsaktamanovṛttiḥ śivanindāparāṅmukhī \|\| 47 \|\|*

46-47. On hearing her words, the brahmin began to say something; but by that time, Pārvatī whose mind was attached to Śiva and who was averse to Śiva being insulted, immediately spoke to her friend Vijayā.

## \|\| 7.33.48 \|\|

*girijovāca \|*  
*vāraṇīyaḥ prayatnena sakhyayaṃ hi dvijādhamaḥ \|*  
*punarvaktumanāścāyaṃ śivanindāṃ kariṣyati \|\| 48 \|\|*

Pārvatī said:—

48\. Friend, this base brahmin must be prevented. He is going to speak again. He is sure to insult Śiva.

## \|\| 7.33.49 \|\|

*na kevalaṃ bhavetpāpaṃ nindākartuḥ śivasya hi \|*  
*yo vai śṛṇoti tannindāṃ pāpabhāksa bhavediha \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. It is not only the censurer of Śiva who becomes sinful but also he who listens to such insults.

## \|\| 7.33.50 \|\|

*śivanindākaro vadhyassarvathā śivakiṃkaraiḥ \|*  
*brāhmaṇaścetsa vai tyājyo gantavyaṃ tatsthalāddrutam \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. A rebuker of Śiva shall be slain by all means by Śiva’s attendants. If he happens to be a brahmin he shall be abandoned. He shall go away from that place immediately.

## \|\| 7.33.51 \|\|

*ayanduṣṭaḥ punarniṃdāṃ kariṣyati śivasya hi \|*  
*brāhmaṇatvādavadhyaśca tyājyo'dṛśyaśca sarvathā \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. This wicked fellow will insult Śiva again. Being a brahmin he cannot be killed. He shall be abandoned. He should not even be looked at.

## \|\| 7.33.52 \|\|

*sthalametaddrutaṃ hitvā yāsyāmo'nyatra mā ciram \|*  
*yathā saṃbhāṣaṇaṃ na syādanenāviduṣā punaḥ \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. Let us leave this place and go elsewhere. Do not delay, lest there should be any further talk with this ignorant man.

## \|\| 7.33.53 \|\|

*\|\| nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityuktvā comayā yāvatpadamutkṣipyate mune \|*  
*asau tāvacchivaḥ sākṣādālalambe paṭaṃ svayam \|\| 53 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

53\. O sage, after saying this Pārvatī was just to walk away when Śiva revealed himself and caught hold of her cloth.

## \|\| 7.33.54 \|\|

*kṛtvā svarūpaṃ divyaṃ ca śivādhyānaṃ yathā tathā \|*  
*darśayitvā śivāyai tāmuvācāvāṅmukhī śivaḥ \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. After assuming that divine form which Pārvatī was meditating upon, Śiva spoke to her as she stood face downwards.

## \|\| 7.33.55 \|\|

*śiva uvāca \|*  
*kutra tvaṃ yāsi māṃ hitvā na tvantyājyā mayā śive \|*  
*mayā parīkṣitāsi tvaṃ dṛḍhabhaktāsi me'naghe \|\| 55 \|\|*

Śiva said:—

55\. “O Pārvatī, where are you going, leaving me? You cannot be forsaken by me. You have been tested by me O faultless one, you are really steady in your devotion to me.

## \|\| 7.33.56 \|\|

*brahmacārisvarūpeṇa bhāvamicchustvadīyakam \|*  
*tavopakaṇṭhamāgatya prāvocaṃ vividhaṃ vacaḥ \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. Desirous of knowing your feelings I have approached you in the guise of a student-asectic and spoke these diverse words.

## \|\| 7.33.57 \|\|

*prasannosmi dṛḍhaṃ bhaktyā śive tava viśeṣataḥ \|*  
*cittepsitaṃ varaṃ brūhi nādeyaṃ vidyate tava \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. O Pārvatī, I am delighted at your steady devotion. Mention the boon that you have cherished in your mind. There is nothing that cannot be given to you.

## \|\| 7.33.58 \|\|

*adyaprabhṛti te dāsastapobhiḥ premanirbhare \|*  
*kṛto'smi tava saundaryyātkṣaṇa eko yugāyate \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. O loving lady, I have been made your slave from now onwards by means of your penance. Due to your beauty, even a moment seems to expand into a Yoga.

## \|\| 7.33.59 \|\|

*tyajyatāṃ ca tvayā lajjā mama patnī sanātanī \|*  
*ehi priye tvayā sākaṃ drutaṃ yāmi svakaṃ girim \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. Leave off your bashfulness. You are my eternal wife. O beloved, come on, I shall go to my hilly abode along with you.”

## \|\| 7.33.60 \|\|

*ityuktavati deveśe śivāti mudamāpa sā \|*  
*tapoduḥkhantu yatsarvaṃ tajjahau drutameva hi \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. When the lord of gods said thus, Pārvatī rejoiced much. Whatever misery she experienced in performing the penance, she cast off immediately.

## \|\| 7.33.61 \|\|

*tataḥ prahṛṣṭā sā dṛṣṭvā divyarūpaṃ śivasya tat \|*  
*pratyuvāca prabhuṃ prītyā lajjayādho mukhī śivā \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. On seeing that divine form of Śiva, Pārvatī replied to the lord with face lowered due to bashfulness.

## \|\| 7.33.62-63 \|\|

*śivovāca \|*  
*yadi prasanno deveśa karoṣi ca kṛpāṃ mayi \|*  
*patirme bhava deveśa ityuktaśśivayā śivaḥ \|\| 62 \|\|*

*gṛhītvā vidhivatpāṇiṃ kailāsaṃ sa tayā yayau \|*  
*patiṃ taṃ girijā prāpya devakāryaṃ cakāra sā \|\| 63 \|\|*

Pārvatī said:—

62-63. O lord of gods, if you are delighted, if you have pity on me, be my lord and husband.”

## \|\| 7.33.64 \|\|

*iti proktastu te tāta brahmacāri svarūpakaḥ \|*  
*śivāvatāro hi mayā śivābhāvaparīkṣakaḥ \|\| 64 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

Thus requested by Pārvatī Śiva took her hand duly and went to Kailāsa along with her. On getting him as her lord, she carried out the task of the gods.

64\. O dear, thus the incarnation of Śiva in the form of a student-ascctic wherein he tested the feelings of Pārvatī has been narrated to you.

## \|\| 7.33.65 \|\|

*idamākhyānamanaghaṃ paramaṃ vyāhṛtaṃ mayā \|*  
*ya etacchṛṇuyātprītyā sukhī gatimāpnuyāt \|\| 65 \|\|*

65\. This narrative explained by me is faultless and magnificent. He who listens to this with pleasure becomes happy and attains salvation.

## Chapter 34 - The incarnation of Śiva as Sunartaka naṭa

## \|\| 7.34.1 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*sanatkumāra sarvajña śivasya paramātmanaḥ \|*  
*avatāraṃ śṛṇu vibhossunartakanaṭāhvayam \|\| 1 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

1\. O omniscient Sanatkumāra, listen to the incarnation of lord Śiva, the great soul, as Sunartaka naṭa.

## \|\| 7.34.2-3 \|\|

*yadā hi kālikā devī pārvatī himavatsutā \|*  
*tepe tapastuvimalaṃ vanaṃ gatvā śivāptaye \|\| 2 \|\|*

*tadā śivaḥ prasanno bhūttasyāssutapaso mune \|*  
*tadvṛttasuparīkṣārthaṃ varaṃ dātummudā yayau \|\| 3 \|\|*

2-3. O sage, when goddess Pārvatī, daughter of Himavat, performed the pious penance in order to attain Śiva, after going to the forest, Śiva was much delighted at her austerity. He approached to test her conduct and out of pleasure to grant her the desired boon.

## \|\| 7.34.4 \|\|

*svarūpandarśayāmāsa tasyai suprītamānasaḥ \|*  
*varambrūhīti covāca tāṃ śivāṃ śaṃkaro mune \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. O sage, the delighted Śiva revealed his real form to her and told Pārvatī, “Mention the boon you wish to have.”

## \|\| 7.34.5 \|\|

*tacchrutvā śambhuvacanaṃ dṛṣṭvā tadrūpamuttamam \|*  
*sujaharṣa śivātīva prāha taṃ supraṇamya sā \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. On hearing the words of Śiva and on seeing his excellent form, Pārvatī was delighted. She spoke to him after paying her homage.

## \|\| 7.34.6 \|\|

*pārvatyuvāca \|*  
*yadi prasanno deveśa mahyaṃ deyo varo yadi \|*  
*patirbhava mameśāna kṛpāṃ kuru mamopari \|\| 6 \|\|*

Pārvatī said:—

6\. O lord of gods, if you are delighted, if a boon shall be granted to me, O Śiva, you be my lord and husband. Please take pity on me.

## \|\| 7.34.7 \|\|

*piturgṛhe mayā samyaggamyate tvadanujñayā \|*  
*gantavyambhavatā nātha matpituḥ pārśvataḥ prabho \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. With your permission I shall go to my father’s abode. O lord, you shall approach my father.

## \|\| 7.34.8 \|\|

*yācasva māntato bhikṣuḥ khyāpayaṃśca yaśaḥ śubham \|*  
*piturme saphalaṃ sarvaṃ kuru prītyā gṛhā śramam \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. In the form of a mendicant and making your spotless fame known, beg me of my father and make the householder’s life fruitful.

## \|\| 7.34.9 \|\|

*tato yathoktavidhinā kartumarhasi bho prabho \|*  
*vivāhaṃ tvaṃ maheśāna devānāṃ kāryyasiddhaye \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. O lord Śiva, it behoves to marry me with due rites for the fulfilment of the task of the gods.

## \|\| 7.34.10 \|\|

*kāmaṃ me pūraya vibho nirvikāro bhavānsadā \|*  
*bhaktavatsalanāmā hi tava bhaktāsmyamahaṃ sadā \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. O lord, you are without aberrations. Please fulfil my desires. You are favourably disposed to the devotees. I am always your devotee.

## \|\| 7.34.11 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityuktassa tayā śaṃbhurmaheśo bhaktavatsalaḥ \|*  
*tathāstviti vacaḥ procyāntarhitassvagiriṃ yayau \|\| 11 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

11\. Thus requested by her, Śiva who is favourably disposed towards his devotees said “So be it” and vanished from there. He returned to his mountainous abode.

## \|\| 7.34.12 \|\|

*pārvatyapi tataḥ prītyā svasakhībhyāṃ vayonvitā \|*  
*jagāma svapiturgehaṃ rūpaṃ kṛtvā tu sārthakam \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. After making her beauty fruitful, Pārvatī returned to her father’s abode jealously in the company of her female friends.

## \|\| 7.34.13 \|\|

*pārvatyāgamanaṃ śrutvā menayā sa himācalaḥ \|*  
*parivārayuto draṣṭuṃ svasutāṃ tāṃ yayau mudā \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. On hearing Pārvatī’s return, Himavat accompanied by his attendants and his wife Menā joyously came to see his daughter.

## \|\| 7.34.14 \|\|

*dṛṣṭvā tāṃ suprasannāsyāmānayāmāsaturgṛham \|*  
*kārayāmāsatuḥ prītyā mahānandī mahotsavam \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. On seeing her delighted, both of them received her suitably within the house and glad at heart celebrated the event with pleasure.

## \|\| 7.34.15 \|\|

*dhanandadau dvijādibhyo menāgirivarastathā \|*  
*maṃgalaṃ kārayāmāsa savedadhvanimādarāt \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. Menā and the lord of mountains made gifts to brahmins and others\* The auspicious mantras of the Vedas were chanted.

## \|\| 7.34.16 \|\|

*tataḥ svakanyayā sārddhamuvāsa prāṃgaṇe mudā \|*  
*menā ca himavāñchailaḥ snātuṃ gaṃgāṃ jagāma saḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Then Menā joyously stayed for a while in the yard with her daughter and Himavat went to the Ganges for a bath.

## \|\| 7.34.17 \|\|

*etasminnantare śambhuḥ sulīlo bhaktavatsalaḥ \|*  
*sunartakanaṭo bhūtvā menakāsannidhiṃ yayau \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. In the meantime Śiva, favourably disposed towards his devotees and indulging in good sports, assumed the form of a dancer and approached Menakā.

## \|\| 7.34.18 \|\|

*śṛṃgaṃ vāme kare dhṛtvā dakṣiṇe ḍamaruntathā \|*  
*pṛṣṭhe kanthāṃ raktavāsā nṛtyagānaviśāradaḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. He held the horn in his left hand and the small drum in his right. He carried a bundle of patched garments on his back. He wore a red cloth. He appeared to be an expert in dance and music.

## \|\| 7.34.19 \|\|

*tatastu naṭarūpo'sau menakāprāṃgaṇe mudā \|*  
*cakre sa nṛtyaṃ vividhaṃ gānañcāti manoharam \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. Then the lord in the form of a dancer performed various dances joyously in the yard of Menakā and sang sweetly.

## \|\| 7.34.20 \|\|

*śṛṃgañca ḍamaruntatra vādayāmāsa sudhvanim \|*  
*mahotiṃ vividhāmprītyā sa cakāra manoharām \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. He blew on the horn and played on Ḍamaru producing sweet notes. With great pleasure he exhibited a variety of splendid sports.

## \|\| 7.34.21 \|\|

*tandraṣṭuṃ nāgarāssarve puruṣāśca striyastathā \|*  
*ājagmussahasā tatra bālā vṛddhā api dhruvam \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. The citizens including men, women, children and old women gathered there suddenly to see him.

## \|\| 7.34.22 \|\|

*śrutvā saṃgītaṃ tandṛṣṭvā sunṛtyaṃ ca manoharam \|*  
*sahasā murmuhuḥ sarve menāpi ca tadā mune \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. O sage, hearing the sweet song and witnessing the splendid dance, the people were fascinated and Menā too was highly delighted.

## \|\| 7.34.23 \|\|
