*tato menāśu ratnāni svarṇapātrasthitāni ca \|*  
*tasmai dātuṃ yayau prītyā tadūtiprī tamānasā \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Immediately Menā whose mind was in exultation, thanks to his clever feats, went to give him potfuls of gems and jewels.

## \|\| 7.34.24 \|\|

*tāni na svīcakārāsau bhikṣāṃ cete śivāṃ ca tām \|*  
*punastu nṛtyaṃ gānaṃ ca kautukātkartumudyataḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. He did not accept those gems. He wished to have Pārvatī for his alms. Eagerly he began to dance and sing again.

## \|\| 7.34.25 \|\|

*menā tadvacanaṃ śrutvā cukopāti suvismitā \|*  
*bhikṣukambhartsayāmāsa bahiṣkartumiyeṣa sā \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. On hearing bis words Menā was surprised. She became furious as well. She rebuked the mendicant and wanted to drive him out.

## \|\| 7.34.26 \|\|

*etasminnantare tatra gaṃgāto girirāḍyayau \|*  
*dadarśa purato bhikṣuṃ prāṃgaṇasthaṃ narākṛtim \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. In the meantime the lord of the mountains returned from the Ganges. He saw the mendicant in the form of a man standing before him in the yard.

## \|\| 7.34.27 \|\|

*śrutvā menāmukhādvṛttantatsarvaṃ sucukopa saḥ \|*  
*ājñāṃ cakārānucarānbahiḥ kartuṃ ca bhikṣukam \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. On hearing from Menā all the details he too was infuriated. He ordered his attendants to drive out the mendicant.

## \|\| 7.34.28 \|\|

*mahāgnimiva duḥsparśaṃ prajvalantaṃ sutejasam \|*  
*na śaśāka bahiḥ kartuṃ ko'pi taṃ munisattama \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. O excellent sage, no one could drive him out. He was blazing and brilliant like a big fire and could not be touched.

## \|\| 7.34.29 \|\|

*tataḥ sa bhikṣukastāta nānālīlāviśāradaḥ \|*  
*darśayāmāsa śailāya svaprabhāvamanantakam \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. O dear, then that mendicant, an expert in different sports, exhibited his infinite power to the mountain.

## \|\| 7.34.30 \|\|

*śailo dadarśa tantatra viṣṇurūpadharandrutam \|*  
*tato brahmasvarūpaṃ ca sūryyarūpaṃ tataḥ kṣaṇāt \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. Immediately the mountain saw him assuming the form of Viṣṇu, then the form of Brahmā and in a trice that of the sun.

## \|\| 7.34.31 \|\|

*tato dadarśa taṃ tāta rudrarūpaṃ mahādbhutam \|*  
*pārvatī sahitaṃ ramyaṃ vihasantaṃ sutejasam \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. O dear, then he saw him in the excessively wonderful form of Śiva accompanied by Pārvatī. He was exquisitely handsome and brilliant. He was laughing.

## \|\| 7.34.32 \|\|

*evaṃ subahurūpāṇi tasya tatra dadarśa saḥ \|*  
*suvismito babhūvāśu paramānandasaṃplutaḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. Thus he saw him in various forms. He was much surprised and extremely delighted.

## \|\| 7.34.33 \|\|

*athāsau bhikṣuvaryo hi tasmāttasyāśca sūtikṛt \|*  
*bhikṣāṃ yayāce durgāntānnānyajjagrāha kiñcana \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. Then the leading mendicant of good sports begged for Durgā as his alms, of her (Menā) and of him (the mountain). He did not accept anything else.

## \|\| 7.34.34 \|\|

*tataścāntardadhe bhikṣusvarūpaḥ parameśvaraḥ \|*  
*svālayaṃ sa jagāmāśu durgāvākyapraṇoditaḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. Then lord Śiva in the form of the mendicant, urged by Durgā’s words vanished and went away to his abode suddenly.

## \|\| 7.34.35 \|\|

*tadā babhūva sujñānaṃ menāśaileśayorapi \|*  
*āvāṃ śivo vañcayitvā gatavānsvālayaṃ vibhuḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. Perfect knowledge then dawned on Menā and the lord of mountains. They thought: “The lord Śiva has deceived us and returned to his abode.

## \|\| 7.34.36 \|\|

*asmai deyā svakanyeyaṃ pārvatī sutapa svinī \|*  
*evaṃ vicārya ca tayoḥ śivebhaktirabhūtparā \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. Our Pārvatī who has performed a hard penance shall be given to him”, thinking thus they became extremely devoted to Śiva.

## \|\| 7.34.37 \|\|

*ato rudro mahotīśca kṛtvā bhaktamudāvaham \|*  
*vivāhaṃ kṛtavānprītyā pārvatyā sa vidhānataḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. Thence after performing great sports, Śiva performed the marriage rites duly with Pārvatī delighting all devotees.

## \|\| 7.34.38 \|\|

*iti proktastu te tāta sunartakanaṭāhvayaḥ \|*  
*śivāvatāro hi mayā śivāvākyaprapūrakaḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. O dear, thus the incarnation of Śiva as Sunartaka naṭa in which the desire of Pārvatī was fulfilled has been explained to you by me.

## \|\| 7.34.39 \|\|

*idamākhyānamanaghaṃ paramaṃ vyāhṛtammayā \|*  
*ya etacchṛṇuyātprītyā sa sukhī gatimāpnuyāt \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. Thus the pious narrative of great value has been explained by me. He who listens to this with pleasures becomes happy and attains salvation.

## Chapter 35 - The incarnation of Śiva as a saintly brahmin

## \|\| 7.35.1 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*sanatkumāra sarvajña śivasya paramātmanaḥ \|*  
*avatāraṃ śṛṇu vibhossādhuveṣadvijāhvayam \|\| 1 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

1\. O omniscient Sanatkumāra, listen to the incarnation of Śiva, the supreme soul, as Brahmin in the garb of a saint.

## \|\| 7.35.2 \|\|

*menāhimālayorbhaktiṃ śive jñātvā mahottamām \|*  
*cintāmāpusturāssarve mantrayāmāsurādarāt \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. On realising the excellent devotion of Menā and Himavat towards Śiva, the gods became worried and they consulted one another eagerly.

## \|\| 7.35.3 \|\|

*ekāntabhaktyā śailaścetkanyāṃ dāsyati śambhave \|*  
*dhruvaṃ nirvāṇatāṃ sadyaḥ samprāpsyati śivasya vai \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. If the mountain were to give his daughter to Śiva with undiluted devotion, certainly he will attain salvation immediately.

## \|\| 7.35.4 \|\|

*anantaratnādhāro'sau cetprayāsyati mokṣatām \|*  
*ratnagarbhābhidhā bhūmirmithyaiva bhavitā dhruvam \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. He is the receptacle of infinite gems. If he attains liberation, the name of the earth, Ratnagarbhā (having gems within) will certainly be meaningless.

## \|\| 7.35.5 \|\|

*asthiratvamparityajya divyarūpaṃ vidhāya saḥ \|*  
*kanyāṃ śūlabhṛte dattvā śivālokaṃ gamiṣyati \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. He will cast off the stationary state of a mountain and assume a divine form. After giving his daughter in marriage to the trident-bearing lord he will go to Śiva’s region.

## \|\| 7.35.6 \|\|

*mahādevasya sārūpyaṃ prāpya śambhoranugrahāt \|*  
*tatra bhuktvā mahābhogāṃstato mokṣamavāpsyati \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. With the blessings of Śiva he will attain identity in form with the great god. He will enjoy all great pleasures there and then attain liberation,

## \|\| 7.35.7 \|\|

*ityālocya surāssarve jagmurgurugṛhaṃ mune \|*  
*cakrurnivedanaṃ gatvā gurave svārthasādhakāḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. O sage, after discussing like this, the gods went to the abode of their preceptor. In order to achieve their self-interest they submitted to the preceptor thus.

## \|\| 7.35.8 \|\|

*devā ūcuḥ \|*  
*guro himālayagṛhaṃ gacchāsmatkāryyasiddhaye \|*  
*kṛtvā niṃdāṃ maheśasya giribhaktiṃ nivāraya \|\| 8 \|\|*

The gods said:—

8\. O preceptor, please go to the abode of Himavat for the accomplishment of our task. Reproach lord Śiva and try to prevent the mountain’s excessive devotion to him.

## \|\| 7.35.9 \|\|

*svaśraddhayā sutāṃ dattvā śivāya sa girirguro \|*  
*labheta muktimatraiva dharaṇyāṃ sa hi tiṣṭhatu \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. O preceptor, if the mountain gives his daughter to Śiva with great faith he will attain salvation. Let him stay on the Earth itself.

## \|\| 7.35.10 \|\|

*iti devavacaḥ śrutvā provāca ca vicāryya tān \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. On hearing the words of the gods, he thought over that and spoke to them.

## \|\| 7.35.11 \|\|

*gururuvāca \|*  
*kaścinmadhye ca yuṣmākaṃ gacchecchailāntikaṃ surāḥ \|*  
*sampādayetsvābhimatamahaṃ tatkartumakṣamaḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

The preceptor said:—

11\. O gods, one of you shall go to the mountain for achieving the end. I am incapable of doing it.

## \|\| 7.35.12 \|\|

*athavā gacchata surā brahmalokaṃ savāsavāḥ \|*  
*tasmai vṛttaṃ kathaya svaṃ sa vaḥ kāryaṃ kariṣyati \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Or you all, including Indra, go to Brahmā’s region. Tell him all your details. He will carry out your work.

## \|\| 7.35.13 \|\|

*\|\| nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*tacchrutvā te samālocya jagmurvidhisabhāṃ surāḥ \|*  
*sarvaṃ nivedayāmāsustadvṛttaṃ purato vidheḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

Nandüvara said:—

13\. On hearing these words and reflecting thereon, the gods went to Brahmā’s assembly. They submitted all the details to Brahmā.

## \|\| 7.35.14 \|\|

*avocattānvidhiḥ śrutvā tadvacaḥ suviciṃtya vai \|*  
*nāhaṃ kariṣye tanniṃdāṃ duḥkhadāṃ kaharāṃ sadā \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. On hearing their words and reflecting deeply Brahmā told them—“I shall not censure him, for that will yield misery to us and dispel out happiness for ever.

## \|\| 7.35.15 \|\|

*surā gacchata kailāsaṃ saṃtoṣayata śaṅkaram \|*  
*prasthāpayata taṃ devaṃ himālayagṛhaṃ prati \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. O gods, you go to Kailāsa and propitiate Śiva. Request that lord to go to the abode of Himavat.

## \|\| 7.35.16 \|\|

*sa gacchedatha śaileśamātmanindāṃ karotu vai \|*  
*paranindā vināśāya svanindā yaśase matā \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Let him approach the lord of mountains and censure himself. Censuring others is conducive to destruction and censuring oneself is conducive to glory.

## \|\| 7.35.17 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*tataste prayayuḥ śīghraṃ kailāsaṃ nikhilāssurāḥ \|*  
*supraṇamya śivaṃ bhaktyā taddrutaṃ nikhilā jaguḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

17\. Then the gods hastened to Kailāsa and bowed to Śiva with great devotion. They mentioned everything in detail to him.

## \|\| 7.35.18 \|\|

*tacchrutvā devavacanaṃ svīcakāra maheśvaraḥ \|*  
*devānsuyāpayāmāsa tānāśvāsya vihasya saḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. On hearing the words of the gods, lord Śiva accepted their proposal. He laughingly consoled the gods and made them return without worry.

## \|\| 7.35.19 \|\|

*tataḥ sa bhagavāñchambhurmaheśo bhaktavatsalaḥ \|*  
*gantumaicchacchailamūlaṃ māyeśo na vikāravān \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. Then lord Śiva, who is favourably disposed towards his devotees, who is the wielder of Māyā and who is free from aberrations, desired to approach Himavat.

## \|\| 7.35.20-21 \|\|

*daṇḍī chatrī dibyavāsā bibhrattilakamujjvalam \|*  
*kare sphaṭikamālāṃ ca śālagrāmaṃ gale dadhat \|\| 20 \|\|*

*japannāma harerbhaktyā sādhuveṣadharo dvijaḥ \|*  
*himācalaṃ jagāmāśu bandhuvargessamanvitam \|\| 21 \|\|*

20-21. He assumed the form of a brahmin Saint. He held a staff and an umbrella. He wore divine garments. A brilliant caste mark shone on his forehead. He had tied a Śālagrāma stone round his neck and held a string of crystal beads in his hand. He frequently repeated the names of Viṣṇu with devotion. He approached Himavat who was accompanied by his kinsmen.

## \|\| 7.35.22 \|\|

*taṃ ca dṛṣṭvā samuttasthau sagaṇo'pi himālayaḥ \|*  
*nanāma daṇḍavadbhūmau sāṣṭāṅgaṃ vidhipūrvakam \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. On seeing him, Himavat and his attendants stood up and then prostrated before him. They performed Aṣṭāṅga salvation on the ground.

## \|\| 7.35.23 \|\|

*tataḥ papraccha śaileśastaṃ dvijaṃ ko bhavāniti \|*  
*uvāca śīghraṃ viprendrassa yogyadrimmahādarāt \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Then the lord of mountains asked him “Who are you please?” The brahmin Yogin replied immediately with reverence.

## \|\| 7.35.24 \|\|

*sādhudvija uvāca \|*  
*sādhu dvijāhvaḥ śailāhaṃ vaiṣṇavaḥ paramārthadṛk \|*  
*paropakārī sarvajñaḥ sarvagāmī gurorbalāt \|\| 24 \|\|*

The Saintly brahmin said:—

24\. O mountain, I am a devotee of Viṣṇu known as Sādhudvija. I possess true vision. I am omniscient and I assist others. By the power of the preceptor I can go everywhere.

## \|\| 7.35.25 \|\|

*mayā jñātaṃ svavijñānātsvasthāne śailasattama \|*  
*tacchṛṇu prītito vacmi hitvā dambhantavāṃtikam \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. O excellent mountain, by my divine knowledge, I have known something while I was in my abode. Listen. I shall mention the same with pleasure to you without any arrogance.

## \|\| 7.35.26 \|\|

*śaṅkarāya sutāndātuntvamicchasi nijodbhavām \|*  
*imāmpadmāsamāṃ ramyāmajñātakulaśīline \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. You wish to give your daughter who is as comely as Lakṣmī to Śiva whose family and conduct are unknown.

## \|\| 7.35.27 \|\|

*iyaṃ matiste śailendra na yuktā maṅgalapradā \|*  
*nibodha jñānināṃ śreṣṭha nārāyaṇakulodbhava \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. O lord of mountains, this idea of yours is not conducive to auspiciousness. O most excellent one among the wise, born of Viṣṇu’s race, you should know this.

## \|\| 7.35.28 \|\|

*paśya śailādhipatvaṃ ca na tasyaiko'sti bāndhavaḥ \|*  
*bāndhavānsvānprayatnena pṛccha menāṃ ca svapriyām \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. O lord of mountains, see for yourself. He has no single relative. You can verify your relatives and your beloved.

## \|\| 7.35.29 \|\|

*sarvānsaṃpṛccha yatnena menādīnpā rvatī vinā \|*  
*rogiṇe nauṣadhaṃ śaila kupathyaṃ rocate sadā \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. Ask Menā and others excluding Pārvatī. O mountain, the patient never likes the medicine but relishes undesirable diet.

## \|\| 7.35.30 \|\|

*na te pātrānurūpaśca pārvatīdānakarmmaṇi \|*  
*mahājanaḥ smeramukhaḥ śruti mātrādbhaviṣyati \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. In the matter of gifting away Pārvatī, he is not the deserving person. As soon as this alliance is announced, the public will smile derisively.

## \|\| 7.35.31-32 \|\|

*nirāśrayassadāsaṅgo virūpo nigurṇo'vyayaḥ \|*  
*smaśānavāsī vikaṭo vyālagrāhī digambaraḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

*vibhūtibhūṣaṇo vyālavarāveṣṭitamastakaḥ \|*  
*sarvāśramaparibhraṣṭastvavijñātagatissadā \|\| 32 \|\|*

31-32. He is without support. He is unattached. He is ugly, devoid of attributes and unchanging. He is hideous. He resides in the cremation ground. He is naked and handles serpents He smears ashes as decoration. Serpents twine round his head. He has fallen from all stages of life. His ways are ever unknown.

## \|\| 7.35.33 \|\|

*brahmovāca \|*  
*ityādyuktvā vacastathyaṃ śivanindāparaṃ sa hi \|*  
*jagāma svālayaṃ śīghrannānā līlākaraḥ śivaḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

Brahmā said:—

33\. After saying these factual statements insulting to Śiva, Śiva of diverse sports, quickly returned to his abode.

## \|\| 7.35.34 \|\|

*tacchrutvā vipravacanamabhūtāñca tanū tayoḥ \|*  
*viparītānarthapare kiṃ kariṣyāvahe dhruvam \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. On hearing the words of the brahmin, they thought thus—“What can we do with regard to him who is engrossed in adverse things?”

## \|\| 7.35.35 \|\|

*tato rudro mahotiṃ ca kṛtvā bhaktamudāvahām \|*  
*vivāhayitvā girijāṃ devakāryyaṃ cakāra saḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. Then Śiva performed great sports delighting the devotees. He married Pārvatī aud carried out the task of the gods.

## \|\| 7.35.36 \|\|

*iti proktastu te tāta sādhuveṣo dvijāhvayaḥ \|*  
*śivāvatāro hi mayā devakāryyakaraḥ prabho \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. O dear, thus the incarnation of lord Śiva as Sādhuveṣadvija, that carried out the task of the gods has been narrated to you.

## \|\| 7.35.37 \|\|

*idamākhyānamanaghaṃ svargyamāyuṣyamuttamam \|*  
*yaḥ paṭhecchṛṇuyādvāpi sa sukhī gatimāpnuyāt \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. He who reads or listens to this pious narrative, conducive to heaven and longevity, becomes happy and attains salvation.

## Chapter 36 - Śiva’s incarnation as Aśvatthāman

## \|\| 7.36.1 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*sanatkumāra sarvajña śivasya paramātmanaḥ \|*  
*avatāraṃ śṛṇu vibhoraśvatthāmāhvayaṃ param \|\| 1 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

1\. O omniscient Sanatkumāra, listen to the incarnation of lord Śiva, the great soul Aśvatthāman.

## \|\| 7.36.2 \|\|

*bṛhaspatermahābuddherdevarṣeraṃśato mune \|*  
*bharadvājātsamutpanno droṇo'yonija ātmavān \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. O sage, though a part of the celestial sage of great intellect Bṛhaspati, the self-possessed and prudent Droṇa was born of Bharadvāja but not of any womb.

## \|\| 7.36.3 \|\|

*dhanurbhṛtāṃ varaḥ śūro viprarṣissarvaśāstravit \|*  
*bṛhatkīrtirmahātejā yaḥ sarvāstraviduttamaḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. He was the foremost among the archers, heroes, a brahminical sage, well-versed in all lore, of great fame and brilliance and the most excellent of all the wielders of weapons and missiles.

## \|\| 7.36.4 \|\|

*dhanurvede ca vede ca niṣṇātaṃ yaṃ vidurbudhāḥ \|*  
*variṣṭhaṃ citrakarmāṇaṃ droṇaṃ svakulavardhanam \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. Learned men knew Droṇa as the most excellent, skilful in the lore of archery as well as the Vedas, of variegated activities, and as one who made his race flourish.

## \|\| 7.36.5 \|\|

*kauravāṇāṃ sa ācaryyaṃ āsītsvabalato dvija \|*  
*mahārathiṣu vikhyātaḥ ṣaṭsu kauravamadhyataḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. O brahmin, thanks to his strength, he was the instructor of the Kauravas. He was the most well known among the six Mahārathas among the Kauravas.

## \|\| 7.36.6 \|\|

*sāhāyyārthaṃ kauravāṇāṃ sa tepe vipulantapaḥ \|*  
*śivamuddiśya putrārthaṃ droṇācāryyo dvijottamaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. The most excellent brahmin Droṇa performed an elaborate penance with Śiva in view for obtaining a son in order to help the Kauravas.

## \|\| 7.36.7 \|\|

*tataḥ prasanno bhagavācchaṃkaro bhaktavatsalaḥ \|*  
*āvirbabhūva purato droṇasya munisattama \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. O excellent sage, then the delighted lord Śiva, favourably disposed to his devotees, appeared in front of Droṇa.

## \|\| 7.36.8 \|\|

*tandṛṣṭvā sa dvijo droṇastuṣṭāvāśu praṇamya tam \|*  
*mahāprasannahṛdayo natakassukṛtāñjaliḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. On seeing him, the brahmin Drona whose heart was highly delighted bowed to him with palms joined in reverence and eulogised him.

## \|\| 7.36.9 \|\|

*tasya stutyā ca tapasā santuṣṭaḥ śaṃkara prabhuḥ \|*  
*varambrūhīti covāca droṇantaṃ bhaktavatsalaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. Satisfied and pleased with his eulogy and penance, lord Śiva, favourably disposed towards his devotees told Droṇa—“Mention the boon you wish to choose”.

## \|\| 7.36.10 \|\|

*tacchrutvā śambhuvacanaṃ droṇaḥ prāhātha sannataḥ \|*  
*svāṃśajantanayandehi sarvājeyammahābalam \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. On hearing the words of Śiva, Droṇa bowed low and said—“Please give me a son born of your part who will be invincible to every one and who will be very powerful.

## \|\| 7.36.11 \|\|

*tacchrutvā droṇavacanaṃ śambhuḥ proce tathāstviti \|*  
*abhūdantarhitastāta kautukī sukhakṛnmune \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. On hearing the words of Droṇa, Śiva said, “So be it.” O dear sage, the lord who bestows happiness, then vanished.

## \|\| 7.36.12 \|\|

*droṇo'pagacchatsvandhāma mahāhṛṣṭo gatabhramaḥ \|*  
*svapatnyai kathayāmāsa tadvṛtaṃ sakalaṃ mudā \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Droṇa returned to his abode highly delighted and free from illusion. He joyously narrated all the details to his wife.

## \|\| 7.36.13 \|\|

*athāvasaramāsādya rudraḥ sarvāntakaḥ prabhuḥ \|*  
*svāṃśena tanayo jajñe droṇasya sa mahābalaḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Taking the due opportunity, lord Śiva, the annihilator of all and very powerful was born as Droṇa’s son through a part of himself.

## \|\| 7.36.14 \|\|

*aśvatthāmeti vikhyātastasya babhūva kṣitau mune \|*  
*pravīraḥ kaṃjapatrākṣaśśatrupakṣakṣayaṅkaraḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. O sage, he became well known as Aśvatthāman on the earth. He was heroic, had lotus-like eyes and he could reduce the strength of his enemy.

## \|\| 7.36.15 \|\|

*yo bhārate raṇe khyātaḥ piturājñāmavāpya ca \|*  
*sahāyakṛdbabhūvāta kauravāṇāṃ mahābalaḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. Securing the permission and behest of his father that famous hero helped the Kauravas in the Mahābhārata war.

## \|\| 7.36.16 \|\|

*yamāśritya mahāvīraṃ kauravāssuma hābalāḥ \|*  
*bhīṣmādayo babhūvuste'jeyā api divaukasām \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. By resorting to that powerful hero, Bhīsma and other Kauravas became invincible even to the gods.

## \|\| 7.36.17 \|\|

*yadbhayātpāṇḍavāssarve kauravāñjetumakṣamāḥ \|*  
*āsannaṣṭāmahāvīrā api sarve ca kovidāḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Fearing him, the Pāṇḍavas, though they were great heroes and very skilful, became incapable of conquering the Kauravas.

## \|\| 7.36.18 \|\|

*kṛṣṇopadeśataśśambhostapaḥ kṛtvātidāruṇam \|*  
*prāpya cāstraṃ śambhuvarājjigye tānarjunastataḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. At the instance of Kṛṣṇa, Arjuna, performed the severe penance of Śiva and obtained a divne missile duo to Śiva’s boon. He then conquered the Kauravas.

## \|\| 7.36.19 \|\|

*aśvatthāmā mahāvīro mahādevāṃśajo mune \|*  
*tadāpi tadbhaktivaśaḥ svapratāpamadarśayat \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. O sage, although the great hero Aśvatthāman was

born of a part of lord Śiva. Still due to his devotion to the Kauravas, he exhibited his full vigour.

## \|\| 7.36.20 \|\|

*vināśya pāṇḍavasutāñchikṣitānapi yatnataḥ \|*  
*kṛṣṇādibhirmahāvīrairanivāryyabalaḥ paraiḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. He destroyed the sons of Pāṇḍavas though they were trained in warfare by Kṛṣṇa and other great heroes. He had the strength that could not be withstood bv the enemy.

## \|\| 7.36.21 \|\|

*putraśokena vikalamāpatantaṃ tamarjunam \|*  
*rathenācyutavaṃtaṃ hi dṛṣṭvā sa ca parādravat \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. On seeing Arjuna dejected by the death of his son but rushing on him along with Kṛṣṇa he fled away from the battle-field.

## \|\| 7.36.22 \|\|

*astraṃ brahmaśiro nāma taduparyyasṛjatsa hi \|*  
*tataḥ prādurabhūttejaḥ pracaṇḍaṃ sarvato diśam \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. He discharged the divine missile Brahmaśiras on Arjuna. Then a fierce splendour enveloping all the quarters manifested itself.

## \|\| 7.36.23 \|\|

*prāṇāpadamabhiprekṣya sorjunaḥ kleśasaṃyutaḥ \|*  
*uvāca kṛṣṇaṃ viklānto naṣṭatejā mahābhayaḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Sensing danger to his own life Arjuna was much distressed. Losing all his brilliance and highly frightened he spoke to Kṛṣṇa becoming utterly exhausted.

## \|\| 7.36.24 \|\|

*arjuna uvāca \|*  
*kimidaṃ svitkuto veti kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇa na vedmyaham \|*  
*sarvatomukhamāyāti tejaścedaṃ sudu्ssaham \|\| 24 \|\|*

Arjuna said:—

24\. O Kṛṣṇa, O Kṛṣṇa, what is this? Whence it has arisen I do not know. This unbearable fiery splendour comes from all sides.

## \|\| 7.36.25 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*śrutvārjunavacaścedaṃ sa kṛṣṇaśśaivasattamaḥ \|*  
*dadhyau śivaṃ sadāraṃ ca pratyāhārjunamādarāt \|\| 25 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

25\. On hearing the words of Arjuna, Kṛṣṇa the most excellent among the devotees of Śiva meditated on Śiva and Pārvatī and then spoke to Arjuna.

## \|\| 7.36.26 \|\|

*kṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*vetthedandroṇaputrasya brāhmamastraṃ maholbaṇam \|*  
*na hyasyānyatamaṃ kiñcidastraṃ pratyavakarśanam \|\| 26 \|\|*

Kṛṣṇa said:—

26\. Know this to be the most powerful Brāhma missile of Aśvatthāman. There is no other missile to counteract this.

## \|\| 7.36.27 \|\|

*śivaṃ smara drutaṃ śambhuṃ svaprabhumbhaktarakṣakam \|*  
*yena dattaṃ hi te svāstraṃ sarvakāryyakaramparam \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Immediately think of Śiva, your lord, the protector of the devotees who had given you a weapon capable of every action.

## \|\| 7.36.28 \|\|

*jahyastrateja unnaddhantvantacchaivāstratejasā \|*  
*ityuktvā ca svayaṃ kṛṣṇaśśivandadhyau tadarthakaḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. Counteract the fiery splendour of the Brāhma missile with your Śaiva missile. After saying this Kṛṣṇa himself meditated on Śiva for that purpose.

## \|\| 7.36.29 \|\|

*tacchrutvā kṛṣṇavacanaṃ pārthassmṛtvā śivaṃ hṛdi \|*  
*spṛṣṭvāpastaṃ \|*  
*praṇamyāśu cikṣepāstrantato mune \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. O sage, on hearing the words of Kṛṣṇa. Arjuna remembered Śiva, touched water, bowed to Śiva and discharged the Śaiva missile.

## \|\| 7.36.30 \|\|

*yadyapyastraṃ brahmaśirastvamoghañcāpratikriyam \|*  
*śaivāstratejasā sadyassamaśāmyanmahāmune \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. O great sage, although the Brahmaśiras missile

never goes futile and cannot be counteracted, it subsided before the splendour of the Śaiva missile.

## \|\| 7.36.31 \|\|

*maṃsthā mā hyetadāścaryyaṃ sarvacitramaye śive \|*  
*yassvaśaktyākhilaṃ viśvaṃ sṛjatyavati hantyajaḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. Do not consider this very surprising in Śiva who is full of wondrous things and who, the unborn, creates, protects and annihilates the entire universe by his own power.

## \|\| 7.36.32 \|\|

*aśvatthāmā tato jñātvā vṛttametacchivāṃśajaḥ \|*  
*śaivanna vivyathe kiñcicchivecchātuṣṭadhīrmune \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. O sage, knowing that this happened by the influence of Śiva, Aśvatthāman, born of Śiva’s part and delighted in heart at Śiva’s wish, was not at all sorry.

## \|\| 7.36.33 \|\|

*atha drauṇiridaṃ viśvaṃ kṛtsnaṃ kartumapāṇḍavam \|*  
*uttarāgarbhagaṃ bālaṃ nāśitummana ādadhe \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. Then the son of Droṇa wishing the world to be rid of Pāṇḍava lineage desired to kill the child in the womb of Uttarā.

## \|\| 7.36.34 \|\|

*brahmāstramanivāryyaṃ tadanyairastrairmahāprabham \|*  
*uttarāgarbhamuddiśya cikṣepa sa mahāprabhuḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. The powerful Aśvatthāman hurled the excessively lustrous Brāhma-missile that could not be countermanded by any other missile, aiming it at the womb of Uttarā.

## \|\| 7.36.35 \|\|

*tataśca sottarā jiṣṇuvadhūrvikalamānasā \|*  
*kṛṣṇantuṣṭāva lakṣmīśandahyamānā tadastrataḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. Then Uttarā the daughter-in-law of Arjuna, burning within due to that missile and agitated in the mind eulogised Kṛṣṇa, the lord of Lakṣmī.

## \|\| 7.36.36 \|\|

*tataḥ kṛṣṇaḥ śivaṃ dhyātvā hṛdā nutvā praṇamya ca \|*  
*apāṇḍavamidaṃ kartuṃ drauṇerastramabudhyata \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. Then meditating on Śiva mentally, bowing to and eulogising him, Kṛṣṇa understood that to be the missile of the son of Droṇa.

## \|\| 7.36.37 \|\|

*svarakṣārthendradattena tadastreṇa suvarcasā \|*  
*sudarśanena tasyāśca vyadhādrakṣāṃ śivājñayā \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. By means of his missile—the brilliant Sudarśana, bestowed on him by Śiva for his protection, he saved her at the behest of Śiva.

## \|\| 7.36.38 \|\|

*svarūpaṃ śaṃkarādeśātkṛtaṃ śaivavareṇa ha \|*  
*kṛṣṇena caritaṃ jñātvā vimanaskaḥ śanairabhūt \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Aśvatthāman then knew that Kṛṣṇa had assumed his form at the behest of śiva and did it as he was the foremost among the devotees of Śiva, slowly refrained from the thought of destroying Uttarā.

## \|\| 7.36.39 \|\|

*tatassa kṛṣṇaḥ prītātmā pāṇḍavānsakalānapi \|*  
*apātayattadaṃghryostu tuṣṭaye tasya śaivarāṭ \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. Then the leading devotee of Śiva, the delighted Kṛṣṇa, made all the Pāṇḍavas fall at the feet of Aśvatthāman for pleasing him.

## \|\| 7.36.40 \|\|

*atha drauṇiḥ prasannātmā pāṇḍavānkṛṣṇameva ca \|*  
*nānāvarāndadau prītyā so'śvatthāmānugṛhya ca \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. Then Aśvatthāman, son of Droṇa, highly delighted in the mind, blessed the Pāṇḍavas and Kṛṣṇa with various boons.

## \|\| 7.36.41 \|\|

*itthaṃ maheśvarastāta cakre līlāmparāmprabhuḥ \|*  
*avatīryya kṣitau drauṇirūpeṇa munisattama \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. O excellent sage, incarnating thus on the earth in the form of Droṇa’s son, lord Śiva performed various sports.

## \|\| 7.36.42 \|\|

*śivāvatāro'śvatthāmā mahābalaparākramaḥ \|*  
*trailokyamukhado'dyāpi vartate jāhnavītaṭe \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. The incarnation of Śiva Aśvatthāman of great strength and exploit is even now alive on the banks of the river Gaṅgā bestowing happiness on the three worlds.

## \|\| 7.36.43 \|\|

*aśvatthāmāvatāraste varṇitaśśaṃkara prabhoḥ \|*  
*sarvasiddhikaraścāpi bhaktābhīṣṭaphalapradaḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. The incarnation in the form of Aśvatthāman of lord Śiva has been explained to you. It is the cause of all Siddhis and it fulfils all desires of the devotees.

## \|\| 7.36.44 \|\|

*ya idaṃ śṛṇuyādbhaktyā kīrtayedvā samāhitaḥ \|*  
*sa siddhimprāpnuyādiṣṭāmante śivapuraṃ vrajet \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. He who listens to this with devotion or narrates it with pure mind attains all Siddhis here and attains Śiya’s abode hereafter.

## Chapter 37 - Instruction of Vyāsa in the context of Siva’s incarnation as Kirāta

## \|\| 7.37.1 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*śṛṇu prājña kirātākhyamavatārampinākinaḥ \|*  
*mūkaṃ ca hatavānprīto yo'rjunāya varandadau \|\| 1 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

1\. O intelligent one, listen to the incarnation of the Pināka-bearing lord Śiva as Kirāta who killed Mūka and delightedly granted the boon to Arjuna.

## \|\| 7.37.2 \|\|

*suyodhanajitāste vai pāṇḍavāḥ pravarāśca te \|*  
*draupadyā ca tayā sādhvyā dvaitākhyaṃ vanamāyayuḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Defeated by Suyodhana, the excellent Pāṇḍavas went to the forest Dvaita accompanied by their chaste wife Draupadī.

## \|\| 7.37.3 \|\|

*tatraiva sūryyadattāṃ vai sthālīṃ cāśritya te tadā \|*  
*kālaṃ ca vāhayāmāsussukhena kila pāṇḍavāḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Depending upon the cooking-pot given by the sun, the Pāṇḍavas spent their time happily.

## \|\| 7.37.4 \|\|

*chalārthaṃ preritastena durvāsā munipuṅgavaḥ \|*  
*suyodhanena viprendra pāṇḍavāntikamādarāt \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. O leading brahmin, Durvāsas the leading sage was induced by Suyodhana to go to the Pāṇḍavas in order to deceive them.

## \|\| 7.37.5 \|\|

*chātraiḥ svairvāyutaissārddhaṃ yayāce tatra tānmudā \|*  
*bhojyaṃ cittepsitaṃ vai sa tebhyaścaiva samāgataḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Accompanied by his ten thousand disciples he approached them and asked for foodstuffs and other things for his disciples.

## \|\| 7.37.6 \|\|

*svīkṛtya pāṇḍavaistaistaiḥ snānārthaṃ preṣitāstadā \|*  
*durvāsaḥpramukhāścaiva munayaśca tapasvinaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. After accepting it, Durvāsas and other sages and saints were sent by the Pāṇḍavas for their ablution.

## \|\| 7.37.7 \|\|

*atha te pāṇḍavāḥ sarve annābhāvānmunīśvara \|*  
*duḥkhitāśca tadā prāṇāṃstyaktuṃ citte samādadhuḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. O great sage, by want of food, all the Pāṇḍavas were sad and resolved to cast off their lives.

## \|\| 7.37.8 \|\|

*draupadyā ca smṛtaḥ kṛṣṇa āgatastatkṣaṇādapi \|*  
*śākaṃ ca bhakṣayitvā tu teṣāṃ tṛptiṃ samādadhat \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Kṛṣṇa appeared on being remembered by Draupadī Draupadī. After eating a bit of vegetable he made them satiated at the very moment.

## \|\| 7.37.9 \|\|

*durvāsāśca tadā śiṣyāṃstṛptāñjñātvā yayau punaḥ \|*  
*pāṇḍavāḥ kṛcchranirmuktāḥ kṛṣṇasya kṛpayā tadā \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. On coming to know that the disciples were fully satisfied Durvāsas went away. By the grace of Kṛṣṇa, the Pāṇḍavas were freed from a difficult situation.

## \|\| 7.37.10 \|\|

*atha te pāṇḍavāḥ kṛṣṇaṃ papracchuḥ kimbhaviṣyati \|*  
*balavāñchatrurutpannaḥ kiṃ kāryyantadvada prabho \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Then they asked Kṛṣṇa “O lord, what will happen now? A very powerful enemy has arisen. What shall be done at this moment.”

## \|\| 7.37.11 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*iti pṛṣṭhastadā taistu śrīkṛṣṇaḥ pāṇḍavairmune \|*  
*smṛtvā śivapadāmbhojau pāṇḍavānidamabravīt \|\| 11 \|\|*

Nandīśvara. said:—

11\. O sage, on being asked thus by the Pāṇḍavas, lord Kṛṣṇa remembered the lotus-like feet of Śiva and spoke to them.

## \|\| 7.37.12 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*śrūyatāṃ pāṇḍavāḥ śreṣṭhāḥ śrutvā kartavyameva hi \|*  
*madvṛttāntaṃ viśeṣeṇa śivasevāsamanvitam \|\| 12 \|\|*

Lord Kṛṣṇa said:—

12\. O excellent Pāṇḍavas, now listen to the details of my experience involving service to Śiva. After hearing you shall act accordingly.

## \|\| 7.37.13-14 \|\|

*dvārakāṃ ca mayā gatvā śatrūṇāṃ vijigīṣayā \|*  
*vicāryya copadeśāṃśca upamanyormahātmanaḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

*mayā hyārādhitaḥ śambhuḥ prasannaḥ parameśvaraḥ \|*  
*baṭuke parvataśreṣṭhe saptamāsaṃ susevitaḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

13-14. I went to Dvārakā to conquer my enemies. After obeying the instructions of noble Upamanyu I propitiated lord Śiva. I propitiated him for seven months in the excellent mountain Baṭuka. Thus Śiva was pleased with me.

## \|\| 7.37.15 \|\|

*iṣṭānkāmānadānmahyaṃ viśveśaśca svayaṃ sthitaḥ \|*  
*tatprabhāvānmayā sarvasāmarthyaṃ labdhamuttamam \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. Standing there himself he fulfilled all my desires. By his power I acquired excellent efficiency in everything.

## \|\| 7.37.16 \|\|

*idānīṃ sevyate devo bhuktimukti phalapradaḥ \|*  
*yūyaṃ sevata taṃ śambhumapi sarvasukhāvaham \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. The lord, the bestower of worldly pleasures and salvation is propitiated by me even now. You too serve Śiva who is the bestower of happiness to all.

## \|\| 7.37.17 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityuktvāntardadhe kṛṣṇa āśvāsyātha ca pāṇḍavān \|*  
*dvārakāmagamacchīghraṃ smaracchivapadāmbujam \|\| 17 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

17\. After saying thus and cheering the Pāṇḍavas, Kṛṣna vanished from there and remembering the lotus feet of Śiva he hastened to Dvārakā.

## \|\| 7.37.18 \|\|

*pāṇḍavā atha bhillaṃ ca preṣayāmāsurojasā \|*  
*guṇānāṃ ca parīkṣārthaṃ tasya duryodhanasya ca \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. Pāṇḍavas then sent a Bhilla to test the virtues and the strength of Duryodhana.

## \|\| 7.37.19 \|\|

*sopi sarvaṃ ca tatratyanduryodhanaguṇodayam \|*  
*samīcīnaṃ ca tajjñātvāpunaḥ prāpa prabhūnprati \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. He understood the prosperity, rise and the virtues of Duryodhana and returned to the Pāṇḍavas again.

## \|\| 7.37.20 \|\|

*taduktante niśamyaivaṃ dukhamprāpurmunīśvara \|*  
*parasparaṃ samūcuste pāṇḍavā atiduḥkhitāḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. On hearing what he had said, O great sāge, the Pāṇḍavas were very sad and in their distress spoke to one another.

## \|\| 7.37.21 \|\|

*kiṅkartavyaṃ kva gantavyamasmābhiradhunā yudhi \|*  
*samarthā api vai sarve satyapāśena yantritāḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. What shall be done now? Where shall we go? Though we can fight now we have been restrained by the bonds of truth.

## \|\| 7.37.22 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*etasminsamaye vyāso bhasmabhūṣitamastakaḥ \|*  
*rudrākṣābharaṇaścāyājjaṭājūṭavibhūṣitaḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

22\. In the meantime Vyāsa came there with the ashes decorating his forehead, with Rudrākṣa as an ornament and well bedecked in matted hair.

## \|\| 7.37.23 \|\|

*pañcākṣaraṃ japanmaṃtraṃ śivapremasamākulaḥ \|*  
*tejasāṃ ca svayaṃrāśissākṣāddharma ivāparaḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. He was repeating the five-syllabled mantra. He was fully engrossed in the love of Śiva. He was a heap of lustrous brilliance, a manifestation of virtue itself.

## \|\| 7.37.24 \|\|

*tandṛṣṭvā te tadā prītā utthāya purataḥ sthitāḥ \|*  
*dattvāsanaṃ tadā tasmai kuśājinasuśobhitam \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. When they saw him they were delighted and they stood up to welcome him. Offering him a seat of deer-hide brightened by Kuśa grass they waited in front of him.

## \|\| 7.37.25 \|\|

*tatropaviṣṭaṃ taṃ vyāsaṃ pūjayanti sma harṣitāḥ \|*  
*stutiṃ ca vividhāṃ kṛtvā dhanyāḥ sma iti vādinaḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. When he was duly seated they honoured him with great delight after eulogising him in various ways and saying “Ah! we are blessed”.

## \|\| 7.37.26 \|\|

*tapaścaiva susantaptaṃ dānāni vividhāni ca \|*  
*tatsarvaṃ saphalaṃ jātaṃ tṛptāste darśanātprabho \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. “O holy lord, by seeing you, we are all satisfied. We have attained the fruits of our austerities and our diverse charitable gifts.

## \|\| 7.37.27 \|\|

*duḥkhaṃ ca dūrato jātandarśanātte pitāmaha \|*  
*duṣṭaiścaiva mahāduḥkhaṃ dattaṃ naḥ krūrakarmabhiḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. O grandfather, by seeing you, all our misery has disappeared, the misery that had been inflicted on us by the wicked people of ruthless activities.

## \|\| 7.37.28 \|\|

*śrīmatāndarśane jāte duḥkhaṃ caiva gamiṣyati \|*  
*kadācinna gataṃ tatra niścayoyaṃ vicāritaḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. Misery disappears at your sight. Was there ever a time when it did not disappear? We know it truly.

## \|\| 7.37.29 \|\|

*mahatāmāśrame prāpte samarthe sarvakarmaṇi \|*  
*yadi duḥkhaṃ na gacchetu daivamevātra kāraṇam \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. When a person reaches the hermitage of a sage efficient in all activities, if misery is not dispelled, fate alone is the cause there.

## \|\| 7.37.30 \|\|

*niścayenaiva gacchetu dāridyaṃ duḥkhakāraṇam \|*  
*mahatāṃ ca svabhāvoyaṃ kalpavṛkṣasamo mataḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. Poverty, the cause of misery, will surely be dispelled. Such is the nature of great men. In the fulfilment of desires they are equal to the wish-yielding Kalpa tree.

## \|\| 7.37.31 \|\|

*tadguṇāneva gaṇayenmahato vastumātrataḥ \|*  
*āśrayasya vaśādeva puṃso vai jāyate prabho \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. Thus the qualities of the great shall be observed objectively. A person becomes famous by merely resorting to them.

## \|\| 7.37.32 \|\|

*laghutvaṃ ca mahattvaṃ ca nātra kāryyā vicāraṇā \|*  
*uttamānāṃ svabhāvoyaṃ yaddīnapratipālanam \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. The difference between the small and the great shall not be thought of. This is the nature of the great that they will always protect the distressed.

## \|\| 7.37.33 \|\|

*raṃkasya lakṣaṇaṃ loke hyatiśreyaskaraṃ matam \|*  
*puro'sya parayatno vai sujanānāṃ ca sevanam \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. The characteristics of the wretched and the miserable are conducive to his glory, for there lies before him an opportunity for strenuous efforts and for serving the good people.

## \|\| 7.37.34 \|\|

*ataḥ paraṃ ca bhāgyaṃ vai doṣaścaiva na dīyatām \|*  
*etasmātkāraṇātsvāmiṃstvayi dṛṣṭo śubhantadā \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. Thereafter it is one’s own destiny. No one is to blame for it. Hence O lord, when you are seen, everything auspicious takes place.

## \|\| 7.37.35 \|\|

*tvadāgamanamātreṇa santuṣṭāni manāṃsi naḥ \|*  
*diśopadeśaṃ yenāśu duḥkhaṃ naṣṭambhavecca naḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. By your mere arrival our minds are satisfied. Give us instructions whereby our misery can be dispelled.

## \|\| 7.37.36 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityetadvacanaṃ śrutvā pāṇḍavānāṃ mahāmuniḥ \|*  
*prasannamānaso bhūtvā vyāsaścaivābravīdidam \|\| 36 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

36\. On hearing the words of the Pāṇḍavas the great sage Vyāsa was delighted in the mind and said thus.

## \|\| 7.37.37 \|\|

*he pāṇḍavāśca yūyaṃ vai na kaṣṭaṃ kartumarhatha \|*  
*dhanyāḥ stha kṛtakṛtyāḥ stha satyaṃ naiva vilopitam \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. O Pāṇḍavas, you need not be over-anxious. You are all blessed and contented. You have not let truth slip by.

## \|\| 7.37.38 \|\|

*sujanānāṃ svabhāvoyaṃ prāṇānte'pi suśobhanaḥ \|*  
*dharmaṃ tyajanti naivātra satyaṃ saphalabhājanam \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. The noble nature of the good remains unchanged even at death. They do not forsake virtue. They fruitfully resort to Truth.

## \|\| 7.37.39 \|\|

*asmākaṃ caiva yūyaṃ ca te cāpi samatāṅgatāḥ \|*  
*tathāpi pakṣapāto vai dharmiṣṭhānāṃ mato budhaiḥ \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. As far as we are concerned, you and the Kauravas ought to be equal. Still our partiality is towards the virtuous. This partiality is approved by the learned.

## \|\| 7.37.40 \|\|

*dhṛtarāṣṭrena duṣṭena prathamaṃ ca hyacakṣuṣā \|*  
*dharmastyaktaḥ svayaṃ lobhādyuṣmākaṃ rājyamāhṛtam \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. Virtue was at the outset abandoned by the wicked and blind Dhṛtarāṣṭra due to greed. Your kingdom was seized.

## \|\| 7.37.41 \|\|

*tasya yūyaṃ ca te cāpi putrā eva na saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*pitaryyuparate bālā anukaṃpyā mahātmanaḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. Undoubtedly you and they are his sons when your real father is dead. You are noble souls and hence you deserve to be viewed sympathetically.

## \|\| 7.37.42 \|\|

*paścātputraśca tenaiva vārito na kadācana \|*  
*anartho naiva jāyeta yaccaivaṃ ca kṛtantadā \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. Afterwards his son was never dissuaded by him. If only he had done so, this calamity would not have occurred.

## \|\| 7.37.43 \|\|

*ataḥ paraṃ ca yajjātaṃ tajjātaṃ nānyathābhavet \|*  
*ayanduṣṭo bhavantaśca dharmiṣṭhāḥ satyavādinaḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. Whatever has happened cannot be otherwise. He is wicked while all of you are virtuous and truthful.

## \|\| 7.37.44 \|\|

*tasmādante ca tasyaivāśubhaṃ hi bhavitā dhuvam \|*  
*yaccaiva vāpitaṃ bījaṃ tatpraroho bhavediha \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. Hence evil will befall only him in the end, to be sure. The seed that has been sown will germinate into a sprout.

## \|\| 7.37.45 \|\|

*tasmādduḥkhaṃ na kartavyaṃ bhavadbhiḥ sarvathā dhruvam \|*  
*bhaviṣyati śubhaṃ vo hi nātra kāryyā vicāraṇā \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. Hence you need not feel distressed at all. Welfare will dawn on you. You need not entertain any doubt.

## \|\| 7.37.46 \|\|

*\|\| nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityuktvā pāṇḍavāḥ sarve tena vyāsena prīṇitāḥ \|*  
*yudhiṣṭhiramukhāste ca punarevābruvanvacaḥ \|\| 46 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

46\. Thus the Pāṇḍavas were consoled and cheered by Vyāsa. Yudhiṣṭhira and others then spoke these words.

## \|\| 7.37.47 \|\|

*pāṇḍavā ūcuḥ \|*  
*satyamuktantvayā nātha duṣṭairduḥkhaṃ niraṃtaram \|*  
*duṣṭātmabhirvane cāpi dīyate hi muhurmuhuḥ \|\| 47 \|\|*

The Pāṇḍavas said:—

47\. O lord, I have mentioned the truth. Even in the forest misery is being inflicted on us incessantly by the wicked and the evil-minded Kauravas again and again.

## \|\| 7.37.48 \|\|

*tannāśayāśubhammedya kiṃciddeyaṃ śubhaṃ vibho \|*  
*kṛṣṇena kathitaṃ pūrvamārādhyaśśaṅkarassadā \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. O holy lord, please dispel our distress. Something auspicious shall be bestowed upon us. We have been advised by Kṛṣṇa that Śiva shall be propitiated always.

## \|\| 7.37.49 \|\|

*pramādaśca kṛto'smābhistadvacaśśithilīkṛtam \|*  
*sa devamārgastu punaridānīmupadiśyatām \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. We have committed a blunder in not following his advice. May divine lead be given to us again now.

## \|\| 7.37.50 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityetadvacanaṃ śrutvā vyāso harṣasamanvitaḥ \|*  
*uvāca pāṇḍavānprītyā smṛtvā śivapadāṃbujam \|\| 50 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

50\. On hearing these words, the delighted Vyāsa, remembered the lotus-like feet of Śiva and spoke to the Pāṇḍavas with pleasure.

## \|\| 7.37.51 \|\|

*vyāsa uvāca \|*  
*śrūyatāṃ vacanaṃ medya pāṃḍavā dharmabuddhayaḥ \|*  
*satyamuktaṃ tu kṛṣṇena mayā saṃsevyate śivaḥ \|\| 51 \|\|*

Vyāsa said:—

51\. O Pāṇḍavas, of virtuous mind, listen to my words. It has been truly stated by Kṛṣṇa. Śiva is being served by me too.

## \|\| 7.37.52 \|\|

*bhavadbhiḥ sevyatāṃ prītyā sukhaṃ syādatulaṃ sadā \|*  
*sarvaduḥkhaṃ bhavatyeva śivā'sevāta eva hi \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. Let him be served by you with pleasure. Unequalled happiness shall always follow. It is only by not serving Śiva that miseries crop up.

## \|\| 7.37.53-54 \|\|

*\|\| naṃdīśvara uvāca \|*  
*atha paṃcasu teṣveva vicāryya śivapūjane \|*  
*arjunaṃ yogyamuccārya vyāso munivarastathā \|\| 53 \|\|*

*tapaḥsthānaṃ vicāryyaivaṃ tatassa munisattamaḥ \|*  
*pāṇḍavāndharmasanniṣṭhānpunarevābravīdidam \|\| 54 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

53-54. After considering everything, the great sage Vyāsa pronounced Arjuna the most deserving among the five brothers in the worship of Śiva. Then pondering over the place worthy of the penance, the excellent sage spoke again to the Pāṇḍavas who were firmly established in virtuous rites.

## \|\| 7.37.55-56 \|\|

*\|\| vyāsa uvāca \|*  
*śrūyatāmpāṇḍavāssarve kathayāmi hitaṃ sadā \|*  
*śivaṃ sarvaṃ paraṃ dṛṣṭvā paraṃ brahma satāṅgatim \|\| 55 \|\|*

*brahmāditriparārddhāntaṃ yatkiṃciddṛśyate jagat \|*  
*tatsarvaṃ śivarūpaṃ ca pūjyandhyeyaṃ ca tatpunaḥ \|\| 56 \|\|*

Vyāsa said:—

55-56. O Pāṇḍavas, all of you hear. I shall tell you, what is ever good and wholesome. Śiva shall be viewed as the great Brahman and the goal of the good. Beginning with Brahmā and ending with infinity whatever is seen in the universe is Śiva’s form. It shall be honoured and meditated upon.

## \|\| 7.37.57 \|\|

*sarveṣāṃ caiva seṣyosau śaṅkarassarvaduḥkhahā \|*  
*śivaḥ svalpena kālena saṃprasīdati bhaktitaḥ \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. Śiva, the dispeller of all misery shall be served by all. Due to devotion, Śiva becomes pleased in a short time.

## \|\| 7.37.58 \|\|

*suprasanno maheśo hi bhaktebhyaḥ sakalapradaḥ \|*  
*bhuktiṃ muktimihāmutra yacchatīti suniścitam \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. It is certain that the delighted Śiva bestows everything on his devotees. He gives worldly pleasures here and salvation hereafter.

## \|\| 7.37.59 \|\|

*tasmātsevyassadā śabhbhurbhuktimuktiphalepsubhiḥ \|*  
*puruṣaśśaṅkaraḥ sākṣādduṣṭahantā satāṃgatiḥ \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. Hence Śiva shall be always served by those who desire the fruits of worldly pleasures here and salvation hereafter. Śiva, the divine Puruṣa himself, is the slayer of the wicked and the goal of the good.

## \|\| 7.37.60 \|\|

*parantu prathamaṃ śakravidyāṃ dṛḍhamanā japet \|*  
*kṣatriyasya parākhyasya cedameva samāhitam \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. But one shall repeat the lore of Indra at the outset with steady mind. For a Kṣatriya of great lustre this alone is conducive to welfare.

## \|\| 7.37.61 \|\|

*atorjunaśca prathamaṃ śakravidyāṃ japeddṛḍhaḥ \|*  
*kariṣyati parīkṣāmprāk saṃtuṣṭastadbhaviṣyati \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. Hence let Arjuna repeat the lore of Indra at first, steadily. In the beginning the lord will test him and will like to be satisfied with the test.

## \|\| 7.37.62 \|\|

*suprasannaśca vighnāni saṃhariṣyati sarvadā \|*  
*punaścaivaṃ śivasyaiva varaṃ mantraṃ pradāsyati \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. If he is delighted he will remove all obstacles. The lore will yield the granting of the boon by Śiva.

## \|\| 7.37.63 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityuktvārjunamāhūyopendravidyāmupādiśat \|*  
*snātvā ca prāṅmukho bhūtvā jagrāhārjuna ugradhīḥ \|\| 63 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

63\. After saying this he called Arjuna and instructed him in the lore of Indra. Arjuna had his ceremonial bath and facing the east, he, of powerful intellect, received the lore.

## \|\| 7.37.64 \|\|

*pārthivasya vidhānaṃ ca tasmai munivaro dadau \|*  
*pratyuvāca ca taṃ vyāso dhanaṃjayamudāradhīḥ \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. The great sage gave him the rules of procedure for a king. The liberal-minded Vyāsa thus spoke to Arjuna.

## \|\| 7.37.65 \|\|

*vyāsa uvāca \|*  
*ito gacchādhunā pārtha indrakīle suśobhane \|*  
*jāhnavyāśca samīpe vai sthitvā samyak tapaḥ kuru \|\| 65 \|\|*

Vyāsa said:—

65\. O Arjuna, start from here and go this way to the beautiful mountain Indrakīla. There standing beside the river Gaṅgā perform the penance well.

## \|\| 7.37.66 \|\|

*adṛśyā caiva vidyā syātsadā te hitakāriṇī \|*  
*ityāśiṣandadau tasmai tataḥ provāca tānmuniḥ \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. The invisible lore will be beneficial to you. He gave him the blessings thus. The sage then spoke to all.

## \|\| 7.37.67 \|\|

*dharmmamāsthāya sarvaṃ vai tiṣṭhantu nṛpasattamāḥ \|*  
*siddhiḥ syātsarvathā śreṣṭhā nātra kāryā vicāraṇā \|\| 67 \|\|*

67\. May all the excellent kings stand by virtue. The result will be excellent in every respect. No doubt need be entertained in this respect.

## \|\| 7.37.68 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*iti dattvāśiṣantebhyaḥ pāṇḍavebhyo munīśvaraḥ \|*  
*smṛtvā śivapadāmbhojaṃ vyāsaścāntardadhe kṣaṇāt \|\| 68 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

68\. Blessing the Pāṇḍavas thus and remembering the lotus-like feet of Śiva, the great sage Vyāsa vanished in a moment.

## Chapter 38 - The description of Arjuna’s penance

## \|\| 7.38.1 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*arjunopi tadā tatra dīpyamāno vyadṛśyata \|*  
*mantreṇa śivarūpeṇa tejaścātulamāvahat \|\| 1 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

1\. Arjuna then appeared very brilliant and lustrous assuming unequalled splendour by means of Śiva mantra.

## \|\| 7.38.2 \|\|

*te sarve cārjunandṛṣṭvā pāṇḍavā niścayaṃ gatāḥ \|*  
*jayo'smākaṃ dhuvañjātantejaśca vipulaṃ yataḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. On seeing Arjuna the Pāṇḍavas were convinced. “Surely success will be ours, since Arjuna has achieved an extensive splendour.

## \|\| 7.38.3 \|\|

*idaṅkāryyantvayā sādhyannānyena ca kadācana \|*  
*vyāsasya vacanādbhāti saphalaṃ kuru jīvitam \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. The task can be achieved only by you and not by anyone else. It appears so through the words of Vyāsa. Make your life fruitful.”

## \|\| 7.38.4 \|\|

*iti procyārjunante vai virahautsukyakātarāḥ \|*  
*anicchantopi tatraiva preṣayāmāsurādarāt \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. After saying this, all of them, dejected by the impending separation, reluctantly bade farewell to Arjuna.

## \|\| 7.38.5 \|\|

*draupadī duḥkhasaṃyuktā netrāśrūṇi nirudhya ca \|*  
*preṣayantī śubhaṃ vākyantadovāca pativratā \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. The grief-stricken, chaste Draupadī, controlling her tears, bade farewell to Arjuna and said.

## \|\| 7.38.6 \|\|

*draupadyuvāca \|*  
*vyāsopadiṣṭaṃ yadrājaṃstvayā kāryaṃ prayatnataḥ \|*  
*śubhaprado'stu te panthāśśaṃkaraśśaṃkarostu vai \|\| 6 \|\|*

Draupadī said:—

6\. O king, you shall strenuously act according to the instructions of Vyāsa. May your path be auspicious. May Śiva be the benefactor.

## \|\| 7.38.7 \|\|

*te sarve cāvasaṃstatra visṛjyārjunamādarāt \|*  
*atyantaduḥkhamāpannā militvā pañca eva ca \|\| 7 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

7\. After bidding farewell to Arjuna lovingly all those five, remained there, highly distressed.

## \|\| 7.38.8 \|\|

*sthitāstatra vadanti sma śrūyatāmṛṣisattama \|*  
*duḥkhepi priyasaṃgo vai na duḥkhāya prajāyate \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. O excellent sage, remaining there they spoke to one another. Listen. “Even in misery, if there is a meeting with beloved persons it lessens sorrow.

## \|\| 7.38.9 \|\|

*viyoge dviguṇantasya duḥkhambhavati nityaśaḥ \|\| \|*  
*tatra dhairyyadharasyāpi kathandhairyyambhavediha \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. In separation, misery is doubled. Even the boldest cannot retain his courage.”

## \|\| 7.38.10 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*kurvatastveva tadā duḥkhampāṇḍaveṣu munīśvaraḥ \|*  
*kṛpāsiṃdhuśca sa vyāsa ṛṣivaryyassamāgata \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. O great sage, while the Pāṇḍavas were thus in grief, the excellent sage Vyāsa, the ocean of mercy, arrived there.

## \|\| 7.38.11 \|\|

*tantadā pāṇḍavāste vai natvā sampūjya cādarāt \|*  
*dattvāsanaṃ hi duḥkhāḍhyāḥ karau baddhvā vaco'bruvan \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. The Pāṇḍavas honoured and bowed to him with respect after offering him a seat. In their misery they joined their palms in reverence and spoke.

## \|\| 7.38.12 \|\|

*pāṇḍavā ūcuḥ \|*  
*śrūyatāmṛṣabhaśreṣṭha duḥkhadagdhā vayamprabho \|*  
*darśanante'dya samprāpya hyānandaṃ prāpnumo mune \|\| 12 \|\|*

The Pāṇḍavas said:—

12\. O holy lord, O excellent sage, we are burning in grief but on seeing you we derive consolation and pleasure.

## \|\| 7.38.13 \|\|

*kiyatkālaṃ vasātraiva duḥkhanāśāya naḥ prabho \|*  
*darśanāttava viprarṣessarvaṃ duḥkhaṃ vilīyate \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. O lord, O brahmin sage, please stay here for sometime and dispel our grief. By your very sight our miseries are dispelled.

## \|\| 7.38.14 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityuktassa ṛṣiśreṣṭho nyavasattatsukhāya vai \|*  
*kathābhirvividhābhiśca tadduḥkhaṃ nodayaṃstadā \|\| 14 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

14\. Thus requested, the excellent sage stayed there for their happiness, dispelling their grief by narrating various legends.

## \|\| 7.38.15 \|\|

*vārtāyāṃ kriyamāṇāyāntena vyāsena sanmune \|*  
*supraṇamya vinītātmā dharmarājo'bravīdidam \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. O good sage, in the course of conversation pursued with Vyāsa, Yudhiṣṭhira bowed to him humbly and spoke.

## \|\| 7.38.16 \|\|

*dharmarāja uvāca \|*  
*śṛṇu tvaṃ hi ṛṣiśreṣṭha duḥkhaśāntirmatā mama \|*  
*pṛcchāmi tvāṃ mahāprājña kathanīyantvayā prabho \|\| 16 \|\|*

Yudhiṣṭhira said:—

16\. O excellent sage, listen. I seek for the alleviation of our grief. O intelligent one, I am putting this humble question. O lord, you shall kindly explain.

## \|\| 7.38.17 \|\|

*īdṛśaṃ caiva duḥkhaṃ ca purā prāptaśca kaścana \|*  
*vayameva paraṃ duḥkhaṃ prāptā vai naiva kaścana \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Has anyone formerly suffered this sort of grief? Verily we alone have had it. None else.

## \|\| 7.38.18 \|\|

*vyāsa uvāca \|*  
*rājñastu nalanāmno vai niṣadhādhipateḥ purā \|*  
*bhavadduḥkhādhikaṃ duḥkhaṃ jātantasya mahātmanaḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

Vyāsa said:—

18\. Formerly, Nala, the king of Niṣadha, the noble soul, had experienced a misery more than yours.

## \|\| 7.38.19 \|\|

*hariścandrasya nṛpaterjātaṃ duḥkhammahattaram \|*  
*akathyantadviśeṣeṇa paraśokāvahantathā \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. A great calamity and distress befell king Hariścandra. It cannot be adequately described. It makes others too distressed.

## \|\| 7.38.20 \|\|

*duḥkhamtathaiva vijñeyaṃ rāmasyāpyatha pāṇḍava \|*  
*yacchrutvā strīnarāṇāṃ ca bhavenmoho mahattaraḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. O Pāṇḍavas, even Rāma had to suffer that misery on hearing which men and women would be greatly affected.

## \|\| 7.38.21 \|\|

*tasmādvarṇayitunnaiva śakyate hi mayā punaḥ \|*  
*śarīraṃ duḥkharāśiṃ ca matvā tyājyantvayādhunā \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. Hence it is very difficult to explain this misery. The body is a heap of miseries. Considering this, you shall eschew all miseries.

## \|\| 7.38.22 \|\|

*yenedañca dhṛtantena vyāptameva na saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*prathamammātṛgarbhe vai janma duḥkhasya kāraṇam \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. By this misery alone is the body held and pervaded. There is no doubt in this. At the outset, the very birth in the mother’s womb is a cause of misery.

## \|\| 7.38.23 \|\|

*kaumāre'pi mahāduḥkhaṃ bālalīlānusāri yat \|*  
*tatopi yauvane kāmānbhunjāno duḥkharūpiṇaḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Even in infancy and childhood there is great misery in child’s sports. In youth man experiences misery even while enjoying pleasures.

## \|\| 7.38.24 \|\|

*gatāgatairdinānāṃ hi kāryabhārairanekaśaḥ \|*  
*āyuśca kṣīyate nityaṃ na jānāti ha tatpunaḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. When days come and go, when burdens of tasks are born in plenty, the life nears its end everyday. But man does not realize it.

## \|\| 7.38.25 \|\|

*ante ca maraṇaṃ caiva mahāduḥkhamataḥ param \|*  
*nānānarakapīḍāśca bhujyaṃtejñairnaraissadā \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. In the end there is death. There is greater misery thereafter. There are different sorts of hellish trouble. All these are experienced by ignorant men.

## \|\| 7.38.26 \|\|

*tasmādidamasatyaṃ ca tvantu satyaṃ samācara \|*  
*yenaiva tuṣyate śambhustathā kāryaṃ nareṇa ca \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Hence this world is untrue. You shall follow truth always. Man shall do such things as will statisfy Śiva.

## \|\| 7.38.27 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*evaṃ vividhavārtābhiḥ kālaniryāpaṇantadā \|*  
*cakruste bhrātaraḥ sarve manorathapathaiḥ punaḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

Nandīívara said:—

27\. Thus the brothers spent their time by various talks, discussions and schemes of all sorts.

## \|\| 7.38.28-30 \|\|

*arjunopi svayaṃ gacchandurgādriṣu dṛḍhavrataḥ \|*  
*yakṣaṃ labdhvā ca tenaiva dasyūnnighnannanekaśaḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

*manasā harṣasaṃyukto jagāmācalamuttamam \|*  
*tatra gatvā ca gaṃgāyāssamīpaṃ sundaraṃ sthalam \|\| 29 \|\|*

*aśokakānanaṃ yatra tiṣṭhati svarga uttamaḥ \|*  
*tatra tasthau svayaṃ snātvā natvā ca gurumuttamam \|\| 30 \|\|*

28-30. Arjuna went over impassable mountains for the practice of his steady rites. He met a Yakṣa on the way and through him he slew many robbers. He was delighted in his mind and reached the excellent mountain. Near Gaṅgā he saw a beautiful spot with Aśoka groves. It was excellent like the heaven. He halted there, took his bath and bowed to his venerable preceptor.

## \|\| 7.38.31 \|\|

*yathopadiṣṭaṃ veṣādi tathā caivākarotsvayam \|*  
*indriyāṇyapakṛṣyādau manasā saṃsthito'bhavat \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. He assumed dress and features according to his instructions. He curbed his sense organs and the mind and stood steady.

## \|\| 7.38.32 \|\|

*punaśca pārthivaṃ kṛtvā sundaraṃ samasūtrakam \|*  
*tadagre praṇidadhyau sa tejorāśimanuttamam \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. He made a clay idol of Śiva even and beautiful. In front of it he stood in meditation on the excellent heap of splendour.

## \|\| 7.38.33 \|\|

*trikālaṃ caiva susnātaḥ pūjanaṃ vividhaṃ tadā \|*  
*cakāropāsanantatra harasya ca punaḥ punaḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. He took baths thrice a day. He performed various sorts of worship. He performed the Upāsti form of worship of Śiva again and again.

## \|\| 7.38.34-35 \|\|

*tasyaiva śirasastejo nissṛtantaccarāstadā \|*  
*dṛṣṭvā bhayaṃ samāpannāḥ praviṣṭaśca kadā hyayam \|\| 34 \|\|*

*punaste ca vicāryaivaṃ kathanīyaṃ biḍaujase \|*  
*ityuktvā tu gatāste vai śakrasyāntikamañjasā \|\| 35 \|\|*

34-35. Indra’s spies saw the splendour-column coming out of his head. They were frightened by it. They thought within themselves—“When did he come in? Indra must be informed.” Saying so they went to Indra.

## \|\| 7.38.36 \|\|

*carā ūcuḥ \|*  
*devo vā'tha ṛṣiścaiva sūryo vātha vibhāvasuḥ \|*  
*tapaścarati deveśa na jānīmo vane ca tam \|\| 36 \|\|*

Indra’s spies said:—

36\. O lord of gods, we do not know who it is. Some one is performing a penance in the forest, a god, a sage, the sun or the fire.

## \|\| 7.38.37 \|\|

*tasyaiva tejasā dagdhā āgatāstava sannidhau \|*  
*niveditañcaritraṃ tatkriyatāmucitantu yat \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. We are scorched by his splendour and have come near you. We have told you the details. Please do what is proper.

## \|\| 7.38.38 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityuktastaiścaraissarvaṃ jñātvā putracikīrṣitam \|*  
*sagotrapānvisṛjyaiva tatra gantuṃ mano dadhe \|\| 38 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

38\. Thus informed by the spies, Indra, the enemy of mountains, understood whatever his son Arjuna desired to do. He dismissed them and decided to go there to test him.

## \|\| 7.38.39 \|\|

*sa vṛddhabrāhmaṇo bhūtvā brahmacārī śacīpatiḥ \|*  
*jagāma tatra viprendra parīkṣārthaṃ hi tasya vai \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. O leading brahmin, Indra, the lord of Śacī, assumed the form of an elderly brahmin bachelor and went there to test him.

## \|\| 7.38.40 \|\|

*tamāgatantadā dṛṣṭvākārṣītpūjāśca pāṇḍavaḥ \|*  
*sthitogre ca stutiṃ kṛtvā kvāyātosi vadādhunā \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. On seeing him come, Arjuna worshipped him. After eulogising him he stood in front of him and said—“Whence do you come now? Please tell me”

## \|\| 7.38.41 \|\|

*ityuktastena deveśo dhairyyārthantasya prītitaḥ \|*  
*parīkṣāgarvitaṃ vākyaṃ pāṇḍavantaṃ tato'bravīt \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. The lord of gods thus requested by him spoke with a hint at testing his courage, out of love.

## \|\| 7.38.42 \|\|

*brāhmaṇa uvāca \|*  
*nave vayasi vai tāta kintapasyasi sāmpratam \|*  
*muktyarthaṃ vā jayārthaṃ kiṃ sarvathaitattapastava \|\| 42 \|\|*

The brahmin said:—

42\. O dear, why do you perform the penance in your prime of youth? Is it for salvation or for victory? In every respect this penance of yours is useless.

## \|\| 7.38.43 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*iti pṛṣṭastadā tena sarvaṃ saṃveditampunaḥ \|*  
*tacchrutvā sa punarvākyamuvāca brāhmaṇastadā \|\| 43 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

43\. Thus questioned by him he informed him of everything. On hearing that the brahmin spoke again.

## \|\| 7.38.44 \|\|

*brāhmaṇa uvāca \|*  
*yuktaṃ na kriyate vīra sukhaṃ prāptuṃ ca yattapaḥ \|*  
*kṣātradharmeṇa kriyate muktyarthaṃ kurusattama \|\| 44 \|\|*

The brahmin said:—

44\. This is not proper on your part to pursue this penance for achieving happiness or for salvation by means of Kṣatraic rites, O excellent scion of the Kurus.

## \|\| 7.38.45 \|\|

*indrastu sukhadātā vai muktidātā bhavenna hi \|*  
*tasmāttvaṃ sarvathā śreṣṭha kartumarhasi sattapaḥ \|*  
*nandīśvara uvāca \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. Indra is the bestower of happiness and not of salvation. O excellent one, it behoves you therefore to perform a more excellent penance.

## \|\| 7.38.46 \|\|

*idantadvacanaṃ śrutvā krodhaṃ cakre'rjunastadā \|*  
*pratyuvāca vinītātmā tadanādṛtya suvrataḥ \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. On hearing his words Arjuna was angry. Slighting it but humbly, Arjuna of good rites replied.

## \|\| 7.38.47 \|\|

*arjuna uvāca \|*  
*rājyārthaṃ na ca muktyartha kimarthaṃ bhāṣase tvidam \|*  
*vyāsasya vacanenaiva kriyate tapa īdṛśam \|\| 47 \|\|*

Arjuna said:—

47\. It is not for kingdom nor for salvation that this penance is resorted to. Why do you speak like this? I am performing this penance on the advice of Vyāsa.

## \|\| 7.38.48 \|\|

*ito gaccha brahmacārinmā pātayitumicchasi \|*  
*prayojanaṃ kimatrāsti tava vai brahmacāriṇaḥ \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. O bachelor, go hence. Do you wish to cause my downfall? Of what use is this to you a bachelor?

## \|\| 7.38.49 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityuktaḥ sa prasannobhūtsundaraṃ rūpamadbhutam \|*  
*svopaskaraṇasaṃyuktaṃ darśayāmāsa vai nijam \|\| 49 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

49\. On being addressed thus he was delighted. He revealed his wonderfully beautiful form with all paraphernalia to him.

## \|\| 7.38.50 \|\|

*śakrarūpantadā dṛṣṭvā lajjitaścārjunastadā \|*  
*sa indrastaṃ samāśvāsya punareva vacobravīt \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. On seeing the form of Indra, Arjuna was ashamed. Indra spoke to him again after consoling him.

## \|\| 7.38.51 \|\|

*iṃdra uvāca \|*  
*varaṃ vṛṇīṣva he tāta dhanaṃjaya mahāmate \|*  
*yadicchasi manobhīṣṭannādeyaṃ vidyate tava \|\| 51 \|\|*

Indra said:—

51\. “O dear, O Arjuna of great intellect, choose your boon, whatever you wish in your mind. There is nothing which cannot be given to you.”

## \|\| 7.38.52 \|\|

*tacchrutvā śakravacanampratyuvācārjunastadā \|*  
*vijayandehi me tāta śatrukliṣṭasya sarvathā \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. On hearing the words of Indra, Arjuna replied “O father, bless me with victory. I am afflicted by enemies in every respect.”

## \|\| 7.38.53 \|\|

*śakra uvāca \|*  
*baliṣṭhāśśatravaste ca duryodhanapuraḥsarāḥ \|*  
*droṇo bhīmaśca karṇaśca sarve te durjayā dhuvam \|\| 53 \|\|*

Indra said:—

53\. Your enemies, Duryodhana and others are very powerful. Droṇa, Bhīṣma, Karṇa and others are certainly invincible.

## \|\| 7.38.54 \|\|

*aśvatthāmā droṇaputro raudroṃśo durjayo'ti saḥ \|*  
*mayā sādhyā bhaveyuste sarvathā svahitaṃ śṛṇu \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. Aśvatthāman, son of Droṇa and a part of Rudra, is extremely invincible. But they can be withstood through me. Listen to what is wholesome to you.

## \|\| 7.38.55 \|\|

*etadvīra japaṃ kartuṃ na śaktaḥ kaścanādhunā \|*  
*vartate hi śivovaryastasmācchambhorjayo' dhunā \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. O hero, none is capable of performing this Japa. Śiva is the greatest of all. Hence perform the Japa of Śiva now.

## \|\| 7.38.56 \|\|

*śaṃkaraḥ sarvalokeśaścarācarapatiḥ svarāṭ \|*  
*sarvaṃ kartuṃ samarthosti bhuktimuktiphalapradaḥ \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. Śiva is the lord of worlds, the mobile and immobile beings. He is the lord of heaven. He can do everything. He is the bestower of worldly pleasures and salvation.

## \|\| 7.38.57 \|\|

*ahaṃ manye ca brahmādyā viṣṇuḥ sarvavarapradaḥ \|*  
*anye jigīṣavo ye ca te sarve śivapūjakāḥ \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. I, Brahmā and others, Viṣṇu the bestower of boons and those who crave for victory are the devotees of Śiva.

## \|\| 7.38.58 \|\|

*adyaprabhṛti tanmantraṃ hitvā bhaktyā śivaṃ bhaja \|*  
*pārthivena vidhānena dhyānenaiva śivasya ca \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. Now onwards leave that mantra and worship Śiva with devotion through the rules laid down for the worship of his day phallic image and by contemplating on him.

## \|\| 7.38.59 \|\|

*upacārairanekaiśca sarvabhāvena bhārata \|*  
*siddhiḥ syādacalā tedya nātra kāryā vicāraṇā \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. O Bhārata, by your devotional feelings and modes of service, you worship Śiva. Your achievement will be unshakable. No doubt need be entertained in this respect.

## \|\| 7.38.60 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityuktvā ca carānsarvānsamāhūyābravīdidam \|*  
*sāvadhānena vai stheyametatsaṃrakṣaṇe sadā \|\| 60 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

60\. After saying this he called out his spies and said—“All of you shall be cautious and attentive in protecting him always.”

## \|\| 7.38.61 \|\|

*prabodhya svacarānindro'rjunasaṃrakṣaṇādikam \|*  
*vātsalyapūrṇahṛdayaḥ punarūce kapidhvajam \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. Thus alerting his spies for the protection to be accorded to Arjuna, Indra spoke again to Arjuna with his heart full of fondness and affection.

## \|\| 7.38.62 \|\|

*indra uvāca \|*  
*rājyaṃ tvayā pramādādvai na kartavyaṃ kadācana \|*  
*śreyase bhadra vidyeyaṃ bhavettava parantapa \|\| 62 \|\|*

Indra said:—

62\. O Gentle one, O terrifier of enemies, you shall not commit blunders in the administration of your kingdom. This mantra will be conducive to your glory.

## \|\| 7.38.63 \|\|

*dhairyaṃ dhārya sādhakena sarvathā rakṣakaḥ śivaḥ \|*  
*saṃpattīśca phalantulyaṃ dāsyate nātra saṃśayaḥ \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. Always courage shall be maintained by an aspirant. Śiva the protector will give you riches and the fruits of your desire. This is undoubtedly true.

## \|\| 7.38.64 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*iti dattvā varantasya bhāratasya sureśvaraḥ \|*  
*smarañchivapadāmbhojañjagāma bhavanaṃ svakam \|\| 64 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

64\. After giving the boon to Arjuna, the lord of gods remembered the lotus-like feet of Śiva and went to his abode.

## \|\| 7.38.65 \|\|

*arjunopi mahāvīrassupraṇamya sureśvaram \|*  
*tapastepe saṃyatātmā śivamuddiśya tadvidham \|\| 65 \|\|*

65\. The heroic Arjuna bowing to the lord of gods, performed the penance with controlled mind with Śiva as his goal.

## Chapter 39 - The demon Mūka is killed in the context of the incarnation of Kirāta

## \|\| 7.39.1 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*snānaṃ sa vidhivatkṛtvā nyāsādi vidhivattathā \|*  
*dhyānaṃ śivasya sadbhaktyā vyāsoktaṃ yattathā'karot \|\| 1 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

1\. He performed the ablution duly, and the Nyāsa (the mystic placing of fingers etc.) in accordance with the Śāstric injunctions. He performed the meditation of Śiva with great devotion in the manner explained by Vyāsa.

## \|\| 7.39.2 \|\|

*ekapādatalenaiva tiṣṭhanmunivaro yathā \|*  
*sūryye dṛṣṭiṃ nibadhyaikāṃ maṃtramāvartayansthitaḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Like a great sage, he stood on the sole of a single foot. He fixed one of his eyes at the sun and stood repeating the mantra.

## \|\| 7.39.3 \|\|

*tapastepeti saṃprītyā saṃsmaranmanasā śivam \|*  
*paṃcākṣaraṃ manuṃ śaṃbhorjapansarvo ttamottamam \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Repeating the five-syllabled mantra of Śiva the most excellent of all the mantras and mentally remembering Śiva with pleasure he performed the penance.

## \|\| 7.39.4 \|\|

*tapasasteja evāsīdyathā devā visismiyuḥ \|*  
*punaścaiva śivaṃ yātāḥ pratyūcuste samāhitāḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. The splendour of the penance was such that the gods were surprised. They went to Śiva again and told him sincerely.

## \|\| 7.39.5 \|\|

*devā ūcuḥ \|*  
*nareṇaikena sarveśa tvadarthe tapa āhitam \|*  
*yadicchati narassoyaṃ kinna yacchati tatprabho \|\| 5 \|\|*

The gods said:—

5\. O lord of all, a penance is being performed by a man for your propiation. O lord, why don’t you grant him what he desires.

## \|\| 7.39.6 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityuktvā tu stutiṃ cakurvividhānte tadā surāḥ \|*  
*tatpādayordṛśaḥ kṛtvā tatra tasthuḥ sthirādhayaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

6\. After saying this the gods eulogised him in different ways. Fixing their eyes to his feet they stood there in agony.

## \|\| 7.39.7 \|\|

*śivastu tadvacaḥ śrutvā mahāprabhurudāraghīḥ \|*  
*suvihasya prasannātmā surānvacanamabravīt \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. On hearing their words the lord of benevolent mind, the delighted Śiva laughingly spoke thus to the gods,

## \|\| 7.39.8 \|\|

*śiva uvāca \|*  
*svasthānaṃ gacchata surāḥ sarve satyanna saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*sarvathāhaṃ kariṣyāmi kāryaṃ vo nātra saṃśaya \|\| 8 \|\|*

Śiva said:—

8\. O gods, you go to your abodes. Undoubtedly I shall carry out your task in every respect.

## \|\| 7.39.9 \|\|

*naṃdīśvara uvāca \|*  
*tacchrutvā śaṃbhuvacananniścayaṃ paramaṃ gatāḥ \|*  
*parāvṛtya gatāḥ sarve svasvathānaṃ te hi nirjarāḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

9\. On hearing those words of Śīva, they became assured. They therefore returned to their abodes.

## \|\| 7.39.10-13 \|\|

*etasminnaṃtare daityo mūkanāmāgatastadā \|*  
*saukaraṃ rūpamāsthāya preṣitaśca durātmanā \|\| 10 \|\|*

*duryodhanena vipreṃdra māyinā cārjunaṃ tadā \|*  
*yatrārjunasthitaścāsīttena mārgeṇa vai tadā \|\| 11 \|\|*

*śṛṅgāṇi parvatasyaiva cchindanvṛkṣānanekaśaḥ \|*  
*śabdaṃ ca vividhaṃ kurva nnativegena saṃyutaḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

*ajunopi ca taṃ dṛṣṭvā mūkanāmāsuraṃ tadā \|*  
*smṛtvā śivapadāṃbhojaṃ vicāre tatparo'bhavat \|\| 13 \|\|*

10-13. In the meantime there came the demon Mūka, instigated by the wicked Duryodhana, wielding Māyā against Arjuna. O great brahmin, he had assumed the form of a boar. He came along the path where Arjuna was staying. He was digging up the peaks of mountains and trees in plenty. He was roaring and grunting in diverse ways and was rushing at a great speed. On seeing the demon Mūka, Arjuna remembered the lotus-like feet of Śiva and began to think.

## \|\| 7.39.14 \|\|

*arjuna uvāca \|*  
*koyaṃ vā kuta āyāti krūrakarmā ca dṛśyate \|*  
*mamāniṣṭaṃ dhruvaṃ kartuṃ samāgacchatyasaṃśayam \|\| 14 \|\|*

Arjuna said:—

14\. Who is this? Where does he come from? He seems to be very ruthless in actions. Certainly he is coming here to harm me.

## \|\| 7.39.15 \|\|

*mamaivaṃ mana āyāti śatrureva na saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*mayā vinihatāḥ pūrvamaneke daityadānavāḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. It occurs to me that he is my enemy, to be sure. Formerly many Daityas and Dānavas had been killed by me.

## \|\| 7.39.16 \|\|

*tadīyaḥ kaścidāyāti vairaṃ sādhayitumpunaḥ \|*  
*athavā ca sakhā kaścidduryodhanahitāvahaḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. He may be one of those come here to wreak vengeance. Or he may be some friend of Duryodhana acting for his welfare.

## \|\| 7.39.17 \|\|

*yasmindṛṣṭe prasīdetsvaṃ manaḥ sa hitakṛddhruvam \|*  
*yasmindṛṣṭe tadeva syādākulaṃ śatrureva saḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Verily he is a benefactor on seeing whom the mind becomes pleased. If on seeing anyone the mind is agitated he is surely an enemy.

## \|\| 7.39.18 \|\|

*ācāraḥ kulamākhyāti vapurākhyāti bhojanam \|*  
*vacanaṃ śrutamākhyāti snehamākhyāti locanam \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. The conduct declares the family, the body declares the diet, the mode of speech declares the amount of learning and the eye indicates the affection.

## \|\| 7.39.19 \|\|

*ākāreṇa tathā gatyā ceṣṭayā bhāṣitairapi \|*  
*netravaktravikārābhyāṃ jñāyate'ntarhitaṃ manaḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. The concealed mind is revealed by the general mien, the mode of walking, the activities, the speech, the features of the eyes and the face.

## \|\| 7.39.20 \|\|

*ujjvalaṃ sarasañcaiva vakramārakta kantathā \|*  
*netraṃ caturvidhaṃ proktaṃ tasya bhāvaṃ pṛthagbudhāḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. The eye is of four sorts—the resplendent, the impassioned the squinted and the ruddy. The features are likewise different, O scholars.

## \|\| 7.39.21 \|\|

*ujjvalaṃ mitrasaṃyoge sarasamputradarśane \|*  
*vakraṃ ca kāminīyoge āraktaṃ śatrudarśane \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. It is resplendent, when friends meet. It is impassioned at the sight of the son. It is squinted in contact with the beloved and it is ruddy at the sight of the enemy.

## \|\| 7.39.22 \|\|

*asminmama tu sarvāṇi kaluṣānīndriyāṇi ca \|*  
*ayaṃ śatrurbhavedeva māraṇīyo na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. In regard to him my sense-organs are excited. He must be an enemy. Undoubtedly he shall be killed.

## \|\| 7.39.23 \|\|

*gurośca vacanaṃ medya vartate duḥkhadastvayā \|*  
*hantavyaḥ sarvathā rājannātra kāryā vicāraṇā \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. I remember the words of my preceptor now—“O king, a person causing misery shall be killed by you by all means. No doubt need be entertained in this respect.

## \|\| 7.39.24 \|\|

*etadarthaṃ tvāyudhāni mama caiva na saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*vicāryyeti ca tatraiva bāṇaṃ saṃsthāya saṃsthitaḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. It is only for this purpose that I hold the weapons. There is no doubt in this.” After thinking thus he placed the arrow ready there and stood up.

## \|\| 7.39.25-26 \|\|

*etasminnantare tatra rakṣārthaṃ hyarjunasya vai \|*  
*tadbhakteśca parīkṣārthaṃ śaṃkaro bhaktavatsalaḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

*vidagdhabhillarūpaṃ hi gaṇaiḥ sārdha mahādbhutam \|*  
*tasya daityasya nāśārthaṃ drutaṃ kṛtvā samāgataḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

25-26. In the meantime, Śiva, favourably disposed to his devotees, assumed the wonderful form of an efficient hunter and came there along with his Gaṇas in order to slay that Daitya, to protect Arjuna as well as to test his devotion.

## \|\| 7.39.27 \|\|

*baddhakacchaśca vastrībhirvaddheśānadhvajastadā \|*  
*śarīre śvetarekhāśca dhanurbāṇayutaḥ svayam \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. The hunter had tucked up his lion-cloth and folded up Śiva’s banner. His body was lined with the white ash. He held a bow and arrows.

## \|\| 7.39.28 \|\|

*bāṇānāntūṇakaṃ pṛṣṭhe dhṛtvā vai sa jagāma ha \|*  
*gaṇaścaiva tathā jāto bhillarājo'bhavacchivaḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. He carried the quiver suspended over his back. The Gaṇas too assumed the form of hunters. Śiva too became the chief of Bhillas.

## \|\| 7.39.29 \|\|

*śabdāṃśca vividhānkṛtvā niryayau vāhinīpatiḥ \|*  
*sūkarasya sasārātha śabdaśca pradiśo daśa \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. The Commander-in-Chief started various sounds. The grunting sound of the boar too spread over the quarters.

## \|\| 7.39.30 \|\|

*vanecareṇa śabdena vyākulaścārjunastadā \|*  
*parvatādyāśca taiśśabdaiste sarve vyākulāstadā \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. Arjuna was excited by the sound that spread in the forest. Even the mountains were agitated by these sounds.

## \|\| 7.39.31 \|\|

*aho kinnu bhavedeṣa śivaḥ śubhakarastviha \|*  
*mayā caiva śrutampūrvaṃ kṛṣṇena kathitampunaḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. “Oh, can this be Śiva the benefactor? I have heard about this as mentioned by Kṛṣṇa.

## \|\| 7.39.32 \|\|

*vyāsena kathitaṃ caivaṃ smṛtvā devai stathā punaḥ \|*  
*śivaḥ śubhakaraḥ proktaḥ śivaḥ sukhakarastathā \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. Vyāsa too has mentioned and the gods too. Śiva is mentioned as the benefactor. Śiva is the cause of happiness.

## \|\| 7.39.33 \|\|

*muktidaśca svayaṃ prokto muktidānānna saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*tannāmasmaraṇātpuṃsāṃ kalyāṇaṃ jāyate dhuvam \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. He is called “the bestower of salvation” because he bestows salvation upon his devotees. Undoubtedly welfare befalls those who remember his names.

## \|\| 7.39.34 \|\|

*bhajatāṃ sarvabhāvena duḥkhaṃ svapne'pi no bhavet \|*  
*yadā kadācijjāyeta tadā karmasamudbhavam \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. Even in dreams, misery does not befall those who worship him with devotion. If it happens at all, it is due to their previous activities.

## \|\| 7.39.35 \|\|

*tadetadbahvapi jñeyaṃ nūnamalpaṃ na saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*prārabdhasyātha vā doṣo nūnaṃ jñeyo viśeṣataḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. That must happen whether big or small. The fault is particularly that of the action that has begun to fructify.

## \|\| 7.39.36 \|\|

*atha vā bahu cālpaṃ hi bhogyaṃ nistīrya śaṃkaraḥ \|*  
*kadācidicchayā tasya dūrīkuryyānna saṃśayaḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. Or Śiva may dispel the misery by his will, after one has experienced the fruits of one’s action big or small.

## \|\| 7.39.37 \|\|

*viṣaṃ caivāmṛtaṃ kuryādamṛtaṃ viṣameva vā \|*  
*yadicchati karotyeva samarthaḥ kinniṣidhyate \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. He can convert poison into nectar and nectar into poison. Whatever he wishes he certainly does. How can an efficient man be thwarted?

## \|\| 7.39.38-40 \|\|

*itthaṃ vicāryyamāṇe'pi bhaktairanyaiḥ purātanaiḥ \|*  
*bhāvibhiśca sadā bhaktairihānīya manaḥ sthiram \|\| 38 \|\|*

*lakṣmīrgaccheccāvatiṣṭhenmaraṇaṃ nikaṭe puraḥ \|*  
*nindāṃ vātha prakurvantu stutiṃ vā duḥkhasaṃkṣayam \|\| 39 \|\|*

*jayate puṇyapāpābhyāṃ śaṃkarassukhadaḥ sadā \|*  
*kadācicca parīkṣārthaṃ duḥkhaṃ yacchati vai śivaḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

38-40. Devotees of yore and future devotees must keep their mind steady after considering all those aspects, whether one is in affluent circumstances or not, whether death is imminent or not or whether people praise or censure. Misery is quelled. Śiva is always the bestower of happiness through the merit and sin of individuals. Sometimes Śiva makes us miserable in order to test us.

## \|\| 7.39.41 \|\|

*aṃte ca sukhadaḥ prokto dayālutvānna saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*yathā caiva suvarṇaṃ ca śodhi taṃ śuddhatāṃ vrajet \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. Since he is compassionate he is said to be the bestower of happiness ultimately. A gold piece can become pure only when it is put into the fire and purified.

## \|\| 7.39.42 \|\|

*evaṃ caivaṃ mayā pūrvaṃ śrutaṃ munimukhāttathā \|*  
*atastadbhajanenaiva lapsye'haṃ sukhamuttamam \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. This has been heard by me from the Sage. Hence I shall attain blessed happiness through his worship alone.”

## \|\| 7.39.43 \|\|

*ityevantu vicāraṃ sa karoti yāvadeva hi \|*  
*tāvacca sūkaraḥ prāpto bāṇasaṃmocanāvadhiḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. While he was musing thus, the boar came within the range of his arrow-discharge.

## \|\| 7.39.44-45 \|\|

*śivopi pṛṣṭhato lagno hyāyātaḥ śūkarasya hi \|*  
*tayormadhye tadā soyaṃ dṛśyate śṛṃgamadbhutam \|\| 44 \|\|*

*tasya proktaṃ ca māhātmyaṃ śivaḥ śīghrataraṃ gataḥ \|*  
*arjunasya ca rakṣārthaṃ śaṃkaro bhaktava tsalaḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

44-45. In order to protect Arjuna, Śiva, favourably disposed towards his devotees, came there closely pursuing the boar. Between the two the animal rushed on and then halted. A wonderful horn was seen.

## \|\| 7.39.46 \|\|

*etasminsamaye tābhyāṃ kṛtaṃ bāṇavimocanam \|*  
*śivabāṇastu pucche vai hyarjunasya mukhe tathā \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. Then both of them discharged the arrow. Śiva’s arrow hit the tail, Arjuna’s arrow hit the snout.

## \|\| 7.39.47-48 \|\|

*śivasya pucchato gatvā mukhānnissṛtya śīghrataḥ \|*  
*bhūmau vilīnaḥ saṃyātastasya vai pucchato gataḥ \|\| 47 \|\|*

*papāta pārśvataścaiva bāṇaścaivārjunasya ca \|*  
*sūkarastatkṣaṇaṃ daityo mṛto bhūmau papāta ha \|\| 48 \|\|*

47-48. Śiva’s arrow hit the tail and stopped at the snout. It struck the ground and entered it. Arjuna’s arrow pierced through the body. It came out of the tail and fell at its side. The boar who was a Daitya fell dead on the ground.

## \|\| 7.39.49 \|\|

*devā harṣaṃ paramprāpuḥ puṣpavṛṣṭiṃ ca cakrire \|*  
*jayapūrva stutikarāḥ praṇamya ca punaḥpunaḥ \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. The gods were much delighted. They showered flowers. Bowing down and eulogising they cried shouts of victory.

## \|\| 7.39.50 \|\|

*śivastuṣṭamanā āsīdarjunaḥ sukhamāgataḥ \|*  
*daityasya ca tadā dṛṣṭvā krararūpaṃ ca tau tadā \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. On seeing the cruel form of the Daitya, Śiva was glad and Arjuna was happy.

## \|\| 7.39.51-52 \|\|

*arjunastu viśeṣeṇa sukhinā prāha cetasā \|*  
*aho daityavaraścāyaṃ rūpaṃ tu paramādbhutam \|\| 51 \|\|*

*kṛtvāgato madvadhārthaiṃ śivenāhaṃ surakṣitaḥ \|*  
*īśvareṇa mamādyaiva buddhirdattā na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 52 \|\|*

51-52. With an excessively happy mind Arjuna said—“O this is a huge Daitya. He had assumed this wonderful form and had come here to kill me. But I have been saved by śiva. Lord Śiva has given me keen intelligence. There is no doubt in this.”

## \|\| 7.39.53 \|\|

*vicāryyetyarjunastatra jagau śivaśiveti ca \|*  
*praṇanāma śivaṃ bhūyastuṣṭāva ca punaḥ punaḥ \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. After thinking thus Arjuna repeated Śiva’s name. He bowed to Śiva and eulogised him again and again.

## Chapter 40 - The Kirāta-Arjuna dialogue

## \|\| 7.40.1 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*sanatkumāra sarvajña śṛṇu līlāmparātmanaḥ \|*  
*bhaktavātsalyasaṃyuktāṃ taddṛḍhatvavidarbhitām \|\| 1 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

1\. O omniscient Sanatkumāra, listen to the sport of the great soul Śiva full of affection for the devotee and to the test of its stability.

## \|\| 7.40.2 \|\|

*śivopyatha svabhṛtyaṃ vai preṣayāmāsa sa drutam \|*  
*bāṇārthe ca tadā tatrārjunopi samagāttataḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Śiva immediately sent his attendant to fetch the arrow. The Arjuna too came there.

## \|\| 7.40.3 \|\|

*ekasmin samaye prāptau bāṇārthaṃ tadgaṇārjunau \|*  
*arjunastaṃ parābhartsya svabāṇaṃ cāgrahīttadā \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. The Gaṇa and Arjuna reached the place simultaneously for the arrow. Arjuna rebuked him and took up his arrow.

## \|\| 7.40.4 \|\|

*gaṇa provāca taṃ tatra kimarthaṃ gṛhyate śaraḥ \|*  
*bāṇaścaivāsmadīyo vai mucyatāṃ ṛṣisattama \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. The Gaṇa said—“O excellent sage, why do you take this arrow? The arrow is mine. Leave it off.”

## \|\| 7.40.5 \|\|

*ityuktastena bhillasya gaṇena munisattamaḥ \|*  
*sorjunaḥ śaṃkaraṃ smṛtvā vacanaṃ ca tamabravīt \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. O excellent sage, thus addressed by the Gaṇa of the Bhilla, Arjuna remembered Śiva and spoke.

## \|\| 7.40.6 \|\|

*arjuna uvāca \|*  
*ajñātvā kiṃca vadasi mūrkhosi tvaṃ vanecara \|*  
*bāṇaśca mocito me'dya tvadīyaśca kathaṃ punaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

Arjuna said:—

6\. O forest-roamer, what do you say without understanding? You are a fool. This arrow had been discharged by me just now. How can it be yours?

## \|\| 7.40.7 \|\|

*rekhārūpaṃ ca picchāni mannāmāṃkita eva ca \|*  
*tvadīyaśca kathaṃ jātaḥ svabhāvo dustyajastava \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. It is engraved with my name in many lines. The feathers are mine. How does it become yours? You insist according to your wild nature.

## \|\| 7.40.8 \|\|
