*\|\| nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityevantadvacaḥ śrutvā vihasya sa gaṇeśvaraḥ \|*  
*arjunaṃ ṛṣirūpaṃ taṃ bhillo vākyamupādade \|\| 8 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

8\. On hearing his words, the leader of the Gaṇas laughed and spoke to Arjuna who was in the garb of a sage.

## \|\| 7.40.9 \|\|

*tāpasa śrūyatāṃ re tvaṃ na tapaḥ kriyate tvayā \|*  
*veṣataśca tapasvī tvaṃ na yathārthaṃ chalāyate \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. O sage, listen. You are a sage in disguise. You are not a true sage. You do not perform penance. People are being deceived by you.

## \|\| 7.40.10 \|\|

*tapasvī ca kathaṃ mithyā bhāṣate kurute naraḥ \|*  
*naikākinaṃ ca māṃ tvaṃ ca jānīhi vāhinīpatim \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. How can a saint utter a lie? Do not take me to be single-handed. Know me to be the commander-in-chief.

## \|\| 7.40.11 \|\|

*bahubhirvanabhillaiśca yuktaḥ svāmī sa āsata \|*  
*samarthassarvathā kartuṃ vigrahānugrahau punaḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. My master is sitting there with many hunters. In every respect, he is capable of destroying or blessing you.

## \|\| 7.40.12 \|\|

*vartate tasya vāṇīyaṃ yo nītaśca tvayādhunā \|*  
*ayaṃ bāṇaśca te pārśve na sthāsyati kadācana \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. It is his arrow that has been taken by you now. This arrow will never remain with you.

## \|\| 7.40.13-14 \|\|

*tapaḥphalaṃ kathaṃ tvaṃ ca hātumicchasi tāpasa \|*  
*cauryyācchalārdyamānācca vismayātsatya bhañjanāt \|\| 13 \|\|*

*tapasā kṣīyate satyametadeva mayā śrutam \|*  
*tasmācca tapasastedya bhaviṣyati phalaṃ kutaḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

13-14. O sage, why do you wish to lose the fruit of your penance? Truly have I heard that penance is marred and wasted by theft, pride afflicted by deceit and the breaking of promise. Hence O sage, whence will you derive the fruit of penance?

## \|\| 7.40.15 \|\|

*tasmācca mucyate bāṇātkṛtaghnastvaṃ bhaviṣyasi \|*  
*mamaiva svāmino bāṇastavārthe mocito dhruvam \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. If you do not give up this arrow you will be an ungrateful fellow. It was certainly to save you that my master discharged this arrow.

## \|\| 7.40.16 \|\|

*śatruśca māritastena punarbāṇaśca rakṣitaḥ \|*  
*atyantaṃ ca kṛtaghnosi tapośubhakarastathā \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Your enemy has been killed by him and you have kept the arrow to yourself. Very ungrateful indeed are you. You are making your own penance fruitless.

## \|\| 7.40.17 \|\|

*satyaṃ na bhāṣase tvaṃ ca kimataḥ siddhimicchasi \|*  
*prayojanaṃ cedbāṇena svāmī ca yācyatāṃ mama \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. You do not speak the truth. What you wish to gain thereby? If you have any need for the arrow, you may approach my master and request him.

## \|\| 7.40.18 \|\|

*īdṛśāṃśca bahūnbāṇāṃstadā dātuṃ kṣamaḥ svayam \|*  
*rājā ca vartate me'dya kiṃ tvevaṃ yācyate tvayā \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. He can give you many similar arrows. My master is here. Why don’t you request him?

## \|\| 7.40.19 \|\|

*upakāraṃ parityajya hyapakāraṃ samīhase \|*  
*naitadyuktaṃ tvayādyaiva kriyate tyaja cāpalam \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. Unmindful of his help to you, you wish him harm. This does not behove you. Eschew this fickleness;

## \|\| 7.40.20 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityevaṃ vacanantasya śrutvā pārthorjunastadā \|*  
*krodhaṃ kṛtvā śivaṃ smṛtvā mitaṃ vākyamathābravīt \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. On hearing his words, Arjuna, son of Pṛthā, was angry. After remembering Śiva, he spoke these measured words.

## \|\| 7.40.21 \|\|

*\|\| arjuna uvāca \|*  
*śṛṇu bhilla pravakṣyāmi na satyaṃ tava bhāṣaṇam \|*  
*yathā jātistathā tvāṃ ca jānāmi hi vanecara \|\| 21 \|\|*

Arjuna said:—

21\. O hunter, listen. I shall explain. Your words are not true. O forest-wanderer, I know you. You are true to the caste in which you are born.

## \|\| 7.40.22 \|\|

*ahaṃ rājā bhavāṃścauraḥ kathaṃ yuddhaprayuktatā \|*  
*yuddhaṃ me sabalaiḥ kāryaṃ nādhamairhi kadācana \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. I am a king. You are a thief. How can a war be pursued? I must fight with people equal in strength to me, never with base people.

## \|\| 7.40.23 \|\|

*tasmātte ca tathā svāmī bhaviṣyati bhavādṛśaḥ \|*  
*dātāraśca vayaṃ proktāścaurā yūyaṃ vanecarāḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Your master will be only like you. We are the benevolent donors and you the forest-dwellers, thieves.

## \|\| 7.40.24 \|\|

*kathaṃ yācyo mayā bhillarāja evaṃ ca sāmpratam \|*  
*tvameva yācase naiva bāṇaṃ māṃ kiṃ vanecaraḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. How can the king of foresters be requested by me now? O forest-wanderer, why don’t you beg the arrow of me?

## \|\| 7.40.25 \|\|

*dadāmi te tathā bāṇānsanti me bahavo dhruvam \|*  
*rājā ca grahaṇaṃ caiva na dāsyati tathā bhavet \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. I shall readily give you the arrows. Certainly I have many. If a king docs not give what is taken, your objection may come up.

## \|\| 7.40.26 \|\|

*kimpunaśca tathā bāṇānprayacchāmi vanecara \|*  
*yadi me yā cikīrṣā syātkathaṃ nāgamyate'dhunā \|\| 26 \|\|*

26 Then O forester, how can I simply give away the arrow, if what I am desirous of doing is not being understood?

## \|\| 7.40.27-28 \|\|

*yathāgacchatu te bhartā kimarthaṃ bhāṣate'dhunā \|*  
*āgatya ca mayā sārddhaṃ jitvā yuddhe ca māmpunaḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

*nītvā bāṇamimaṃ bhilla svāmī te vāhinīpatiḥ \|*  
*nijālayaṃ sukhaṃ yātu vilaṃbaḥ kriyate katham \|\| 28 \|\|*

27-28. Why should there be a long talk? Let your master come and fight with me. Defeating me in the battle, let him take the arrow and return to his abode. Why should there be any delay?

## \|\| 7.40.29 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*maheśvarakṛpāprāptasadbalasyārjunasya hi \|*  
*ityetadvacanaṃ śrutvā bhillo vākyamathābravīt \|\| 29 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

29\. On hearing these words of Arjuna who derived his strength from the mercy of lord Śiva, the forester spoke these words.

## \|\| 7.40.30 \|\|

*bhilla uvāca \|*  
*ajñosi tvaṃ ṛṣirnāsi maraṇaṃ tvīhase katham \|*  
*dehi bāṇaṃ sukhantiṣṭha tvanyathā kleśabhāgbhaveḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

The forester said:—

30\. You are ignorant. You are not a sage. Why do you invite death? Give up the arrow and stay here happily or you will come to grief.

## \|\| 7.40.31 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityuktastena bhillena śivasacchaktiśobhinā \|*  
*gaṇena pāṇḍavastaṃ ca prāha smṛtvā ca śaṅkaram \|\| 31 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

31\. Thus challenged by the forester who derived his strength from Śiva and who was a Gaṇa, Arjuna, son of Pāṇḍu remembered Śiva and said.

## \|\| 7.40.32 \|\|

*arjuna uvāca \|*  
*madvākyantattvato bhilla śṛṇu tvaṃ ca vanecara \|*  
*āgamiṣyati te svāmī darśayiṣye phalantadā \|\| 32 \|\|*

Arjuna said:—

32\. O forester, listen attentively to my words. If your master comes here I shall show my strength.

## \|\| 7.40.33 \|\|

*na śobhate tvayā yuddhaṃ kariṣye svāminā tava \|*  
*upahāsakaraṃ jñeyaṃ yuddhaṃ siṃhasṛgālayoḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. It does not behove me to fight with you. I shall fight with your master. The fight between a lion and a jackal is very ridiculous.

## \|\| 7.40.34 \|\|

*śrutaṃ ca madvacaste'dya drakṣyasi tvaṃ mahābalam \|*  
*gaccha svasvāminaṃ bhilla yathecchasi tathā kuru \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. You have heard my words. You shall see my strength now. O Bhilla, return to your master. Do as you please.

## \|\| 7.40.35 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityuktastu gatastatra bhillaḥ pārthena vai mune \|*  
*śivāvatāro yatrāste kirāto vāhinīpatiḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

35\. O sage, thus addressed by Arjuna, the Bhilla returned to the place where the general Kirāta, the incarnation of Śiva, stood.

## \|\| 7.40.36 \|\|

*athārjunasya vacanaṃ bhillanāthāya vistarāt \|*  
*sarvaṃ nivedayāmāsa tasyai bhillaparātmane \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. He mentioned Arjuna’s talk in detail to lord Śiva, the great soul in the form of Bhilla.

## \|\| 7.40.37 \|\|

*sa kirāteśvaraḥ śrutvā tadvaco harṣamāgataḥ \|*  
*ājagāma svasainyena śaṃkaro bhillarūpadhṛk \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. On hearing his words, lord Kirāta was much delighted. It was Śiva dressed as a Bhilla who had come there with his army.

## \|\| 7.40.38 \|\|

*arjunaśca tadā senāṃ kirātasya ca pāṇḍavaḥ \|*  
*dṛṣṭvā gṛhītvā saśarandhanuḥ sanmukha āyayau \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. On seeing Kirāta with his army, Arjuna, son of Pāṇdu, took up his bow and arrows and faced him.

## \|\| 7.40.39 \|\|

*atho kirātaśca punaḥ preṣayāmāsa taṃ caram \|*  
*tanmukhena jagau vākyambhāratāya mahātmane \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. Then Kirāta sent the messenger again and through him delivered the message to Arjuna, the great soul.

## \|\| 7.40.40 \|\|

*kirāta uvāca \|*  
*paśya sainyaṃ tapasvistvaṃ muñca bāṇaṃ vrajādhunā \|*  
*maraṇaṃ svalpakāryārthaṃ kathamicchasi sāmpratam \|\| 40 \|\|*

Kirāta said:—

40\. O sage, see this army coming. Give up the arrow and go away. Why do you seek death for a trifle.

## \|\| 7.40.41 \|\|

*bhrātarastava duḥkhārttāḥ kalatraṃ ca tataḥ param \|*  
*pṛthivī hastatastedya yāsyatīti matirmama \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. Your brothers are in distress. Your wife is grief-stricken. I think your kingdom will slip off your hands, I am sure.

## \|\| 7.40.42 \|\|

*\|\| nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityuktaṃ parameśena pārthadārḍhyaparīkṣayā \|*  
*sarvathārjunarakṣārthaṃ dhṛtarūpeṇa śaṃbhunā \|\| 42 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

42\. It was just to test the firmness of Arjuna that the great lord said this, the great lord Śiva who had assumed that form to protect Arjuna by all means.

## \|\| 7.40.43 \|\|

*ityuktastu tadāgatya sagaṇaśśaṃkaraśca tat \|*  
*vistarādvṛttamakhilamarjunāya nyavedayat \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. What the lord said, the Gaṇa of Śiva mentioned that to Arjuna in detail after approaching him.

## \|\| 7.40.44 \|\|

*tacchrutvā tu punaḥ prāha prārthastaṃ dūtamāgatam \|*  
*vāhinīpataye vācyamviparītambhaviṣyati \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. On hearing that, Arjuna replied to the messenger “You shall tell your general that the reverse of what he says will happen to him.

## \|\| 7.40.45 \|\|

*yadyahaṃ caiva te bāṇaṃ yacchāmi ca madīyakam \|*  
*kulasya dūṣaṇaṃ cāhaṃ bhaviṣyāmi na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. If I were to give up the arrow that belongs to me I shall be the cause of ignominy to my family.

## \|\| 7.40.46 \|\|

*bhrātaraścaiva dukhārtāḥ bhavantu ca tathā dhruvam \|*  
*vidyāśca niṣphalāḥ syustāstasmādāgaccha vai dhruvam \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. Well may my brothers be distressed. Let all my knowledge be fruitless. So, come on to fight with me.

## \|\| 7.40.47 \|\|

*siṃhaścaiva śṛgālādvā bhīto naiva mayā śrutaḥ \|*  
*tathā vanecarādrājā na bibheti kadācana \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. No lion is ever frightened of a jackal. A king is never afraid of a forester.”

## \|\| 7.40.48 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityuktastaṃ punargatvā svāminaṃ pāṇḍavena saḥ \|*  
*sarvaṃ nivedayāmāsa taduktaṃ hi viśeṣataḥ \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. Thus replied by Arjuna the Bhilla returned to his master and conveyed all that Arjuna had said.

## \|\| 7.40.49 \|\|

*atha sopi kirātāhvo mahādevassasainyakaḥ \|*  
*tacchrutvā sainyasaṃyukto hyarjunaṃ cāgamattadā \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. Lord Śiva, a Kirāta, heard the message and approached Arjuna along with his army.

## Chapter 41 - The incarnation of lord Śiva as Kirāta

## \|\| 7.41.1 \|\|

*tamāgatantato dṛṣṭvā dhyānaṃ kṛtvā śivasya saḥ \|*  
*gatvā tatrārjunastena yuddhaṃ cakre sudāruṇam \|\| 1 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

1\. On seeing him come, Arjuna meditated on Śiva and fought with him a terrible battle.

## \|\| 7.41.2 \|\|

*gaṇaiśca vividhaistīkṣṇairāyudhaistaṃ nyapīḍayat \|*  
*taistadā pīḍitaḥ pārthaḥ sasmāra svāminaṃ śivam \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. He was severely afflicted by the Gaṇas with many sharp weapons. Thus tortured by them Arjuna remembered lord Śiva.

## \|\| 7.41.3 \|\|

*arjunaśca tadā teṣāṃ bāṇāvalimathācchinat \|*  
*yadāyuddhaṃ ca taiḥ kṣiptaṃ tataḥ śarvaṃ parāmṛśat \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Arjuna split the arrows discharged by Kirāta and his gaṇas. Then he thought upon Śiva.

## \|\| 7.41.4 \|\|

*pīḍitāste gaṇāstena yayuścaiva diśo daśa \|*  
*gaṇeśā vāritāste ca nājagmussvāminamprati \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. When distressed by him, the Gaṇas fled to the quarters. Then Śiva summoned the chief Gaṇas. But they did not respond to his call.

## \|\| 7.41.5 \|\|

*śivaścaivārjunaścaiva yuyudhāte parasparam \|*  
*nānāvidhaiścāyudhairhi mahābalaparākramau \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Śiva and Arjuna of great strength and exploit fought each other with various weapons of war.

## \|\| 7.41.6 \|\|

*śivo'pi manasā nūnaṃ dayāṃ kṛtvārjunaṃ hyagāt \|*  
*arjunaśca dṛḍhaṃ tatra prahāraṃ kṛtavāṃstadā \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Taking pity on him Śiva approached Arjuna, but Arjuna hit him heavily.

## \|\| 7.41.7 \|\|

*āyudhāni śivasso vai hyarjunasyācchinattadā \|*  
*kavacāni ca sarvāṇi śarīraṃ kevalaṃ sthitam \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Śiva split the weapons of Arjuna as well as his armour. Arjuna stood in his bare body.

## \|\| 7.41.8 \|\|

*tadārjunaḥ śivaṃ smṛtvā mallayuddhaṃ cakāra saḥ \|*  
*vāhinīpatinā tena bhayātkliṣṭopi dhairyavān \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Although afflicted by fear, Arjuna remembered Śiva and fought a duel with the general spiritedly.

## \|\| 7.41.9 \|\|

*tadyuddhena mahī sarvā cakaṃpe sasamudrakā \|*  
*devā duḥkhaṃ samāpannaḥ kiṃ bhaviṣyati vā punaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. The earth along with the oceans shook due to their fight. “What will happen now?” Thinking thus, the gods were distressed.

## \|\| 7.41.10 \|\|

*etasmitraṃtare devaḥ śivo gaganamāsthitaḥ \|*  
*yuddhaṃ cakāra tatrasthassorjunaśca tathā'karot \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. In the meantime, lord Śiva stood in mid-air and fought. Arjuna too did likewise.

## \|\| 7.41.11 \|\|

*uḍḍīyoḍḍīya tau yuddhaṃ cakraturdevapārthivau \|*  
*devāśca vismayaṃ prāpū raṇaṃ dṛṣṭvā tadādbhutam \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Flying at each other, lord Śiva and the earthly king Arjuna fought with each other. On seeing that wonderful battle the gods were surprised.

## \|\| 7.41.12 \|\|

*athārjunottare jñātvā smṛtvā śivapadāṃbujam \|*  
*dadhāra pādayostaṃ vai taddhyānādāptasadbalaḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Then Arjuna came to the ground, remembered the lotus-like feet of Śiva and caught hold of his feet deriving sufficient strength, thanks to his meditation on Śiva.

## \|\| 7.41.13 \|\|

*dhṛtvā pādau tadā tasya bhrāmayāmāsa sorjunaḥ \|*  
*vijahāsa mahādevo bhaktavatsala ūtikṛt \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Catching hold of his feet, Arjuna whirled him. Lord Śiva, favourably disposed towards his disciples, laughed indulging in divine sports.

## \|\| 7.41.14 \|\|

*dātuṃ svadāsatāṃ tasmai bhaktavaśyatayā mune \|*  
*śivenaiva kṛtaṃ hyetaccaritannānyathā bhavet \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. O sage, it was surely done by Śiva himself who is subservient to his devotees. It could not be otherwise as this was just to grant him his devotion.

## \|\| 7.41.15 \|\|

*paścādvihasya tatraiva śaṃkaro rūpama dbhutam \|*  
*darśayāmāsa sahasā bhaktavaśyatayā śubham \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. Then Śiva smiled and revealed his wonderful auspicious form as he is always well disposed to his devotees.

## \|\| 7.41.16-17 \|\|

*yathoktaṃ vedaśāstreṣu purāṇe puruṣottamam \|*  
*vyāsopadiṣṭaṃ dhyānāya tasya yatsarvasiddhidam \|\| 16 \|\|*

*taddṛṣṭvā suṃdaraṃ rūpaṃ dhyānaprāptaṃ śivasya tu \|*  
*babhūva vismitotīva hyarjuno lajjitaḥ svayam \|\| 17 \|\|*

16-17. On seeing that beautiful form, the form of the excellent supreme being as mentioned in the Vedas, Śāstras and Purāṇas, the form which Vyāsa had advised him to meditate upon, the form which bestows alt Siddhis, the form of Śiva, Arjuna was abashed and surprised much.

## \|\| 7.41.18 \|\|

*aho śivaśśivassoya yo me prabhutayā vṛtaḥ \|*  
*trilokeśaḥ svayaṃ sākṣāddhā kṛtaṃ kiṃ mayā'dhunā \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. “Hā, this is Śiva, this is Śiva who has been chosen by me as my lord, the lord of the three worlds himself. O what a thing have I committed now!

## \|\| 7.41.19 \|\|

*prabhorbalavatī māyā māyināmapi mohinī \|*  
*kiṃ kṛtaṃ rūpamācchādya prabhuṇā chalito hyaham \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. Powerful indeed is the Māyā of the lord. It deludes even the wielders of Māyā. Why have I been deceived by the lord who concealed his own form.”

## \|\| 7.41.20 \|\|

*dhiyeti saṃvicāryyaiva sāñjalirnatamastakaḥ \|*  
*praṇanāma prabhuṃ prītyā tadovāca sa khinnadhīḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. After thinking thus within himself he joined his palms in reverence and bending his head he bowed to the lord with pleasure. Then he spoke in a delighted manner.

## \|\| 7.41.21 \|\|

*arjuna uvāca \|\| devadeva mahādeva karuṇākara śaṃkara \|*  
*mamāparādhaḥ sarveśa kṣantavyaśca tvayā punaḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

Arjuna said:—

21\. O great god, O lord of gods, O Śiva the merciful, O lord of all, my fault shall be forgiven by you.

## \|\| 7.41.22 \|\|

*kiṃ kṛtaṃ rūpamācchādya cchalito'smi tvayādhunā \|*  
*dhiṅ māṃ samarakartāraṃ svāminā bhavatā prabho \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. What you have done? By concealing your own form you have deceived me now. Fie on me who fought with you, my master, O lord.

## \|\| 7.41.23 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityevaṃ pāṇḍavassotha paścāttāpamavāpa saḥ \|*  
*pādayornipapātāśu śaṃkarasya mahāprabhoḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

23\. Thus Arjuna, son of Pāṇḍu, repented. He fell at the feet of lord Śiva.

## \|\| 7.41.24 \|\|

*atheśvaraḥ prasannātmā pratyuvācārjunaṃ ca tam \|*  
*samāśvāsyeti bahuśo maheśo bhaktavatsalaḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. Cheering up in various ways the delighted lord Śiva, favourably disposed to his devotees, replied to Arjuna.

## \|\| 7.41.25 \|\|

*śaṃkara uvāca \|*  
*na khidya pārtha bhaktosi mama tvaṃ hi viśeṣataḥ \|*  
*parīkṣārthaṃ mayā te'dya kṛtamevaṃ śucañjahi \|\| 25 \|\|*

Śiva said:—

25\. O Arjuna, do not worry yourself. You are a great devotee of mine. I have done this just to test you. Give up your sorrow.

## \|\| 7.41.26 \|\|

*naṃdīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityuktvā taṃ svahastābhyāmutthāpya prabhurarjunam \|*  
*vilajjaṃ kārayāmāsa gaṇaiśca svāmino gaṇaiḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

26\. After saying this and lifting Arjuna with his own hands, the lord rid him of his bashfulness through his own lordly qualities.

## \|\| 7.41.27 \|\|

*punaśśivo'rjunamprāha pāṇḍavaṃ vīrasammatam \|*  
*harṣayan sarvathā prītyā śaṃkaro bhaktavatsalaḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Delighting Arjuna, son of Pāṇḍu, honoured by heroes, Śiva, favourably disposed towards his devotees, spoke to him with pleasure.

## \|\| 7.41.28 \|\|

*śiva uvāca \|*  
*he pārtha pāṇḍavaśreṣṭha prasannosmi varaṃ vṛṇu \|*  
*prahāraistāḍanaiste'dya pūjanammānitammayā \|\| 28 \|\|*

Śiva said:—

28\. O Arjuna, foremost among the sons of Pāṇḍu, I am delighted. Choose your boon. By your hits and blows I have been worshipped by you. I consider it so.

## \|\| 7.41.29 \|\|

*icchayā ca kṛtaṃ me'dya nāparādhastavādhunā \|*  
*nādeyaṃ vidyate tubhyaṃ yadicchasi vṛṇīṣva tat \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. This has been done by my own will. The fault is not yours. There is nothing which cannot be given to you. Choose whatever you desire.

## \|\| 7.41.30 \|\|

*te śatruṣu yaśorājyasthāpanāya śubhaṃ kṛtam \|*  
*etadduḥkhaṃ na kartavyaṃ vaiklavyaṃ ca tyajākhilam \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. In order to establish the rule of your fame among your enemies what you have done is auspicious. You shall not be struck with grief. Eschew all your feebleness.

## \|\| 7.41.31 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityuktastvarjunastena prabhuṇā śaṃkareṇa saḥ \|*  
*uvāca śaṃkaraṃ bhaktyā sāvadhānatayā sthitaḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

31\. Thus addressed by lord Śiva, Arjuna stood carefully and spoke to Śiva with devotion.

## \|\| 7.41.32 \|\|

*arjuna uvāca \|*  
*bhaktapriyasya śambhoste suprabho kiṃ samīhitam \|*  
*varṇanīyaṃ mayā deva kṛpālustvaṃ sadāśiva \|\| 32 \|\|*

Arjuna said:—

32\. O good lord, O Sadāśiva, is your desire, is your compassion to be described by me? You are the favourite of your devotees, you are Śiva, the bestower of welfare.

## \|\| 7.41.33 \|\|

*ityuktvā saṃstutiṃ tasya śaṃkarasya mahāprabhoḥ \|*  
*cakāra pāṇḍavassotha sadbhaktiṃ vedasaṃmatām \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. After saying this, the son of Pāṇḍu eulogised Śiva the great lord, devotedly, in the manner approved of by the Vedas.

## \|\| 7.41.34 \|\|

*arjuna uvāca \|*  
*namaste devadevāya namaḥ kailāsavāsine \|*  
*sadāśiva namastubhyaṃ pañcavaktrāya te namaḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. “Obeisance to you the lord of gods. Obeisance to you the resident of Kailāsa. O Sadāśiva, obeisance to you. Obeisance to you, the five-faced one.

## \|\| 7.41.35 \|\|

*kapardine namastubhyantrinetrāya namo'stu te \|*  
*manaḥ prasannarūpāya sahasravadanāya ca \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. Obeisance to Kapardin (one with matted hair), obeisance to you, the three-eyed. Obeisance to the delighted one, obeisance to the thousand-faced.

## \|\| 7.41.36 \|\|

*nīlakaṃṭha namastestu sadyojātāya vai namaḥ \|*  
*vṛṣadhvaja namastestu vāmāṃgagirijāya ca \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. O blue-necked one, obeisance to you. Obeisance to Sadyojāta. O bull-bannered one, obeisance to you with Pārvatī as your left half.

## \|\| 7.41.37 \|\|

*daśadoṣa namastubhyannamaste paramātmane \|*  
*ḍamarukapālahastāya namaste muṇḍamāline \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. O ten-armed one, Obeisance to you the great soul. Obeisance to you holding the drum and the skull in your hands. Obeisance to you, the one wearing a garland of skulls.

## \|\| 7.41.38 \|\|

*śuddhasphaṭikasaṃkāśaśuddhakarpūravarṣmaṇe \|*  
*pinākapāṇaye tubhyantriśūlavaradhāriṇe \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Obeisance to you with the body refulgent like pure crystal and pure camphor, obeisance to you with the Pināka in your hand, obeisance to the bearer of the excellent Trident.

## \|\| 7.41.39 \|\|

*vyāghracarmottarīyāya gajāmbaravidhāriṇe \|*  
*nāgāṃgāya namastubhyaṃ gaṃgādhara namostu te \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. Obeisance to the one with the tiger hide\[2\] as the upper cloth; obeisance to the wearer of elephant’s hide. Obeisance to you with your body encircled by the serpents. Obeisance to you the supporter of Gaṅgā.

## \|\| 7.41.40 \|\|

*supādāya namaste'stu āraktacaraṇāya ca \|*  
*nandyādigaṇasevyāya gaṇeśāya ca te namaḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. Obeisance to you of splendid, reddish feet, worthy to be served by Nandin and other Gaṇas. Obeisance to you, the chief of Gaṇas.

## \|\| 7.41.41 \|\|

*namo gaṇeśarūpāya kārtikeyānugāya ca \|*  
*bhaktidāya ca bhaktānāṃ muktidāya namonamaḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. Obeisance to one in the form of Gaṇeśa, followed by Kārttikeya. Obeisance to the bestower of devotion and salvation to the devotees.

## \|\| 7.41.42 \|\|

*aguṇāya namastestu saguṇāya namonamaḥ \|*  
*arūpāya sarūpāya sakalāyākalāya ca \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. Obeisance to you devoid of attributes. Obeisance to you possessed of attributes. Obeisance to one with and without forms, having digits and devoid of digits.

## \|\| 7.41.43 \|\|

*namaḥ kirātarūpāya madanugrahakāriṇe \|*  
*yuddhapriyāya vīrāṇāṃ nānālīlānukāriṇe \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. Obeisance to the one who blessed me in the form of Kirāta. Obeisance to the one who is fond of fighting with heroes, obeisance to the one performing various sports.

## \|\| 7.41.44 \|\|

*yatkiṃciddṛśyate rūpantattejastāvakaṃ smṛtam \|*  
*cidrūpastvaṃ trilokeṣu ramasenvayabhedataḥ \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. Whatever splendid form is seen, its splendour is yours. You are in the form of pure consciousness. You sport about inṣṭhe three worlds in different guises.

## \|\| 7.41.45 \|\|

*guṇānānte na saṃkhyāsti yathā bhūrajasāmiha \|*  
*ākāśe tārakāṇāṃ hi kaṇānāṃ vṛṣṭyapāmapi \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. There is no limit to your excellences as that of the dust particles, or stars in the sky or rain-drops.

## \|\| 7.41.46 \|\|

*na te guṇāstu saṃkhyātuṃ vedā vai sambhavanti hi \|*  
*mandabuddhirahaṃ nātha varṇayāmi kathampunaḥ \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. Even the Vedas cannot enumerate your good qualities. O lord, how can I, deficient in intellect, describe any?

## \|\| 7.41.47 \|\|

*sosi yosi namaste'stu kṛpāṃ kartumihārhasi \|*  
*dāsohaṃ te maheśāna svāmī tvaṃ me maheśvara \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. Whatever be your nature, obeisance be to you. It behoves you to take pity. O lord, I am your slave, O lord Śiva, you arc my master.

## \|\| 7.41.48 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*iti śrutvā vacastasya punaḥ provāca śaṃkara \|*  
*suprasannataro bhūtvā vihasanprabhurarjunam \|\| 48 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

48\. On hearing his words, lord Śiva, who was highly delighted spoke to Arjuna delightedly.

## \|\| 7.41.49 \|\|

*śaṃkara uvāca \|*  
*vacasā kimbahūktena śṛṇuṣva vacanammama \|*  
*śīghraṃ vṛṇu varamputra sarvantacca dadāmi te \|\| 49 \|\|*

Śiva said:—

49\. Of what avail is a long talk? Listen to my words. O son, choose your boon quickly. I shall give you everything.

## \|\| 7.41.50 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityuktaścārjunastena praṇipatya sadāśivam \|*  
*sāñjalirnatakaḥ premṇā provāca gadgadākṣaram \|\| 50 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

50\. Thus urged by him, Arjuna bowed to Sadāśiva with palms joined in reverence and with head bending down. He spoke with words choked with devotion

Arjuna said

## \|\| 7.41.51 \|\|

*arjuna uvāca \|*  
*kiṃ brūyāṃ tvaṃ ca sarveṣāmantaryāmitayā sthitaḥ \|*  
*tathāpi varṇitaṃ me'dya śrūyatāṃ ca tvayā vibho \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. What shall I say? You are the immanent Soul of all. Still let my detailed explanation be heard by you, O lord.

## \|\| 7.41.52 \|\|

*śatrūṇāṃ saṃkaṭaṃ yacca tadgatandarśanāttava \|*  
*aihikīṃ ca parāṃ siddhimprāpnuyāṃ vai tathā kuru \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. Whatever danger I had from enemies has vanished at your sight. Please do that which may confer worldly Siddhis on me.

## \|\| 7.41.53 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityuktvā taṃ namaskṛtya śaṃkarambhaktavatsalam \|*  
*nataskandho'rjunastatra baddhāñjalirupasthitaḥ \|\| 53 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

53\. After saying this and bowing to Śiva who is favourably disposed to his devotees, Arjuna stood by with shoulder drooping down and palms joined in reverence.

## \|\| 7.41.54 \|\|

*śivopi ca tathābhūtañjñātvā pāṇḍavamarjunam \|*  
*nijabhaktavaraṃ svāmī mahātuṣṭo babhūva ha \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. Seeing Arjuna, son of Pāṇḍu, the foremost of his devotees, lord Śiva was extremely glad and satisfied.

## \|\| 7.41.55 \|\|

*astrampāśupataṃ svīyandurjayaṃ sarvadākhilaiḥ \|*  
*dadau tasmai maheśāno vacanaścedamabravīt \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. Lord Śiva gave him Pāśupata missile that could not be thwarted by anyone at any time and spoke thus.

## \|\| 7.41.56 \|\|

*śiva uvāca \|*  
*svaṃ mahāstrammayā dattandurjayastvambhaviṣyati \|*  
*anena sarvaśatrūṇāṃ jayakṛtyamavāpnuhi \|\| 56 \|\|*

Śiva said:—

56\. I have given you my own great missile. You will become invincible thereby. You will reap success over your enemies through this.

## \|\| 7.41.57 \|\|

*kṛṣṇaṃ ca kathayiṣyāmi sāhāyyante kariṣyati \|*  
*sa vai mamātmabhūtaśca madbhaktaḥ kāryyakārakaḥ \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. I shall tell Kṛṣṇa also. He will help you. He is my own soul, my devotee and he carries out my task.

## \|\| 7.41.58 \|\|

*matprabhāvānbhārata tvaṃ rājyannikaṇṭakaṃ kuru \|*  
*dharmyānnānāvidhānbhrātrā kāraya tvaṃ ca sarvadā \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. O Bharata, due to my power, you will rule over your kingdom unhindered. You shall perform righteous deeds of various kinds always along with your brother.

## \|\| 7.41.59 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityuktvā nijahastaṃ ca dhṛtvā śirasi tasya saḥ \|*  
*pūjito hyarjunenāśu śaṃkarontaradhīyata \|\| 59 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

59\. After saying this and placing his hand over his head, Śiva vanished after being duly worshipped by Arjuna.

## \|\| 7.41.60 \|\|

*athārjunaḥ prasannātmā prāpyāstraṃ ca varaṃ prabhoḥ \|*  
*jagāma svāśrame mukhyaṃ smaranbhaktyā guruṃ śivam \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. The delighted Arjuna, after obtaining the excellent missile from the lord, returned to his hermitage remembering devotedly his chief preceptor and Śiva.

## \|\| 7.41.61 \|\|

*sarvve te bhrātaraḥ prītāstanvaḥ prāṇamivāgatam \|*  
*militvā taṃ sukhaṃ prāpurdraupadī cāti suvratā \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. When he arrived, his brother felt fully revived as the body with vital airs. They felt happy at his sight. Draupadī of good rites was also delighted.

## \|\| 7.41.62 \|\|

*śivaṃ paraṃ ca santuṣṭampāṇḍavāḥ sarva eva hi \|*  
*nātṛpyansarvavṛttāntaṃ śrutvā harṣamupāgatāḥ \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. The delighted Pāṇḍavas were not satiated by hearing the details from Arjuna and knowing that Śiva was satisfied.

## \|\| 7.41.63 \|\|

*āśrame puṣpavṛṣṭiśca candanena samanvitā \|*  
*papāta sukarārthaṃ ca teṣāñcaiva mahātmanām \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. There was a shower of flowers and the sandal paste in the hermitage for honouring Arjuna the enemy of the boar and the Pāṇḍavas.

## \|\| 7.41.64 \|\|

*dhanyaṃ ca śaṃkaraṃ caiva namaskṛtya śivammudā \|*  
*avadhiṃ cāgataṃ jñātvā jayaścaiva bhaviṣyati \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. By bowing to Śiva joyously and realising that the time-limit had arrived, they knew that success was at hand.

## \|\| 7.41.65 \|\|

*etasminnantare kṛṣṇaśśrutvārjunamathāgatam \|*  
*melanāya samāyātaśśrutvā sukhamupāgataḥ \|\| 65 \|\|*

65\. In the meantime, on hearing that Arjuna had returned, Kṛṣṇa came there to see him. He was very happy,

## \|\| 7.41.66 \|\|

*ataścaiva mayākhyātaḥ śaṃkaraḥ sarvaduḥkhahā \|*  
*sa sevyate mayā nityaṃ bhavadbhirapi sevyatām \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. Therefore I say that Śiva is the dispeller of all miseries. He is always served by me. Let him be served by you.

## \|\| 7.41.67 \|\|

*ityuktaste kirātāhvovatāraśśaṃkasya vai \|*  
*taṃ śrutvā śrāvayanvāpi sarvānkāmānavāpnuyāt \|\| 67 \|\|*

67\. Thus the incarnation of Śiva as Kirāta has been explained to you. He who hears and narrates this obtains all desires.

## Chapter 42 - The Twelve Jyotirliṅga incarnations

## \|\| 7.42.1 \|\|

*nandīśvara uvāca \|*  
*avatārāñchṛṇu vibhordvādaśapramitānparān \|*  
*jyotirliṅgasvarūpānvai nānoti kārakānmune \|\| 1 \|\|*

Nandīśvara said:—

1\. O sage, now listen to the twelve incarnations of the lord in the form of jyotirliṅgas, the cause of diverse sports.

## \|\| 7.42.2-4 \|\|

*saurāṣṭre somanāthaśca śrīśaile mallikārjunaḥ \|*  
*ujjayinyāṃ mahākāla oṃkāre cāmareśvaraḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

*kedāro himava tpṛṣṭe ḍākinyāmbhīmaśaṃkaraḥ \|*  
*vārāṇasyāṃ ca viśveśastryambako gautamītaṭe \|\| 3 \|\|*

*vaidyanāthaścitābhūmau nāgeśo dārukāvane \|*  
*setubandhe ca rāmeśo ghuśmeśaśca śivālaye \|\| 4 \|\|*

2-4. The jyotirliṅgas\[1\] are—Somanātha in the Saurāṣṭra\[2\] territory; Mallikārjuna on the mountain Śrīśaila\[3\]; Mahākāla in Ujjayinī\[4\]; Amareśvara in Oṃkāra\[5\] Māndhatā; Kedāra\[6\] on the Himalaya; Bhīmaśaṅkara in the city of Dākinī;\[7\] Viśveśvara in the city of Vārāṇasī\[8\]; Tryambaka on the banks of Gautamī\[9\]; Vaidyanātha in the cremation ground\[10\]; Nāgeśa in the forest of Dārukā\[11\]; Rāmeśvara in the Setubandha Ghuśmeśvara in the Śiva temple.

## \|\| 7.42.5 \|\|

*avatāradvādaśakametacchambhoḥ parātmanaḥ \|*  
*sarvānandakaraṃ puṃsāndarśanātsparśanānmune \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. O sage, the twelve incarnations of the supreme soul are wholly delightful to all men for seeing and touching.

## \|\| 7.42.6 \|\|

*tatrādyassomanātho hi candraduḥkhakṣayaṃkaraḥ \|*  
*kṣayakuṣṭhādirogāṇāṃ nāśakaḥ pūjanānmune \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. There, the first one, Somanātha, annihilated the distress of the moon. O sage, by worshipping, it dispels the ailments of consumption, leprosy etc.

## \|\| 7.42.7 \|\|

*śivāvatārassomeśo liṃgarūpeṇa saṃsthitaḥ \|*  
*saurāṣṭre śubhadeśe ca śaśinā pūjitaḥ purā \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. This incarnation of Śiva, Someśa, is stationed in the auspicious land of Saurāṣṭra, in the form of a phallic image. It was worshipped by the moon formerly.

## \|\| 7.42.8 \|\|

*caṃdrakuṇḍaṃ ca tatraiva sarvapāpavināśakam \|*  
*tatra snātvā naro dhīmānsarvarogaiḥ pramucyate \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Candarakuṇḍa (the pond of the Moon) is there itself and it destroys all sins. By bathing there a man becomes intelligent and gets rid of his ailments.

## \|\| 7.42.9 \|\|

*someśvaraṃ mahāliṃgaṃ śivasya paramātmakam \|*  
*dṛṣṭvā pramucyate pāpādbhuktiṃ muktiṃ ca vindati \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. On seeing Someśvara, the great phallic image of Śīva, people are freed from sin. They attain worldly pleasures and salvation.

## \|\| 7.42.10 \|\|

*mallikārjunasaṃjñaścāvatāraśśaṃkarasya vai \|*  
*dvitīyaḥ śrīgirau tāta bhaktābhīṣṭaphalapradaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. O dear, the second incarnation of Śiva Mallikārjuna, on the mountain, Śrīśaila, is the bestower of the desires of devotees.

## \|\| 7.42.11 \|\|

*saṃstuto liṃgarūpeṇa sutadarśanahetutaḥ \|*  
*gatastatra mahāprītyā sa śivaḥ svagirermune \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. O sage, Śiva went there from his mountain, with great pleasure in order to see his son Kārttikeya. It is stationed there in the form of the phallic image.

## \|\| 7.42.12 \|\|

*jyotirliṃgaṃ dvitīyantaddarśanātpūjanānmune \|*  
*mahāsukhakaraṃ cānte muktidannātra saṃśayaḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. It is the second Jyotirliṅga. O sage, it, is very pleasing. By seeing and worshipping it one derives salvation hereafter. There is no doubt in this.

## \|\| 7.42.13 \|\|

*mahākālābhidhastātāvatāraśśaṃkarasya vai \|*  
*ujjayinyāṃ nagaryyāṃ ca babhūva svajanāvanaḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. O dear, Śiva incarnated himself as Mahākāla in the city of Ujjayinī for the protection of his devotees.

## \|\| 7.42.14-15 \|\|

*dūṣaṇākhyāsuraṃ yastu vedadharmapramardakam \|\| \|*  
*ujjayinyāṃ gataṃ vipradveṣiṇaṃ sarvanāśanam \|\| 14 \|\|*

*vedaviprasutadhyāto huṅkāreṇaiva sa drutam \|*  
*bhasmasātkṛtavāṃstaṃ ca ratnamāla nivāsinam \|\| 15 \|\|*

14-15. The demon Dūṣaṇa, a resident of Ratnamāla, was a defiler of Vedic rites, a hater of brahmins and destroyer of everyone. When he reached Ujjayinī, the lord was meditated upon by the sons of the Vedic brahmins. He reduced the demon to ashes by a mere Huṃkāra.

## \|\| 7.42.16 \|\|

*taṃ hatvā sa mahākālo jyotirliṃgasvarūpataḥ \|*  
*devaissa prārthito'tiṣṭhatsvabhaktaparipālakaḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. After killing him, on being requested by the gods; lord Mahākāla stayed there in the form of a Jyotirliṅga. He continues to protect his devotees.

## \|\| 7.42.17 \|\|

*mahākālāhvayaṃ liṃgaṃ dṛṣṭvābhyarcya prayatnataḥ \|*  
*sarvānkāmānavāpnoti labhate parato gatim \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. By seeing and strenuously worshipping the phallic image of Mahākāla, the devotee attains his desires and the greatest goal.

## \|\| 7.42.18 \|\|

*oṅkāraḥ parameśāno dhṛtaḥ śambho parātmanaḥ \|*  
*avatāraścaturtho hi bhaktābhīṣṭaphalapradaḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. The fourth incarnation of Śiva the supreme soul, the great lord in Oṃkāra Māndhātā is a bestower of the desires of his devotees.

## \|\| 7.42.19 \|\|

*vidhinā sthāpito bhaktyā svaliṃgātpārthivānmune \|*  
*prādurbhūto mahādevo vindhyakāmaprapūrakaḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. O sage, lord Śiva manifested himself from his earthen phallic image devoutly installed by Brahmā and he fulfilled the desires of the mountain.

## \|\| 7.42.20 \|\|

*devaissaṃprārthitastatra dvidhārūpeṇa saṃsthitaḥ \|*  
*bhuktimuktiprado liṃgarūpo vai śaktavatsala \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. On being requested by the gods the lord in the form of his phallic image stayed there two-fold, bestowing worldly pleasures and salvation and being favourably disposed towards his devotees.

## \|\| 7.42.21 \|\|

*praṇave caiva coṃkāranāmāsīlliṃgamuttamam \|*  
*parameśvaranāmāsītpārthivaśca munīśvara \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. The excellent phallic image abides in the syllable Oṃkāra. O great sage, it also abides in the earthen phallic image in Oṃkāra Māndhātā.

## \|\| 7.42.22 \|\|

*bhaktābhīṣṭaprado jñeyo yopi dṛṣṭorcito mune \|*  
*jyotirliṃge mahādivye varṇite te mahāmune \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. O sage, O great sage, whatever is seen or worshipped ill the extremely divine phallic image described to you is the bestower of the desires of all devotees.

## \|\| 7.42.23 \|\|

*kedāreśovatārastu paṃcamaḥ paramaśśivaḥ \|*  
*jyotirliṃgasvarūpeṇa kedāre saṃsthitasya ca \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. The fifth great incarnation of Śiva is the Kedāreśā. He is stationed in Kedāra in the form of Jyotirliṅga.

## \|\| 7.42.24 \|\|

*naranārāyaṇākhyau yāvavatārau harermune \|*  
*tatprārthitaśśivastatsthaiḥ kedāre himabhūdhare \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. It was at the request of the twofold incarnations of Viṣṇu, named Nara and Nārāyaṇa that Śiva stayed in Kedāra on the mountain Himavat, O sage.

## \|\| 7.42.25 \|\|

*tābhyāṃ ca pūjito nityaṃ kedāreśvarasaṃjñakaḥ \|*  
*bhaktābhīṣṭapradaḥ śambhurdarśanādarcanādapi \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Śiva named Kedāreśvara was worshipped everyday by them. He is the bestower of the desires of the devotees, by his sight and worship.

## \|\| 7.42.26 \|\|

*asya khaṇḍasya sa svāmī sarveśopi viśeṣataḥ \|*  
*sarvakāmapradastāta sovatāraśśivasya vai \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Although he is the lord of all, he is the particular reigning deity of this territory. O dear that incarnation of Śiva is the bestower of all desires.

## \|\| 7.42.27 \|\|

*bhīmaśaṃkarasaṃjñastu ṣaṣṭhaḥ śambhormahāprabhoḥ \|*  
*avatāro mahālīlo bhīmāsuravināśanaḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. The sixth incarnation of Śiva, the great lord named Bhīmaśaṅkara, performed great sports. He destroyed the demon Bhīma.

## \|\| 7.42.28 \|\|

*sudakṣiṇābhidhambhaktaṅkāmarūpeśvarannṛpam \|*  
*yo rarakṣādbhutaṃ hatvāsurantaṃ bhaktaduḥkhadam \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. After killing this wonderfully lusty demon who caused misery to the devotees, the lord saved the devotee Sudakṣiṇa who was the king of Kāmarūpa.

## \|\| 7.42.29 \|\|

*bhīmaśaṅkaranāmā sa ḍākinyāṃ saṃsthitassvayam \|*  
*jyotirliṃgasvarūpeṇa prārthitastena śaṃkaraḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. Requested by him Śiva himself in the name of Bhimaśaṅkara stayed in Ḍākinī in the form of Jyotirliṅga.

## \|\| 7.42.30 \|\|

*viśveśvarāvatārastu kāśyāṃ jāto hi saptamaḥ \|*  
*sarvabrahmāṇḍarūpaśca bhuktimuktiprado mune \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. O sage, the seventh incarnation as Viśveśvara at Kāśī is in the form of the entire cosmic egg. It yields worldly pleasures and salvation.

## \|\| 7.42.31 \|\|

*pūjitassarvadevaiśca bhaktyā viṣṇvādibhissadā \|*  
*kailāsapatinā cāpi bhairaveṇāpi nityaśaḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. He is always worshipped by Viṣṇu and all other gods as well as Kubera and Bhairava.

## \|\| 7.42.32 \|\|

*jyotirliṃgasvarūpeṇa saṃsthitastatra muktidaḥ \|*  
*svayaṃ siddhasvarūpo hi tathā svapuri sa prabhuḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. He is stationed there in the form of Jyotirliṅga. In his own city the lord is in the form of Siddha and he confers salvation.

## \|\| 7.42.33 \|\|

*kāśīviśveśayorbhaktyā tannāmajapakārakāḥ \|*  
*nirliptāḥ karmabhirnnityaṃ kevalyapadabhāginaḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. Those who repeat the names of Kāśī and Viśvśa and perform their Japa with devotion become unaffected by actions and reach the state of Kaivalya.

## \|\| 7.42.34 \|\|

*tryaṃbakākhyo'vatāro yaḥ soṣṭamo gautamītaṭe \|*  
*prārthito gautamenāvirbabhūva śaśimaulinaḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. The eighth incarnation Tryambaka of the moon-crested lord Śiva made its appearance on the banks of the river Gautamī on being requested by the sage Gautama.

## \|\| 7.42.35 \|\|

*gautamasya prārthanayā jyotirliṃga svarūpataḥ \|*  
*sthitastatrācalaḥ prītyā tanmuneḥ prītikāmyayā \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. At the request of Gautama and with a desire to delight that sage, the lord stood there motionless in the form of Jyotirliṅga.

## \|\| 7.42.36 \|\|

*tasya sandarśanātsparśāddarśanācca maheśituḥ \|*  
*sarve kāmāḥ prasidhyanti tato muktirbhavedaho \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. All desires fructify at the sight and touch of the great lord. Thereafter salvation too is attained.

## \|\| 7.42.37 \|\|

*śivānugrahatastatra gaṃgā nāmnā tu gautamī \|*  
*saṃsthitā gautamaprītyā pāvanī śaṃkarapriyā \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. With the blessings of Śiva, and pleased with Gautama, the holy river Gaṅgā, the beloved of Śiva, stayed there in the name of Gautamī.

## \|\| 7.42.38 \|\|

*vaidyanāthāvatāro hi navamastatra kīrtitaḥ \|*  
*āvirbhūto rāvaṇārthaṃ bahulīlākaraḥ prabhuḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. The ninth incarnation is glorified as Vaidyanātha. The lord indulging in many sports manifested himself for the sake of Rāvaṇa.

## \|\| 7.42.39 \|\|

*tadānayanarūpaṃ hi vyājaṃ kṛtvā maheśvaraḥ \|*  
*jyotirliṃgasvarūpeṇa citābhūmau pratiṣṭhitaḥ \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. Under the pretext of fetching him, lord Śiva was installed in the cremation ground in the form of Jyotirliṅga.

## \|\| 7.42.40 \|\|

*vaidyanātheśvaro nāmnā prasiddhobhūjjagattraye \|*  
*darśanātpūjanādbhaktyā bhuktimuktipradaḥ sa hi \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. The lord in the name of Vaidyanātha became famous in the three worlds. Seeing and worshipping him with devotion yields worldly pleasures and salvation.

## \|\| 7.42.41 \|\|

*vaidyanātheśvaraśivamāhātmyamanuśāsanam \|*  
*paṭhatāṃ śṛṇvatāṃ cāpi bhuktimuktipradaṃ mune \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. O sage, this benedictory passage referring to the greatness of Śiva in the name of Vaidyanātheśvara yields worldly pleasures and salvation to those who read and listen.

## \|\| 7.42.42 \|\|

*nāgeśvarāvatārastu daśamaḥ parikīrtitaḥ \|*  
*āvirbhūtaḥ svabhaktārthaṃ duṣṭānāṃ daṇḍadassadā \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. The incarnation of Nāgeśvara is glorified as the tenth one. The lord manifested himself as the chastiser of the wicked for his devotees.

## \|\| 7.42.43 \|\|

*hatvā dārukanāmānaṃ rākṣasandharmaghātakam \|*  
*svabhaktaṃ vaiśyanāthaṃ ca prārakṣatsupriyābhidham \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. He killed the Rākṣasa Dāruka, a violator of virtue. He saved his devotee Supriya who was a merchant king.

## \|\| 7.42.44 \|\|

*lokānāmupakārārthaṃ jyotirliṃgasvarūpadhṛk \|*  
*santasthau sāṃbikaśśambhurbahulīlākaraḥ paraḥ \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. In order to help all the worlds, Śiva, the great indulgent in sports, remained there assuming the form of Jyotirliṅga, accompanied by Pārvatī.

## \|\| 7.42.45 \|\|

*taddṛṣṭvā śivaliṃgantu mune nāgeśvarābhidham \|*  
*vinaśyanti drutaṃ cārcya mahāpātakarāśayaḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. O sage, at the sight of Śiva’s phallic image named Nāgeśvara and thanks to its worship, heaps of great sins perish immediately.

## \|\| 7.42.46 \|\|

*rāmeśvarāvatārastu śivasyaikādaśaḥ smṛtaḥ \|*  
*rāmacandrapriyakaro rāmasaṃsthāpito mune \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. O sage, the incarnation of Śiva is the eleventh one. It was pleasing to Rāma and it was installed by him.

## \|\| 7.42.47 \|\|

*dadau jayavaraṃ prītyā yo rāmāya sutoṣitaḥ \|*  
*āvirbhūtasya liṃgastu śaṃkaro bhaktavatsalaḥ \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. Duly propitiated by Rāma, Śiva who is favourably disposed towards his devotees manifested himself in the phallic image and granted the boon of victory to him.

## \|\| 7.42.48 \|\|

*rāmeṇa prārthito'tyarthaṃ jyotirliṃgasvarūpataḥ \|*  
*santasthau setubandhe ca rāmasaṃsevito mune \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. O sage, the lord, implored ardently by Rāma and propitiated by Rāma stayed at Setubandha in the form of Jyotirliṅga.

## \|\| 7.42.49 \|\|

*rāmeśvarasya mahimādbhuto'bhūdbhuvi cātulaḥ \|*  
*bhuktimuktipradaścaiva sarvadā bhaktakāmadaḥ \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. The grandeur of Rāmeśvara in the world was wonderful and incomparable, yielding worldly pleasures and salvation and bestowing the desires of the devotees ever.

## \|\| 7.42.50 \|\|

*taṃ ca gaṃgājalenaiva snāpayiṣyati yo naraḥ \|*  
*rāmeśvaraṃ ca sadbhaktyā sa jīvanmukta eva hi \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. The man who performs the ablution of Rāmeśvara is really a living liberated soul.

## \|\| 7.42.51 \|\|

*iha bhuktvākhilānbhogāndevatādurllabhānapi \|*  
*ataḥ prāpya paraṃ jñānaṃ kaivalyaṃ mokṣamāpnuyāt \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. After enjoying all worldly pleasures here, and then getting perfect knowledge, he shall obtain salvation hereafter.

## \|\| 7.42.52 \|\|

*ghuśmeśvarāvatārastu dvādaśaśśaṃkarasya hi \|*  
*nānālīlākaro ghuśmānandado bhaktavatsalaḥ \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. The incarnation of Śiva as Ghuśmeśvara is the twelfth wherein the lord indulged in different sports and conferred bliss on Ghuśmā. He is favourably disposed towards his devotees.

## \|\| 7.42.53 \|\|

*dakṣiṇasyāndiśi mune devaśailasamīpataḥ \|*  
*āvirbabhūva sarasi ghuśmāpriyakaraḥ prabhuḥ \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. O sage, the lord manifested himself in a lake near the Devaśaila in the southern quarter acting favourably to Ghuśmā.

## \|\| 7.42.54 \|\|

*sudehyamāritaṃ ghuśmāputraṃ sākalyato mune \|*  
*tuṣṭastadbhaktitaśśambhuryorakṣadbhaktavatsalaḥ \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. O sage, Śiva who is favourably disposed towards his devotees entirely revived the son of Ghuśmā who was killed by Sudehya. He was so pleased with her devotion.

## \|\| 7.42.55 \|\|

*tatprārthitassa vai śambhustaḍāge tatra kāmadāḥ \|*  
*jyotirliṃga svarūpeṇa tasthau ghuśmeśvarābhidhaḥ \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. At her request, Śiva stayed in the lake in the form of Jyotirliṅga Ghuśmeśvara conferring desires to the devotees.

## \|\| 7.42.56 \|\|

*tandṛṣṭvā śivaliṃgantu samabhyarcya ca bhaktitaḥ \|*  
*iha sarvasukhambhuktvā tato muktiṃ ca vindati \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. The man who secs that phallic image and worships it with devotion enjoys happiness here and attains salvation hereafter.

## \|\| 7.42.57 \|\|

*iti te hi samākhyātā jyotirliṃgāvalī mayā \|*  
*dvādaśapramitā divyā bhuktimuktipradāyinī \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. Thus I have explained to you the series of divine Jyotirliṅgas twelve in number yielding both worldly pleasures and salvation.

## \|\| 7.42.58 \|\|

*etāṃ jyotirliṃgakathāṃ yaḥ paṭhecchṛṇuyādapi \|*  
*mucyate sarvapāpebhyo bhuktiṃ muktiṃ ca vindati \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. He who reads or listens to this narrative of Jyotirliṅgas becomes freed of all sins and derives worldly pleasures and salvation.

## \|\| 7.42.59 \|\|

*śatarudrābhidā ceyamvarṇitā saṃhitā mayā \|*  
*śatāvatārasatkīrtissarvakāmaphalapradā \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. Thus I have described the compendium Śatarudra by name which glorifies the hundred incarnations of Śiva and yields the fruits of desires.

## \|\| 7.42.60 \|\|

*imāṃ yaḥ paṭhate nityaṃ śṛṇuyādvā samāhitaḥ \|*  
*sarvānkāmānavāpnoti tato muktiṃ labheddhruvam \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. He who reads this always or listens to it devotedly attains all desires and then becomes liberated, to be sure.
